### Task 176f (loom-ui repo): markdown 4 of 9: blocks (paragraphs, headings, rules, fences, quotes) and `parseChatMarkdown`

**Repo and branch:** `~/workspace/loom-ui`, branch `feat/chat-md-blocks`, cut from 176e, a draft PR, rebase-merged, stacked in the order of the task numbers. Same conventions as Task 176a (loom-ui's `AGENTS.md`; `typecheck:tests` is a gate; renderer packages resolve `@loom-ui/core` from its built `dist`, so build first). The gate is `pnpm build && pnpm typecheck && pnpm typecheck:tests && pnpm test && pnpm lint:fix && pnpm lint`. 

**Why (generic).** The block layer and the public entry of the parser: the text is cleaned with `sanitizeChatText`, split into lines and read as paragraphs, ATX headings, thematic breaks, fenced code and nested blockquotes, with the inline parser of 176d and 176e inside. Lists and tables are the next PR (176g); until then a `- item` line is simply paragraph text. Still unexported (176h).

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/src/chatMdBlock.ts`, `packages/core/src/chatMarkdown.ts`, `packages/core/src/chatMarkdown.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 176c to 176e.
- Produces: `parseChatMarkdown(text: unknown, options?: { markers?: boolean; streaming?: boolean }): MdDoc` (total, linear, never throws; a non-string is an empty document); `parseBlocks(lines, depth, state)` (internal). Block nesting stops at `MD_MAX_DEPTH` (deeper `>` is text); an unclosed fence runs to the end of its container; indented text is a paragraph, not code; html is text.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `packages/core/src/chatMarkdown.test.ts`:

`````ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { type MdBlock, type MdInline, mdPlainText, parseChatMarkdown } from "./chatMarkdown";

const doc = (t: string, o: { markers?: boolean; streaming?: boolean } = {}) =>
  parseChatMarkdown(t, o);
const first = (t: string, o = {}): MdBlock => doc(t, o).blocks[0] as MdBlock;
const txt = (text: string): MdInline => ({ type: "text", text });

describe("paragraphs, headings, rules", () => {
  it("splits paragraphs on a blank line and keeps a soft break inside one", () => {
    expect(doc("a\nb\n\nc").blocks).toEqual([
      { type: "paragraph", inlines: [txt("a\nb")] },
      { type: "paragraph", inlines: [txt("c")] },
    ]);
  });
  it("reads ATX headings only, with a closing run of # removed", () => {
    expect(first("## Title ##")).toEqual({ type: "heading", level: 2, inlines: [txt("Title")] });
    expect(first("#nospace").type).toBe("paragraph");
    expect(first("####### seven").type).toBe("paragraph");
    expect(first("Title\n=====").type).toBe("paragraph");
  });
  it("reads a thematic break", () => {
    expect(doc("a\n\n---\n\nb").blocks.map((b) => b.type)).toEqual([
      "paragraph",
      "rule",
      "paragraph",
    ]);
    expect(doc("* * *").blocks).toEqual([{ type: "rule" }]);
  });
  it("treats indented text as a paragraph, not code, and html as text", () => {
    expect(first("    indented").type).toBe("paragraph");
    expect(first("<div>\nx\n</div>")).toEqual({
      type: "paragraph",
      inlines: [txt("<div>\nx\n</div>")],
    });
  });
});

describe("fenced code", () => {
  it("keeps its text verbatim (no markup, no markers) and takes a safe language word", () => {
    expect(first("```ts\nconst a = **x**;\n```")).toEqual({
      type: "code",
      lang: "ts",
      text: "const a = **x**;",
    });
    expect(first("```\na [[a1]] <b>\n```", { markers: true })).toEqual({
      type: "code",
      text: "a [[a1]] <b>",
    });
    expect(first("```<img onerror=1>\nx\n```")).toEqual({ type: "code", text: "x" });
    expect(first("~~~py extra words\nx\n~~~")).toMatchObject({ type: "code", lang: "py" });
  });
  it("runs an unclosed fence to the end, which is what a half-written one looks like", () => {
    expect(first("```js\nlet a")).toEqual({ type: "code", lang: "js", text: "let a" });
    expect(first("````\n```\nstill code\n````")).toEqual({ type: "code", text: "```\nstill code" });
  });
});

describe("quotes", () => {
  it("nests, and stops nesting at MD_MAX_DEPTH (the rest is text)", () => {
    expect(first("> a\n> b")).toMatchObject({ type: "quote", blocks: [{ type: "paragraph" }] });
    let b: MdBlock | undefined = doc(`${"> ".repeat(20)}x`).blocks[0];
    let depth = 0;
    while (b?.type === "quote") {
      depth++;
      b = b.blocks[0];
    }
    expect(depth).toBe(6);
    expect(b?.type).toBe("paragraph");
  });
});

describe("the whole parse", () => {
  it("is total: non-strings are empty and controls are gone", () => {
    expect(parseChatMarkdown(undefined)).toEqual({ blocks: [], truncated: false });
    const dirty = doc(`a${String.fromCharCode(27)}[2Jb${String.fromCharCode(0x202e)}`);
    expect(JSON.stringify(dirty)).toBe(JSON.stringify(doc("a[2Jb")));
  });
  it("cuts at MD_MAX_NODES and says so", () => {
    const d = doc("a\n\n".repeat(5000));
    expect(d.truncated).toBe(true);
    expect(d.blocks.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(2000);
  });
  it("is linear on adversarial input", () => {
    const cases = [
      "> ".repeat(30000),
      "\n".repeat(60000),
      "```\n".repeat(15000),
      "#".repeat(60000),
      "---\n".repeat(15000),
      "a\n".repeat(30000),
      "    ".repeat(15000),
    ];
    for (const c of cases) {
      const t = Date.now();
      doc(c, { markers: true, streaming: true });
      expect(Date.now() - t, c.slice(0, 6)).toBeLessThan(1500);
    }
  });
  it("settles: every prefix parses, and the whole text equals the settled parse", () => {
    const full =
      "# T\n\nSome **bold** and `code` [[a1]] and [l](https://e.example).\n\n> q\n\n```js\nlet a;\n```\n";
    for (let k = 0; k <= full.length; k++)
      expect(() => doc(full.slice(0, k), { markers: true, streaming: true })).not.toThrow();
    expect(doc(full, { markers: true, streaming: true })).toEqual(doc(full, { markers: true }));
    const half = doc("see [x", { streaming: true }).blocks[0];
    expect(half?.type === "paragraph" && mdPlainText(half.inlines)).toBe("see [x");
  });
});
`````

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it to see it fail.** `pnpm --filter @loom-ui/core exec vitest run src/chatMarkdown.test.ts`. Expected: FAIL (module not found).

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `packages/core/src/chatMdBlock.ts` (the list marker helpers are here because a paragraph must stop before a list; the list and table branches of `parseBlocks` come in 176g):

```ts
import { parseInlines } from "./chatMdInline";
import {
  MD_MAX_DEPTH,
  type MdBlock,
  type MdState,
  take,
} from "./chatMdTypes";

// ── Markdown blocks: fences, headings, rules, quotes, lists, tables, paragraphs (U8c) ─────────────────

// ── blocks ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

const lead = (l: string): number => {
  let k = 0;
  while (l.charCodeAt(k) === 32) k++;
  return k;
};
const blank = (l: string): boolean => lead(l) === l.length;

function fenceOf(l: string): { ch: string; len: number; indent: number; info: string } | undefined {
  const indent = lead(l);
  const ch = l[indent];
  if (indent > 3 || (ch !== "`" && ch !== "~")) return undefined;
  let k = indent;
  while (l[k] === ch) k++;
  const info = l.slice(k).trim();
  if (k - indent < 3 || (ch === "`" && info.includes("`"))) return undefined;
  return { ch, len: k - indent, indent, info };
}
function headingOf(l: string): { level: 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6; text: string } | undefined {
  const indent = lead(l);
  if (indent > 3) return undefined;
  let k = indent;
  while (l[k] === "#") k++;
  const level = k - indent;
  if (level < 1 || level > 6 || (k < l.length && l[k] !== " ")) return undefined;
  let text = l.slice(k).trim();
  let e = text.length;
  while (e > 0 && text[e - 1] === "#") e--;
  if (e < text.length && (e === 0 || text[e - 1] === " ")) text = text.slice(0, e).trimEnd();
  return { level: level as 1 | 2 | 3 | 4 | 5 | 6, text };
}
function isRule(l: string): boolean {
  if (lead(l) > 3) return false;
  const t = l.trim();
  const ch = t[0];
  if (ch !== "-" && ch !== "*" && ch !== "_") return false;
  let n = 0;
  for (const c of t) {
    if (c === ch) n++;
    else if (c !== " ") return false;
  }
  return n >= 3;
}
function quoteOf(l: string): string | undefined {
  const indent = lead(l);
  if (indent > 3 || l[indent] !== ">") return undefined;
  const rest = l.slice(indent + 1);
  return rest[0] === " " ? rest.slice(1) : rest;
}
interface Marker {
  readonly ordered: boolean;
  readonly start: number;
  readonly ch: string;
  readonly offset: number;
  readonly rest: string;
}
function markerOf(l: string): Marker | undefined {
  const indent = lead(l);
  if (indent > 3 || indent >= l.length) return undefined;
  let k = indent;
  let ordered = false;
  let start = 1;
  let ch = l[k] as string;
  if (ch === "-" || ch === "*" || ch === "+") k++;
  else {
    let d = k;
    while (d < l.length && d - k < 9 && l.charCodeAt(d) >= 48 && l.charCodeAt(d) <= 57) d++;
    if (d === k || (l[d] !== "." && l[d] !== ")")) return undefined;
    ordered = true;
    start = Number(l.slice(k, d));
    ch = l[d] as string;
    k = d + 1;
  }
  if (k < l.length && l[k] !== " ") return undefined;
  let sp = 0;
  while (l[k + sp] === " ") sp++;
  const empty = k + sp >= l.length;
  const pad = empty || sp > 4 ? 1 : sp;
  return { ordered, start, ch, offset: k + pad, rest: empty ? "" : l.slice(k + pad) };
}
function startsBlock(l: string, nest: boolean): boolean {
  if (fenceOf(l) || headingOf(l) || isRule(l)) return true;
  if (!nest) return false;
  if (quoteOf(l) !== undefined) return true;
  const m = markerOf(l);
  return m !== undefined && m.rest.trim() !== "" && (!m.ordered || m.start === 1);
}

export function parseBlocks(lines: readonly string[], depth: number, st: MdState): MdBlock[] {
  const out: MdBlock[] = [];
  const nest = depth < MD_MAX_DEPTH;
  const n = lines.length;
  let i = 0;
  while (i < n) {
    const l = lines[i] as string;
    if (blank(l)) {
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (!take(st)) break;
    const fence = fenceOf(l);
    if (fence) {
      const body: string[] = [];
      for (i++; i < n; i++) {
        const c = lines[i] as string;
        const g = fenceOf(c);
        if (g && g.ch === fence.ch && g.len >= fence.len && g.info === "") {
          i++;
          break;
        }
        body.push(c.slice(Math.min(lead(c), fence.indent)));
      }
      const word = fence.info.split(" ")[0] ?? "";
      out.push({
        type: "code",
        ...(/^[A-Za-z0-9_+#.-]{1,24}$/.test(word) && { lang: word }),
        text: body.join("\n"),
      });
      continue;
    }
    const h = headingOf(l);
    if (h) {
      out.push({ type: "heading", level: h.level, inlines: parseInlines(h.text, st) });
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (isRule(l)) {
      out.push({ type: "rule" });
      i++;
      continue;
    }
    if (nest && quoteOf(l) !== undefined) {
      const inner: string[] = [];
      for (; i < n && quoteOf(lines[i] as string) !== undefined; i++)
        inner.push(quoteOf(lines[i] as string) as string);
      out.push({ type: "quote", blocks: parseBlocks(inner, depth + 1, st) });
      continue;
    }
    const para: string[] = [l.trimStart()];
    for (i++; i < n && !blank(lines[i] as string) && !startsBlock(lines[i] as string, nest); i++)
      para.push((lines[i] as string).trimStart());
    out.push({ type: "paragraph", inlines: parseInlines(para.join("\n"), st) });
  }
  return out;
}
```

  `packages/core/src/chatMarkdown.ts` (the public entry; its header is the contract in one place):

```ts
// ── Markdown in a chat transcript (U8c) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//
// A text part MAY say `format: "markdown"`. The text is still UNTRUSTED. This module is the whole of what
// that opt-in means: a hand-written parser for a FIXED, CLOSED subset of CommonMark/GFM that produces a
// small, bounded AST ({@link MdDoc}); renderers build their own nodes from the AST. Nothing here produces
// HTML, and the AST is never carried in a value (a source cannot forge a `link` node): every renderer parses
// the sanitised text itself.
//
//  IN:  paragraphs, ATX headings, emphasis and strong, inline code, fenced code blocks, ordered and bullet lists,
//       blockquotes, thematic breaks, GFM tables, `[label](url)` links, hard breaks, `[[id]]` reference markers.
//  OUT: raw HTML (`<b>` is text), entities (`&amp;` is text), images (the alt text is shown; an image address is the classic
//       exfiltration channel), autolinks and bare URLs (never linked), reference-style links, setext headings, indented
//       code, footnotes, strikethrough, task lists. Backslash escapes work for ASCII punctuation.
//  LINKS: only `http:`, `https:` and `mailto:`, no whitespace or control character, no userinfo (`https://a@b`), at most 2048
//       characters. A link that fails the rule is dropped and its label stays as text.
//  BOUNDS: {@link MD_MAX_NODES} nodes in all (the rest is cut and `truncated` is set), blocks nest at most
//       {@link MD_MAX_DEPTH} deep (deeper container syntax is plain text), inline nesting at most
//       {@link MD_MAX_INLINE_DEPTH}, at most 200 emphasis delimiter runs per inline run (more are literal), tables at most
//       {@link MD_MAX_TABLE_COLS} columns and {@link MD_MAX_TABLE_ROWS} rows. Every scan is a single pass or a
//       pre-indexed lookup, so the cost is linear in the (cut) input; `chatMarkdown.test.ts` pins absolute bounds on
//       adversarial input. Total: it never throws.
//  STREAMING: parsing is a pure function of the text so far, so a half-written reply is just a shorter text. The
//       CommonMark rules already settle it (an unclosed fence runs to the end, an unmatched `*` is a literal `*`); with
//       `streaming: true` a trailing half-written `[[id`, and a half-written `[label](ur`, are not drawn at all.

import { parseBlocks } from "./chatMdBlock";
import type { ChatMarkdownOptions, MdDoc, MdState } from "./chatMdTypes";
import { sanitizeChatText } from "./chatText";

export {
  MD_MAX_DEPTH,
  MD_MAX_INLINE_DEPTH,
  MD_MAX_NODES,
  MD_MAX_TABLE_COLS,
  MD_MAX_TABLE_ROWS,
  MD_MAX_URL,
  mdPlainText,
  safeLinkHref,
} from "./chatMdTypes";
export type { ChatMarkdownOptions, MdAlign, MdBlock, MdDoc, MdInline } from "./chatMdTypes";

/**
 * Parse untrusted `text` as the markdown subset above. The text is cleaned first (`sanitizeChatText`: controls, bidi,
 * TAG characters, vertical-space bombs, the {@link MAX_CHAT_TEXT} cut), so every string in the AST is already safe to draw.
 * Total, linear, never throws.
 */
export function parseChatMarkdown(text: unknown, options: ChatMarkdownOptions = {}): MdDoc {
  const st: MdState = {
    nodes: 0,
    truncated: false,
    markers: options.markers === true,
    streaming: options.streaming === true,
  };
  const clean = sanitizeChatText(text).replace(/\t/g, "    ");
  const blocks = parseBlocks(clean.split("\n"), 0, st);
  return { blocks, truncated: st.truncated };
}
```

Unclosed fences run to the end, which is also what a half-written one looks like. Linear (adversarial tests). Unexported."

### Task 176g (loom-ui repo): markdown 5 of 9: lists and GFM tables

**Repo and branch:** `~/workspace/loom-ui`, branch `feat/chat-md-lists-tables`, cut from 176f, a draft PR, rebase-merged, stacked in the order of the task numbers. Same conventions as Task 176a (loom-ui's `AGENTS.md`; `typecheck:tests` is a gate; renderer packages resolve `@loom-ui/core` from its built `dist`, so build first). The gate is `pnpm build && pnpm typecheck && pnpm typecheck:tests && pnpm test && pnpm lint:fix && pnpm lint`. 

**Why (generic).** Bullet and ordered lists (nested by indentation, tolerant of the two-space nesting models write under `1.`, with lazy continuation lines) and GFM tables (a header row, an alignment row of the same width, rows padded or cut to the header's width), the two structures answers use most. Tables are bounded to 12 columns and 100 rows (the rest is cut and the document says `truncated`); list nesting shares the block depth bound of 6.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/src/chatMdLists.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/chatMdBlock.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 176f's `parseBlocks`.
- Produces: `list` blocks (`ordered`, `start`, `items` as block lists) and `table` blocks (`align`, `head`, `rows`) from `parseChatMarkdown`. A bullet or `1.` interrupts a paragraph, `2.` does not (`year\n2024. was` stays a paragraph); a different marker character starts a new list; a table needs its alignment row, else it is a paragraph.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `packages/core/src/chatMdLists.test.ts`:

````ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { type MdBlock, type MdInline, parseChatMarkdown } from "./chatMarkdown";

const doc = (t: string) => parseChatMarkdown(t);
const first = (t: string): MdBlock => doc(t).blocks[0] as MdBlock;
const txt = (text: string): MdInline => ({ type: "text", text });

describe("lists", () => {
  it("reads bullet and ordered lists, with the first number kept", () => {
    expect(first("- a\n- b")).toEqual({
      type: "list",
      ordered: false,
      start: 1,
      items: [
        [{ type: "paragraph", inlines: [txt("a")] }],
        [{ type: "paragraph", inlines: [txt("b")] }],
      ],
    });
    expect(first("3. x\n4. y")).toMatchObject({ type: "list", ordered: true, start: 3 });
    expect(doc("- a\n\n- b").blocks).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it("nests by indentation, even two spaces under a numbered item", () => {
    const l = first("- a\n- b\n  - c\n- d");
    expect(l.type === "list" && l.items).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(l.type === "list" && (l.items[1] as MdBlock[]).map((b) => b.type)).toEqual([
      "paragraph",
      "list",
    ]);
    expect(doc("1. a\n  - sub\n2. b").blocks).toHaveLength(1);
  });
  it("lets a line continue its item, and a different marker start a new list", () => {
    const l = first("- one\ncontinues\n- two");
    expect(l.type === "list" && l.items).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(doc("- a\n* b").blocks).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(doc("- a\n\npara").blocks.map((b) => b.type)).toEqual(["list", "paragraph"]);
  });
  it("interrupts a paragraph for a bullet or for 1., but not for 2.", () => {
    expect(doc("text\n- a").blocks.map((b) => b.type)).toEqual(["paragraph", "list"]);
    expect(doc("text\n1. a").blocks.map((b) => b.type)).toEqual(["paragraph", "list"]);
    expect(doc("year\n2024. was").blocks.map((b) => b.type)).toEqual(["paragraph"]);
  });
  it("holds code, quotes and markers in an item as it would anywhere else", () => {
    const l = first("- a\n  ```\n  x\n  ```\n  > q");
    expect(l.type === "list" && (l.items[0] as MdBlock[]).map((b) => b.type)).toEqual([
      "paragraph",
      "code",
      "quote",
    ]);
  });
  it("stops nesting at MD_MAX_DEPTH and is linear", () => {
    let b: MdBlock | undefined = first(`${"- ".repeat(20)}x`);
    let depth = 0;
    while (b?.type === "list") {
      depth++;
      b = (b.items[0] as MdBlock[])[0];
    }
    expect(depth).toBe(6);
    for (const c of [
      "- ".repeat(30000),
      `${"1. a\n  ".repeat(8000)}`,
      "-\n".repeat(30000),
      "- a\n".repeat(30000),
    ]) {
      const t = Date.now();
      doc(c);
      expect(Date.now() - t, c.slice(0, 6)).toBeLessThan(1500);
    }
  });
});

describe("tables", () => {
  it("reads a header, an alignment row and rows, padding or cutting each row to the header's width", () => {
    const t = first("| a | b |\n|:--|--:|\n| 1 | 2 | 3 |\n| 4 |");
    expect(t).toMatchObject({
      type: "table",
      align: ["left", "right"],
      head: [[txt("a")], [txt("b")]],
    });
    expect(t.type === "table" && t.rows).toEqual([
      [[txt("1")], [txt("2")]],
      [[txt("4")], []],
    ]);
    expect(first("a | b\n--|--\n1 | 2").type).toBe("table");
    expect(first("| a |\n|:-:|\n| `x\\|y` |")).toMatchObject({ align: ["center"] });
  });
  it("needs an alignment row of the same width, else it is a paragraph", () => {
    expect(first("| a | b |\n| - |").type).toBe("paragraph");
    expect(first("| a | b |\n|---|---|---|").type).toBe("paragraph");
    expect(first("| a | b |\nnot a row").type).toBe("paragraph");
  });
  it("is bounded in columns and rows", () => {
    expect(first(`${"|a".repeat(13)}|\n${"|-".repeat(13)}|`).type).toBe("paragraph");
    const big = parseChatMarkdown(`|a|\n|-|\n${"|x|\n".repeat(150)}`);
    expect(big.truncated).toBe(true);
    const table = big.blocks[0];
    expect(table?.type === "table" && table.rows.length).toBe(100);
  });
  it("ends at a blank line, and its cells take inline markup but no html", () => {
    expect(doc("|a|\n|-|\n|**b**|\n\ntext").blocks.map((b) => b.type)).toEqual([
      "table",
      "paragraph",
    ]);
    const t = first("|a|\n|-|\n|<b>x</b>|");
    expect(t.type === "table" && t.rows[0]).toEqual([[txt("<b>x</b>")]]);
  });
});
````

  with

  ```ts
  function splitRow(l: string): string[] {
    let t = l.trim();
    if (t.startsWith("|")) t = t.slice(1);
    if (t.endsWith("|") && !t.endsWith("\\|")) t = t.slice(0, -1);
    const cells: string[] = [];
    let cur = "";
    for (let k = 0; k < t.length; k++) {
      const c = t[k] as string;
      if (c === "\\" && t[k + 1] === "|") {
        cur += "|";
        k++;
      } else if (c === "|") {
        cells.push(cur.trim());
        cur = "";
      } else cur += c;
    }
    cells.push(cur.trim());
    return cells;
  }
  const DELIM_CELL = /^:?-+:?$/;
  function tableAlign(l: string, cols: number): MdAlign[] | undefined {
    if (!l.includes("-")) return undefined;
    const cells = splitRow(l);
    if (cells.length !== cols || !cells.every((c) => DELIM_CELL.test(c))) return undefined;
    return cells.map((c) =>
      c.startsWith(":") ? (c.endsWith(":") ? "center" : "left") : c.endsWith(":") ? "right" : null
    );
  }

  export function parseBlocks
  ```

- Edit 3 of 3.
  In `packages/core/src/chatMdBlock.ts` (the list and table branches go before the paragraph branch): replace

  ```ts
      const para: string[]
  ```

  with

  ```ts
      const mk = nest ? markerOf(l) : undefined;
      if (mk) {
        const items: MdBlock[][] = [];
        while (i < n) {
          let next = i;
          while (next < n && blank(lines[next] as string)) next++;
          const m = next < n ? markerOf(lines[next] as string) : undefined;
          if (!m || m.ordered !== mk.ordered || m.ch !== mk.ch || isRule(lines[next] as string))
            break;
          i = next;
          const body: string[] = [m.rest];
          for (i++; i < n; i++) {
            const c = lines[i] as string;
            if (blank(c)) {
              let j = i;
              while (j < n && blank(lines[j] as string)) j++;
              if (j < n && lead(lines[j] as string) >= 2) {
                body.push("");
                continue;
              }
              break;
            }
            const ind = lead(c);
            if (ind >= 2) body.push(c.slice(Math.min(ind, m.offset)));

              markerOf(c) === undefined &&
              body[body.length - 1] !== ""

            else break;

          if (!take(st)) break;
          items.push(parseBlocks(body, depth + 1, st));
        }
        out.push({ type: "list", ordered: mk.ordered, start: mk.start, items });
        continue;
      }
      // a table: a header row, then a delimiter row of the same width
      if (l.includes("|") && i + 1 < n) {
        const head = splitRow(l);
        const align =
          head.length <= MD_MAX_TABLE_COLS
            ? tableAlign(lines[i + 1] as string, head.length)
            : undefined;
        if (align) {
          const rows: MdInline[][][] = [];
          for (
            i += 2;
            i < n && !blank(lines[i] as string) && !startsBlock(lines[i] as string, nest);
            i++
          ) {
            if (rows.length >= MD_MAX_TABLE_ROWS) {
              st.truncated = true;
              continue;
            }
            const cells = splitRow(lines[i] as string);
            rows.push(head.map((_, c) => parseInlines(cells[c] ?? "", st)));
          }
          out.push({ type: "table", align, head: head.map((c) => parseInlines(c, st)), rows });
          continue;
        }
      }

### Task 176h (loom-ui repo): markdown 6 of 9: the value contract, `ChatFeed({ markdown })`, marker resolution and the exports

**Repo and branch:** `~/workspace/loom-ui`, branch `feat/chat-markdown-format`, cut from 176g, a draft PR, rebase-merged, stacked in the order of the task numbers. Same conventions as Task 176a (loom-ui's `AGENTS.md`; `typecheck:tests` is a gate; renderer packages resolve `@loom-ui/core` from its built `dist`, so build first). The gate is `pnpm build && pnpm typecheck && pnpm typecheck:tests && pnpm test && pnpm lint:fix && pnpm lint`. 

**Why (generic).** The opt-in itself. A text part gains `format?: "plain" | "markdown"`; the Chat contract (`checkKindValue`, which every store and renderer trusts) refuses any other value, markdown on a person's turn, and a stray field next to it; `ChatFeed` gets a `markdown` option that marks the text parts of assistant entries (live reply included); `resolveMdMarkers` applies Task 176b's `[[id]]` rules to a parsed document; and the parser is exported from `@loom-ui/core/chat-ui` (so the main entry too, through `./chat`). The AST is deliberately **not** part of any value: a value carries text and a format, and every renderer parses it, so a source cannot hand a renderer a node it did not earn. With the option off nothing changes: the feed's value has no `format` key anywhere (tested).

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/src/chatMdMarkers.test.ts`, `packages/core/src/chatMarkdownValue.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/chatMarkdown.ts`, `packages/core/src/chatValue.ts`, `packages/core/src/chatFeed.ts`, `packages/core/src/chatUi.ts`, `packages/hydration/src/chat.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 176b (`refMarkers`, `refs`, the marker rules) and 176c to 176g.
- Produces: `ChatTextFormat`; `ChatPart` text `format?`; `ChatFeedOptions.markdown`; `resolveMdMarkers(doc, known, { hideAll }): MdDoc`; the public exports `parseChatMarkdown`, `resolveMdMarkers`, `mdPlainText`, `safeLinkHref`, the `Md*` types and the `MD_MAX_*` bounds.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** `packages/core/src/chatMdMarkers.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { type MdInline, parseChatMarkdown, resolveMdMarkers } from "./chatMarkdown";

const txt = (text: string): MdInline => ({ type: "text", text });
const known = new Set(["a1", "b2"]);
const res = (t: string, hideAll = false, streaming = false) =>
  resolveMdMarkers(parseChatMarkdown(t, { markers: true, streaming }), known, { hideAll });
const first = (d: ReturnType<typeof res>): readonly MdInline[] => {
  const b = d.blocks[0];
  return b?.type === "paragraph" ? b.inlines : [];
};

describe("resolveMdMarkers", () => {
  it("numbers a listed marker by first appearance of its id", () => {
    expect(first(res("x [[b2]] y [[a1]] z [[b2]]."))).toEqual([
      txt("x "),
      { type: "ref", id: "b2", n: 1 },
      txt(" y "),
      { type: "ref", id: "a1", n: 2 },
      txt(" z "),
      { type: "ref", id: "b2", n: 1 },
      txt("."),
    ]);
  });
  it("removes a marker that is not listed, with the one space before it", () => {
    expect(first(res("Net 30 [[zzz]]."))).toEqual([txt("Net 30.")]);
    expect(first(res("[[zzz]]x"))).toEqual([txt("x")]);
  });
  it("numbers across blocks and into emphasis, lists and table cells, in reading order", () => {
    const d = res("**s [[b2]]**\n\n- i [[a1]]\n\n|h|\n|-|\n|c [[b2]]|");
    const refs: string[] = [];
    const walk = (v: unknown): void => {
      if (Array.isArray(v)) v.forEach(walk);
      else if (v && typeof v === "object") {
        const o = v as { type?: string; id?: string; n?: number };
        if (o.type === "ref") refs.push(`${o.id}:${o.n}`);
        Object.values(v).forEach(walk);
      }
    };
    walk(d.blocks);
    expect(refs).toEqual(["b2:1", "a1:2", "b2:1"]);
  });
  it("hides every marker while the reply streams, with a half-written one", () => {
    expect(first(res("Due [[a1]] in 30", true))).toEqual([txt("Due in 30")]);
    expect(first(res("Due [[a1", true, true))).toEqual([txt("Due")]);
  });
  it("never leaves a marker node for a renderer to draw", () => {
    expect(JSON.stringify(res("a [[a1]] [[zzz]]"))).not.toContain('"marker"');
    expect(JSON.stringify(res("a [[a1]]", true))).not.toContain('"ref"');
  });
});
```

import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { checkKindValue } from "./browser";
import { ChatFeed } from "./chatFeed";
import { isChatValue } from "./chatValue";

const part = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  type: "text",
  text: "Due in **30 days**.",
  ...over,
});
const entry = (role: "user" | "assistant", over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  id: role === "user" ? "m1" : "t1.assistant",
  turn: 1,
  role,
  status: "complete",
  parts: [part()],
  ...over,
});
const value = (...entries: unknown[]) => ({ entries });

describe("a text part's format", () => {
  it("is plain when absent, and may say plain or markdown on an assistant entry", () => {
    expect(isChatValue(value(entry("assistant")))).toBe(true);
    expect(isChatValue(value(entry("assistant", { parts: [part({ format: "plain" })] })))).toBe(
      true
    );
    expect(isChatValue(value(entry("assistant", { parts: [part({ format: "markdown" })] })))).toBe(
      true
    );
    expect(
      checkKindValue("Chat", value(entry("assistant", { parts: [part({ format: "markdown" })] })))
        .ok
    ).toBe(true);
  });
  it.each([
    ["an unknown format", entry("assistant", { parts: [part({ format: "html" })] })],
    ["a non-string format", entry("assistant", { parts: [part({ format: true })] })],
    ["markdown on a user entry", entry("user", { parts: [part({ format: "markdown" })] })],
    [
      "a stray field next to format",
      entry("assistant", { parts: [part({ format: "markdown", html: "<b>" })] }),
    ],
  ])("refuses %s", (_name, e) => expect(checkKindValue("Chat", value(e)).ok).toBe(false));
  it("refuses a format on a restricted placeholder, which carries no parts at all", () => {
    expect(
      checkKindValue(
        "Chat",
        value({
          id: "t1.assistant",
          turn: 1,
          role: "assistant",
          status: "restricted",
          parts: [part({ format: "markdown" })],
        })
      ).ok
    ).toBe(false);
  });
});

describe("ChatFeed({ markdown: true })", () => {
  const wire = (
    turn: number,
    role: "user" | "assistant",
    text: string,
    over: Record<string, unknown> = {}
  ) => ({
    turn,
    role,
    text,
    status: "complete",
    cursor: `1.${turn * 2 + (role === "user" ? 0 : 1)}`,
    ...over,
  });
  const fed = (opts: { markdown?: boolean }) => {
    const f = new ChatFeed(opts);
    f.applyHistory(
      {
        entries: [wire(1, "assistant", "Due **now**", {}), wire(1, "user", "q **x**")],
        done: true,
      },
      "backward"
    );
    return f;
  };
  it("marks an assistant reply's text, and never a person's own turn", () => {
    const v = fed({ markdown: true }).value();
    expect(
      v.entries.map((e) => [
        e.role,
        e.parts?.map((p) => (p.type === "text" ? p.format : undefined)),
      ])
    ).toEqual([
      ["user", [undefined]],
      ["assistant", ["markdown"]],
    ]);
    expect(isChatValue(v)).toBe(true);
  });
  it("leaves the value exactly as it was when the option is off", () => {
    const v = fed({}).value();
    expect(JSON.stringify(v)).not.toContain("format");
    expect(JSON.stringify(fed({ markdown: false }).value())).toBe(JSON.stringify(v));
  });
  it("marks the reply while it streams too, so the same renderer path settles it", () => {
    const f = new ChatFeed({ markdown: true });
    f.applyEvent("delta", { turn: 1, text: "Due **no" });
    const live = f.value().entries.find((e) => e.status === "streaming");
    expect(live?.parts).toEqual([{ type: "text", text: "Due **no", format: "markdown" }]);
    expect(isChatValue(f.value())).toBe(true);
  });
  it("marks nothing on a restricted or failed turn that has no text", () => {
    const f = new ChatFeed({ markdown: true });
    f.applyHistory(
      {
        entries: [
          wire(1, "assistant", "x", { status: "restricted" }),
          wire(2, "assistant", "", { status: "failed", reason: "model-error" }),
        ],
        done: true,
      },
      "backward"
    );
    expect(JSON.stringify(f.value())).not.toContain("markdown");
  });
});

  Append to `packages/core/src/chatMarkdown.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The same numbering and removal rules as `splitRefMarkers`, over a parsed document: a marker the entry listed becomes
 * `ref` (numbered by first appearance of the id), any other marker, and every marker while `hideAll`, is removed with
 * the one space before it. A renderer draws `ref` and never `marker`.
 */
export function resolveMdMarkers(
  doc: MdDoc,
  known: ReadonlySet<string>,
  o: { readonly hideAll: boolean }
): MdDoc {
  const numbers = new Map<string, number>();
  const inl = (nodes: readonly MdInline[]): MdInline[] => {
    const out: MdInline[] = [];
    for (const n of nodes) {
      const prev = out[out.length - 1];
      if (n.type === "text" && prev?.type === "text")
        out[out.length - 1] = { type: "text", text: prev.text + n.text };
      else if (n.type === "marker") {
        if (o.hideAll || !known.has(n.id)) {
          if (prev?.type === "text" && prev.text.endsWith(" "))
            out[out.length - 1] = { type: "text", text: prev.text.slice(0, -1) };
          continue;
        }
        let num = numbers.get(n.id);
        if (num === undefined) {
          num = numbers.size + 1;
          numbers.set(n.id, num);
        }
        out.push({ type: "ref", id: n.id, n: num });
      } else if (n.type === "em" || n.type === "strong" || n.type === "link")
        out.push({ ...n, children: inl(n.children) });
      else out.push(n);
    }
    return out;
  };
  const blk = (blocks: readonly MdBlock[]): MdBlock[] =>
    blocks.map((b): MdBlock => {
      switch (b.type) {
        case "paragraph":
        case "heading":
          return { ...b, inlines: inl(b.inlines) };
        case "quote":
          return { ...b, blocks: blk(b.blocks) };
        case "list":
          return { ...b, items: b.items.map(blk) };
        case "table":
          return { ...b, head: b.head.map(inl), rows: b.rows.map((r) => r.map(inl)) };
        default:
          return b;
      }
    });
  return { blocks: blk(doc.blocks), truncated: doc.truncated };
}
```

### Task 176i (loom-ui repo): markdown 7 of 9: `lu-chat` draws markdown as DOM

**Repo and branch:** `~/workspace/loom-ui`, branch `feat/chat-markdown-lit`, cut from 176h, a draft PR, rebase-merged, stacked in the order of the task numbers. Same conventions as Task 176a (loom-ui's `AGENTS.md`; `typecheck:tests` is a gate; renderer packages resolve `@loom-ui/core` from its built `dist`, so build first). The gate is `pnpm build && pnpm typecheck && pnpm typecheck:tests && pnpm test && pnpm lint:fix && pnpm lint`. 

**Why (generic).** The web renderer for the tree. `drawMarkdown` turns the AST into Lit templates (text bindings and a few bounded attribute bindings, nothing else); `lu-chat` uses it for an assistant part that says `format: "markdown"` and leaves every other part on today's path, character for character. Reference markers (Task 176b) compose: the same numbered buttons appear inside emphasis, list items and table cells, never inside code or a link label, and none while the reply streams.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/renderer-lit/src/widgets/chatMarkdown.ts`, `packages/renderer-lit/src/widgets/chatMarkdown.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/renderer-lit/src/widgets/lu-chat.ts`, `packages/core/src/messages.ts`, `packages/renderer-lit/README.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 176h's exports; 176b's `refMarkers`, `refs` and the `button.ref` template.
- Produces: `drawMarkdown(doc, { ref }): TemplateResult`; the `.md` container (`data-format="markdown"`); the message key `lu.chat.table` ("Table", the accessible name of a table's scroll region).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `packages/renderer-lit/src/widgets/chatMarkdown.test.ts` (it mounts the real `lu-chat`, like Task 176b's `chatRefs.test.ts`):

````ts
import { CHAT_REF_OPEN_ACTION } from "@loom-ui/core/chat-ui";
import { customWidget$ } from "@loom/blueprint-runtime";
import { readFileSync } from "node:fs";
import { resolve } from "node:path";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import "../elements/lu-chat";

const b = { projectionId: "hydration", path: "values.h" };
beforeEach(() => vi.stubGlobal("IntersectionObserver", undefined));
type El = HTMLElement & { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> };
async function mount(value: unknown, onAction?: (a: unknown) => unknown): Promise<El> {
  const el = document.createElement("lu-chat") as El;
  Object.assign(el, {
    widget: customWidget$({ kind: "Chat", id: "chat", title: "t", binding: b, props: {} }),
    value,
    onAction,
  });
  document.body.appendChild(el);
  for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    await Promise.resolve();
    await el.updateComplete;
  }
  return el;
}
const part = (text: string, format?: string) => ({ type: "text", text, ...(format && { format }) });
const reply = (text: string, over: Record<string, unknown> = {}, format = "markdown") => ({
  id: "t1.assistant",
  turn: 1,
  role: "assistant",
  status: "complete",
  parts: [part(text, format)],
  ...over,
});
const md = (el: El) =>
  el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("article[data-role='assistant'] .md") as HTMLElement;
const all = (root: Element) => [root, ...root.querySelectorAll("*")];

describe("lu-chat, a markdown part", () => {
  it("draws structure as elements, and the text of every node as text", async () => {
    const el = await mount({
      entries: [
        reply(
          "# Terms\n\nDue in **30 days** and `net`.\n\n- one\n- two\n\n1. a\n2. b\n\n> quoted\n\n---\n\n```ts\nlet a = 1 < 2;\n```"
        ),
      ],
    });
    const root = md(el);
    expect(root.getAttribute("data-format")).toBe("markdown");
    expect(root.querySelector("[role=heading]")?.getAttribute("aria-level")).toBe("3");
    expect(root.querySelector("strong")?.textContent).toBe("30 days");
    expect(root.querySelector("p code")?.textContent).toBe("net");
    expect([...root.querySelectorAll("ul > li")].map((x) => x.textContent?.trim())).toEqual([
      "one",
      "two",
    ]);
    expect(root.querySelectorAll("ol > li")).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(root.querySelector("blockquote")?.textContent?.trim()).toBe("quoted");
    expect(root.querySelector("hr")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(root.querySelector("pre code")?.textContent).toBe("let a = 1 < 2;");
    expect(root.querySelector("pre")?.getAttribute("tabindex")).toBe("0");
  });
  it("draws a table with scoped headers and alignment, in a focusable, named region", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ entries: [reply("| item | qty |\n|:--|--:|\n| nut | 4 |")] });
    const region = md(el).querySelector(".table") as HTMLElement;
    expect(region.getAttribute("role")).toBe("region");
    expect(region.getAttribute("aria-label")).toBe("Table");
    expect(
      [...region.querySelectorAll("th")].map((x) => [
        x.textContent,
        x.getAttribute("scope"),
        x.getAttribute("data-align"),
      ])
    ).toEqual([
      ["item", "col", "left"],
      ["qty", "col", "right"],
    ]);
    expect([...region.querySelectorAll("td")].map((x) => x.textContent)).toEqual(["nut", "4"]);
  });
  it("shows html as the characters, and never makes an element the parser did not", async () => {
    const el = await mount({
      entries: [reply('<b>raw</b> <img src=x onerror="alert(1)"> <script>alert(1)</script> &amp;')],
    });
    const root = md(el);
    expect(root.textContent).toContain(
      '<b>raw</b> <img src=x onerror="alert(1)"> <script>alert(1)</script> &amp;'
    );
    expect(all(root).map((n) => n.localName)).toEqual(["div", "p"]);
  });
  it("links only allowed addresses, in a new context with no opener and no referrer, and shows the address", async () => {
    const el = await mount({
      entries: [
        reply(
          "[a](https://example.com/x?y=1) [b](javascript:alert(1)) [c](mailto:a@b.example) [d](data:text/html,x) ![e](https://tracker.example/p.png)"
        ),
      ],
    });
    const links = [...md(el).querySelectorAll("a")];
    expect(links.map((a) => a.getAttribute("href"))).toEqual([
      "https://example.com/x?y=1",
      "mailto:a@b.example",
    ]);
    for (const a of links) {
      expect(a.getAttribute("target")).toBe("_blank");
      expect(a.getAttribute("rel")).toBe("noopener noreferrer nofollow");

    expect(md(el).innerHTML).not.toContain("tracker.example");

  it("never produces a tag, attribute or address outside the closed list, for hostile input", async () => {
    const hostile = [
      "[x](javascript:alert(1)) <a href=javascript:alert(1)>y</a> [[a1]] **<img src=x onerror=alert(1)>**",
      "```<script>\n</script>\n```\n\n> <iframe src=//evil.example></iframe>\n\n| <b> |\n|-|\n| <svg onload=alert(1)> |",
      '[click](https://evil.example/" onmouseover="alert(1))  [z](https://a.example/<b>)',
      `${String.fromCharCode(27)}[2J${String.fromCharCode(0x202e)} [a](https://e.example/${String.fromCharCode(0)}) \`${String.fromCharCode(7)}\``,
    ];
    const TAGS = new Set([
      "div",
      "p",
      "em",
      "strong",
      "code",
      "a",
      "br",
      "pre",
      "blockquote",
      "ul",
      "ol",
      "li",
      "table",
      "thead",
      "tbody",
      "tr",

      "td",

      "data-format",
      "data-ref",
      "href",
      "title",
      "target",
      "rel",
      "referrerpolicy",
      "start",
      "scope",
      "type",

    for (const text of hostile) {
      const el = await mount({
        refMarkers: true,
        entries: [reply(text, { refs: [{ id: "a1" }] })],

      for (const n of all(md(el))) {
        expect(TAGS.has(n.localName), n.localName).toBe(true);
        for (const a of n.getAttributeNames()) expect(ATTRS.has(a), a).toBe(true);
        if (n.localName === "a")
          expect(n.getAttribute("href")).toMatch(/^(https?:\/\/[^\s@/]+|mailto:\S+)/i);
      }
      const shown = md(el).textContent ?? "";
      const bad = [...shown].filter((ch) => {
        const c = ch.charCodeAt(0);
        return (c < 32 && c !== 9 && c !== 10) || c === 0x202e;
      });
      expect(bad).toEqual([]);
    }
  });
  it("draws a part with no format, or a person's turn, exactly as typed", async () => {
    const el = await mount({
      entries: [
        {
          id: "m1",
          turn: 1,
          role: "user",
          status: "complete",
          parts: [part("**u** [x](https://e.example)")],
        },
        reply("**a** [x](https://e.example)", {}, ""),
      ],
    });
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(".md")).toBeNull();
    expect(
      [...(el.shadowRoot as ShadowRoot).querySelectorAll("p.text")].map((p) => p.textContent)
    ).toEqual(["**u** [x](https://e.example)", "**a** [x](https://e.example)"]);
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("a")).toBeNull();
  });
  it("a person's turn that claims markdown is refused by the contract, and nothing of it is drawn", async () => {
    const el = await mount({
      entries: [
        {
          id: "m1",
          turn: 1,
          role: "user",
          status: "complete",
          parts: [part("secret-user-md", "markdown")],
        },
      ],
    });
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.textContent).not.toContain("secret-user-md");
  });
});

describe("lu-chat, markdown and reference markers", () => {
  const refs = [{ id: "a1" }, { id: "b2" }];
  it("draws a listed marker as the same numbered button, inside any markup but a code span, a code block or a link", async () => {
    const seen: unknown[] = [];
    const el = await mount(
      {
        refMarkers: true,
        entries: [
          reply(
            "**Due [[b2]]** and _net_ [[a1]] [[zzz]]\n\n- item [[a1]]\n\nSee `[[a1]]` and [t [[a1]]](https://e.example)\n\n```\n[[a1]]\n```",
            { refs }
          ),
        ],
      },
      (a) => void seen.push(a)
    );
    const buttons = [...md(el).querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>("button.ref")];
    expect(
      buttons.map((x) => [x.textContent, x.getAttribute("aria-label"), x.getAttribute("data-ref")])
    ).toEqual([
      ["1", "Source 1", "b2"],
      ["2", "Source 2", "a1"],
      ["2", "Source 2", "a1"],
    ]);
    expect(md(el).querySelector("strong button.ref")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(md(el).querySelector("code")?.textContent).toBe("[[a1]]");
    expect(md(el).querySelector("pre code")?.textContent).toBe("[[a1]]");
    expect(md(el).querySelector("a button")).toBeNull();
    expect(md(el).textContent).not.toContain("zzz");
    buttons[1]?.click();
    expect(seen).toEqual([
      {
        type: CHAT_REF_OPEN_ACTION,
        widgetId: "chat",
        payload: { entryId: "t1.assistant", refId: "a1", n: 2 },
      },
    ]);
  });
  it("hides every marker, and a half-written one, while the reply streams, and does not show a half-written markup", async () => {
    const el = await mount({
      refMarkers: true,
      entries: [reply("Due **in 30** [[a1]] and [the d", { status: "streaming", refs })],
    });
    expect(md(el).querySelectorAll("button.ref")).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(md(el).textContent).not.toContain("[[");
    expect(md(el).querySelector("strong")?.textContent).toBe("in 30");
    expect(md(el).querySelector(".caret")).not.toBeNull();
    expect(md(el).textContent).toContain("the d");
    const half = await mount({
      refMarkers: true,
      entries: [reply("Due [[a1", { status: "streaming", refs })],
    });
    expect(md(half).textContent?.trim()).toBe("Due");
  });
  it("settles when the durable reply replaces the live one: the same node set, now with its markers", async () => {
    const el = (await mount({
      refMarkers: true,
      entries: [reply("Due **now** [[a1]]", { status: "streaming", refs })],
    })) as El & { value: unknown };
    expect(md(el).querySelectorAll("button.ref")).toHaveLength(0);
    el.value = { refMarkers: true, entries: [reply("Due **now** [[a1]]", { refs })] };
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
      await Promise.resolve();
      await el.updateComplete;
    }
    expect(md(el).querySelectorAll("button.ref")).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(md(el).querySelector(".caret")).toBeNull();
  });
  it("leaves [[id]] as text when the source does not write markers", async () => {
    const el = await mount({ entries: [reply("Due [[a1]].", { refs })] });
    expect(md(el).querySelectorAll("button.ref")).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(md(el).textContent).toContain("[[a1]]");
  });
});

describe("the markdown renderer's source", () => {
  it("never reaches for HTML-parsing APIs", () => {
    for (const f of ["chatMarkdown.ts", "lu-chat.ts"]) {
      const src = readFileSync(resolve(__dirname, f), "utf8")
        .replace(/\/\*[\s\S]*?\*\//g, "")
        .replace(/^\s*\/\/.*$/gm, "");

        /unsafeHTML|unsafeSVG|innerHTML|outerHTML|insertAdjacentHTML|createContextualFragment|document\.write|DOMParser|\.setAttribute\(\s*["']on|style=/
      );
    }
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `packages/renderer-lit/src/widgets/chatMarkdown.ts`:

```ts
import { type MdBlock, type MdDoc, type MdInline, safeLinkHref } from "@loom-ui/core/chat-ui";
import { type TemplateResult, html, nothing } from "lit";
import { say } from "../say";

// ── Markdown parts of a transcript, drawn (U8c) ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//
// `drawMarkdown` turns the AST of `parseChatMarkdown` (core) into Lit templates. The ONLY dynamic things in
// those templates are text bindings (a node's text) and three attribute bindings (a link's `href` and `title`,
// a heading's `aria-level`, a list's `start`, a cell's `data-align`, a code block's `data-lang`), each of them a
// value the parser bounded and this file re-checks (`safeLinkHref`). No `unsafeHTML`, no `innerHTML`, no
// `insertAdjacentHTML`, no `style`, no event attribute: a test scans this file for them and walks the rendered
// DOM for any tag, attribute or address outside the list below.
//
// LINKS open in a new browsing context (`target="_blank"`) with `rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow"` and no
// referrer: a reader leaving a transcript must not hand the destination the transcript's address, and a link
// the model wrote is not an endorsement. The address is also the link's `title`, so a label cannot hide it.

export interface MarkdownDraw {
  /** How a numbered reference marker is drawn (the same button a plain-text marker gets). */
  readonly ref: (id: string, n: number) => TemplateResult;
}

const inlines = (nodes: readonly MdInline[], d: MarkdownDraw): unknown[] =>
  nodes.map((n) => inline(n, d));

function inline(n: MdInline, d: MarkdownDraw): unknown {
  switch (n.type) {
    case "text":
      return n.text;
    case "code":
      return html`<code>${n.text}</code>`;
    case "em":
      return html`<em>${inlines(n.children, d)}</em>`;
    case "strong":
      return html`<strong>${inlines(n.children, d)}</strong>`;
    case "link": {
      const href = safeLinkHref(n.href);
      return href === undefined
        ? inlines(n.children, d)
        : html`<a href=${href} title=${href} target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" referrerpolicy="no-referrer">${inlines(n.children, d)}</a>`;
    }
    case "br":
      return html`<br />`;
    case "ref":
      return d.ref(n.id, n.n);
    default:
      return nothing; // an unresolved marker is never drawn
  }
}

function block(b: MdBlock, d: MarkdownDraw): TemplateResult {
  switch (b.type) {
    case "paragraph":
      return html`<p>${inlines(b.inlines, d)}</p>`;
    case "heading":
      // A transcript is not a page: its headings sit below the page's own, so `#` is level 3 and the deepest is 6.
      return html`<div class="h h${b.level}" role="heading" aria-level=${Math.min(6, b.level + 2)}>${inlines(b.inlines, d)}</div>`;
    case "code":
      return html`<pre tabindex="0"><code data-lang=${b.lang ?? nothing}>${b.text}</code></pre>`;
    case "quote":
      return html`<blockquote>${b.blocks.map((x) => block(x, d))}</blockquote>`;
    case "list": {
      const items = b.items.map((item) => html`<li>${item.map((x) => block(x, d))}</li>`);
      return b.ordered ? html`<ol start=${b.start}>${items}</ol>` : html`<ul>${items}</ul>`;
    }
    case "table":
      return html`<div class="table" tabindex="0" role="region" aria-label=${say("lu.chat.table")}><table>
        <thead><tr>${b.head.map((c, k) => html`<th scope="col" data-align=${b.align[k] ?? nothing}>${inlines(c, d)}</th>`)}</tr></thead>
        <tbody>${b.rows.map((r) => html`<tr>${r.map((c, k) => html`<td data-align=${b.align[k] ?? nothing}>${inlines(c, d)}</td>`)}</tr>`)}</tbody>
      </table></div>`;
    case "rule":
      return html`<hr />`;
  }
}

/** The document as Lit templates. A document a bound cut short ends in an ellipsis nobody has to read out. */
export function drawMarkdown(doc: MdDoc, d: MarkdownDraw): TemplateResult {
  return html`${doc.blocks.map((b) => block(b, d))}${doc.truncated ? html`<p class="cut" aria-hidden="true">…</p>` : nothing}`;
}
```

  const mdCache = new WeakMap<ChatPart, { key: string; doc: MdDoc }>();
  /** The parsed markdown of a text part, memoised per part while its reading (markers, streaming) is the same. */
  const markdownOf = (part: ChatPart & { type: "text" }, markers: boolean, streaming: boolean): MdDoc => {
    const key = `${markers ? "m" : "-"}${streaming ? "s" : "-"}`;
    const hit = mdCache.get(part);
    if (hit?.key === key) return hit.doc;
    const doc = parseChatMarkdown(part.text, { markers, streaming });
    mdCache.set(part, { key, doc });
    return doc;
  };

  const reasoningCache = new WeakMap
  ```

    /** A reference marker the host listed, as the button that reports its activation. */
    private refButton(entryId: string, refId: string, n: number): TemplateResult {
      return html`<button type="button" class="ref" data-ref=${refId} aria-label=${say("lu.chat.ref", { n })} @click=${() => this.openRef(entryId, refId, n)}>${n}</button>`;
    }

    /** One text part: as typed (plain, the default), or its markdown drawn as DOM when an assistant entry's part says so. */
    private textPart(e: ChatEntry, p: ChatPart & { type: "text" }): TemplateResult {
      const caret = e.status === "streaming" ? html`<span class="caret" aria-hidden="true"></span>` : nothing;
      if (p.format !== "markdown" || e.role !== "assistant")
        return html`<p class="text" data-text>${this.textOf(e, p)}${caret}</p>`;
      const markers = (this.value as ChatValue).refMarkers === true;
      const streaming = e.status === "streaming";
      const doc = markdownOf(p, markers, streaming);
      const shown = markers ? resolveMdMarkers(doc, new Set((e.refs ?? []).map((r) => r.id)), { hideAll: streaming }) : doc;
      return html`<div class="md" data-text data-format="markdown">${drawMarkdown(shown, { ref: (id, n) => this.refButton(e.id, id, n) })}${caret}</div>`;
    }
  ```

### Task 176j (loom-ui repo): markdown 8 of 9: plain-text lines for any renderer without a DOM, and the terminal

**Repo and branch:** `~/workspace/loom-ui`, branch `feat/chat-markdown-terminal`, cut from 176i, a draft PR, rebase-merged, stacked in the order of the task numbers. Same conventions as Task 176a (loom-ui's `AGENTS.md`; `typecheck:tests` is a gate; renderer packages resolve `@loom-ui/core` from its built `dist`, so build first). The gate is `pnpm build && pnpm typecheck && pnpm typecheck:tests && pnpm test && pnpm lint:fix && pnpm lint`. 

**Why (generic).** A terminal, a speech engine or a log reads the same tree as plain lines. `chatMarkdownLines` (core) is that one function; `chatTranscriptLines`, the shared words both shipped renderers and the conformance reference renderer already use, calls it for an assistant markdown part, so `@loom-ui/renderer-terminal` needs no change of its own. Plain parts keep their one line per turn, byte for byte. No ANSI is emitted: `renderExperience` makes the frame inert (no ESC, C0, C1 or bidi controls) and its snapshot output must stay byte-identical, so styling would be a separate, opt-in feature.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/core/src/chatMdText.ts`, `packages/core/src/chatMdText.test.ts`, `packages/core/src/chatMarkdownLines.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/core/src/chatWords.ts`, `packages/core/src/chatUi.ts`, `packages/renderer-terminal/src/chat.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 176h's `parseChatMarkdown`, `resolveMdMarkers`.
- Produces: `chatMarkdownLines(doc): string[]` (at most `MD_MAX_LINES` = 200 lines then `…`, each at most 2000 characters, never a control character; headings keep `#`, emphasis keeps `*`, a link is `label (address)`, a marker `[n]`, a table in pipe form, code lines under `| `); `chatTranscriptLines` prints a markdown reply as several lines (the first under the speaker, the rest indented two spaces).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.** `packages/core/src/chatMdText.test.ts`:

````ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { chatMarkdownLines } from "./chatMdText";
import { parseChatMarkdown, resolveMdMarkers } from "./chatMarkdown";

const lines = (t: string, o: { known?: string[]; hideAll?: boolean } = {}) =>
  chatMarkdownLines(
    resolveMdMarkers(parseChatMarkdown(t, { markers: true }), new Set(o.known ?? []), {
      hideAll: o.hideAll === true,
    })
  );

describe("chatMarkdownLines", () => {
  it("reads as plain text, link targets shown, markers numbered", () => {
    const src =
      "# Terms\n\nDue in **30 days** [[a1]] see [docs](https://example.com/a) or `x`.\n\n- one\n- two\n  - sub\n\n1. a\n2. b\n\n> quoted\n\n| h1 | h2 |\n|---|---|\n| 1 | a\\|b |\n\n```js\nlet a = 1;\n```\n\n---";
    expect(lines(src, { known: ["a1"] })).toEqual([
      "# Terms",
      "",
      "Due in **30 days** [1] see docs (https://example.com/a) or `x`.",
      "",
      "- one",
      "- two",
      "  - sub",
      "",
      "1. a",
      "2. b",
      "",
      "> quoted",
      "",
      "| h1 | h2 |",
      "| --- | --- |",
      "| 1 | a\\|b |",
      "",
      "| let a = 1;",
      "",
      "---",
    ]);
  });
  it("shows no unsafe link target, no image address, no marker that is not listed", () => {
    const out = lines(
      "[x](javascript:alert(1)) ![alt](https://t.example/p.png) [[zzz]] <b>h</b>"
    ).join("\n");
    expect(out).toBe("x alt <b>h</b>");
  });
  it("is bounded in lines and in line length, and says so", () => {
    const many = chatMarkdownLines(parseChatMarkdown("- a\n".repeat(500)));
    expect(many).toHaveLength(201);
    expect(many.at(-1)).toBe("…");
    expect(chatMarkdownLines(parseChatMarkdown("x".repeat(60000)))[0]?.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(
      2000
    );
  });
  it("never carries a control character", () => {
    const out = lines("a\u001b[2J `b\u0007` [c](https://e.example/\u0001)").join("");
    expect([...out].some((ch) => ch.charCodeAt(0) < 32 && ch !== "\n")).toBe(false);
  });
});
````

  `packages/core/src/chatMarkdownLines.test.ts` (the shared transcript words):

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { chatTranscriptLines } from "./chatWords";
import type { ChatValue } from "./chatValue";

const md = (text: string, over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => ({
  id: "t1.assistant",
  turn: 1,
  role: "assistant",
  status: "complete",
  parts: [{ type: "text", text, format: "markdown" }],
  ...over,
});
const user = (text: string) => ({
  id: "m1",
  turn: 1,
  role: "user",
  status: "complete",
  parts: [{ type: "text", text }],
});
const lines = (value: Record<string, unknown>) =>
  chatTranscriptLines({ ...value } as unknown as ChatValue);

describe("chatTranscriptLines, markdown replies", () => {
  it("prints a markdown reply as readable lines under the speaker, and a plain turn exactly as before", () => {
    expect(
      lines({
        entries: [
          user("a **b**"),
          md("# Terms\n\nDue in **30 days**, see [docs](https://example.com/a).\n\n- one\n- two"),
        ],
      })
    ).toEqual([
      "User: a **b**",
      "Assistant: # Terms",
      "",
      "  Due in **30 days**, see docs (https://example.com/a).",
      "",
      "  - one",
      "  - two",
    ]);
  });
  it("numbers listed reference markers, drops the others, and hides all of them while streaming", () => {
    const entries = (status: string) => [
      md("Due [[a1]] and [[zz]].", { status, refs: [{ id: "a1" }] }),
    ];
    expect(lines({ refMarkers: true, entries: entries("complete") })).toEqual([
      "Assistant: Due [1] and.",
    ]);
    expect(lines({ refMarkers: true, entries: entries("streaming") })[0]).toBe(
      "Assistant (replying): Due and."
    );
    expect(lines({ entries: entries("complete") })).toEqual(["Assistant: Due [[a1]] and [[zz]]."]);
  });
  it("is never markdown for a person's turn, whatever a source claims, and a part without a format stays plain", () => {
    const u = {
      ...user("**x** [a](https://e.example)"),
      parts: [{ type: "text", text: "**x** [a](https://e.example)", format: "markdown" }],
    };
    expect(lines({ entries: [u] })).toEqual(["User: **x** [a](https://e.example)"]);
    expect(
      lines({
        entries: [
          md("**x** [a](https://e.example)", {
            parts: [{ type: "text", text: "**x** [a](https://e.example)" }],
          }),
        ],
      })
    ).toEqual(["Assistant: **x** [a](https://e.example)"]);
  });
  it("keeps the failure sentence and tool line after a markdown body, and never emits a control character", () => {
    const out = lines({
      entries: [md("ok\u001b[2J", { tools: [{ name: "search", status: "done" }] })],
    }).join("\n");
    expect(out).toContain("Assistant: ok");
    expect(out).toContain("search done");
    expect([...out].some((ch) => ch.charCodeAt(0) < 32 && ch !== "\n")).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

describe("terminal chat transcript: markdown replies", () => {

          { refs: [{ id: "a1" }] }

    expect(plain).toContain("**x** [a](https://e.example)");

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** `packages/core/src/chatMdText.ts`:

```ts
// ── Markdown as plain lines (U8c) ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// What a terminal, a speech engine or a log shows for a parsed document: readable plain text, never an escape
// sequence (the AST's strings were cleaned by `sanitizeChatText`, and nothing here adds a control character).
// Emphasis keeps its `*` marks (there is no bold without styling), a link shows its target next to its label (a
// terminal cannot hide where a link goes), a reference marker is `[n]`, a table is its own pipe form.

import type { MdBlock, MdDoc, MdInline } from "./chatMdTypes";

export const MD_MAX_LINES = 200;
const MAX_LINE = 2000;

const inline = (nodes: readonly MdInline[]): string =>
  nodes
    .map((n): string => {
      switch (n.type) {
        case "text":
          return n.text.replace(/\n/g, " ");
        case "code":
          return `\`${n.text}\``;
        case "em":
          return `*${inline(n.children)}*`;
        case "strong":
          return `**${inline(n.children)}**`;
        case "link": {
          const label = inline(n.children);
          return label === n.href || label === "" ? n.href : `${label} (${n.href})`;
        }
        case "br":
          return "\n";
        case "ref":
          return `[${n.n}]`;
        default:
          return "";
      }
    })
    .join("");

const cell = (nodes: readonly MdInline[]): string =>
  inline(nodes).replace(/\n/g, " ").replace(/\|/g, "\\|");

function blockLines(b: MdBlock): string[] {
  switch (b.type) {
    case "paragraph":
      return inline(b.inlines).split("\n");
    case "heading":
      return [`${"#".repeat(b.level)} ${inline(b.inlines).replace(/\n/g, " ")}`];
    case "code":
      return b.text.split("\n").map((l) => `| ${l}`);
    case "quote":
      return blocksLines(b.blocks).map((l) => (l === "" ? ">" : `> ${l}`));
    case "list":
      return b.items.flatMap((item, k) => {
        const mark = b.ordered ? `${b.start + k}. ` : "- ";
        return blocksLines(item, false).map((l, j) =>
          j === 0 ? `${mark}${l}` : l === "" ? "" : `${" ".repeat(mark.length)}${l}`
        );
      });
    case "table": {
      const row = (cells: readonly (readonly MdInline[])[]): string =>
        `| ${cells.map(cell).join(" | ")} |`;
      return [row(b.head), `| ${b.head.map(() => "---").join(" | ")} |`, ...b.rows.map(row)];
    }
    case "rule":
      return ["---"];
  }
}
function blocksLines(blocks: readonly MdBlock[], spaced = true): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  for (const b of blocks) {
    if (spaced && out.length > 0) out.push("");
    out.push(...blockLines(b));
  }
  return out;
}

/** The document as plain lines: at most {@link MD_MAX_LINES} (then `…`), each at most 2000 characters, none with a control character. */
export function chatMarkdownLines(doc: MdDoc): string[] {
  const all = blocksLines(doc.blocks);
  const cut = all.length > MD_MAX_LINES;
  const lines = (cut ? all.slice(0, MD_MAX_LINES) : all).map((l) =>
    l.length > MAX_LINE ? `${l.slice(0, MAX_LINE - 1)}…` : l
  );
  return cut || doc.truncated ? [...lines, "…"] : lines;
}
```

  /** A markdown reply as plain lines (one string, newline-joined): markers numbered when listed, hidden while the reply streams. */
  function markdownBody(e: ChatEntry, text: string, value: ChatValue): string {
    const markers = value.refMarkers === true;
    const streaming = e.status === "streaming";
    const doc = parseChatMarkdown(text, { markers, streaming });
    const shown = markers
      ? resolveMdMarkers(doc, new Set((e.refs ?? []).map((r) => r.id)), { hideAll: streaming })
      : doc;
    return chatMarkdownLines(shown).join("\n");
  }

### Task 176k (loom-ui repo): markdown 9 of 9: the conformance check, the docs and the reference example

**Repo and branch:** `~/workspace/loom-ui`, branch `feat/chat-markdown-conformance`, cut from 176j, a draft PR, rebase-merged, stacked in the order of the task numbers. Same conventions as Task 176a (loom-ui's `AGENTS.md`; `typecheck:tests` is a gate; renderer packages resolve `@loom-ui/core` from its built `dist`, so build first). The gate is `pnpm build && pnpm typecheck && pnpm typecheck:tests && pnpm test && pnpm lint:fix && pnpm lint`. 

**Why (generic).** loom-ui's rule: a new contract clause goes into the conformance kit in the same change, with a fault proving the kit fails without it. The `chat-markdown` check holds every renderer, shipped or third-party, to the safety half of markdown (html literal, no unsafe link target, no image address, a part with no format never parsed, a person's turn never markdown), and, for an adapter that claims `chatMarkdown`, that the markdown source itself is not what a viewer reads. The docs stop saying "there is no markdown" and say what is true; a compiling reference snippet shows a support assistant (refunds, ticket numbers) end to end.

- Create: `docs/snippets/src/chatMarkdown.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/renderer-conformance/src/checks.ts`, `packages/renderer-conformance/src/adapter.ts`, `packages/renderer-conformance/src/speech.test.ts`, `packages/renderer-conformance/src/speech.test-support.ts`, `packages/renderer-lit/src/conformanceAdapter.test-support.ts`, `packages/renderer-terminal/src/conformanceAdapter.test-support.ts`, `packages/core/README.md`, `AGENTS.md`, `docs/guides/writing-a-renderer.md`, `packages/core/src/chatText.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: 176h to 176j.
- Produces: the check id `chat-markdown`; `AdapterCapabilities.chatMarkdown`; six faults in `speech.test.ts`; the snippet.

  `docs/snippets/src/chatMarkdown.test.ts` (the reference example, not tied to any one product):

```ts
import {
  ChatFeed,
  chatMarkdownLines,
  checkKindValue,
  parseChatMarkdown,
  resolveMdMarkers,
} from "@loom-ui/core";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

// The reference example for markdown in a chat transcript: a support assistant that answers in markdown and cites
// ticket numbers. It is not any one product's app: any transcript source that says `markdown: true` gets the same.

const TICKET = "TCK-1042";
const answer = [
  `Your refund was **approved** [[${TICKET}]].`,
  "",
  "| step | owner |",
  "|---|---|",
  "| approve | billing |",
  "| pay out | finance |",
  "",
  "Read the [refund policy](https://example.com/policy), or run `refund --status`.",
].join("\n");

describe("markdown in a chat transcript", () => {
  it("the feed marks the assistant's text, and the value passes the Chat contract", () => {
    const feed = new ChatFeed({ markdown: true, refMarkers: true });
    feed.applyHistory(
      {
        entries: [
          {
            turn: 1,
            role: "assistant",
            text: answer,
            status: "complete",
            cursor: "1.2",
            refs: [TICKET],
          },
          {
            turn: 1,
            role: "user",
            text: "Is my **refund** done?",
            status: "complete",
            cursor: "1.1",
          },
        ],
        done: true,
      },
      "backward"
    );
    const value = feed.value();
    expect(checkKindValue("Chat", value).ok).toBe(true);
    const [user, reply] = value.entries;
    expect(user?.parts).toEqual([{ type: "text", text: "Is my **refund** done?" }]);
    expect(reply?.parts?.[0]).toMatchObject({ type: "text", format: "markdown" });
  });

  it("the text is parsed into a closed tree: structure, a safe link, a numbered marker", () => {
    const doc = resolveMdMarkers(parseChatMarkdown(answer, { markers: true }), new Set([TICKET]), {
      hideAll: false,
    });
    expect(doc.blocks.map((b) => b.type)).toEqual(["paragraph", "table", "paragraph"]);
    expect(JSON.stringify(doc)).toContain('"type":"ref"');
    expect(JSON.stringify(doc)).toContain("https://example.com/policy");
  });

  it("a renderer without a DOM reads the same tree as plain lines", () => {
    const lines = chatMarkdownLines(
      resolveMdMarkers(parseChatMarkdown(answer, { markers: true }), new Set([TICKET]), {
        hideAll: false,
      })
    );
    expect(lines[0]).toBe("Your refund was **approved** [1].");
    expect(lines).toContain("| approve | billing |");
    expect(lines.at(-1)).toBe(
      "Read the refund policy (https://example.com/policy), or run `refund --status`."
    );
  });
});
```

  The check (it reuses that file's `HOSTILE`, `FORBIDDEN`, `bound`, `viewOf`, `must`, `has` and `show`):

  In `packages/renderer-conformance/src/checks.ts` (the new check goes just above the `i18n` check): replace

  ```ts
  // ── i18n ─
  ```

  with

  ````ts
  // ── chat markdown ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // An assistant part that says `format: "markdown"` is read as the closed subset core parses (`parseChatMarkdown`), never as
  // HTML: markup stays the literal characters, a link keeps its target only when it is http, https or mailto, an image is
  // its alt text, a part with no format is never parsed, and a person's turn is never markdown. A surface that draws the
  // transcript with `chatTranscriptLines` gets all of it for free; one that draws it itself must pass here.
  const MARKDOWN_SOURCE = [
    "Total is **due-in-30** days.",
    "",
    "<b>raw-html-leak</b> &amp; <script>x</script>",
    "",
    "[good-label](https://docs.example/terms) and [evil-label](javascript:alert-sentinel)",
    "",
    "![alt-sentinel](https://tracker.example/pixel.png)",
    "",
    "- item-one",
    "",
    "```",
    HOSTILE,
    "```",
  ].join("\n");
  const turn = (id: string, n: number, role: string, t: string, format?: string) => ({
    id,
    turn: n,
    role,
    status: "complete",
    parts: [{ type: "text", text: t, ...(format === undefined ? {} : { format }) }],
  });
  check(
    "chat-markdown",
    "markdown in an assistant reply is drawn as text: html stays literal, only safe links keep a target, an image is its alt text, a part with no format is never parsed",
    (adapter) =>
      withSession(adapter, async (s) => {
        if (!adapter.kinds.includes("Chat")) throw new ConformanceSkip("adapter does not claim Chat");
        const value = {
          mode: "owner",
          entries: [
            turn("t1.user", 1, "user", "user-plain **kept**"),
            turn("t1.assistant", 1, "assistant", MARKDOWN_SOURCE, "markdown"),
            turn("t2.assistant", 2, "assistant", "**stay-literal** [x](https://plain.example)"),
          ],
        };
        const m = await s.mount(viewOf([bound("Chat", "chat", "Support chat", "h-chat")]), {
          "h-chat": { value, state: "ready" },
        });
        const out = await m.text();
        must(has(out, "due-in-30") && has(out, "item-one"), `markdown text is missing: ${show(out)}`);
        must(out.includes("<b>raw-html-leak</b>"), `html was interpreted or dropped: ${show(out)}`);
        must(!out.includes("javascript:"), `an address outside the allowlist reached the surface: ${show(out)}`);
        must(has(out, "evil-label"), `the label of a refused link was dropped: ${show(out)}`);
        must(
          has(out, "good-label") && has(out, "docs.example/terms"),
          `a safe link lost its label or its target: ${show(out)}`
        );
        must(
          has(out, "alt-sentinel") && !out.includes("tracker.example"),
          `an image address reached the surface (or its alt text was lost): ${show(out)}`
        );
        for (const [what, ch] of FORBIDDEN)
          must(!out.includes(ch), `${what} reached the surface through a markdown code block`);
        must(
          has(out, "**stay-literal**") && has(out, "[x](https://plain.example)"),
          `a part with no format was parsed: ${show(out)}`
        );
        must(has(out, "user-plain **kept**"), `a person's turn was altered: ${show(out)}`);
        if (adapter.capabilities?.chatMarkdown === true)
          must(
            !out.includes("[good-label]") && !out.includes("```"),
            `the adapter claims markdown but shows its source: ${show(out)}`
          );
        // A person's own turn is never markdown: the contract refuses it, and the view says so instead of drawing it.
        await m.set("h-chat", {
          value: { mode: "owner", entries: [turn("t1.user", 1, "user", "user-md-refused", "markdown")] },
          state: "ready",
        });
        const refused = await m.text();
        must(!has(refused, "user-md-refused"), `a person's turn was drawn as markdown: ${show(refused)}`);
      })
  );

  // ── i18n ─
  ````

  In `packages/core/README.md`: replace

  ```ts
  **Reasoning (loom ADR 0133 D6), opt-in.**
  ```

  with

  ```ts
  **Markdown, opt-in.** A text part with `format: "markdown"` (assistant entries only; the contract refuses it
  on a person's turn, and refuses any other format value) is read as a CLOSED subset of CommonMark/GFM:
  paragraphs, ATX headings, emphasis and strong, inline code, fenced code, bullet and ordered lists,
  blockquotes, rules, tables, `[label](url)` links and the `[[id]]` reference markers. Raw html and entities
  are shown as the characters; images, autolinks, reference links and footnotes do not exist (a markdown image
  is an address fetched on render, the classic exfiltration channel). A link is kept only for `http:`,
  `https:` and `mailto:` (no whitespace, controls or userinfo, at most 2048 characters); otherwise its label
  stays as text. The parser (`parseChatMarkdown`, hand-written, no dependency) is a pure, total, linear
  function of the cleaned text into a bounded tree (2000 nodes, block depth 6, inline depth 4, tables 12 x 100);
  the tree is NEVER part of a value, so every renderer parses the text itself and a source cannot forge a
  node. `resolveMdMarkers` applies the reference-marker rules (listed ids numbered, unlisted removed, all
  hidden while streaming); markers never act inside code or a link label. Streaming needs no special case:
  the text so far is parsed each time, an unclosed fence is code to the end, an unmatched `*` is literal, a
  half-written `[label](ur` shows its label only. Renderers: `lu-chat` builds elements from the tree (links
  open in a new context with `rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow"`); everything without a DOM reads it as plain
  lines through `chatMarkdownLines` (no ANSI). `docs/snippets/src/chatMarkdown.test.ts` is the reference
  example.

  **Reasoning (loom ADR 0133 D6), opt-in.**
  ```

  In `AGENTS.md`: replace

  ```ts
  UNTRUSTED and drawn as text only (no markdown, no links; `sanitizeChatText`).
  ```

  with

  UNTRUSTED and drawn as text (`sanitizeChatText`); markdown ONLY when the source opts in per assistant text part
    (`format: "markdown"`, `ChatFeed({ markdown: true })`), as a closed, bounded tree (`parseChatMarkdown`: hand-written,
    no dependency, never part of a value), drawn as DOM, links http/https/mailto only, no images, never HTML.

import { ChatStreamEvent } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { compileFingerprint } from "@loom-ui/core/browser";
import { ChatFeed } from "@loom-ui/core/chat";
import { HydrationStore } from "@loom-ui/hydration/client";

import "@loom-ui/renderer-lit/elements/lu-note";

import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";

// A hand-built blueprint: this test proves the links resolve and the renderer draws; composing views is the server's job.
const note = {
  _tag: "Blueprint", irVersion: "2.5.0", id: "scaffold@sha256:0", title: "",
  layout: { type: "grid", id: "root", columns: 12, children: [{ span: 12, node: { _tag: "Widget", id: "n", kind: "Note", props: { text: "Hello <b>there</b>" }, source: { type: "Note", version: "1.0.0" } } }] },
  projections: [], projectionVersions: {},
};

describe("the web scaffold", () => {
  it("resolves the browser-safe loom-ui entries and Loom's ESM runtime, and renderer-lit draws a Note as text", async () => {
    const runtime = new InMemoryProjectionRuntime();
    const store = new HydrationStore(runtime, { experienceId: "scaffold" });
    const host = document.createElement("div");
    document.body.append(host);
    const handle: DomRenderHandle = renderBlueprintDom(host, note as never, { projectionRuntime: runtime, executionId: store.executionId, registry: litRegistry, title: "none" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(host.querySelector("lu-note")?.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Hello <b>there</b>"));

    handle.dispose();

`apps/web/src/guards.test.ts` (the standing checks of D6 and D14; they scan the sources, so they stay true as later tasks add files):

```ts
import { readFileSync, readdirSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { join } from "node:path";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const SRC = new URL(".", import.meta.url).pathname;
function files(dir: string): string[] {
  return readdirSync(dir).flatMap((n) => {
    const p = join(dir, n);
    return statSync(p).isDirectory() ? (n === "testing" ? [] : files(p)) : /\.(ts|css)$/.test(n) && !n.endsWith(".test.ts") ? [p] : [];
  });
}
const app = files(SRC);
const text = (p: string) => readFileSync(p, "utf8");

describe("the dependency and security rules of apps/web", () => {
  it("draws nothing as markup (D6): no unsafeHTML, innerHTML or insertAdjacentHTML", () => {
    for (const p of app) expect(text(p), p).not.toMatch(/unsafeHTML|\.innerHTML\s*=|insertAdjacentHTML/);
  });
  it("is a thin surface (D14): no catalogue, no experience composition, no reshaper, no server package", () => {
    for (const p of app)
      expect(text(p), p).not.toMatch(/@docmind\/experiences|@docmind\/auth|@loom-ui\/core\/log|from "@loom-ui\/core"|from "@loom-ui\/hydration"|defineSemanticCatalog|defineReshapers|genesis\(/);
  });
  it("imports no Loom package but the two browser runtimes", () => {
    for (const p of app) for (const m of text(p).matchAll(/from "(@loom\/[\w-]+)/g)) expect(["@loom/projection-runtime", "@loom/blueprint-runtime"], p).toContain(m[1]);
  });
  it("stores nothing in the browser but the theme (D6)", () => {
    for (const p of app)
      for (const m of text(p).matchAll(/(?:localStorage|sessionStorage|indexedDB)[\s\S]{0,40}/g)) expect(`${p}: ${m[0]}`).toMatch(/dm-theme|^$/);
  });
});
```
