- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `http/staticFiles.ts`.**

```ts
import { createReadStream, existsSync, statSync } from "node:fs";
import { realpath, stat } from "node:fs/promises";
import { extname, join, resolve, sep } from "node:path";
import { StartupConfigError } from "../startupError";
import { type Handler, sendError } from "./respond";

/** The policy of the web app: everything from this origin, no inline script or style, no remote font, image or frame. */
export const WEB_CSP =
  "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self'; img-src 'self' data:; font-src 'self'; connect-src 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'self'";

const TYPES: Record<string, string> = {
  ".html": "text/html; charset=utf-8",
  ".js": "text/javascript; charset=utf-8",
  ".css": "text/css; charset=utf-8",
  ".json": "application/json",
  ".map": "application/json",
  ".svg": "image/svg+xml",
  ".png": "image/png",
  ".ico": "image/x-icon",
  ".woff2": "font/woff2",
  ".txt": "text/plain; charset=utf-8",
  ".webmanifest": "application/manifest+json",
};

/** `DOCMIND_WEB_DIR`: unset or empty means the server serves the API only; otherwise a directory with an index.html. */
export function loadWebDir(raw: string | undefined): string | undefined {
  if (raw === undefined || raw === "") return undefined;
  const dir = resolve(raw);
  const ok = existsSync(dir) && statSync(dir).isDirectory() && existsSync(join(dir, "index.html"));
  if (!ok) throw new StartupConfigError("DOCMIND_WEB_DIR must be a directory that holds index.html");
  return dir;
}

/** Serves the built app: files by path, and `index.html` for any path with no extension (a client route). GET and HEAD. */
export function staticHandler(dir: string): Handler {
  const root = resolve(dir);
  return async (req, res) => {
    let rel: string;
    try {
      rel = decodeURIComponent(new URL(req.url ?? "/", "http://localhost").pathname);
    } catch {
      return sendError(res, 400, "bad_request", "invalid path");
    }
    if (rel.includes("\u0000")) return sendError(res, 400, "bad_request", "invalid path");
    // A dotfile or dot-directory anywhere in the path is never served.
    if (rel.split("/").some((part) => part.startsWith("."))) return sendError(res, 404, "not_found", "no such file");
    const isAsset = extname(rel) !== "";
    const wanted = resolve(root, `.${rel}`);
    if (wanted !== root && !wanted.startsWith(root + sep)) return sendError(res, 404, "not_found", "no such file");
    const file = isAsset ? wanted : join(root, "index.html"); // a path with no extension is a client route
    try {
      // The real path must stay inside the real root: a symlink in the directory cannot lead out of it.
      const real = await realpath(file);
      const realRoot = await realpath(root);
      if (!real.startsWith(realRoot + sep)) throw new Error("outside");
      const info = await stat(real);
      if (!info.isFile()) throw new Error("not a file");
      const type = TYPES[extname(real)] ?? "application/octet-stream";
      const page = file === join(root, "index.html");
      res.writeHead(200, {
        "content-type": type,
        "content-length": info.size,
        "cache-control": page ? "no-cache" : rel.startsWith("/assets/") ? "public, max-age=31536000, immutable" : "no-cache",
        "content-security-policy": WEB_CSP,
        "x-content-type-options": "nosniff",
        "referrer-policy": "no-referrer",
      });
      if (req.method === "HEAD") return void res.end();
      await new Promise<void>((done) => {
        const body = createReadStream(real);
        body.on("error", () => { res.destroy(); done(); });
        res.on("close", () => { body.destroy(); done(); });
        body.pipe(res);
      });
    } catch {
      sendError(res, 404, "not_found", "no such file");
    }
  };
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { AuthStore, authErrorText } from "./auth";

const ME = { userId: "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10", tenantId: "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11", email: "a@example.com" };
const api = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) =>
  ({ me: vi.fn(async () => ME), login: vi.fn(async () => ME), logout: vi.fn(async () => {}), signUp: vi.fn(async () => {}), ...over }) as never;

describe("AuthStore", () => {
  it("bootstrap: a live session is signed-in, a 401 is anonymous, and a dead network stays unknown-free (anonymous)", async () => {
    const live = new AuthStore(api());
    await live.bootstrap();
    expect(live.get()).toEqual({ status: "signed-in", me: ME });
    const gone = new AuthStore(api({ me: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(401, "unauthorized"); }) }));
    await gone.bootstrap();
    expect(gone.get()).toEqual({ status: "anonymous" });
    const down = new AuthStore(api({ me: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(0, "network"); }) }));
    await down.bootstrap();
    expect(down.get()).toEqual({ status: "anonymous" });
  });

  it("login signs in; a refused login stays anonymous and rethrows for the form", async () => {
    const store = new AuthStore(api({ login: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(401, "invalid_credentials"); }) }));
    await store.bootstrap();
    await expect(store.login({ email: "a@example.com", password: "x" })).rejects.toMatchObject({ code: "invalid_credentials" });
    expect(store.get()).toEqual({ status: "anonymous" });
    const ok = new AuthStore(api());
    await ok.login({ email: "a@example.com", password: "x" });
    expect(ok.get()).toEqual({ status: "signed-in", me: ME });
  });

  it("signUp then logs in with the same credentials (sign-up answers 202 whether or not the address exists)", async () => {
    const a = api();
    const store = new AuthStore(a);
    await store.signUp({ email: "a@example.com", password: "a long enough password" });
    expect((a as never as { signUp: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).signUp).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect((a as never as { login: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).login).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ email: "a@example.com", password: "a long enough password" });
    expect(store.get().status).toBe("signed-in");
  });

  it("logout clears the state even when the server call fails; expired() and signedOut() go anonymous", async () => {
    const store = new AuthStore(api({ logout: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(0, "network"); }) }));
    await store.login({ email: "a@example.com", password: "x" });
    await store.logout();
    expect(store.get()).toEqual({ status: "anonymous" });
    await store.login({ email: "a@example.com", password: "x" });
    store.expired();
    expect(store.get()).toEqual({ status: "anonymous" });
  });
});

describe("authErrorText", () => {
  it("answers with fixed sentences by status and code, and the wait time for a throttle", () => {
    expect(authErrorText(new ApiError(401, "invalid_credentials"))).toBe("The email or password is not right.");
    expect(authErrorText(new ApiError(429, "too_many_requests", 12))).toBe("Too many attempts. Try again in 12 seconds.");
    expect(authErrorText(new ApiError(429, "too_many_requests"))).toBe("Too many attempts. Try again in a minute.");
    expect(authErrorText(new ApiError(400, "invalid_request"))).toBe("Check the email and password and try again.");
    expect(authErrorText(new ApiError(0, "network"))).toBe("Could not reach the server. Check your connection.");
    expect(authErrorText(new ApiError(500, "internal"))).toBe("Something went wrong. Try again.");
    expect(authErrorText(new Error("sentinel-title-0123456789"))).toBe("Something went wrong. Try again.");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { createServices } from "../services";
import { AuthStore } from "../state/auth";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import "./dm-auth-form";

const ME = { userId: "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10", tenantId: "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11", email: "a@example.com" };
function setup(over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const api = { me: vi.fn(async () => ME), login: vi.fn(async () => ME), logout: vi.fn(async () => {}), signUp: vi.fn(async () => {}), ...over } as never;
  const auth = new AuthStore(api);
  const services = createServices({ api, auth, fetch: (async () => new Response("{}")) as never });
  return { services, auth, api };
}
const root = (el: Element) => el.shadowRoot as ShadowRoot;
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => root(el).querySelector(sel) as T;
const settle = (el: Element) => (el as unknown as { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete;
async function fill(el: HTMLElement, email: string, password: string) {
  q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[type=email]").value = email;
  q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[name=password]").value = password;
  q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[type=email]").dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
  q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[name=password]").dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
}

describe("dm-auth-form: sign in", () => {
  it("labels its fields, submits the credentials, and goes to the chats", async () => {
    const { services, api } = setup();
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-auth-form", { mode: "sign-in" });
    expect(q(el, "label[for=email]").textContent).toBe("Email");
    expect(q(el, "h1").textContent).toBe("Sign in");
    await fill(el, "a@example.com", "pw");
    q<HTMLFormElement>(el, "form").dispatchEvent(new Event("submit", { cancelable: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "chat" }));
    expect((api as never as { login: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).login).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ email: "a@example.com", password: "pw" });
  });

  it("shows a fixed sentence for a wrong password, marks the field invalid, and never the server's text", async () => {
    const { services } = setup({ login: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(401, "invalid_credentials"); }) });
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-auth-form", { mode: "sign-in" });
    await fill(el, "a@example.com", "nope");
    q<HTMLFormElement>(el, "form").dispatchEvent(new Event("submit", { cancelable: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "[role=alert]")?.textContent).toContain("The email or password is not right."));
    expect(q(el, "input[name=password]").getAttribute("aria-invalid")).toBe("true");
    expect(services.router.route).not.toEqual({ name: "chat" });
  });

  it("lets the password be shown and hidden, with a name on the toggle", async () => {
    const { services } = setup();
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-auth-form", { mode: "sign-in" });
    const toggle = q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[aria-label='Show password']");
    toggle.click();
    await settle(el);
    expect(q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[name=password]").type).toBe("text");
    expect(q(el, "button[aria-label='Hide password']")).not.toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("dm-auth-form: sign up", () => {
  it("states the 12-character rule, refuses a short password before calling the server, then signs in", async () => {
    const { services, api } = setup();
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-auth-form", { mode: "sign-up" });
    expect(q(el, "h1").textContent).toBe("Create your account");
    expect(root(el).textContent).toContain("At least 12 characters");
    await fill(el, "a@example.com", "short");
    q<HTMLFormElement>(el, "form").dispatchEvent(new Event("submit", { cancelable: true }));
    await settle(el);
    expect(q(el, "[role=alert]").textContent).toContain("At least 12 characters");
    expect((api as never as { signUp: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).signUp).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    await fill(el, "a@example.com", "a long enough password");
    q<HTMLFormElement>(el, "form").dispatchEvent(new Event("submit", { cancelable: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "chat" }));
  });

  it("when the sign-in after sign-up fails (the address may already be registered) it says so without saying which", async () => {
    const { services } = setup({ login: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(401, "invalid_credentials"); }) });
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-auth-form", { mode: "sign-up" });
    await fill(el, "a@example.com", "a long enough password");
    q<HTMLFormElement>(el, "form").dispatchEvent(new Event("submit", { cancelable: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "[role=alert]")?.textContent).toContain("We could not sign you in."));
    expect(q(el, "[role=alert]").textContent).toContain("If you already have an account, sign in instead.");
  });

  it("links between the two modes", async () => {
    const { services } = setup();
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-auth-form", { mode: "sign-in" });
    expect(q<HTMLAnchorElement>(el, "a[href='/sign-up']")).not.toBeNull();
    const up = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-auth-form", { mode: "sign-up" });
    expect(q<HTMLAnchorElement>(up, "a[href='/sign-in']")).not.toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`state/auth.ts`:

```ts
import type { LoginRequest, MeResponse, SignUpRequest } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { Api } from "../api/client";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { Store } from "./store";

export type AuthState =
  | { status: "unknown" }
  | { status: "anonymous" }
  | { status: "signed-in"; me: MeResponse };

/** Fixed sentences by status and code. The server's own message is never shown: it could echo what was typed. */
export function authErrorText(err: unknown): string {
  if (err instanceof ApiError) {
    if (err.status === 0) return "Could not reach the server. Check your connection.";
    if (err.status === 401) return "The email or password is not right.";
    if (err.status === 429)
      return err.retryAfter !== undefined ? `Too many attempts. Try again in ${err.retryAfter} seconds.` : "Too many attempts. Try again in a minute.";
    if (err.status === 400) return "Check the email and password and try again.";
  }
  return "Something went wrong. Try again.";
}

export class AuthStore extends Store<AuthState> {
  constructor(private readonly api: Pick<Api, "me" | "login" | "logout" | "signUp">) {
    super({ status: "unknown" });
  }
  /** Asks the server who this browser is. Any failure means anonymous: the sign-in screen is the safe place to land. */
  async bootstrap(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      this.set({ status: "signed-in", me: await this.api.me() });
    } catch {
      this.set({ status: "anonymous" });
    }
  }
  async login(req: LoginRequest): Promise<void> {
    this.set({ status: "signed-in", me: await this.api.login(req) }); // a refusal throws and leaves the state alone
  }
  /** Sign-up answers 202 whether or not the address exists, so the account is only known to work once the sign-in succeeds. */
  async signUp(req: SignUpRequest): Promise<void> {
    await this.api.signUp(req);
    await this.login({ email: req.email, password: req.password });
  }
  async logout(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await this.api.logout();
    } catch {
      // the cookie may still be valid, but this browser is done: the sign-in screen, and a later 401 if it was not cleared
    }
    this.signedOut();
  }
  /** A 401 'unauthorized' came back from any call: the session ended. */
  expired(): void {
    this.signedOut();
  }
  signedOut(): void {
    this.set({ status: "anonymous" });
  }
}
```

`components/dm-auth-form.ts` (the layout follows the mocks: a form column of at most 400px; the marketing panel of the mocks is not built):

```ts
import { SignUpRequest } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { authErrorText } from "../state/auth";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";

export class DmAuthForm extends LitElement {
  static properties = {
    mode: { type: String },
    email: { state: true },
    password: { state: true },
    shown: { state: true },
    busy: { state: true },
    problem: { state: true },
  };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: grid; place-items: center; min-height: 100%; padding: 32px 16px; }
      form { width: min(400px, 100%); display: grid; gap: 16px; }
      h1 { font-size: 30px; margin-bottom: 4px; }
      .field { position: relative; }
      .reveal { position: absolute; right: 2px; top: 22px; }
      .hint { font-size: 12px; color: var(--dm-muted); margin-top: 4px; }
      .btn.primary { min-height: 40px; }
      p.switch { color: var(--dm-muted); }
    `,
  ];
  declare mode: "sign-in" | "sign-up";
  declare email: string;
  declare password: string;
  declare shown: boolean;
  declare busy: boolean;
  declare problem: string | undefined;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this);
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.mode = "sign-in";
    this.email = "";
    this.password = "";
    this.shown = false;
    this.busy = false;
  }
  private async submit(e: Event) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const auth = this.ctx.services?.auth;
    const router = this.ctx.services?.router;
    if (!auth || !router || this.busy) return;
    this.problem = undefined;
    if (this.mode === "sign-up") {
      const ok = SignUpRequest.safeParse({ email: this.email, password: this.password });
      if (!ok.success) {
        this.problem = this.password.length < 12 ? "At least 12 characters." : "Enter a valid email address.";
        return;
      }
    }
    this.busy = true;
    try {
      if (this.mode === "sign-up") await auth.signUp({ email: this.email, password: this.password });
      else await auth.login({ email: this.email, password: this.password });
      this.password = "";
      router.navigate({ name: "chat" }, { replace: true });
    } catch (err) {
      this.problem =
        this.mode === "sign-up" && (err as { status?: number }).status === 401
          ? "We could not sign you in. If you already have an account, sign in instead."
          : authErrorText(err);
    } finally {
      this.busy = false;
    }
  }
  render() {
    const up = this.mode === "sign-up";
    const invalid = this.problem !== undefined;
    return html`
      <form @submit=${this.submit} novalidate>
        <div>
          <h1>${up ? "Create your account" : "Sign in"}</h1>
          <p class="muted">${up ? "Your documents stay private to you." : "Welcome back."}</p>
        </div>
        <div>
          <label for="email">Email</label>
          <input class="input" id="email" name="email" type="email" autocomplete="email" required aria-invalid=${invalid} .value=${this.email} @input=${(e: Event) => (this.email = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value)} />
        </div>
        <div class="field">
          <label for="password">Password</label>
          <input class="input" id="password" name="password" type=${this.shown ? "text" : "password"} autocomplete=${up ? "new-password" : "current-password"} required aria-invalid=${invalid} .value=${this.password} @input=${(e: Event) => (this.password = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value)} />
          <button type="button" class="btn icon reveal" aria-label=${this.shown ? "Hide password" : "Show password"} @click=${() => (this.shown = !this.shown)}><dm-icon name=${this.shown ? "eye-slash" : "eye"}></dm-icon></button>
          ${up ? html`<div class="hint">At least 12 characters</div>` : null}
        </div>
        ${this.problem ? html`<div class="error" role="alert"><dm-icon name="warning-circle"></dm-icon><span>${this.problem}</span></div>` : null}
        <button class="btn primary" type="submit" ?disabled=${this.busy}>${this.busy ? "One moment..." : up ? "Create account" : "Sign in"}</button>
        <p class="switch">${up ? html`Already have an account? <a href="/sign-in">Sign in</a>` : html`New to DocMind? <a href="/sign-up">Create an account</a>`}</p>
      </form>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-auth-form", DmAuthForm);
```

  (The reveal button needs the `eye` and `eye-slash` icons: add them to `icons.ts` (`eye.svg`, `eye-slash.svg`) in this task and raise the icon test's minimum from 30 to 37.)

  `services.ts`: add `auth: AuthStore` to `Services` and to `createServices` (`const auth = o.auth ?? new AuthStore(api)`; `hooks.unauthorized = () => auth.expired()` inside `createServices`, after the stores exist). `views.ts`: `"sign-in": "dm-auth-form"`, `"sign-up": "dm-auth-form"`; `dm-app.render` passes the mode: `html\`<${tag} mode=${route.name}></${tag}>\``. `components/index.ts` imports `./dm-auth-form`.

  The guard in `dm-app`. Replace its `connectedCallback`, `render` and add `decide` and `updated` (the rest of the file from Task 181 stays):

```ts
  private authState: StoreController<AuthState> | undefined;

  connectedCallback() {
    this.services ??= createServices();
    this.provider ??= new ContextProvider(this, { context: servicesContext, initialValue: this.services });
    this.route ??= new StoreController(this, this.services.router);
    this.authState ??= new StoreController(this, this.services.auth);
    super.connectedCallback();
    this.services.router.start();
    this.services.theme.apply();
    this.addEventListener("click", this.onClick);
    if (this.services.auth.get().status === "unknown") void this.services.auth.bootstrap(); // never rejects
  }

  /** What to draw and where to go, from the session and the route alone (pure). */
  private decide(): { wait: boolean; to?: Route } {
    const state = this.services.auth.get();
    if (state.status === "unknown") return { wait: true };
    const name = this.services.router.get().name;
    const open = name === "sign-in" || name === "sign-up" || name === "account-deleted";
    if (state.status === "anonymous" && !open) return { wait: false, to: { name: "sign-in" } };
    if (state.status === "signed-in" && (name === "sign-in" || name === "sign-up")) return { wait: false, to: { name: "chat" } };
    return { wait: false };
  }

  updated() {
    const { to } = this.decide();
    if (to) this.services.router.navigate(to, { replace: true });
  }

  render() {
    if (this.decide().wait) return html`<div class="boot" aria-busy="true"></div><dm-toasts></dm-toasts>`;
    const route = this.route?.value ?? this.services.router.get();
    const tag = unsafeStatic(VIEWS[route.name] ?? "dm-not-found"); // the tag comes from our own table, never from the URL
    return route.name === "sign-in" || route.name === "sign-up"
      ? html`<${tag} mode=${route.name}></${tag}><dm-toasts></dm-toasts>`
      : html`<${tag}></${tag}><dm-toasts></dm-toasts>`;
  }
```

  with `import type { Route } from "../router"; import type { AuthState } from "../state/auth";` added. The guard test helper in `dm-app.test.ts`:

```ts
const ME = { userId: "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10", tenantId: "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11", email: "a@example.com" };
function servicesWithAuth(path: string, over: Record<string, unknown>) {
  const win = { location: { pathname: path, search: "" }, history: { pushState: (_s: unknown, _t: string, u: string) => { win.location.pathname = u; }, replaceState: (_s: unknown, _t: string, u: string) => { win.location.pathname = u; } }, addEventListener: () => {}, removeEventListener: () => {} };
  const api = { me: async () => ME, login: async () => ME, logout: async () => {}, signUp: async () => {}, ...over } as never;
  return createServices({ router: new Router(win as never), api, auth: new AuthStore(api) });
}
```

  (imports: `ApiError` from `../api/http`, `AuthStore` from `../state/auth`; the `services(path)` helper of Task 181 is kept for the cases that do not need a session, and passes `auth: new AuthStore({ me: async () => ME, ... })` so its views are reachable, which it already is because the not-found and toast cases run with a signed-in or open route: give `services(path)` the same `auth` as `servicesWithAuth`.) Because the app now draws a busy marker until the session is known, the Task 181 cases that look for `dm-not-found` or `dm-toasts` right after mounting wait for it: wrap those first assertions in `await vi.waitFor(() => expect(...))`.

### Task 184a: `dm-dialog`, an accessible modal

**Spec:** §9 item 37 ("Delete my account"), `ui-mocks.md` §4 Dialogs ("Not mocked ... follow the component sheet's existing styles"). Decision D7.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/dm-dialog.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-dialog.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `<dm-dialog heading="..." ?open ?persistent>`: default slot is the body, `slot="actions"` the buttons. It sets `role="dialog"`, `aria-modal="true"` and `aria-labelledby` on its panel, moves focus into the panel on open (the element with `autofocus`, else the first focusable), keeps Tab and Shift+Tab inside it, closes on Escape and (unless `persistent`) on a backdrop click by firing `dm-close` (it never closes itself: the owner sets `open` to false), and gives focus back to what had it when it opened. Also exports `deepActiveElement(): Element | null`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `dm-dialog.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { deepActiveElement } from "./dm-dialog";
import "./dm-dialog";

type Dlg = HTMLElement & { updateComplete: Promise<unknown>; open: boolean };
async function mount(opts: { persistent?: boolean } = {}) {
  const opener = document.createElement("button");
  opener.textContent = "open";
  document.body.append(opener);
  opener.focus();
  const el = document.createElement("dm-dialog") as Dlg;
  el.setAttribute("heading", "Delete chat?");
  if (opts.persistent) el.setAttribute("persistent", "");
  el.innerHTML = `<p>Body</p><input id="first" aria-label="Password"><button slot="actions" id="cancel">Cancel</button><button slot="actions" id="go">Delete</button>`;
  document.body.append(el);
  await el.updateComplete;
  return { el, opener };
}
const panel = (el: Dlg) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector("[role=dialog]") as HTMLElement;

describe("dm-dialog", () => {
  it("draws nothing while closed, and a labelled modal panel when open", async () => {
    const { el } = await mount();
    expect(panel(el)).toBeNull();
    el.open = true;
    await el.updateComplete;
    expect(panel(el).getAttribute("aria-modal")).toBe("true");
    const labelled = panel(el).getAttribute("aria-labelledby") as string;
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.getElementById(labelled)?.textContent).toBe("Delete chat?");
  });

  it("moves focus in, wraps Tab at both ends, and gives focus back when it closes", async () => {
    const { el, opener } = await mount();
    el.open = true;
    await el.updateComplete;
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect((deepActiveElement() as HTMLElement | null)?.id).toBe("first"));
    const go = el.querySelector("#go") as HTMLButtonElement;
    go.focus();
    panel(el).dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "Tab", bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
    expect((deepActiveElement() as HTMLElement).id).toBe("first");
    panel(el).dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "Tab", shiftKey: true, bubbles: true, cancelable: true }));
    expect((deepActiveElement() as HTMLElement).id).toBe("go");
    el.open = false;
    await el.updateComplete;
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(deepActiveElement()).toBe(opener));
  });

  it("asks to close on Escape and on a backdrop click, but not on a click inside the panel or for a persistent one", async () => {
    const { el } = await mount();
    el.open = true;
    await el.updateComplete;
    const closes = vi.fn();
    el.addEventListener("dm-close", closes);
    panel(el).dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "Escape", bubbles: true }));
    expect(closes).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    (el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(".backdrop") as HTMLElement).dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent("click", { bubbles: true }));
    expect(closes).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    panel(el).dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent("click", { bubbles: true }));
    expect(closes).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(el.open).toBe(true); // it only asks
    const sticky = await mount({ persistent: true });
    sticky.el.open = true;
    await sticky.el.updateComplete;
    const stickyCloses = vi.fn();
    sticky.el.addEventListener("dm-close", stickyCloses);
    (sticky.el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(".backdrop") as HTMLElement).dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent("click", { bubbles: true }));
    expect(stickyCloses).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    panel(sticky.el).dispatchEvent(new KeyboardEvent("keydown", { key: "Escape", bubbles: true }));
    expect(stickyCloses).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1); // Escape always asks
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `dm-dialog.ts`.**

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html, type PropertyValues } from "lit";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";

const FOCUSABLE = "a[href], button:not([disabled]), input:not([disabled]), select:not([disabled]), textarea:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex='-1'])";

/** The element that has focus, looking through open shadow roots. */
export function deepActiveElement(): Element | null {
  let el: Element | null = document.activeElement;
  while (el?.shadowRoot?.activeElement) el = el.shadowRoot.activeElement;
  return el;
}

let ids = 0;

export class DmDialog extends LitElement {
  static properties = { heading: { type: String }, open: { type: Boolean, reflect: true }, persistent: { type: Boolean } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      .backdrop { position: fixed; inset: 0; z-index: 20; display: grid; place-items: center; padding: 16px; background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--dm-neutral-900) 50%, transparent); }
      .panel { width: min(440px, 100%); max-height: 100%; overflow: auto; padding: 22px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-lg); background: var(--dm-surface); box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-lg); display: grid; gap: 14px; }
      h2 { font-size: 20px; }
      .body { font-size: 14px; display: grid; gap: 12px; }
      .actions { display: flex; justify-content: flex-end; gap: 8px; }
    `,
  ];
  declare heading: string;
  declare open: boolean;
  declare persistent: boolean;
  private readonly titleId = `dm-dialog-title-${++ids}`;
  private opener: Element | null = null;

  constructor() {
    super();
    this.heading = "";
    this.open = false;
    this.persistent = false;
  }

  private close() {
    this.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-close", { bubbles: true, composed: true }));
  }
  private focusables(): HTMLElement[] {
    const own = [...(this.shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE) ?? [])];
    const slotted = [...(this.shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll("slot") ?? [])].flatMap((s) =>
      s.assignedElements({ flatten: true }).flatMap((e) => [e as HTMLElement, ...e.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(FOCUSABLE)]).filter((e) => e.matches(FOCUSABLE)),
    );
    return [...slotted, ...own].filter((e) => !e.hasAttribute("disabled"));
  }
  private onKey(e: KeyboardEvent) {
    if (e.key === "Escape") {
      e.stopPropagation();
      return this.close();
    }
    if (e.key !== "Tab") return;
    const items = this.focusables();
    const first = items[0];
    const last = items[items.length - 1];
    if (!first || !last) return e.preventDefault();
    const current = deepActiveElement();
    if (e.shiftKey && (current === first || !items.includes(current as HTMLElement))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      last.focus();
    } else if (!e.shiftKey && (current === last || !items.includes(current as HTMLElement))) {
      e.preventDefault();
      first.focus();
    }
  }
  protected updated(changed: PropertyValues<this>) {
    if (!changed.has("open")) return;
    if (this.open) {
      this.opener = deepActiveElement();
      const target = this.focusables().find((e) => e.hasAttribute("autofocus")) ?? this.focusables()[0];
      (target ?? this.shadowRoot?.querySelector<HTMLElement>(".panel"))?.focus();
    } else if (changed.get("open") === true) {
      (this.opener as HTMLElement | null)?.focus?.();
      this.opener = null;
    }
  }
  render() {
    if (!this.open) return html``;
    return html`
      <div class="backdrop" @click=${() => !this.persistent && this.close()}>
        <div class="panel" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" aria-labelledby=${this.titleId} tabindex="-1" @click=${(e: Event) => e.stopPropagation()} @keydown=${this.onKey}>
          <h2 id=${this.titleId}>${this.heading}</h2>
          <div class="body"><slot></slot></div>
          <div class="actions"><slot name="actions"></slot></div>
        </div>
      </div>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-dialog", DmDialog);
```

  **Probe.** The default slot also receives the `slot="actions"` buttons' siblings only; `slot="actions"` elements go to the named slot, so `focusables()` visits both. If happy-dom's `assignedElements` returns nothing for a slot, collect `[...this.children]` instead (the dialog's light-DOM children) and filter by `matches(FOCUSABLE)` plus their descendants; record it in the PR body.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { createServices } from "../services";
import { AuthStore } from "../state/auth";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import "./dm-settings";
import "./dm-dialog";

const ME = { userId: "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10", tenantId: "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11", email: "a@example.com" };
const RECEIPT = "A".repeat(43);

async function setup(over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const api = { me: async () => ME, login: async () => ME, logout: vi.fn(async () => {}), signUp: async () => {}, deleteAccount: vi.fn(async () => ({ erasure: "accepted", receipt: RECEIPT })), ...over } as never;
  const auth = new AuthStore(api);
  await auth.login({ email: ME.email, password: "x" });
  const services = createServices({ api, auth, fetch: (async () => new Response("{}")) as never });
  const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-settings");
  return { services, api, el };
}
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as T;
const settle = (el: Element) => (el as unknown as { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete;

describe("dm-settings", () => {
  it("shows the signed-in email, signs out, and changes the theme", async () => {
    const { services, api, el } = await setup();
    expect(q(el, "[data-email]").textContent).toBe("a@example.com");
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-sign-out]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "sign-in" }));
    expect((api as never as { logout: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).logout).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    const select = q<HTMLSelectElement>(el, "select[aria-label='Theme']");
    select.value = "light";
    select.dispatchEvent(new Event("change"));
    expect(services.theme.get()).toBe("light");
  });

  it("asks for the password in a dialog that says the deletion is permanent, and does nothing on Cancel", async () => {
    const { api, el } = await setup();
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-delete]").click();
    await settle(el);
    const dlg = q<HTMLElement & { open: boolean }>(el, "dm-dialog");
    expect(dlg.open).toBe(true);
    expect(dlg.textContent).toContain("This cannot be undone");
    expect(dlg.textContent).toContain("all your documents and all your chats");
    (dlg.querySelector("button[data-cancel]") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await settle(el);
    expect(dlg.open).toBe(false);
    expect((api as never as { deleteAccount: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).deleteAccount).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("confirms with the password, keeps the receipt in memory only, signs out and goes to the receipt page", async () => {
    const { services, api, el } = await setup();
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-delete]").click();
    await settle(el);
    const dlg = q<HTMLElement>(el, "dm-dialog");
    const input = dlg.querySelector("input[type=password]") as HTMLInputElement;
    input.value = "my password";
    input.dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
    (dlg.querySelector("button[data-confirm]") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "account-deleted" }));
    expect((api as never as { deleteAccount: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).deleteAccount).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ password: "my password" });
    expect(services.receipt.get()).toBe(RECEIPT);
    expect(services.auth.get()).toEqual({ status: "anonymous" });
    expect(JSON.stringify(Object.entries(localStorage))).not.toContain(RECEIPT);
    expect(JSON.stringify(Object.entries(sessionStorage))).not.toContain(RECEIPT);
  });

  it.each([
    [new ApiError(401, "invalid_credentials"), "The password is not right."],
    [new ApiError(409, "erasure_in_progress"), "Your account is already being deleted."],
    [new ApiError(429, "too_many_requests", 5), "Too many attempts. Try again in 5 seconds."],
    [new ApiError(500, "internal"), "Something went wrong. Nothing was deleted. Try again."],
  ])("a refusal (%o) stays in the dialog with a fixed sentence, and the account stays signed in", async (err, text) => {
    const { services, el } = await setup({ deleteAccount: vi.fn(async () => { throw err; }) });
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-delete]").click();
    await settle(el);
    const dlg = q<HTMLElement>(el, "dm-dialog");
    (dlg.querySelector("input[type=password]") as HTMLInputElement).value = "pw";
    (dlg.querySelector("input[type=password]") as HTMLInputElement).dispatchEvent(new Event("input"));
    (dlg.querySelector("button[data-confirm]") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(dlg.querySelector("[role=alert]")?.textContent).toContain(text));
    expect(services.auth.get().status).toBe("signed-in");
    expect(services.receipt.get()).toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { createServices } from "../services";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import "./dm-account-deleted";

const RECEIPT = "B".repeat(43);
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as T;

function setup(receipt: string | undefined, erasureStatus: () => Promise<unknown>) {
  const api = { erasureStatus: vi.fn(erasureStatus) } as never;
  const services = createServices({ api, fetch: (async () => new Response("{}")) as never });
  if (receipt) services.receipt.set(receipt);
  return { services, api };
}

describe("dm-account-deleted", () => {
  it("shows the receipt once with a copy button and the statement that it is not kept", async () => {
    const { services } = setup(RECEIPT, async () => ({ state: "pending" }));
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-account-deleted");
    expect(q(el, "[data-receipt]").textContent).toBe(RECEIPT);
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("shown once");
    const writeText = vi.fn(async () => {});
    Object.defineProperty(navigator, "clipboard", { value: { writeText }, configurable: true });
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-copy]").click();
    expect(writeText).toHaveBeenCalledWith(RECEIPT);
  });

  it("checks the state with the receipt and says in words whether the erasure is done", async () => {
    const answers = [{ state: "pending" }, { state: "done" }];
    const { services, api } = setup(RECEIPT, async () => answers.shift());
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-account-deleted");
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-check]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "[data-state]").textContent).toContain("In progress"));
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-check]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "[data-state]").textContent).toContain("Done"));
    expect((api as never as { erasureStatus: ReturnType<typeof vi.fn> }).erasureStatus).toHaveBeenCalledWith(RECEIPT);
  });

  it("handles an unknown receipt and a dead connection without showing server text", async () => {
    const { services } = setup(RECEIPT, async () => { throw new ApiError(404, "not_found"); });
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-account-deleted");
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-check]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "[data-state]").textContent).toContain("No deletion matches this receipt."));
    const down = setup(RECEIPT, async () => { throw new ApiError(0, "network"); });
    const el2 = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(down.services, "dm-account-deleted");
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el2, "button[data-check]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el2, "[data-state]").textContent).toContain("Could not reach the server."));
  });

  it("without a receipt (a reload) says the page cannot show it, and offers a way back", async () => {
    const { services } = setup(undefined, async () => ({ state: "done" }));
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-account-deleted");
    expect(q(el, "[data-receipt]")).toBeNull();
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("no longer on this page");
    expect(q<HTMLAnchorElement>(el, "a[href='/sign-up']")).not.toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { Store } from "./store";

/** The erasure receipt, kept in memory for this page only: never stored, never logged, gone on reload. */
export class ReceiptStore extends Store<string | undefined> {
  constructor() {
    super(undefined);
  }
}
```

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { authErrorText } from "../state/auth";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import type { ThemeChoice } from "../state/theme";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-dialog";
import "./dm-icon";

function deleteErrorText(err: unknown): string {
  if (err instanceof ApiError) {
    if (err.status === 401) return "The password is not right.";
    if (err.status === 409) return "Your account is already being deleted.";
    if (err.status === 429 || err.status === 0) return authErrorText(err);
  }
  return "Something went wrong. Nothing was deleted. Try again.";
}

export class DmSettings extends LitElement {
  static properties = { confirming: { state: true }, password: { state: true }, busy: { state: true }, problem: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; padding: 28px 40px; max-width: 720px; }
      h1 { font-size: 26px; margin-bottom: 20px; }
      section { padding: 16px 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); display: grid; gap: 10px; }
      h2 { font-size: 16px; }
      .row { display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: space-between; gap: 12px; }
      select { min-height: 36px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); background: var(--dm-surface); color: var(--dm-text); border: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); padding: 0 8px; font: inherit; }
    `,
  ];
  declare confirming: boolean;
  declare password: string;
  declare busy: boolean;
  declare problem: string | undefined;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.auth, s.theme]);
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.confirming = false;
    this.password = "";
    this.busy = false;
  }
  private async signOut() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!svc) return;
    await svc.auth.logout();
    svc.router.navigate({ name: "sign-in" }, { replace: true });
  }
  private async confirmDelete(e: Event) {
    e.preventDefault();
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!svc || this.busy || this.password === "") return;
    this.busy = true;
    this.problem = undefined;
    try {
      const done = await svc.api.deleteAccount({ password: this.password });
      this.password = "";
      svc.receipt.set(done.receipt); // memory only
      svc.auth.signedOut();
      svc.router.navigate({ name: "account-deleted" }, { replace: true });
    } catch (err) {
      this.problem = deleteErrorText(err);
    } finally {
      this.busy = false;
    }
  }
  private closeDialog() {
    this.confirming = false;
    this.password = "";
    this.problem = undefined;
  }
  render() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    const state = svc?.auth.get();
    return html`
      <h1>Settings</h1>
      <section aria-labelledby="account">
        <h2 id="account">Account</h2>
        <div class="row"><span>Signed in as <strong data-email>${state?.status === "signed-in" ? state.me.email : ""}</strong></span>
          <button class="btn secondary" data-sign-out @click=${this.signOut}><dm-icon name="sign-out"></dm-icon>Sign out</button></div>
      </section>
      <section aria-labelledby="appearance">
        <h2 id="appearance">Appearance</h2>
        <div class="row"><span>Theme</span>
          <select aria-label="Theme" .value=${svc?.theme.get() ?? "system"} @change=${(e: Event) => svc?.theme.choose((e.target as HTMLSelectElement).value as ThemeChoice)}>
            <option value="system">Match system</option><option value="dark">Dark</option><option value="light">Light</option>
          </select></div>
      </section>
      <section aria-labelledby="danger">
        <h2 id="danger">Delete account</h2>
        <p class="muted">Permanently deletes your account, your documents and your chats.</p>
        <div><button class="btn danger" data-delete @click=${() => (this.confirming = true)}>Delete my account</button></div>
      </section>
      <dm-dialog heading="Delete your account?" ?open=${this.confirming} persistent @dm-close=${this.closeDialog}>
        <form id="delete-form" @submit=${this.confirmDelete}>
          <p>This deletes your account, all your documents and all your chats. This cannot be undone. Your data is made unreadable first and then removed, which can take a little while if an answer is still being written.</p>
          <label for="del-pw">Enter your password to confirm</label>
          <input class="input" id="del-pw" type="password" autocomplete="current-password" autofocus .value=${this.password} @input=${(e: Event) => (this.password = (e.target as HTMLInputElement).value)} />
          ${this.problem ? html`<div class="error" role="alert"><dm-icon name="warning-circle"></dm-icon><span>${this.problem}</span></div>` : null}
        </form>
        <button slot="actions" class="btn secondary" data-cancel @click=${this.closeDialog}>Cancel</button>
        <button slot="actions" class="btn danger" data-confirm ?disabled=${this.busy || this.password === ""} @click=${this.confirmDelete}>Delete my account</button>
      </dm-dialog>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-settings", DmSettings);
```

`components/dm-account-deleted.ts`:

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";

export class DmAccountDeleted extends LitElement {
  static properties = { stateText: { state: true }, checking: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: grid; place-items: center; min-height: 100%; padding: 32px 16px; }
      main { width: min(520px, 100%); display: grid; gap: 14px; }
      h1 { font-size: 26px; }
      code { display: block; padding: 12px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); background: var(--dm-surface); box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-sm); overflow-wrap: anywhere; user-select: all; }
      .row { display: flex; gap: 8px; flex-wrap: wrap; }
    `,
  ];
  declare stateText: string | undefined;
  declare checking: boolean;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.receipt]);
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.checking = false;
  }
  private async copy(receipt: string) {
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(receipt);
      this.ctx.services?.toasts.push({ kind: "ok", text: "Receipt copied." });
    } catch {
      this.ctx.services?.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not copy. Select the receipt and copy it." });
    }
  }
  private async check(receipt: string) {
    const api = this.ctx.services?.api;
    if (!api || this.checking) return;
    this.checking = true;
    try {
      const { state } = await api.erasureStatus(receipt);
      this.stateText = state === "done" ? "Done. Your account and its data have been erased." : "In progress. Some of your data may remain until it finishes.";
    } catch (err) {
      this.stateText =
        err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404 ? "No deletion matches this receipt." : err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 0 ? "Could not reach the server. Check your connection." : "Could not check right now. Try again.";
    } finally {
      this.checking = false;
    }
  }
  render() {
    const receipt = this.ctx.services?.receipt.get();
    return html`<main>
      <h1>Your account is being deleted</h1>
      ${receipt
        ? html`<p>Keep this receipt. It is the only way to check that the deletion has finished. It is shown once: it is not stored anywhere, and it is gone when you leave this page.</p>
          <code data-receipt>${receipt}</code>
          <div class="row">
            <button class="btn secondary" data-copy @click=${() => this.copy(receipt)}><dm-icon name="copy"></dm-icon>Copy receipt</button>
            <button class="btn primary" data-check ?disabled=${this.checking} @click=${() => this.check(receipt)}>Check status</button>
          </div>
          <p data-state aria-live="polite">${this.stateText ?? ""}</p>`
        : html`<p>The receipt is no longer on this page, so it cannot be shown again. If you deleted your account, it is already unusable.</p>`}
      <p><a href="/sign-up">Create a new account</a></p>
    </main>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-account-deleted", DmAccountDeleted);
```

```ts
async function setup() {
  const api = { me: async () => ME, login: async () => ME, logout: vi.fn(async () => {}), signUp: async () => {} } as never;
  const auth = new AuthStore(api);

  return { services, el };
}
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as T;
const settle = (el: Element) => (el as unknown as { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete;

describe("dm-app-shell", () => {
```

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";

export class DmAppShell extends LitElement {
  static properties = { menuOpen: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: flex; height: 100%; }
      aside { flex: none; width: var(--dm-sidebar-w); display: flex; flex-direction: column; background: var(--dm-side); border-right: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); min-height: 0; }
      header { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; padding: 14px 14px 10px; }
      .logo { width: 26px; height: 26px; border-radius: 7px; display: grid; place-items: center; box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--dm-accent); color: var(--dm-accent); }
      .brand { font-size: 14px; font-weight: 500; }
      .block { display: grid; gap: 4px; padding: 4px 12px 8px; }
      .block .btn { justify-content: flex-start; min-height: 36px; }
      a.nav { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; min-height: 34px; padding: 0 10px; border-radius: var(--dm-radius-md); color: inherit; text-decoration: none; }
      a.nav[aria-current="page"] { background: var(--dm-accent-900); color: var(--dm-accent-200); }
      .list { flex: 1; min-height: 0; overflow: auto; padding: 0 6px; }
      footer { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding: 10px 14px; border-top: 1px solid var(--dm-divider); }
      [data-user] { flex: 1; min-width: 0; font-size: 12.5px; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
      main { flex: 1; min-width: 0; min-height: 0; overflow: auto; }
      .menu { display: none; }
      @media (max-width: 800px) {
        aside { position: fixed; inset: 0 auto 0 0; z-index: 5; transform: translateX(-100%); }
        aside[data-open] { transform: none; box-shadow: var(--dm-shadow-lg); }
        .menu { display: inline-flex; position: fixed; top: 8px; left: 8px; z-index: 6; }
      }
    `,
  ];
  declare menuOpen: boolean;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.router, s.auth, s.theme]);
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.menuOpen = false;
  }
  private async signOut() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!svc) return;
    await svc.auth.logout();
    svc.router.navigate({ name: "sign-in" }, { replace: true });
  }
  render() {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    const route = svc?.router.get();
    const auth = svc?.auth.get();
    return html`
      <button class="btn secondary menu" data-menu aria-expanded=${this.menuOpen} aria-controls="sidebar" @click=${() => (this.menuOpen = !this.menuOpen)}><dm-icon name="sidebar-simple"></dm-icon>Menu</button>
      <aside id="sidebar" ?data-open=${this.menuOpen}>
        <header><span class="logo"><dm-icon name="book-open-text"></dm-icon></span><span class="brand">DocMind</span></header>
        <nav aria-label="Primary" class="block">
          <button class="btn primary" data-new-chat @click=${() => { svc?.router.navigate({ name: "chat" }); this.menuOpen = false; }}><dm-icon name="plus"></dm-icon>New chat</button>
          <a class="nav" data-documents href="/documents" aria-current=${route?.name === "documents" || route?.name === "document" ? "page" : "false"}><dm-icon name="files"></dm-icon>Documents</a>
        </nav>
        <div class="list"><slot name="sidebar"></slot></div>
        <footer>
          <span data-user>${auth?.status === "signed-in" ? auth.me.email : ""}</span>
          <a class="btn icon" href="/settings" aria-label="Settings"><dm-icon name="gear"></dm-icon></a>
          <button class="btn icon" data-theme-toggle aria-label="Toggle theme" @click=${() => svc?.theme.choose(document.documentElement.dataset.theme === "light" ? "dark" : "light")}><dm-icon name=${document.documentElement.dataset.theme === "light" ? "moon" : "sun"}></dm-icon></button>
          <button class="btn icon" data-sign-out aria-label="Sign out" @click=${this.signOut}><dm-icon name="sign-out"></dm-icon></button>
        </footer>
      </aside>
      <main><slot></slot></main>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-app-shell", DmAppShell);
```

## Phase D: the experiences (surface-neutral) and the server's UI plane

Everything in this phase is Node and knows no surface except where it says so. Nothing is visible to a user until Task 189.

### Task 186a: the UI contracts, the `@docmind/experiences` package, the semantic catalogue

**Spec:** §7 (the component table: loom-ui kinds per need), §9 items 36 to 44. Findings U13, U14, U19; decisions D2, D10, D11, D14.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/contracts/src/ui.ts`, `packages/contracts/src/ui.test.ts`
- Create: `packages/experiences/package.json`, `packages/experiences/tsconfig.json`, `packages/experiences/tsconfig.test.json`, `packages/experiences/src/index.ts`, `packages/experiences/src/catalog.ts`, `packages/experiences/src/compose.ts`, `packages/experiences/src/catalog.test.ts`, `packages/experiences/src/compose.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/contracts/src/index.ts` (`export * from "./ui"`), root `package.json` (`pnpm.overrides`: `@loom-ui/renderer-terminal`), `apps/web/package.json` (devDependencies `@docmind/experiences`, `@loom-ui/renderer-terminal`), `pnpm-lock.yaml`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (contracts): `ViewName`, `SurfaceName`, `UiViewResponse`, `UiHydrateRequest`, `UiHydrateResponse` (catalogue). `UiHydrateRequest.requests` has 1 to 32 entries; `params` values are strings, numbers, booleans or null (a loom-ui `LoadParams`).
- Produces (package): `SURFACES = ["web", "terminal"]`; `DOCMIND_CATALOG: SemanticCatalog` (id `docmind.v1`), whose every entry resolves to a loom-ui framework kind on **both** surfaces; the composition helpers `widget`, `panel`, `tabs`, `label`, `build` (`compose.ts`). `build` seals, so every experience has a content address.
- The catalogue entry names and kinds (all `1.0.0`): `LibraryKpi` Kpi, `IngestHealth` StatusCard, `DocumentTable` Table, `StatusFilter` SelectInput, `Notice` Note (`text` is a text prop), `DocumentHealth` StatusCard, `DocumentKpi` Kpi, `DocumentFacts` Table, `DocumentSummary` A2UIView, `DocumentContent` A2UIView, `Transcript` Chat, `Composer` TextInput, `ScopeSelect` SelectInput, `ChatSearch` TextInput, `ChatTable` Table, `SourcesPanel` A2UIView.

`packages/contracts/src/ui.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { SurfaceName, UiHydrateRequest, UiHydrateResponse, UiViewResponse, ViewName } from "./ui";

const req = { experienceId: "docmind.library", versionHash: "sha256:abc", handle: "documents", params: {} };

describe("the UI plane contracts", () => {
  it("names six views and two surfaces", () => {
    expect(ViewName.options).toEqual(["library", "library-empty", "detail", "sessions", "chat", "sources"]);
    expect(SurfaceName.options).toEqual(["web", "terminal"]);
  });
  it("accepts a hydrate request of 1 to 32 loads with scalar params, and refuses nested params, an unknown view and an empty or oversized batch", () => {
    expect(UiHydrateRequest.safeParse({ view: "library", requests: [req] }).success).toBe(true);
    expect(UiHydrateRequest.safeParse({ view: "library", requests: [{ ...req, params: { status: "ready", n: 3, q: null } }] }).success).toBe(true);
    for (const bad of [
      { view: "library", requests: [{ ...req, params: { a: { b: 1 } } }] },
      { view: "nope", requests: [req] },
      { view: "library", requests: [] },
      { view: "library", requests: Array.from({ length: 33 }, () => req) },
    ])
      expect(UiHydrateRequest.safeParse(bad).success).toBe(false);
  });
  it("accepts a view response and refuses one without an inventory or with a bad priority", () => {
    const ok = {
      view: "library", surface: "web", experienceId: "docmind.library", versionHash: "sha256:abc", blueprint: { _tag: "Blueprint" },
      inventory: { experienceId: "docmind.library", versionHash: "sha256:abc", handles: [{ handle: "documents", priority: "P0", params: ["status"], revision: "r" }] },
      fingerprint: { restrictedHandles: [], restrictedProjectionNodes: [], placeholderNodes: [] },
    };
    expect(UiViewResponse.safeParse(ok).success).toBe(true);
    expect(UiViewResponse.safeParse({ ...ok, inventory: undefined }).success).toBe(false);
    expect(UiViewResponse.safeParse({ ...ok, inventory: { ...ok.inventory, handles: [{ ...ok.inventory.handles[0], priority: "P9" }] } }).success).toBe(false);
  });
  it("a hydrate response is an array of results", () => {
    expect(UiHydrateResponse.parse({ results: [{ state: "ready", value: 1 }] })).toEqual({ results: [{ state: "ready", value: 1 }] });
    expect(UiHydrateResponse.safeParse({ results: "x" }).success).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

`packages/experiences/src/catalog.test.ts`:

```ts
import { FRAMEWORK_KINDS } from "@loom-ui/core";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { DOCMIND_CATALOG, ENTRY_KINDS, KINDS, SURFACES } from "./catalog";

describe("the DocMind semantic catalogue", () => {
  it("is surface-neutral: every entry resolves on every surface, to a loom-ui framework kind", () => {
    expect(SURFACES).toEqual(["web", "terminal"]);
    for (const [name, kind] of Object.entries(ENTRY_KINDS)) {
      expect(FRAMEWORK_KINDS, `${name} -> ${kind}`).toContain(kind);
      const entry = DOCMIND_CATALOG.entries.find((e) => e.name === name);
      expect(entry, name).toBeDefined();
      for (const surface of SURFACES) expect(entry?.surfaces?.[surface]?.kind, `${name} on ${surface}`).toBe(kind);
    }
  });
  it("lists exactly the sixteen entries, once each", () => {
    expect(DOCMIND_CATALOG.entries.map((e) => e.name).sort()).toEqual(Object.keys(ENTRY_KINDS).sort());
    expect(Object.keys(ENTRY_KINDS)).toHaveLength(16);
  });
  it("declares the same kind contracts loom-ui's renderers draw (props and binding)", () => {
    expect(KINDS.Table).toEqual({ kind: "Table", props: [], binding: "required" });
    expect(KINDS.A2UIView).toEqual({ kind: "A2UIView", props: ["view"], binding: "required" });
    expect(KINDS.Chat.props).toEqual(["emptyText", "editable"]);
    expect(KINDS.Note.binding).toBe("none");
  });
});
```

`packages/experiences/src/compose.test.ts`:

```ts
import { compile } from "@loom-ui/core";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { DOCMIND_CATALOG, SURFACES } from "./catalog";
import { build, panel, tabs, widget } from "./compose";

const notice = (id: string) => widget({ id, type: "Notice", props: { text: "Hello" } });
const make = () => build({ id: "docmind.test", objective: "Test", children: [notice("a"), tabs("t", [panel("p1", "One", [notice("b")]), panel("p2", "Two", [notice("c")])])] });

describe("compose", () => {
  it("seals: a content address that is stable between builds and changes with the content", () => {
    expect(make().versionHash).toMatch(/^sha256:/);
    expect(make().versionHash).toBe(make().versionHash);
    expect(build({ id: "docmind.test", objective: "Test", children: [notice("a")] }).versionHash).not.toBe(make().versionHash);
  });
  it("gives every node an ordering key, level by level", () => {
    const root = make().root as { children: { id: string; order: string; children?: { order: string }[] }[] };
    expect(root.children.map((c) => c.order)).toEqual(["a", "b"]);
    expect(root.children[1]?.children?.map((c) => c.order)).toEqual(["a", "b"]);
  });
  it("compiles with no finding on every surface (a titled tabs container, widgets with text props)", () => {
    for (const surface of SURFACES) {
      const r = compile(make(), { surface, catalog: DOCMIND_CATALOG, hydrationProjectionId: "loom-ui.hydration", hydrationProjectionVersion: "1.1.0", viewer: {}, placeholderKey: "k".repeat(32) });
      expect(r.findings, surface).toEqual([]);
      expect(r.ok).toBe(true);
    }
  });
  it("refuses more than 26 siblings (the ordering keys are single letters)", () => {
    expect(() => build({ id: "x", objective: "x", children: Array.from({ length: 27 }, (_, i) => notice(`n${i}`)) })).toThrow(/at most 26/);
  });
});
```

`packages/contracts/src/ui.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";

/** The experiences DocMind composes (Task 186b). */
export const ViewName = z.enum(["library", "library-empty", "detail", "sessions", "chat", "sources"]);
export type ViewName = z.infer<typeof ViewName>;

/** The surfaces an experience is compiled for. A new surface adds a value here and a key per catalogue entry. */
export const SurfaceName = z.enum(["web", "terminal"]);
export type SurfaceName = z.infer<typeof SurfaceName>;

const Scalar = z.union([z.string(), z.number(), z.boolean(), z.null()]);

/** `GET /api/ui/views/:view?surface=`: the compiled blueprint and the handle inventory; never a provider, never data. */
export const UiViewResponse = z.object({
  view: ViewName,
  surface: SurfaceName,
  experienceId: z.string(),
  versionHash: z.string(),
  blueprint: z.record(z.unknown()),
  inventory: z.object({
    experienceId: z.string(),
    versionHash: z.string(),
    handles: z.array(
      z.object({
        handle: z.string(),
        priority: z.enum(["P0", "P1", "P2"]),
        params: z.array(z.string()),
        revision: z.string(),
        finding: z.record(z.unknown()).optional(),
      }),
    ),
  }),
  fingerprint: z
    .object({
      audience: z.string().optional(),
      restrictedHandles: z.array(z.string()),
      restrictedProjectionNodes: z.array(z.string()),
      placeholderNodes: z.array(z.string()),
    })
    .passthrough(),
});
export type UiViewResponse = z.infer<typeof UiViewResponse>;

/** `POST /api/ui/hydrate`: loom-ui `HandleLoadRequest`s for one view (1 to 32 per call). */
export const UiHydrateRequest = z.object({
  view: ViewName,
  requests: z
    .array(z.object({ experienceId: z.string(), versionHash: z.string(), handle: z.string(), params: z.record(Scalar) }))
    .min(1)
    .max(32),
});
export type UiHydrateRequest = z.infer<typeof UiHydrateRequest>;

/** One loom-ui `HandleLoadResult` per request, in order. The browser's split controller validates each result itself. */
export const UiHydrateResponse = z.object({ results: z.array(z.unknown()) });
export type UiHydrateResponse = z.infer<typeof UiHydrateResponse>;
```

```ts
import { type KindContract, type SemanticCatalog, defineSemanticCatalog } from "@loom-ui/core";

/** The surfaces DocMind compiles for. Adding one means adding a key to every entry below, nothing else. */
export const SURFACES = ["web", "terminal"] as const;

/** The loom contracts of the framework kinds DocMind uses (the same as `frameworkKindContracts` and `chatKindContracts` of the renderers). */
export const KINDS = {
  Kpi: { kind: "Kpi", props: ["unit", "format"], binding: "required" },
  Table: { kind: "Table", props: [], binding: "required" },
  StatusCard: { kind: "StatusCard", props: [], binding: "required" },
  Note: { kind: "Note", props: ["text"], binding: "none" },
  A2UIView: { kind: "A2UIView", props: ["view"], binding: "required" },
  Chat: { kind: "Chat", props: ["emptyText", "editable"], binding: "required" },
  TextInput: { kind: "TextInput", props: ["label", "placeholder", "multiline", "maxChars", "submitLabel"], binding: "none" },
  SelectInput: { kind: "SelectInput", props: ["label", "options", "placeholder"], binding: "none" },
} as const satisfies Record<string, KindContract>;
type KindName = keyof typeof KINDS;

/** Entry name to kind: DocMind's whole vocabulary. */
export const ENTRY_KINDS: Record<string, KindName> = {
  LibraryKpi: "Kpi",
  IngestHealth: "StatusCard",
  DocumentTable: "Table",
  StatusFilter: "SelectInput",
  Notice: "Note",
  DocumentHealth: "StatusCard",
  DocumentKpi: "Kpi",
  DocumentFacts: "Table",
  DocumentSummary: "A2UIView",
  DocumentContent: "A2UIView",
  Transcript: "Chat",
  Composer: "TextInput",
  ScopeSelect: "SelectInput",
  ChatSearch: "TextInput",
  ChatTable: "Table",
  SourcesPanel: "A2UIView",
};

export const DOCMIND_CATALOG: SemanticCatalog = defineSemanticCatalog({
  id: "docmind.v1",
  entries: Object.entries(ENTRY_KINDS).map(([name, kind]) => ({
    name,
    version: "1.0.0",
    props: [...KINDS[kind].props],
    ...(kind === "Note" && { textProps: ["text"] }),
    binding: KINDS[kind].binding,
    surfaces: Object.fromEntries(SURFACES.map((s) => [s, KINDS[kind]])),
  })),
});
```

`packages/experiences/src/compose.ts`:

```ts
import { type ContainerNode, type Experience, type Priority, type ProviderSpec, type SemanticNode, genesis } from "@loom-ui/core";
import { seal } from "@loom-ui/core/log";

export type Child = SemanticNode | ContainerNode;

/** A `LocalizedText`: the key is for a future catalogue of words, the fallback is what M6 shows. */
export const label = (key: string, fallback: string) => ({ key, fallback });

export function widget(o: { id: string; type: string; span?: number; handle?: string; priority?: Priority; props?: Record<string, unknown>; title?: string }): SemanticNode {
  return {
    id: o.id,
    order: "",
    kind: "semantic",
    type: o.type,
    typeVersion: "1.0.0",
    span: o.span ?? 12,
    props: o.props ?? {},
    ...(o.title !== undefined && { label: label(`ui.${o.id}`, o.title) }),
    ...(o.handle !== undefined && { binding: { handle: o.handle, priority: o.priority ?? "P0" } }),
  };
}

/** A titled section of a tab set: a grid, so its children's spans apply. */
export const panel = (id: string, title: string, children: Child[]): ContainerNode => ({
  id, order: "", kind: "container", direction: "grid", span: 12, title: label(`ui.${id}`, title), children,
});

/** A tab set: the `tabs` role is drawn by `referenceContainers()` on the web and as a titled stack elsewhere. */
export const tabs = (id: string, panels: ContainerNode[]): ContainerNode => ({
  id, order: "", kind: "container", direction: "column", span: 12, role: "tabs", children: panels,
});

function ordered<T extends Child>(nodes: T[]): T[] {
  if (nodes.length > 26) throw new Error("compose: at most 26 siblings (the ordering keys are single letters)");
  return nodes.map((n, i) => ({ ...n, order: String.fromCharCode(0x61 + i), ...(n.kind === "container" && { children: ordered(n.children as Child[]) }) }));
}

/** An experience, sealed (content-addressed). Content-free: nothing a user owns ever enters one. */
export function build(o: { id: string; objective: string; children: Child[]; providers?: Record<string, ProviderSpec> }): Experience {
  const g = genesis({
    experienceId: o.id,
    intent: { objective: o.objective, source: "user" },
    meta: { author: "docmind", createdAt: "2026-10-21T00:00:00Z", sourceKind: "code" },
  });
  return seal({ ...g, root: { ...(g.root as ContainerNode), children: ordered(o.children) }, providers: o.providers ?? {} });
}
```

### Task 186b: the six experiences, the `docmind` provider specs, and `payload(name, surface)`

**Spec:** Decisions D10, D11, D12, D14. Findings U14, U15, U18.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/experiences/src/providers.ts`, `packages/experiences/src/views.ts`, `packages/experiences/src/a2ui.ts`, `packages/experiences/src/payload.ts`, `packages/experiences/src/views.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/experiences/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `build`, `widget`, `panel`, `tabs`, `DOCMIND_CATALOG`, `SURFACES` (186a).
- Produces: constants `LIST_CAP = 200`, `CHAT_LIST_CAP = 50`, `CONTENT_STEP = 20`, `CONTENT_CAP = 200`; `PINS` (the pin of each provider target), `KIND = "docmind"`, `provider(handle, o)`; the A2UI trees `SUMMARY_VIEW`, `CONTENT_VIEW`, `SOURCES_VIEW`; `EXPERIENCES: Record<ViewName, Experience>`; `ViewPayload`, `ViewsOptions`, and `createViews(o)` with `names`, `surfaces`, `payload(name, surface)` (`hydrate` arrives in 186c; this task's `Views` type has it as a method that throws until then is **not** allowed, so 186c adds it to the interface and this task exports `createViewPayloads`, which 186c wraps).
- The handles (D11), their kinds, priorities and parameters: `library`: `doc-total` P0, `doc-ready` P0, `ingest` P0 (step `documents.list {limit: 200}`), `documents` P0 (step `documents.list {limit: 200, status: "all"}`, overridable `status`); `detail` (all step `document.get {id}` overridable `id`, except content): `doc-status` P0, `doc-summary` P0, `doc-words` P1, `doc-pages` P1, `doc-minutes` P1, `doc-facts` P1, and `doc-content` P1 (step `document.content {id, upto: 20, passage: ""}`, overridable `id`, `upto`, `passage`); `sessions`: `chats` P0 (step `chats.list {q: ""}`, overridable `q`); `chat`: `transcript` P2 (published by the page; step `host.published`); `sources`: `sources` P2 (published by the page; step `host.published`); `library-empty` has no handle.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `views.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { SURFACES } from "./catalog";
import { createViewPayloads } from "./payload";
import { EXPERIENCES } from "./views";

const NAMES = ["library", "library-empty", "detail", "sessions", "chat", "sources"] as const;
const views = createViewPayloads({ placeholderKey: "k".repeat(32), revisionKey: "r".repeat(32) });
const handlesOf = (name: (typeof NAMES)[number]) => views.payload(name, "web").inventory.handles.map((h) => `${h.handle}:${h.priority}`).sort();

describe("the experiences", () => {
  it("are six, sealed and content-addressed, and the same bytes every boot", () => {
    expect(Object.keys(EXPERIENCES).sort()).toEqual([...NAMES].sort());
    const again = createViewPayloads({ placeholderKey: "x".repeat(32), revisionKey: "y".repeat(32) });
    for (const n of NAMES) {
      expect(EXPERIENCES[n].versionHash).toMatch(/^sha256:/);
      expect(again.payload(n, "web").versionHash).toBe(views.payload(n, "web").versionHash);
    }
  });

  it.each(SURFACES)("compile with no finding for the %s surface, and the inventory lists exactly the bound handles", (surface) => {
    for (const n of NAMES) {
      const p = views.payload(n, surface);
      expect(p.surface).toBe(surface);
      expect(p.blueprint).toMatchObject({ _tag: "Blueprint", irVersion: "2.5.0" });
      expect(p.inventory.versionHash).toBe(p.versionHash);
    }
    expect(handlesOf("library")).toEqual(["doc-ready:P0", "doc-total:P0", "documents:P0", "ingest:P0"]);
    expect(handlesOf("library-empty")).toEqual([]);
    expect(handlesOf("detail")).toEqual(["doc-content:P1", "doc-facts:P1", "doc-minutes:P1", "doc-pages:P1", "doc-status:P0", "doc-summary:P0", "doc-words:P1"]);
    expect(handlesOf("sessions")).toEqual(["chats:P0"]);
    expect(handlesOf("chat")).toEqual(["transcript:P2"]);
    expect(handlesOf("sources")).toEqual(["sources:P2"]);
  });

  it("names, per handle, only the parameters a load may override", () => {
    const params = (n: (typeof NAMES)[number]) => Object.fromEntries(views.payload(n, "web").inventory.handles.map((h) => [h.handle, h.params]));
    expect(params("library").documents).toEqual(["status"]);
    expect(params("library")["doc-total"]).toEqual([]);
    expect(params("detail")["doc-summary"]).toEqual(["id"]);
    expect(params("detail")["doc-content"]).toEqual(["id", "passage", "upto"]);
    expect(params("sessions").chats).toEqual(["q"]);
  });

  it("the detail view is a tab set of three titled panels", () => {
    const blueprint = JSON.stringify(views.payload("detail", "web").blueprint);
    expect(blueprint).toContain('"role":"tabs"');
    for (const title of ["Summary", "Analysis", "Content"]) expect(blueprint).toContain(`"${title}"`);
  });

  it("carry no provider detail and no data: the payload never names a tool, a pin or a script", () => {
    for (const n of NAMES) {
      const wire = JSON.stringify(views.payload(n, "web"));
      expect(wire).not.toMatch(/documents\.list|document\.get|document\.content|chats\.list|docmind:.*@1|scriptRef|toolName/);
    }
  });

  it("hold the fixed texts of the empty state, and nothing a user owns", () => {
    expect(JSON.stringify(views.payload("library-empty", "web").blueprint)).toContain("No documents yet");
  });
});
```

`providers.ts`:

```ts
import type { ProviderSpec } from "@loom-ui/core";

export const LIST_CAP = 200;
export const CHAT_LIST_CAP = 50;
export const CONTENT_STEP = 20;
export const CONTENT_CAP = 200;

/** The step kind DocMind registers with hydration (Task 186c). */
export const KIND = "docmind";

/**
 * The contract pin of each provider target: bump the number when a target's raw output changes shape. A step carries
 * its pin; the data provider refuses a different one (`schemaDrift`), which is how a stale view is noticed.
 */
export const PINS = {
  "documents.list": "docmind:documents.list@1",
  "document.get": "docmind:document.get@1",
  "document.content": "docmind:document.content@1",
  "chats.list": "docmind:chats.list@1",
  "host.published": "docmind:host.published@1",
} as const;
export type Target = keyof typeof PINS;

export function provider(handle: string, o: { target: Target; args: Record<string, unknown>; overridable?: string[]; reshaper: string }): ProviderSpec {
  return {
    handle,
    executionGraph: [
      { stepId: "read", kind: KIND, toolName: o.target, toolVersion: "1.0.0", toolSchemaHash: PINS[o.target], arguments: o.args, dependsOn: [], idempotent: true, overridable: o.overridable ?? [] },
    ],
    reshaping: { engine: "Named", scriptRef: o.reshaper },
    outputShapeHash: "",
    outputCheck: "contract",
    cachePolicy: { ttlSeconds: 0, scope: "principal" },
  };
}
```

`a2ui.ts` (the three trees; relative bindings inside a List are `"/field"` and resolve against the element first, Finding U18):

```ts
/** The summary tab: the tl;dr, key points, topics and entities, suggested questions as buttons. Data: `SummaryData` (186c). */
export const SUMMARY_VIEW = {
  catalogId: "docmind.v1",
  nodes: [
    { id: "root", component: "Column", children: ["tldr", "kp-h", "kp", "meta", "qs-h", "qs"] },
    { id: "tldr", component: "Text", text: { path: "/tldr" } },
    { id: "kp-h", component: "Text", text: "Key points" },
    { id: "kp", component: "List", children: { path: "/keyPoints", componentId: "kp-item" } },
    { id: "kp-item", component: "Text", text: { path: "/text" } },
    { id: "meta", component: "Text", text: { path: "/meta" } },
    { id: "qs-h", component: "Text", text: "Suggested questions" },
    { id: "qs", component: "List", children: { path: "/questions", componentId: "q-item" } },
    { id: "q-item", component: "Button", label: { path: "/label" }, action: "ask-question" },
  ],
} as const;

/** The content tab: passages with where they sit, and a button for more. Data: `ContentData` (186c). */
export const CONTENT_VIEW = {
  catalogId: "docmind.v1",
  nodes: [
    { id: "root", component: "Column", children: ["note", "list", "more"] },
    { id: "note", component: "Text", text: { path: "/note" } },
    { id: "list", component: "List", children: { path: "/passages", componentId: "p-item" } },
    { id: "p-item", component: "Column", children: ["p-head", "p-text"] },
    { id: "p-head", component: "Text", text: { path: "/head" } },
    { id: "p-text", component: "Text", text: { path: "/text" } },
    { id: "more", component: "List", children: { path: "/more", componentId: "more-item" } },
    { id: "more-item", component: "Button", label: { path: "/label" }, action: "show-more" },
  ],
} as const;

/** The sources panel: each cited passage with its document, place and snippet. Data: published by the page. */
export const SOURCES_VIEW = {
  catalogId: "docmind.v1",
  nodes: [
    { id: "root", component: "Column", children: ["note", "list"] },
    { id: "note", component: "Text", text: { path: "/note" } },
    { id: "list", component: "List", children: { path: "/sources", componentId: "s-item" } },
    { id: "s-item", component: "Column", children: ["s-title", "s-where", "s-snippet", "s-open"] },
    { id: "s-title", component: "Text", text: { path: "/title" } },
    { id: "s-where", component: "Text", text: { path: "/where" } },
    { id: "s-snippet", component: "Text", text: { path: "/snippet" } },
    { id: "s-open", component: "Button", label: "Open in document", action: "open-source" },
  ],
} as const;
```
