  (The "Open in document" button's `context` is the List element, carrying its `index`; Task 186d reads only the index.)

`views.ts`:

```ts
import type { Experience } from "@loom-ui/core";
import type { ViewName } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { CONTENT_VIEW, SOURCES_VIEW, SUMMARY_VIEW } from "./a2ui";
import { build, panel, tabs, widget } from "./compose";
import { CONTENT_STEP, LIST_CAP, provider } from "./providers";

const published = (handle: string) => provider(handle, { target: "host.published", args: {}, reshaper: "host-published" });
const doc = (handle: string, reshaper: string) => provider(handle, { target: "document.get", args: { id: "" }, overridable: ["id"], reshaper });
const summary = (handle: string, reshaper: string) => provider(handle, { target: "documents.list", args: { limit: LIST_CAP }, reshaper });

const library = (): Experience =>
  build({
    id: "docmind.library",
    objective: "Documents",
    children: [
      widget({ id: "total", type: "LibraryKpi", span: 4, handle: "doc-total", title: "Documents" }),
      widget({ id: "ready", type: "LibraryKpi", span: 4, handle: "doc-ready", title: "Ready" }),
      widget({ id: "health", type: "IngestHealth", span: 4, handle: "ingest", title: "Processing" }),
      widget({
        id: "filter", type: "StatusFilter", span: 12,
        props: { label: "Show", options: [{ value: "all", label: "All documents" }, { value: "ready", label: "Ready" }, { value: "processing", label: "Processing" }, { value: "failed", label: "Failed" }] },
      }),
      widget({ id: "table", type: "DocumentTable", span: 12, handle: "documents", title: "All documents" }),
    ],
    providers: {
      "doc-total": summary("doc-total", "library-total"),
      "doc-ready": summary("doc-ready", "library-ready"),
      ingest: summary("ingest", "library-health"),
      documents: provider("documents", { target: "documents.list", args: { limit: LIST_CAP, status: "all" }, overridable: ["status"], reshaper: "library-table" }),
    },
  });

const libraryEmpty = (): Experience =>
  build({
    id: "docmind.library-empty",
    objective: "Documents",
    children: [
      widget({ id: "empty", type: "Notice", props: { text: "No documents yet. Drop a file anywhere on this page, or choose Upload. DocMind reads it, and then you can ask questions about it." } }),
    ],
  });

const detail = (): Experience =>
  build({
    id: "docmind.detail",
    objective: "Document",
    children: [
      widget({ id: "status", type: "DocumentHealth", span: 12, handle: "doc-status", title: "Status" }),
      tabs("tabs", [
        panel("summary", "Summary", [widget({ id: "summary-view", type: "DocumentSummary", handle: "doc-summary", props: { view: SUMMARY_VIEW } })]),
        panel("analysis", "Analysis", [
          widget({ id: "words", type: "DocumentKpi", span: 4, handle: "doc-words", priority: "P1", title: "Words" }),
          widget({ id: "pages", type: "DocumentKpi", span: 4, handle: "doc-pages", priority: "P1", title: "Pages" }),
          widget({ id: "minutes", type: "DocumentKpi", span: 4, handle: "doc-minutes", priority: "P1", title: "Reading time" }),
          widget({ id: "facts", type: "DocumentFacts", span: 12, handle: "doc-facts", priority: "P1", title: "Facts" }),
        ]),
        panel("content", "Content", [widget({ id: "content-view", type: "DocumentContent", handle: "doc-content", priority: "P1", props: { view: CONTENT_VIEW } })]),
      ]),
    ],
    providers: {
      "doc-status": doc("doc-status", "detail-status"),
      "doc-summary": doc("doc-summary", "detail-summary"),
      "doc-words": doc("doc-words", "detail-words"),
      "doc-pages": doc("doc-pages", "detail-pages"),
      "doc-minutes": doc("doc-minutes", "detail-minutes"),
      "doc-facts": doc("doc-facts", "detail-facts"),
      "doc-content": provider("doc-content", { target: "document.content", args: { id: "", upto: CONTENT_STEP, passage: "" }, overridable: ["id", "upto", "passage"], reshaper: "detail-content" }),
    },
  });

const sessions = (): Experience =>
  build({
    id: "docmind.sessions",
    objective: "Chats",
    children: [
      widget({ id: "search", type: "ChatSearch", props: { label: "Search chats", placeholder: "Search chats", submitLabel: "Search", maxChars: 100 } }),
      widget({ id: "chats", type: "ChatTable", handle: "chats", title: "Chats" }),
    ],
    providers: { chats: provider("chats", { target: "chats.list", args: { q: "" }, overridable: ["q"], reshaper: "sessions-table" }) },
  });

const chat = (): Experience =>
  build({
    id: "docmind.chat",
    objective: "Chat",
    children: [
      widget({ id: "transcript", type: "Transcript", handle: "transcript", priority: "P2", title: "Conversation", props: { emptyText: "Ask a question about your documents." } }),
      widget({ id: "scope", type: "ScopeSelect", props: { label: "Search in", options: [{ value: "all", label: "All documents" }, { value: "started", label: "The documents this chat started with" }] } }),
      widget({ id: "composer", type: "Composer", props: { label: "Message", placeholder: "Ask about your documents", multiline: true, maxChars: 4000, submitLabel: "Send" } }),
    ],
    providers: { transcript: published("transcript") },
  });

const sources = (): Experience =>
  build({
    id: "docmind.sources",
    objective: "Sources",
    children: [widget({ id: "panel", type: "SourcesPanel", handle: "sources", priority: "P2", title: "Sources", props: { view: SOURCES_VIEW } })],
    providers: { sources: published("sources") },
  });

export const EXPERIENCES: Record<ViewName, Experience> = {
  library: library(),
  "library-empty": libraryEmpty(),
  detail: detail(),
  sessions: sessions(),
  chat: chat(),
  sources: sources(),
};
```

`payload.ts`:

```ts
import { createHmac } from "node:crypto";
import type { SurfaceName, ViewName } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { type CompileResult, compile } from "@loom-ui/core";
import { HYDRATION_PROJECTION_ID, HYDRATION_PROJECTION_VERSION, type HandleInventory, handleInventory } from "@loom-ui/hydration";
import type { Blueprint } from "@loom/blueprint-runtime";
import { DOCMIND_CATALOG, SURFACES } from "./catalog";
import { EXPERIENCES } from "./views";

export interface ViewPayload {
  view: ViewName;
  surface: SurfaceName;
  experienceId: string;
  versionHash: string;
  blueprint: Blueprint;
  inventory: HandleInventory;
  fingerprint: CompileResult["fingerprint"];
}
export interface ViewsOptions {
  /** 16 or more bytes: keys the aliases loom-ui gives restricted nodes (DocMind has none, but the key is not optional in production). */
  placeholderKey: string;
  /** A server secret: keys each handle's revision, so a client cannot confirm a guessed provider. */
  revisionKey: string;
  /** The hook the server logs through: a handle and a finding CODE, never a message. */
  onFinding?(handle: string, code: string): void;
}

export const VIEW_NAMES = Object.keys(EXPERIENCES) as ViewName[];

/** The sealed, compiled view per (name, surface) with its handle inventory: built once, content-free, the same for everyone. */
export function createViewPayloads(o: ViewsOptions) {
  const revisionOf = (handle: string, fingerprint: string): string => createHmac("sha256", o.revisionKey).update(`${handle}\0${fingerprint}`).digest("hex");
  const cache = new Map<string, ViewPayload>();
  for (const surface of SURFACES)
    for (const view of VIEW_NAMES) {
      const exp = EXPERIENCES[view];
      const compiled = compile(exp, {
        surface,
        catalog: DOCMIND_CATALOG,
        hydrationProjectionId: HYDRATION_PROJECTION_ID,
        hydrationProjectionVersion: HYDRATION_PROJECTION_VERSION,
        viewer: {},
        placeholderKey: o.placeholderKey,
      });
      if (!compiled.ok || compiled.findings.length > 0) throw new Error(`experience ${view} does not compile for ${surface}: ${compiled.findings.map((f) => f.code).join(",")}`);
      cache.set(`${surface}:${view}`, {
        view, surface, experienceId: exp.experienceId, versionHash: exp.versionHash,
        blueprint: compiled.blueprint, inventory: handleInventory(exp, { revisionOf }).inventory, fingerprint: compiled.fingerprint,
      });
    }
  return {
    names: VIEW_NAMES as readonly ViewName[],
    surfaces: SURFACES as readonly SurfaceName[],
    payload(name: ViewName, surface: SurfaceName): ViewPayload {
      const p = cache.get(`${surface}:${name}`);
      if (!p) throw new Error("unknown view or surface");
      return p;
    },
    revisionOf,
  };
}
```

  The error message names a view and finding codes (fixed words), never data. `index.ts`: export `./providers`, `./views`, `./a2ui`, `./payload`.

(b) If `handleInventory` rejects `kind: "docmind"` steps (it only reads `overridable`, priority and a fingerprint), read its error; the provider must not be executed for the inventory. ### Task 186c: reshapers, argument checks, the invoker, and `hydrate`

**Spec:** Decisions D6, D10, D12; Findings U14, U19. Tenancy rule: the invoker is built per request from `UiReaders`, which the server closes over the session's identity (Task 188); nothing in this package knows a tenant or a user.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/contracts/src/locator.ts` (`locatorText`: where a passage sits, in words; reused by Task 197), `packages/contracts/src/locator.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/contracts/src/index.ts` (`export * from "./locator"`)
- Create: `packages/experiences/src/reshapers.ts`, `packages/experiences/src/invoker.ts`, `packages/experiences/src/views-api.ts`, `packages/experiences/src/format.ts`, `packages/experiences/src/reshapers.test.ts`, `packages/experiences/src/hydrate.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/experiences/src/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces: `RawDocuments`, `RawContent`, `UiReaders` (catalogue); `formatBytes(n)` ("0 B", "512 B", "1.5 KB", "3.2 MB"); `reshapers` (a `defineReshapers` registry with the names the specs use: `library-total`, `library-ready`, `library-health`, `library-table`, `detail-status`, `detail-summary`, `detail-words`, `detail-pages`, `detail-minutes`, `detail-facts`, `detail-content`, `sessions-table`, `host-published`); `createInvoker(readers)`; `Views` with `hydrate(name, requests, readers, principalId)`; `createViews(o)`.
- The shapes (each is validated by loom-ui against its kind's contract before it leaves the server):
  - `library-total`: `Kpi {value: items.length, label: truncated ? "Newest 200 shown" : "in your library"}`; `library-ready`: `Kpi {value: ready count}`; `library-health`: `StatusCard`: any failed -> `critical`, headline `"<n> document(s) failed"`, detail `"Open the document to retry it."`; else any `uploaded|parsing|embedding` -> `warning`, headline `"<n> document(s) being processed"`, detail `"They appear as Ready when they are done."`; else `ok`, `"Everything is ready"`.
  - `library-table`: columns `["Name", "Type", "Size", "Status", "Added"]`; row `[title, typeOf(mime), formatBytes(size), STATUS_TEXT[status], createdAt.slice(0, 10)]`; `rowKeys` = ids; `rowActions` per row: `open` (primary, "Open"), `ask` ("Ask", only when `ready`), `retry` ("Retry", only when `failed`), `delete` ("Delete", destructive).
  - `detail-status`: `StatusCard` level `ok` for `ready`, `critical` for `failed` (detail = `error` text, a fixed server sentence), `warning` otherwise; headline `STATUS_TEXT[status]`.
  - `detail-summary` (A2UI data): `{ tldr, keyPoints: [{text}], meta: "Topics: a, b. Entities: c, d.", questions: [{label, index}] }`; with no summary yet: `tldr: "The summary appears when the document is ready."`, empty lists.
  - `detail-words|pages|minutes`: `Kpi` (`unit` "words", "pages", "min"; pages is `0` with `label: "not applicable"` when `pageCount` is null; all `0` with label `"not ready"` without stats).
  - `detail-facts`: `Table` `["Fact", "Value"]`: Language, then each date as "Date", each number as "Number", then each outline heading as "Outline".
  - `detail-content` (A2UI data): `{ note, passages: [{head, text}], more: [{label: "Show 20 more passages"}] | [] }`; `head` = `"<locator> - <headingPath joined with ' > '>"` (`Page 3`, `Section 2`, `Characters 120-480`, `Paragraphs 4-6`, `Page 2, region`), with `"Cited passage - "` in front of the cited one, which is moved first; `note` is `"Showing the first N passages."` when `more` else `"All N passages."`, `"This document has no extracted text."` when empty.
  - `sessions-table`: columns `["Chat", "Updated"]`; row `[title ?? "New chat", (lastMessageAt ?? createdAt).slice(0, 10)]`; `rowKeys` = ids; actions `open` (primary), `rename`, `delete` (destructive).
  - `host-published`: throws (it is never served; the page publishes the value).

```ts
import { checkKindValue } from "@loom-ui/core";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { ChatSessionDto, DocumentDto, DocumentPassage } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { formatBytes } from "./format";
import { shape } from "./reshapers";

const id = (n: number) => `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`;
const doc = (n: number, over: Partial<DocumentDto> = {}): DocumentDto => ({
  id: id(n), title: `Title ${n}`, filename: `f${n}.pdf`, mime: "application/pdf", sizeBytes: 1536, status: "ready", error: null, stats: null, summary: null,
  createdAt: "2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z", ...over,
});
const stats = { wordCount: 1200, pageCount: 4, readingTimeMinutes: 6, language: "en", outline: ["Terms"], dates: ["2026-01-01"], numbers: ["30"] };
const summary = { tldr: "Due in 30 days.", keyPoints: ["Net 30"], entities: ["Acme"], topics: ["Payment"], suggestedQuestions: ["When is it due?"] };
const raw = (items: DocumentDto[], truncated = false) => ({ items, truncated });

const valid = (kind: string, value: unknown) => expect(checkKindValue(kind, value).ok, `${kind}: ${JSON.stringify(value).slice(0, 120)}`).toBe(true);

describe("formatBytes", () => {
  it.each([[0, "0 B"], [512, "512 B"], [1536, "1.5 KB"], [3.2 * 1024 * 1024, "3.2 MB"]])("%s is %s", (n, s) => expect(formatBytes(n)).toBe(s));
});

describe("the library shapes", () => {
  const items = [doc(1), doc(2, { status: "failed", error: "Could not read this file." }), doc(3, { status: "parsing" })];
  it("count the library and the ready documents", () => {
    expect(shape("library-total", raw(items))).toEqual({ value: 3, label: "in your library" });
    expect(shape("library-total", raw(items, true))).toMatchObject({ label: "Newest 200 shown" });
    expect(shape("library-ready", raw(items))).toEqual({ value: 1 });
    valid("Kpi", shape("library-total", raw(items)));
  });
  it("say how the ingest is going: failed beats processing beats ok", () => {
    expect(shape("library-health", raw(items))).toMatchObject({ level: "critical", headline: "1 document failed" });
    expect(shape("library-health", raw([doc(1), doc(3), doc(4, { status: "embedding" })]))).toMatchObject({ level: "warning", headline: "2 documents being processed" });
    expect(shape("library-health", raw([doc(1)]))).toMatchObject({ level: "ok", headline: "Everything is ready" });
    valid("StatusCard", shape("library-health", raw(items)));
  });
  it("draw the table with keys and only the actions that fit each row", () => {
    const t = shape("library-table", raw(items)) as { columns: string[]; rows: unknown[][]; rowKeys: string[]; rowActions: { id: string }[][] };
    expect(t.columns).toEqual(["Name", "Type", "Size", "Status", "Added"]);
    expect(t.rows[0]).toEqual(["Title 1", "PDF", "1.5 KB", "Ready", "2026-10-01"]);
    expect(t.rows[1]?.[3]).toBe("Failed");
    expect(t.rowKeys).toEqual([id(1), id(2), id(3)]);
    expect(t.rowActions.map((a) => a.map((x) => x.id))).toEqual([["open", "ask", "delete"], ["open", "retry", "delete"], ["open", "delete"]]);
    valid("Table", t);
  });
});

describe("the detail shapes", () => {
  const ready = doc(1, { stats, summary });
  it("status: the level follows the document, a failure carries the fixed sentence", () => {
    expect(shape("detail-status", ready)).toEqual({ level: "ok", headline: "Ready" });
    expect(shape("detail-status", doc(2, { status: "failed", error: "Could not read this file." }))).toEqual({ level: "critical", headline: "Failed", detail: "Could not read this file." });
    expect(shape("detail-status", doc(3, { status: "embedding" }))).toMatchObject({ level: "warning", headline: "Indexing" });
  });
  it("summary: text, points, meta and questions with their index", () => {
    expect(shape("detail-summary", ready)).toEqual({ tldr: "Due in 30 days.", keyPoints: [{ text: "Net 30" }], meta: "Topics: Payment. Entities: Acme.", questions: [{ label: "When is it due?", index: 0 }] });
    expect(shape("detail-summary", doc(2))).toMatchObject({ tldr: "The summary appears when the document is ready.", keyPoints: [], questions: [] });
  });
  it("statistics as Kpis, and facts as a table", () => {
    expect(shape("detail-words", ready)).toEqual({ value: 1200, unit: "words" });
    expect(shape("detail-pages", ready)).toEqual({ value: 4, unit: "pages" });
    expect(shape("detail-pages", doc(4, { stats: { ...stats, pageCount: null } }))).toEqual({ value: 0, unit: "pages", label: "not applicable" });
    expect(shape("detail-minutes", doc(5))).toEqual({ value: 0, unit: "min", label: "not ready" });
    expect(shape("detail-facts", ready)).toEqual({ columns: ["Fact", "Value"], rows: [["Language", "en"], ["Date", "2026-01-01"], ["Number", "30"], ["Outline", "Terms"]] });
    for (const h of ["detail-words", "detail-pages", "detail-minutes"]) valid("Kpi", shape(h, ready));
    valid("Table", shape("detail-facts", ready));
  });
});

describe("the content shape", () => {
  const p = (n: number, over: Partial<DocumentPassage> = {}): DocumentPassage => ({ passageId: id(100 + n), ordinal: n, locator: { kind: "page", page: n + 1 }, headingPath: ["Terms", `Clause ${n}`], text: `passage ${n}`, ...over });
  it("lists passages with where they sit, and offers more only when there is more", () => {
    const d = shape("detail-content", { content: { items: [p(0), p(1)], more: true }, cited: undefined }) as { note: string; passages: { head: string; text: string }[]; more: { label: string }[] };
    expect(d.passages).toEqual([{ head: "Page 1 - Terms > Clause 0", text: "passage 0" }, { head: "Page 2 - Terms > Clause 1", text: "passage 1" }]);
    expect(d.note).toBe("Showing the first 2 passages.");
    expect(d.more).toEqual([{ label: "Show 20 more passages" }]);
    expect(shape("detail-content", { content: { items: [p(0)], more: false }, cited: undefined })).toMatchObject({ note: "All 1 passages.", more: [] });
    expect(shape("detail-content", { content: { items: [], more: false }, cited: undefined })).toMatchObject({ note: "This document has no extracted text.", passages: [] });
  });
  it("moves the cited passage first and says so", () => {
    const d = shape("detail-content", { content: { items: [p(0), p(1), p(2)], more: false }, cited: id(102) }) as { passages: { head: string }[] };
    expect(d.passages[0]?.head).toBe("Cited passage - Page 3 - Terms > Clause 2");
    expect(d.passages).toHaveLength(3);
  });
  it.each([
    [{ kind: "section", index: 2 }, "Section 3"], [{ kind: "chars", start: 120, end: 480 }, "Characters 120-480"],
    [{ kind: "paragraphs", from: 4, to: 6 }, "Paragraphs 4-6"], [{ kind: "region", page: 2, x: 0, y: 0, w: 1, h: 1 }, "Page 2, region"],
  ])("names the locator %j as %s", (locator, name) => {
    const d = shape("detail-content", { content: { items: [p(0, { locator: locator as DocumentPassage["locator"], headingPath: [] })], more: false }, cited: undefined }) as { passages: { head: string }[] };
    expect(d.passages[0]?.head).toBe(name);
  });
});

describe("the sessions shape", () => {
  const chat = (n: number, title: string | null): ChatSessionDto => ({ id: id(n), title, scope: "all", createdAt: "2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z", lastMessageAt: n === 1 ? "2026-10-05T10:00:00.000Z" : null, messageCount: 1 });
  it("titles, dates, keys and actions", () => {
    const t = shape("sessions-table", [chat(1, "Invoices"), chat(2, null)]) as { rows: unknown[][]; rowKeys: string[]; rowActions: { id: string }[][] };
    expect(t.rows).toEqual([["Invoices", "2026-10-05"], ["New chat", "2026-10-01"]]);
    expect(t.rowKeys).toEqual([id(1), id(2)]);
    expect(t.rowActions[0]?.map((a) => a.id)).toEqual(["open", "rename", "delete"]);
    valid("Table", t);
  });
  it("a published handle is never shaped from data", () => expect(() => shape("host-published", {})).toThrow(/published/));
});
```

`hydrate.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { createViews } from "./views-api";
import type { UiReaders } from "./invoker";

const id = (n: number) => `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`;
const doc = (n: number): DocumentDto => ({ id: id(n), title: "sentinel-title-0123456789", filename: "f.pdf", mime: "application/pdf", sizeBytes: 10, status: "ready", error: null, stats: null, summary: null, createdAt: "2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z" });
const readers = (over: Partial<UiReaders> = {}): UiReaders => ({
  documents: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [doc(1)], truncated: false })),
  document: vi.fn(async (a) => (a.id === id(1) ? doc(1) : null)),
  content: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [], more: false })),
  chats: vi.fn(async () => []),
  ...over,
});
const views = (onFinding = vi.fn()) => createViews({ placeholderKey: "k".repeat(32), revisionKey: "r".repeat(32), onFinding });
const req = (name: "library" | "detail" | "sessions", handle: string, params: Record<string, string | number> = {}) => {
  const p = views().payload(name, "web");
  return { experienceId: p.experienceId, versionHash: p.versionHash, handle, params };
};

describe("hydrate", () => {
  it("serves a handle by calling a reader, shaping the answer, and checking it against the widget's kind", async () => {
    const r = readers();
    const [out] = await views().hydrate("library", [req("library", "doc-total")], r, "user-1");
    expect(out).toEqual({ state: "ready", value: { value: 1, label: "in your library" } });
    expect(r.documents).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ limit: 200, status: "all" });
  });

  it("handles that share a step share one read", async () => {
    const r = readers();
    const results = await views().hydrate("library", ["doc-total", "doc-ready", "ingest"].map((h) => req("library", h)), r, "user-1");
    expect(results.map((x) => x.state)).toEqual(["ready", "ready", "ready"]);
    expect(r.documents).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("a parameter overrides only what the step declares: status for the table, not for the counts", async () => {
    const r = readers();
    await views().hydrate("library", [req("library", "documents", { status: "failed" })], r, "u");
    expect(r.documents).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ limit: 200, status: "failed" });
    const r2 = readers();
    await views().hydrate("library", [req("library", "doc-total", { status: "failed", limit: 5 })], r2, "u");
    expect(r2.documents).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ limit: 200, status: "all" });
  });

  it("validates a parameter again, on the server: a bad status, id, upto or passage never reaches a reader", async () => {
    const r = readers();
    const bad = await views().hydrate(
      "detail",
      [req("detail", "doc-status", { id: "not-a-uuid" }), req("detail", "doc-content", { id: id(1), upto: 9999 }), req("detail", "doc-content", { id: id(1), passage: "x" })],
      r, "u",
    );
    expect(bad.map((x) => x.state)).toEqual(["error", "error", "error"]);
    expect(r.document).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(r.content).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    const status = await views().hydrate("library", [req("library", "documents", { status: "; drop table" })], readers(), "u");
    expect(status[0]?.state).toBe("error");
  });

  it("a document that is missing and a document that is not yours answer the same way, with a public finding that names no value", async () => {
    const r = readers({ document: vi.fn(async () => null) });
    const [a, b] = await views().hydrate("detail", [req("detail", "doc-status", { id: id(1) }), req("detail", "doc-status", { id: id(9) })], r, "u");
    expect(a).toEqual(b);
    expect(a?.state).toBe("error");
    expect(JSON.stringify(a)).not.toMatch(/sentinel|00000000-0000/);
  });

  it("a request for another version is refused as a version mismatch, and nothing is read", async () => {
    const r = readers();
    const [out] = await views().hydrate("library", [{ ...req("library", "doc-total"), versionHash: "sha256:old" }], r, "u");
    expect(out).toMatchObject({ state: "error", finding: { code: "version-mismatch" } });
    expect(r.documents).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("an unbound handle is unknown, and a published handle is never served", async () => {
    const [unknown] = await views().hydrate("library", [req("library", "nope")], readers(), "u");
    expect(unknown).toMatchObject({ state: "error" });
    const chat = views().payload("chat", "web");
    const [t] = await views().hydrate("chat", [{ experienceId: chat.experienceId, versionHash: chat.versionHash, handle: "transcript", params: {} }], readers(), "u");
    expect(t?.state).toBe("error");
  });

  it("logs only finding codes (never a message), and a reader that throws content-bearing text is not echoed", async () => {
    const onFinding = vi.fn();
    const r = readers({ documents: vi.fn(async () => { throw new Error("sentinel-title-0123456789 exploded"); }) });
    const [out] = await views(onFinding).hydrate("library", [req("library", "doc-total")], r, "u");
    expect(out?.state).toBe("error");
    expect(JSON.stringify(out)).not.toContain("sentinel");
    expect(onFinding).toHaveBeenCalled();
    for (const call of onFinding.mock.calls) {
      expect(call[0]).toBe("doc-total");
      expect(call[1]).toMatch(/^[a-z][a-z0-9-]{0,63}$/);
    }
  });

  it("refuses a batch over 32 whole", async () => {
    const many = Array.from({ length: 33 }, () => req("library", "doc-total"));
    const out = await views().hydrate("library", many, readers(), "u");
    expect(out.every((x) => x.state === "error")).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

`packages/contracts/src/locator.ts`:

```ts
/** Where a passage sits, in words. Accepts a stored locator or a citation's (a citation's keeps extra fields). */
export function locatorText(l: { kind: string; [k: string]: unknown }): string {
  switch (l.kind) {
    case "page": return typeof l.page === "number" ? `Page ${l.page}` : "Somewhere in the document";
    case "section": return typeof l.index === "number" ? `Section ${l.index + 1}` : "Somewhere in the document";
    case "chars": return `Characters ${String(l.start)}-${String(l.end)}`;
    case "paragraphs": return `Paragraphs ${String(l.from)}-${String(l.to)}`;
    case "region": return typeof l.page === "number" ? `Page ${l.page}, region` : "Somewhere in the document";
    default: return "Somewhere in the document";
  }
}
```

`format.ts`:

```ts
const UNITS = ["B", "KB", "MB", "GB"];
/** "0 B", "512 B", "1.5 KB": one decimal from 1 KB. */
export function formatBytes(n: number): string {
  let v = n;
  let u = 0;
  while (v >= 1024 && u < UNITS.length - 1) {
    v /= 1024;
    u++;
  }
  return u === 0 ? `${Math.round(v)} B` : `${v.toFixed(1)} ${UNITS[u]}`;
}
```

`reshapers.ts` (the pure shapes; `shape(name, raw)` is what the tests call, `reshapers` registers the same functions for hydration):

```ts
import type { ChatSessionDto, DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { defineReshapers } from "@loom-ui/hydration";
import { locatorText } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { formatBytes } from "./format";
import { CONTENT_STEP } from "./providers";
import type { RawContent, RawDocuments } from "./invoker";

export const STATUS_TEXT: Record<DocumentDto["status"], string> = { uploaded: "Queued", parsing: "Parsing", embedding: "Indexing", ready: "Ready", failed: "Failed" };
const PROCESSING = new Set<DocumentDto["status"]>(["uploaded", "parsing", "embedding"]);
const plural = (n: number, one: string, many: string) => `${n} ${n === 1 ? one : many}`;
const typeOf = (mime: string): string =>
  mime === "application/pdf" ? "PDF" : mime === "text/plain" ? "Text" : mime === "text/markdown" ? "Markdown" : mime.startsWith("image/") ? "Image" : mime.includes("wordprocessingml") || mime === "application/msword" ? "Word" : "File";

type Doc = DocumentDto;

const SHAPES: Record<string, (raw: never) => unknown> = {
  "library-total": (raw: RawDocuments) => ({ value: raw.items.length, label: raw.truncated ? "Newest 200 shown" : "in your library" }),
  "library-ready": (raw: RawDocuments) => ({ value: raw.items.filter((d) => d.status === "ready").length }),
  "library-health": (raw: RawDocuments) => {
    const failed = raw.items.filter((d) => d.status === "failed").length;
    const busy = raw.items.filter((d) => PROCESSING.has(d.status)).length;
    if (failed > 0) return { level: "critical", headline: `${plural(failed, "document", "documents")} failed`, detail: "Open the document to retry it." };
    if (busy > 0) return { level: "warning", headline: `${plural(busy, "document", "documents")} being processed`, detail: "They appear as Ready when they are done." };
    return { level: "ok", headline: "Everything is ready" };
  },
  "library-table": (raw: RawDocuments) => ({
    columns: ["Name", "Type", "Size", "Status", "Added"],
    rows: raw.items.map((d) => [d.title, typeOf(d.mime), formatBytes(d.sizeBytes), STATUS_TEXT[d.status], d.createdAt.slice(0, 10)]),
    rowKeys: raw.items.map((d) => d.id),
    rowActions: raw.items.map((d) => [
      { id: "open", label: "Open", primary: true },
      ...(d.status === "ready" ? [{ id: "ask", label: "Ask" }] : []),
      ...(d.status === "failed" ? [{ id: "retry", label: "Retry" }] : []),
      { id: "delete", label: "Delete", destructive: true },
    ]),
  }),
  "detail-status": (d: Doc) => ({
    level: d.status === "ready" ? "ok" : d.status === "failed" ? "critical" : "warning",
    headline: STATUS_TEXT[d.status],
    ...(d.status === "failed" && d.error ? { detail: d.error } : {}),
  }),
  "detail-summary": (d: Doc) =>
    d.summary === null
      ? { tldr: "The summary appears when the document is ready.", keyPoints: [], meta: "", questions: [] }
      : {
          tldr: d.summary.tldr,
          keyPoints: d.summary.keyPoints.map((text) => ({ text })),
          meta: `Topics: ${d.summary.topics.join(", ")}. Entities: ${d.summary.entities.join(", ")}.`,
          questions: d.summary.suggestedQuestions.map((label, index) => ({ label, index })),
        },
  "detail-words": (d: Doc) => (d.stats ? { value: d.stats.wordCount, unit: "words" } : { value: 0, unit: "words", label: "not ready" }),
  "detail-pages": (d: Doc) => (d.stats ? (d.stats.pageCount === null ? { value: 0, unit: "pages", label: "not applicable" } : { value: d.stats.pageCount, unit: "pages" }) : { value: 0, unit: "pages", label: "not ready" }),
  "detail-minutes": (d: Doc) => (d.stats ? { value: d.stats.readingTimeMinutes, unit: "min" } : { value: 0, unit: "min", label: "not ready" }),
  "detail-facts": (d: Doc) => ({
    columns: ["Fact", "Value"],
    rows: d.stats
      ? [...(d.stats.language ? [["Language", d.stats.language]] : []), ...d.stats.dates.map((x) => ["Date", x]), ...d.stats.numbers.map((x) => ["Number", x]), ...d.stats.outline.map((x) => ["Outline", x])]
      : [],
  }),
  "detail-content": (raw: { content: RawContent; cited: string | undefined }) => {
    const { items, more } = raw.content;
    const first = raw.cited === undefined ? undefined : items.find((p) => p.passageId === raw.cited);
    const ordered = first ? [first, ...items.filter((p) => p !== first)] : items;
    return {
      note: items.length === 0 ? "This document has no extracted text." : more ? `Showing the first ${items.length} passages.` : `All ${items.length} passages.`,
      passages: ordered.map((p) => ({
        head: `${p === first ? "Cited passage - " : ""}${[locatorText(p.locator), p.headingPath.join(" > ")].filter((x) => x !== "").join(" - ")}`,
        text: p.text,
      })),
      more: more ? [{ label: `Show ${CONTENT_STEP} more passages` }] : [],
    };
  },
  "sessions-table": (chats: ChatSessionDto[]) => ({
    columns: ["Chat", "Updated"],
    rows: chats.map((c) => [c.title ?? "New chat", (c.lastMessageAt ?? c.createdAt).slice(0, 10)]),
    rowKeys: chats.map((c) => c.id),
    rowActions: chats.map(() => [{ id: "open", label: "Open", primary: true }, { id: "rename", label: "Rename" }, { id: "delete", label: "Delete", destructive: true }]),
  }),
  "host-published": () => {
    throw new Error("a published handle is not served: the page publishes its value");
  },
};

/** One named shape, for tests and for `reshapers`. */
export const shape = (name: string, raw: unknown): unknown => (SHAPES[name] as (r: unknown) => unknown)(raw);
export const reshapers = defineReshapers(Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(SHAPES).map((n) => [n, (raw: unknown) => shape(n, raw)])));
```

  The `detail-content` shape receives `{ content, cited }`: the invoker for `document.content` returns that pair (below).

`invoker.ts`:

```ts
import type { ChatSessionDto, DocumentDto, DocumentPassage } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { Uuid } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { TransientInvoker } from "@loom-ui/hydration";
import { TypedFailure } from "@loom/core";
import { z } from "zod";
import { CONTENT_CAP, LIST_CAP } from "./providers";

export interface RawDocuments { items: DocumentDto[]; truncated: boolean }
export interface RawContent { items: DocumentPassage[]; more: boolean }

/** What the server must supply, already bound to the caller's identity. Every method is owner-scoped and tenant-scoped by its implementation. */
export interface UiReaders {
  documents(a: { limit: number; status: "all" | "ready" | "processing" | "failed" }): Promise<RawDocuments>;
  document(a: { id: string }): Promise<DocumentDto | null>;
  content(a: { id: string; upto: number; passage?: string }): Promise<RawContent | null>;
  chats(a: { q: string }): Promise<ChatSessionDto[]>;
}

/** The tool id the data provider calls through the transient invoker. */
export const INVOKE_ID = "docmind.read";

const Id = Uuid.transform((s) => s.toLowerCase());
const ARGS = {
  "documents.list": z.object({ limit: z.number().int().min(1).max(LIST_CAP), status: z.enum(["all", "ready", "processing", "failed"]).default("all") }).strict(),
  "document.get": z.object({ id: Id }).strict(),
  "document.content": z.object({ id: Id, upto: z.number().int().min(1).max(CONTENT_CAP), passage: z.union([Id, z.literal("")]).default("") }).strict(),
  "chats.list": z.object({ q: z.string().max(100) }).strict(),
} as const;

const fail = (channel: string): never => {
  throw new TypedFailure(channel, {});
};

/** An invoker over one request's readers. Arguments are parsed again here: a parameter is untrusted input, whatever the controller let through. */
export function createInvoker(readers: UiReaders): TransientInvoker {
  return {
    async invokeTransient<TIn, TOut>(toolId: string, input: TIn): Promise<TOut> {
      if (toolId !== INVOKE_ID) return fail("unknownTool");
      const { target, args } = input as unknown as { target: string; args: Record<string, unknown> };
      switch (target) {
        case "documents.list": {
          const a = ARGS["documents.list"].safeParse(args);
          return (a.success ? readers.documents(a.data) : fail("invalidArgs")) as Promise<TOut>;
        }
        case "document.get": {
          const a = ARGS["document.get"].safeParse(args);
          if (!a.success) return fail("invalidArgs");
          return ((await readers.document({ id: a.data.id })) ?? fail("notFound")) as TOut;
        }
        case "document.content": {
          const a = ARGS["document.content"].safeParse(args);
          if (!a.success) return fail("invalidArgs");
          const content = (await readers.content({ id: a.data.id, upto: a.data.upto, ...(a.data.passage !== "" && { passage: a.data.passage }) })) ?? fail("notFound");
          return { content, cited: a.data.passage === "" ? undefined : a.data.passage } as TOut;
        }
        case "chats.list": {
          const a = ARGS["chats.list"].safeParse(args);
          return (a.success ? readers.chats({ q: a.data.q }) : fail("invalidArgs")) as Promise<TOut>;
        }
        default:
          return fail("unknownTool");
      }
    },
  };
}
```

  A reader that returns `null` for a document the caller does not own is the same `notFound` as a missing one.

`views-api.ts`:

```ts
import type { SurfaceName, ViewName } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { type HandleLoadRequest, type HandleLoadResult, defineDataProviders, serveHandles } from "@loom-ui/hydration";
import { DOCMIND_CATALOG } from "./catalog";
import { INVOKE_ID, type UiReaders, createInvoker } from "./invoker";
import { type ViewPayload, type ViewsOptions, createViewPayloads } from "./payload";
import { KIND, PINS } from "./providers";
import { reshapers } from "./reshapers";
import { EXPERIENCES } from "./views";
import { TypedFailure } from "@loom/core";

const MAX_BATCH = 32;

const providers = defineDataProviders([
  {
    kind: KIND,
    async invoke(call, invoker, opts) {
      if (call.expectedPin !== (PINS as Record<string, string>)[call.target]) throw new TypedFailure("schemaDrift", { observedHash: (PINS as Record<string, string>)[call.target] ?? "" });
      return invoker.invokeTransient(INVOKE_ID, { target: call.target, args: call.args }, opts);
    },
  },
]);

export interface Views {
  names: readonly ViewName[];
  surfaces: readonly SurfaceName[];
  payload(name: ViewName, surface: SurfaceName): ViewPayload;
  /** Answers handle loads for one view with the caller's readers. Surface-independent: values do not depend on how a view is drawn. */
  hydrate(name: ViewName, requests: readonly HandleLoadRequest[], readers: UiReaders, principalId: string): Promise<HandleLoadResult[]>;
}

export function createViews(o: ViewsOptions): Views {
  const base = createViewPayloads(o);
  return {
    names: base.names,
    surfaces: base.surfaces,
    payload: base.payload,
    async hydrate(name, requests, readers, principalId) {
      const experience = EXPERIENCES[name];
      return [
        ...(await serveHandles(experience, requests, {
          invoker: createInvoker(readers),
          providers,
          reshapers,
          contentRefArgs: "none",
          outputKinds: { catalog: DOCMIND_CATALOG, surface: "web" },
          principalId,
          now: Date.now(),
          maxBatch: MAX_BATCH,
          onFinding: (handle, finding) => o.onFinding?.(handle, finding.code),
        })),
      ];
    },
  };
}
```

  `outputKinds.surface: "web"`: the contract check needs one surface's kinds; the catalogue declares the same kind on every surface, so the check is surface-independent (a test in 186e proves a terminal compile accepts the same values). `index.ts`: export `./invoker`, `./reshapers`, `./format`, `./views-api`. Keep `createViewPayloads` exported for the tests.

(c) If `maxBatch` is not an option name, the batch-too-large refusal is `DEFAULT_MAX_BATCH` (256): then assert the check in `hydrate` itself (`if (requests.length > MAX_BATCH) return requests.map(() => ({ state: "error", finding: ... }))`) with a fixed finding `batch-too-large`. ### Task 186d: the surface-neutral action vocabulary: `Intent` and `intentOf`

**Spec:** Decisions D6, D14. Finding U18. A surface never decides what an action means; this vocabulary does, once. It needs no loom-ui (only `Uuid` and `ViewName`), so it lives in `@docmind/contracts`, which every surface and the server already import.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/contracts/src/intents.ts`, `packages/contracts/src/intents.test.ts`
- Modify: `packages/contracts/src/index.ts` (`export * from "./intents"`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: loom-ui action shapes (`TABLE_ROW_ACTION` `{action, rowKey}`; `A2UI_ACTION` `{action, context}`; `SELECT` `{value, key}`; `SUBMIT_TEXT` `{text, key}`; `CHAT_REF_OPEN` `{entryId, refId, n}`; `LOAD_OLDER`).
- Produces: `Intent`, `Held`, `intentOf(view, action, held?)` (catalogue). `held.rowKeys` is the list of row keys the surface was handed by the last hydration of that view (the surface remembers `value.rowKeys` of the table it drew); `held.list` is the list a surface published itself (the sources of a reply, the suggested questions) for lookups by index. An action that is not on the allow-list of its view, whose ids are not UUIDs (or entry ids), whose row key is not in `held.rowKeys`, or whose index is out of range, returns `undefined`.
- Allow-list: `library`: `TABLE_ROW_ACTION` `open|ask|retry|delete` (-> `document.open|ask|retry|delete`), `SELECT` (-> `documents.filter`, value `all|ready|processing|failed`); `detail`: `A2UI_ACTION` `ask-question` (index into `held.list` of question strings -> `document.ask-question`), `show-more` (-> `content.more`); `sessions`: `TABLE_ROW_ACTION` `open|rename|delete` (-> `chat.open|rename|delete`), `SUBMIT_TEXT` (-> `chats.search`, trimmed, at most 100); `chat`: `SUBMIT_TEXT` (-> `chat.send`, trimmed, 1 to 4000, with the widget's key), `SELECT` (-> `chat.scope` `all|started`), `CHAT_REF_OPEN` (-> `chat.cite`), `LOAD_OLDER` (-> `chat.older`); `sources`: `A2UI_ACTION` `open-source` (index into `held.list` of `{passageId, documentId}` -> `source.open`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** `intents.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { intentOf } from "./intents";

const A = "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10";
const B = "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11";
const row = (action: string, rowKey: string) => ({ type: "TABLE_ROW_ACTION", payload: { action, rowKey } });
const held = { rowKeys: [A, B] };

describe("intentOf: the library", () => {
  it.each(["open", "ask", "retry", "delete"])("%s a row the surface holds", (a) => expect(intentOf("library", row(a, A), held)).toEqual({ type: `document.${a}`, id: A }));
  it("refuses a row key the surface was not given, an id that is not a uuid, and an action name that is not on the list", () => {
    expect(intentOf("library", row("open", "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a99"), held)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(intentOf("library", row("open", "nope"), { rowKeys: ["nope"] })).toBeUndefined();
    expect(intentOf("library", row("rename", A), held)).toBeUndefined();
    expect(intentOf("library", row("open", A))).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it("turns a status choice into a filter, and refuses any other value", () => {
    expect(intentOf("library", { type: "SELECT", payload: { value: "failed", key: "k" } })).toEqual({ type: "documents.filter", status: "failed" });
    expect(intentOf("library", { type: "SELECT", payload: { value: "; drop", key: "k" } })).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe("intentOf: the document", () => {
  it("asks a suggested question by index into the list the surface holds, never by the text in the action", () => {
    expect(intentOf("detail", { type: "A2UI_ACTION", payload: { action: "ask-question", context: { label: "FORGED", index: 1 } } }, { list: ["a?", "b?"] })).toEqual({ type: "document.ask-question", question: "b?" });
    expect(intentOf("detail", { type: "A2UI_ACTION", payload: { action: "ask-question", context: { index: 5 } } }, { list: ["a?"] })).toBeUndefined();
    expect(intentOf("detail", { type: "A2UI_ACTION", payload: { action: "ask-question", context: "x" } }, { list: ["a?"] })).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it("shows more passages, and nothing else from the A2UI vocabulary", () => {
    expect(intentOf("detail", { type: "A2UI_ACTION", payload: { action: "show-more", context: {} } })).toEqual({ type: "content.more" });
    expect(intentOf("detail", { type: "A2UI_ACTION", payload: { action: "delete-everything", context: {} } })).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe("intentOf: chats", () => {
  it("opens, renames and deletes a chat the surface holds; the sessions view cannot delete a document", () => {
    expect(intentOf("sessions", row("rename", B), held)).toEqual({ type: "chat.rename", id: B });
    expect(intentOf("sessions", row("delete", A), held)).toEqual({ type: "chat.delete", id: A });
    expect(intentOf("sessions", row("retry", A), held)).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it("searches with trimmed text of at most 100 characters", () => {
    expect(intentOf("sessions", { type: "SUBMIT_TEXT", payload: { text: "  invoices ", key: "k".repeat(20) } })).toEqual({ type: "chats.search", q: "invoices" });
    expect(intentOf("sessions", { type: "SUBMIT_TEXT", payload: { text: "x".repeat(101), key: "k" } })).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it("sends a trimmed message with the widget's key, and refuses an empty or over-long one", () => {
    expect(intentOf("chat", { type: "SUBMIT_TEXT", payload: { text: " hello ", key: "key-0123456789abcdef" } })).toEqual({ type: "chat.send", text: "hello", key: "key-0123456789abcdef" });
    expect(intentOf("chat", { type: "SUBMIT_TEXT", payload: { text: "   ", key: "key-0123456789abcdef" } })).toBeUndefined();
    expect(intentOf("chat", { type: "SUBMIT_TEXT", payload: { text: "x".repeat(4001), key: "key-0123456789abcdef" } })).toBeUndefined();
  });
  it("switches the scope, cites a source, and loads older turns", () => {
    expect(intentOf("chat", { type: "SELECT", payload: { value: "started", key: "k" } })).toEqual({ type: "chat.scope", scope: "started" });
    expect(intentOf("chat", { type: "SELECT", payload: { value: "everything", key: "k" } })).toBeUndefined();
    expect(intentOf("chat", { type: "CHAT_REF_OPEN", payload: { entryId: "t1.assistant", refId: A, n: 1 } })).toEqual({ type: "chat.cite", entryId: "t1.assistant", refId: A });
    expect(intentOf("chat", { type: "CHAT_REF_OPEN", payload: { entryId: "bad id", refId: A, n: 1 } })).toBeUndefined();
    expect(intentOf("chat", { type: "LOAD_OLDER", payload: { cursor: "1.2" } })).toEqual({ type: "chat.older" });
  });
});

describe("intentOf: sources", () => {
  const list = [{ passageId: A, documentId: B }];
  it("opens a source by index into the sources the surface published", () => {
    expect(intentOf("sources", { type: "A2UI_ACTION", payload: { action: "open-source", context: { index: 0, passageId: "FORGED" } } }, { list })).toEqual({ type: "source.open", passageId: A, documentId: B });
    expect(intentOf("sources", { type: "A2UI_ACTION", payload: { action: "open-source", context: { index: 1 } } }, { list })).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe("intentOf: anything else", () => {
  it("an action type no view lists, a missing payload and a view with no actions are refused", () => {
    expect(intentOf("library", { type: "SANDBOX_ACTION", payload: {} })).toBeUndefined();
    expect(intentOf("library", { type: "TABLE_ROW_ACTION" })).toBeUndefined();
    expect(intentOf("library-empty", { type: "SELECT", payload: { value: "all", key: "k" } })).toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement** `intents.ts`:

```ts
import { Uuid } from "./common";
import type { ViewName } from "./ui";

export type Intent =
  | { type: "document.open"; id: string }
  | { type: "document.ask"; id: string }
  | { type: "document.retry"; id: string }
  | { type: "document.delete"; id: string }
  | { type: "documents.filter"; status: "all" | "ready" | "processing" | "failed" }
  | { type: "document.ask-question"; question: string }
  | { type: "content.more" }
  | { type: "source.open"; passageId: string; documentId: string }
  | { type: "chat.open"; id: string }
  | { type: "chat.rename"; id: string }
  | { type: "chat.delete"; id: string }
  | { type: "chats.search"; q: string }
  | { type: "chat.send"; text: string; key: string }
  | { type: "chat.scope"; scope: "all" | "started" }
  | { type: "chat.cite"; entryId: string; refId: string }
  | { type: "chat.older" };

/** What the surface itself holds, so an action is looked up and never believed: the row keys of the table it drew, the list it published. */
export interface Held {
  rowKeys?: readonly string[];
  list?: readonly unknown[];
}

const isRecord = (v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> => typeof v === "object" && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v);
const ENTRY_ID = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9._:-]{0,127}$/;
const STATUSES = ["all", "ready", "processing", "failed"] as const;

const heldId = (key: unknown, held: Held): string | undefined => {
  if (typeof key !== "string" || !Uuid.safeParse(key).success) return undefined;
  const id = key.toLowerCase();
  return held.rowKeys?.some((k) => k.toLowerCase() === id) ? id : undefined;
};
const index = (context: unknown, list: readonly unknown[] | undefined): number | undefined => {
  const i = isRecord(context) ? context.index : undefined;
  return typeof i === "number" && Number.isInteger(i) && i >= 0 && list !== undefined && i < list.length ? i : undefined;
};

/** The one place that says what a widget action means for DocMind, for every surface. Total; `undefined` means "not an action of this view". */
export function intentOf(view: ViewName, action: { type: string; payload?: unknown }, held: Held = {}): Intent | undefined {
  const p = isRecord(action.payload) ? action.payload : undefined;
  if (p === undefined) return undefined;
  switch (view) {
    case "library": {
      if (action.type === "TABLE_ROW_ACTION") {
        const id = heldId(p.rowKey, held);
        if (id === undefined) return undefined;
        return p.action === "open" || p.action === "ask" || p.action === "retry" || p.action === "delete" ? { type: `document.${p.action}`, id } : undefined;
      }
      if (action.type === "SELECT") return STATUSES.includes(p.value as never) ? { type: "documents.filter", status: p.value as (typeof STATUSES)[number] } : undefined;
      return undefined;
    }
    case "detail": {
      if (action.type !== "A2UI_ACTION") return undefined;
      if (p.action === "show-more") return { type: "content.more" };
      if (p.action === "ask-question") {
        const i = index(p.context, held.list);
        const q = i === undefined ? undefined : held.list?.[i];
        return typeof q === "string" ? { type: "document.ask-question", question: q } : undefined;
      }
      return undefined;
    }
    case "sessions": {
      if (action.type === "TABLE_ROW_ACTION") {
        const id = heldId(p.rowKey, held);
        if (id === undefined) return undefined;
        return p.action === "open" || p.action === "rename" || p.action === "delete" ? { type: `chat.${p.action}`, id } : undefined;
      }
      if (action.type === "SUBMIT_TEXT" && typeof p.text === "string") {
        const q = p.text.trim();
        return q.length <= 100 ? { type: "chats.search", q } : undefined;
      }
      return undefined;
    }
    case "chat": {
      if (action.type === "SUBMIT_TEXT" && typeof p.text === "string" && typeof p.key === "string") {
        const text = p.text.trim();
        return text.length >= 1 && text.length <= 4000 ? { type: "chat.send", text, key: p.key } : undefined;
      }
      if (action.type === "SELECT") return p.value === "all" || p.value === "started" ? { type: "chat.scope", scope: p.value } : undefined;
      if (action.type === "CHAT_REF_OPEN" && typeof p.entryId === "string" && ENTRY_ID.test(p.entryId) && typeof p.refId === "string" && ENTRY_ID.test(p.refId)) return { type: "chat.cite", entryId: p.entryId, refId: p.refId };
      if (action.type === "LOAD_OLDER") return { type: "chat.older" };
      return undefined;
    }
    case "sources": {
      if (action.type !== "A2UI_ACTION" || p.action !== "open-source") return undefined;
      const i = index(p.context, held.list);
      const s = i === undefined ? undefined : held.list?.[i];
      return isRecord(s) && typeof s.passageId === "string" && typeof s.documentId === "string" ? { type: "source.open", passageId: s.passageId, documentId: s.documentId } : undefined;
    }
    default:
      return undefined;
  }
}
```

- Produces: the proof that one set of experiences, one catalogue and one `hydrate` serve two surfaces; no production code changes.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test** `terminal.test.ts`:

```ts
import { renderExperience } from "@loom-ui/renderer-terminal";
import { HydrationStore, SplitHydrationController } from "@loom-ui/hydration";
import { InMemoryProjectionRuntime } from "@loom/projection-runtime";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import type { DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { UiReaders } from "./invoker";
import { createViews } from "./views-api";

const id = (n: number) => `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`;
const doc = (n: number, over: Partial<DocumentDto> = {}): DocumentDto => ({
  id: id(n), title: `Contract ${n}`, filename: `c${n}.pdf`, mime: "application/pdf", sizeBytes: 2048, status: "ready", error: null,
  stats: { wordCount: 900, pageCount: 3, readingTimeMinutes: 4, language: "en", outline: ["Terms"], dates: [], numbers: [] },
  summary: { tldr: "Payment is due in 30 days.", keyPoints: ["Net 30"], entities: ["Acme"], topics: ["Payment"], suggestedQuestions: ["When is it due?"] },
  createdAt: "2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z", ...over,
});
const readers: UiReaders = {
  documents: async () => ({ items: [doc(1), doc(2, { status: "failed", error: "Could not read this file." })], truncated: false }),
  document: async (a) => (a.id === id(1) ? doc(1) : null),
  content: async () => ({ items: [{ passageId: id(50), ordinal: 0, locator: { kind: "page", page: 1 }, headingPath: ["Terms"], text: "Payment is due in 30 days." }], more: false }),
  chats: async () => [{ id: id(7), title: "Invoices", scope: "all", createdAt: "2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z", lastMessageAt: null, messageCount: 0 }],
};
const views = createViews({ placeholderKey: "k".repeat(32), revisionKey: "r".repeat(32) });

/** A terminal surface in miniature: fetch the compiled view, hydrate it through the same server function, draw one frame. */
async function frame(view: "library" | "library-empty" | "detail" | "sessions", params: Record<string, string> = {}): Promise<string> {
  const p = views.payload(view, "terminal");
  const store = new HydrationStore(new InMemoryProjectionRuntime(), { experienceId: p.experienceId });
  const loader = {
    load: async (r: never, _s: AbortSignal) => (await views.hydrate(view, [r], readers, "user-1"))[0],
    loadMany: async (rs: readonly never[]) => views.hydrate(view, rs, readers, "user-1"),
  };
  const split = new SplitHydrationController({ loader: loader as never, store, batchWindowMs: 0 });
  split.setParams(params);
  split.load(p.inventory);
  await vi.waitFor(() => expect(Object.values(store.state().status).every((s) => s.state !== "pending")).toBe(true));
  return renderExperience(p.blueprint, store);
}

describe("the terminal surface", () => {
  it("draws the library: counts, health and the table, from the very data the web is given", async () => {
    const out = await frame("library");
    expect(out).toContain("Contract 1");
    expect(out).toContain("Failed");
    expect(out).toMatch(/2/); // the total
  });
  it("draws the empty state", async () => expect(await frame("library-empty")).toContain("No documents yet"));
  it("draws the document: status, summary text and facts, with the document the caller names", async () => {
    const out = await frame("detail", { id: id(1) });
    expect(out).toContain("Ready");
    expect(out).toContain("Payment is due in 30 days.");
  });
  it("draws the chat list", async () => expect(await frame("sessions")).toContain("Invoices"));
  it("a document that is not the caller's is an error row on the terminal too, with no content", async () => {
    const out = await frame("detail", { id: id(9) });
    expect(out).not.toContain("Contract");
  });
});
```

### Task 187: server: `GET /api/documents/:id/content`

**Spec:** §9 item 44 (document detail: Summary / Analysis / Content tabs), §7 (`dm-doc-detail`), §6.3 ("Open in document" needs the passage), Finding U9. Open question 5 (passages, not a persisted full text).

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/contracts/src/documents.ts` (`DocumentPassage`, `DocumentContentResponse`), `apps/server/app/documents/routes.ts` (the route)
- Create: `apps/server/app/documents/content.ts`
- Test: `packages/contracts/src/documents.test.ts`, `apps/server/app/documents/content.test.ts`, `apps/server/app/documents/content.pg.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (contract): `DocumentPassage {passageId: Uuid; ordinal: int >= 0; locator: Locator; headingPath: string[]; text: string}` and `DocumentContentResponse {items: DocumentPassage[]; nextCursor: Cursor | null}`.
- Produces (server): `CONTENT_PAGE = 20`; `encodeContentCursor(ordinal: number): string` and `decodeContentCursor(cursor: string): number | null` (an opaque base64url of `o:<ordinal>`, decoded strictly: a non-negative integer of at most nine digits, else `null`); `readContent(pool, fields, identity, documentId, after): Promise<DocumentContentResponse | null>` (`after` is the last ordinal already seen, -1 for the start; `null` when the document does not exist or is not the caller's); the route `GET /api/documents/:id/content?cursor`: 200 with the page, 404 `not_found` for a malformed, unknown or foreign id (the same answer), 400 `invalid_cursor`, 401 without a session.
- Not produced: a web client method. The UI reads passages through the `doc-content` handle of Task 186c, whose reader (Task 188) calls `readContent` and `decodeContentCursor` from this task.
- Chunks overlap by about 15%, so consecutive passages repeat a little text; the Content tab (Task 192) shows passages, not a stitched text.

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { DocumentContentResponse } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { FieldCrypto } from "../crypto/fields";
import { PG_URL } from "../db/testSupport";
import { type Chunk, chunkIdFor } from "../flows/ingest-document/chunker";
import { PgChunkStore } from "../flows/ingest-document/pgChunks";
import { ALICE, ALICE2, BOB, type DocumentHarness, seedDoc, startDocumentServer } from "./testHarness";
import { CONTENT_PAGE } from "./content";

describe.skipIf(!PG_URL)("document content (needs DOCMIND_TEST_PG_URL)", () => {
  let h: DocumentHarness;
  let fields: FieldCrypto;
  let mine: string, empty: string, theirs: string, bobs: string;
  const chunk = (n: number): Chunk => ({ ordinal: n, sectionOrdinal: Math.floor(n / 10), headingPath: ["Terms", `Clause ${n}`], text: `sentinel-clause-${n}-text-0123456789`, tokenCount: 8, locator: { kind: "page", page: n + 1 } });
  const get = (path: string, user = "alice") => fetch(`${h.base}${path}`, { headers: user ? { "x-test-user": user } : {} });

  beforeAll(async () => {
    h = await startDocumentServer();
    fields = new FieldCrypto(h.keys);
    const seed = async (who: typeof ALICE, fp: string, n: number) => {
      const id = await seedDoc(h.owner, h.app, fields, who, fp.repeat(64), 10);
      await new PgChunkStore(h.app, fields).replace({ tenantId: who.tenantId, userId: who.userId, documentId: id }, Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => chunk(i)));
      return id;
    };
    mine = await seed(ALICE, "a", 45);
    empty = await seed(ALICE, "b", 0);
    theirs = await seed(ALICE2, "c", 3);
    bobs = await seed(BOB, "d", 3);
  });
  afterAll(async () => { await h?.close(); });

  it("pages through every passage in reading order, 20 at a time, with no gaps or repeats", async () => {
    const seen: number[] = [];
    let cursor: string | null = null;
    let pages = 0;
    do {
      const res = await get(`/api/documents/${mine}/content${cursor ? `?cursor=${cursor}` : ""}`);
      expect(res.status).toBe(200);
      const page = DocumentContentResponse.parse(await res.json());
      expect(page.items.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(CONTENT_PAGE);
      seen.push(...page.items.map((p) => p.ordinal));
      for (const p of page.items) expect(p.passageId).toBe(chunkIdFor(mine, p.ordinal));
      cursor = page.nextCursor;
      pages++;
    } while (cursor);
    expect(pages).toBe(3);
    expect(seen).toEqual(Array.from({ length: 45 }, (_, i) => i));
  });

  it("returns the decrypted text, heading path and locator of a passage", async () => {
    const page = DocumentContentResponse.parse(await (await get(`/api/documents/${mine}/content`)).json());
    expect(page.items[3]).toMatchObject({ ordinal: 3, headingPath: ["Terms", "Clause 3"], text: "sentinel-clause-3-text-0123456789", locator: { kind: "page", page: 4 } });
  });

  it("keeps the text sealed at rest", async () => {
    const { rows } = await h.owner.query("SELECT text_enc::text AS raw FROM chunks WHERE document_id = $1 AND ordinal = 3", [mine]);
    expect(rows[0].raw).toContain("__loomEnc");
    expect(rows[0].raw).not.toContain("sentinel-clause-3");
  });

  it("answers an empty document with an empty page", async () => {
    expect(await (await get(`/api/documents/${empty}/content`)).json()).toEqual({ items: [], nextCursor: null });
  });

  it("answers 404 alike for an unknown id, a malformed id, a colleague's document and another tenant's", async () => {
    const answers = await Promise.all([randomUUID(), "not-a-uuid", theirs, bobs].map(async (id) => { const res = await get(`/api/documents/${id}/content`); return [res.status, await res.json()]; }));
    expect(new Set(answers.map((a) => JSON.stringify(a))).size).toBe(1);
    expect(answers[0]?.[0]).toBe(404);
  });

  it("refuses a bad cursor with 400 and no session with 401", async () => {
    for (const c of ["x", "!!!", Buffer.from("o:-1").toString("base64url")]) {
      const res = await get(`/api/documents/${mine}/content?cursor=${c}`);
      expect(res.status).toBe(400);
      expect(((await res.json()) as { error: { code: string } }).error.code).toBe("invalid_cursor");
    }
    expect((await get(`/api/documents/${mine}/content`, "")).status).toBe(401);
  });
});
```

`packages/contracts/src/documents.ts` (append):

```ts
/** One passage (chunk) of a document in reading order, for the Content tab. Passages overlap by about 15%. */
export const DocumentPassage = z.object({
  passageId: Uuid,
  ordinal: Count,
  locator: Locator,
  headingPath: z.array(z.string()),
  text: z.string(),
});
export type DocumentPassage = z.infer<typeof DocumentPassage>;

/** `GET /api/documents/:id/content?cursor`: 20 passages per page; `nextCursor` is null on the last page. */
export const DocumentContentResponse = z.object({
  items: z.array(DocumentPassage),
  nextCursor: Cursor.nullable(),
});
export type DocumentContentResponse = z.infer<typeof DocumentContentResponse>;
```

`apps/server/app/documents/content.ts`:

```ts
import type { Identity } from "@docmind/auth";
import { type DocumentContentResponse, Locator } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { Pool } from "pg";
import { z } from "zod";
import type { FieldCrypto } from "../crypto/fields";
import { withTenant } from "../db/tenant";
import { chunkBind } from "../flows/ingest-document/pgChunks";
import { getDocument } from "./repo";

/** Passages per page of `GET /api/documents/:id/content`. */
export const CONTENT_PAGE = 20;
const SEALED = z.object({ text: z.string(), headingPath: z.array(z.string()) });

export const encodeContentCursor = (ordinal: number): string => Buffer.from(`o:${ordinal}`).toString("base64url");

/** The ordinal a cursor names, or null: opaque to clients, validated like any input (no sign, no leading zero, nine digits at most). */
export function decodeContentCursor(cursor: string): number | null {
  const match = /^o:(0|[1-9][0-9]{0,8})$/.exec(Buffer.from(cursor, "base64url").toString("utf8"));
  return match ? Number(match[1]) : null;
}

interface Row {
  id: string;
  ordinal: number;
  locator: unknown;
  text_enc: unknown;
}

/**
 * The passages of one of the caller's documents after ordinal `after`, in reading order, with text opened (content: never
 * logged). `null` when the document does not exist or is not the caller's. Owner-scoped inside `withTenant`.
 */
export async function readContent(
  pool: Pool,
  fields: FieldCrypto,
  identity: Identity,
  documentId: string,
  after: number,
): Promise<DocumentContentResponse | null> {
  const rows = await withTenant(pool, identity.tenantId, async (tx) => {
    if (!(await getDocument(tx, identity.userId, documentId))) return null;
    const res = await tx.query<Row>(
      `SELECT c.id, c.ordinal, c.locator, c.text_enc
         FROM chunks c JOIN documents d ON d.id = c.document_id AND d.tenant_id = c.tenant_id
        WHERE d.id = $1 AND d.owner_id = $2 AND c.ordinal > $3
        ORDER BY c.ordinal LIMIT $4`,
      [documentId, identity.userId, after, CONTENT_PAGE + 1],
    );
    return res.rows;
  });
  if (rows === null) return null;
  const page = rows.slice(0, CONTENT_PAGE);
  const cipher = fields.forUser(identity.userId);
  const items = await Promise.all(
    page.map(async (r) => {
      const sealed = SEALED.safeParse(await cipher.open(chunkBind(r.id), r.text_enc));
      const locator = Locator.safeParse(r.locator);
      // A fixed message: a schema error could carry decrypted content.
      if (!sealed.success || !locator.success) throw new Error("the stored chunk has an invalid shape");
      return { passageId: r.id, ordinal: r.ordinal, locator: locator.data, headingPath: sealed.data.headingPath, text: sealed.data.text };
    }),
  );
  const last = page[page.length - 1];
  return { items, nextCursor: rows.length > CONTENT_PAGE && last ? encodeContentCursor(last.ordinal) : null };
}
```

`routes.ts` (inside the object returned by `documentRoutes`, after `GET /api/documents/:id`; import `DocumentContentResponse` with the other contract types and `decodeContentCursor`, `readContent` from `./content`):

```ts
    "GET /api/documents/:id/content": requireAuth(deps.authenticator, async (req, res, identity, ctx) => {
      const parsed = Uuid.safeParse(ctx.params.id);
      const raw = new URL(req.url ?? "/", "http://localhost").searchParams.get("cursor");
      const after = raw === null ? -1 : decodeContentCursor(raw);
      if (after === null) return sendError(res, 400, "invalid_cursor", "cursor is not valid");
      // A malformed id answers exactly like a missing or foreign one.
      const page = parsed.success ? await readContent(deps.pool, deps.fields, identity, parsed.data.toLowerCase(), after) : null;
      if (!page) return sendError(res, 404, "not_found", "document not found");
      json(res, 200, page satisfies DocumentContentResponse);
    }),
```

### Task 188: the server's UI plane: identity-bound readers, `GET /api/ui/views/:view`, `POST /api/ui/hydrate`

**Spec:** §7 (Stack), §8 (tenancy gates), Decisions D6, D10, D12, D14; Global Constraints (tenancy, never-log). Findings U13, U14.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/server/app/ui/readers.ts`, `apps/server/app/ui/routes.ts`, `apps/server/app/ui/routes.test.ts`, `apps/server/app/ui/ui.pg.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/server/package.json` (`@docmind/experiences`), `apps/server/app/compose.ts` (build the views once and mount `uiRoutes`), `apps/server/app/documents/testHarness.ts` (mount `uiRoutes` beside `documentRoutes`), `pnpm-lock.yaml`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `createViews`, `UiReaders`, `Views`, `LIST_CAP`, `CHAT_LIST_CAP`, `CONTENT_CAP` (`@docmind/experiences`); `readContent`, `decodeContentCursor` (Task 187); `listDocuments`, `getDocument`, `toDto`, `UPLOADED`; `listChatsPage`; `withTenant`; `requireAuth`, `readJson`.
- Produces: `UiReaderDeps { pool, fields, status }`; `readersFor(deps, identity): UiReaders` (every method closes over `identity`: tenant from the session, owner from the session); `UiRouteDeps { authenticator, views, readers }`; `uiRoutes(deps)`:
  - `GET /api/ui/views/:view?surface=web|terminal` (surface defaults to `web`): 200 `UiViewResponse` with `cache-control: private, no-store`; 404 `not_found` for an unknown view; 400 `invalid_request` for an unknown surface; 401 without a session. The payload is content-free and the same for every user, but it is not served to anonymous callers.
  - `POST /api/ui/hydrate`: body `UiHydrateRequest` (read through `readJson`, so the size cap and 400 apply); 200 `{results}` in request order, `cache-control: no-store`; 401 without a session. The origin guard in front of every route already refuses a cross-origin POST.
- Server wiring: `createViews({ placeholderKey: randomBytes(32).toString("hex"), revisionKey: randomBytes(32).toString("hex"), onFinding: (_handle, code) => console.error(`ui finding ${code}`) })`, built once in `compose.ts`. The keys are per process: a restart changes the handle revisions, which a connected client sees as one extra reload.
- The tenant and user come from `requireAuth`'s `identity` and from nothing else: no request field, no handle parameter and no inventory entry names one.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

`apps/server/app/ui/routes.test.ts` (no database: fake readers, the real router, origin guard and loom-ui):

```ts
import type { AddressInfo } from "node:net";
import type { Authenticator } from "@docmind/auth";
import { UiViewResponse } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { createViews } from "@docmind/experiences";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createHttpServer } from "../server";
import { uiRoutes } from "./routes";

const ID = { tenantId: "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11", userId: "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10" };
const authenticator: Authenticator = { authenticate: async (req) => (req.headers["x-test-user"] === "alice" ? ID : null) } as never;
const readerCalls: unknown[] = [];
let base = "";
let close = async () => {};

beforeAll(async () => {
  const views = createViews({ placeholderKey: "k".repeat(32), revisionKey: "r".repeat(32), onFinding: (_handle, code) => console.error(`ui finding ${code}`) });
  // the route builds readers from `deps.readers` and the identity; here the pool and fields are never touched, because the fake readers replace `readersFor`
  const server = createHttpServer(uiRoutes({ authenticator, views, readers: {} as never, readersFor: () => ({
    documents: async (a) => { readerCalls.push(["documents", a]); return { items: [], truncated: false }; },
    document: async () => null, content: async () => null, chats: async () => [],
  }) }));
  await new Promise<void>((r) => server.listen(0, "127.0.0.1", r));
  base = `http://127.0.0.1:${(server.address() as AddressInfo).port}`;
  close = () => new Promise<void>((r) => server.close(() => r()));
});
afterAll(() => close());

const get = (path: string, user = "alice") => fetch(`${base}${path}`, { headers: user ? { "x-test-user": user } : {} });
const post = (body: unknown, user = "alice", headers: Record<string, string> = {}) =>
  fetch(`${base}/api/ui/hydrate`, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", ...(user ? { "x-test-user": user } : {}), ...headers }, body: JSON.stringify(body) });

describe("GET /api/ui/views/:view", () => {
  it("serves the compiled view, content-free, with no-store", async () => {
    const res = await get("/api/ui/views/library");
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("private, no-store");
    const body = UiViewResponse.parse(await res.json());
    expect(body).toMatchObject({ view: "library", surface: "web" });
    expect(body.inventory.handles.map((h) => h.handle).sort()).toEqual(["doc-ready", "doc-total", "documents", "ingest"]);
  });
  it("serves the terminal compile of the same view when asked", async () => {
    const web = UiViewResponse.parse(await (await get("/api/ui/views/detail")).json());
    const terminal = UiViewResponse.parse(await (await get("/api/ui/views/detail?surface=terminal")).json());
    expect(terminal.surface).toBe("terminal");
    expect(terminal.versionHash).toBe(web.versionHash); // one experience, two compiles
  });
  it("answers 404 for an unknown view, 400 for an unknown surface, 401 without a session", async () => {
    expect((await get("/api/ui/views/nope")).status).toBe(404);
    expect((await get("/api/ui/views/library?surface=hologram")).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await get("/api/ui/views/library", "")).status).toBe(401);
  });
});

describe("POST /api/ui/hydrate", () => {
  const req = async (handle: string, params: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => {
    const v = UiViewResponse.parse(await (await get("/api/ui/views/library")).json());
    return { experienceId: v.experienceId, versionHash: v.versionHash, handle, params };
  };
  it("answers one result per request, in order, from the readers the identity built", async () => {
    const res = await post({ view: "library", requests: [await req("doc-total"), await req("documents", { status: "ready" })] });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
    const { results } = (await res.json()) as { results: { state: string }[] };
    expect(results.map((r) => r.state)).toEqual(["ready", "ready"]);
    expect(readerCalls).toContainEqual(["documents", { limit: 200, status: "ready" }]);
  });
  it("refuses a malformed body, an anonymous caller and a cross-origin post", async () => {
    expect((await post({ view: "library", requests: [] })).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await post({ view: "library", requests: [await req("doc-total")] }, "")).status).toBe(401);
    const cross = await post({ view: "library", requests: [await req("doc-total")] }, "alice", { "sec-fetch-site": "cross-site" });
    expect(cross.status).toBe(403);
    expect(((await cross.json()) as { error: { code: string } }).error.code).toBe("cross_origin_request");
  });
  it("logs a finding's code and nothing else when a load fails", async () => {
    const error = vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
    await post({ view: "detail", requests: [{ experienceId: "docmind.detail", versionHash: "sha256:stale", handle: "doc-status", params: { id: "sentinel-id-0123456789" } }] });
    for (const call of error.mock.calls) expect(JSON.stringify(call)).not.toMatch(/sentinel|doc-status/);
    error.mockRestore();
  });
});
```
