`apps/server/app/ui/ui.pg.test.ts` (needs `DOCMIND_TEST_PG_URL`; the harness gains the UI routes in Step 3):

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { UiViewResponse } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { FieldCrypto } from "../crypto/fields";
import { PG_URL } from "../db/testSupport";
import { type Chunk } from "../flows/ingest-document/chunker";
import { PgChunkStore } from "../flows/ingest-document/pgChunks";
import { ALICE, ALICE2, BOB, type DocumentHarness, seedDoc, startDocumentServer } from "../documents/testHarness";

describe.skipIf(!PG_URL)("the UI plane against Postgres (needs DOCMIND_TEST_PG_URL)", () => {
  let h: DocumentHarness;
  let fields: FieldCrypto;
  let mine: string, mine2: string, colleagues: string, bobs: string;
  const chunk = (n: number): Chunk => ({ ordinal: n, sectionOrdinal: 0, headingPath: ["Terms"], text: `sentinel-passage-${n}-0123456789`, tokenCount: 6, locator: { kind: "page", page: n + 1 } });
  const get = (path: string, user = "alice") => fetch(`${h.base}${path}`, { headers: user ? { "x-test-user": user } : {} });
  const hydrate = async (user: string, view: "library" | "detail" | "sessions", handle: string, params: Record<string, unknown> = {}) => {
    const v = UiViewResponse.parse(await (await get(`/api/ui/views/${view}`, user)).json());
    const res = await fetch(`${h.base}/api/ui/hydrate`, {
      method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "x-test-user": user },
      body: JSON.stringify({ view, requests: [{ experienceId: v.experienceId, versionHash: v.versionHash, handle, params }] }),
    });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    return ((await res.json()) as { results: { state: string; value?: any; finding?: { code: string } }[] }).results[0] as { state: string; value?: any; finding?: { code: string } };
  };

  beforeAll(async () => {
    h = await startDocumentServer();
    fields = new FieldCrypto(h.keys);
    mine = await seedDoc(h.owner, h.app, fields, ALICE, "a".repeat(64), 10);
    mine2 = await seedDoc(h.owner, h.app, fields, ALICE, "b".repeat(64), 10);
    colleagues = await seedDoc(h.owner, h.app, fields, ALICE2, "c".repeat(64), 10);
    bobs = await seedDoc(h.owner, h.app, fields, BOB, "d".repeat(64), 10);
    await new PgChunkStore(h.app, fields).replace({ tenantId: ALICE.tenantId, userId: ALICE.userId, documentId: mine }, Array.from({ length: 45 }, (_, i) => chunk(i)));
  });
  afterAll(async () => { await h?.close(); });

  it("the library lists only the caller's own documents: not a colleague's in the same tenant, not another tenant's", async () => {
    const r = await hydrate("alice", "library", "documents");
    expect(r.state).toBe("ready");
    expect([...r.value.rowKeys].sort()).toEqual([mine, mine2].sort());
    expect(JSON.stringify(r)).not.toContain(colleagues);
    expect(JSON.stringify(r)).not.toContain(bobs);
    expect((await hydrate("bob", "library", "documents")).value.rowKeys).toEqual([bobs]);
    expect((await hydrate("alice2", "library", "documents")).value.rowKeys).toEqual([colleagues]);
  });

  it("a document that is not the caller's answers exactly like one that does not exist, for every handle of the detail view", async () => {
    for (const handle of ["doc-status", "doc-summary", "doc-words", "doc-facts", "doc-content"]) {
      const answers = await Promise.all([colleagues, bobs, randomUUID()].map((id) => hydrate("alice", "detail", handle, { id })));
      expect(new Set(answers.map((a) => JSON.stringify(a))).size, handle).toBe(1);
      expect(answers[0]?.state).toBe("error");
      expect(JSON.stringify(answers[0])).not.toMatch(/sentinel|[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-/);
    }
  });

  it("serves the caller's own document through the same handles, with the passage the caller names first", async () => {
    expect((await hydrate("alice", "detail", "doc-status", { id: mine })).state).toBe("ready");
    const page = await hydrate("alice", "detail", "doc-content", { id: mine, upto: 20 });
    expect(page.value.passages).toHaveLength(20);
    expect(page.value.more).toHaveLength(1);
    const list = await get(`/api/documents/${mine}/content?cursor=${Buffer.from("o:29").toString("base64url")}`);
    const target = ((await list.json()) as { items: { passageId: string }[] }).items[0]?.passageId as string;
    const deep = await hydrate("alice", "detail", "doc-content", { id: mine, upto: 20, passage: target });
    expect(deep.value.passages[0].head.startsWith("Cited passage - ")).toBe(true);
    expect(deep.value.passages.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(31);
  });

  it("a parameter that is not what the handle takes is an error that touches no row", async () => {
    for (const params of [{ status: "; drop table documents" }, { status: "ready; select" }, { limit: 5000 }]) {
      const r = await hydrate("alice", "library", "documents", params);
      expect(r.state === "error" || r.value.rowKeys.length === 2, JSON.stringify(params)).toBe(true);
    }
    expect((await hydrate("alice", "detail", "doc-status", { id: `${mine}' OR '1'='1` })).state).toBe("error");
    expect((await hydrate("alice", "detail", "doc-content", { id: mine, upto: 100000 })).state).toBe("error");
  });

  it("filters by status for the table and not for the counts", async () => {
    expect((await hydrate("alice", "library", "documents", { status: "failed" })).value.rowKeys).toEqual([]);
    expect((await hydrate("alice", "library", "doc-total", { status: "failed" })).value.value).toBe(2);
  });

  it("answers 401 without a session, and the views route refuses an anonymous caller too", async () => {
    expect((await get("/api/ui/views/library", "")).status).toBe(401);
    const res = await fetch(`${h.base}/api/ui/hydrate`, { method: "POST", headers: { "content-type": "application/json" }, body: "{}" });
    expect(res.status).toBe(401);
  });

  it("never logs content, an id or an email while serving, and fails closed for an erased user's stale session", async () => {
    const log = vi.spyOn(console, "error").mockImplementation(() => {});
    await hydrate("alice", "detail", "doc-status", { id: colleagues });
    for (const call of log.mock.calls) expect(JSON.stringify(call)).not.toMatch(/sentinel|@|[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-/);
    log.mockRestore();
  });

  it("the sessions handle lists the caller's chats only", async () => {
    const r = await hydrate("bob", "sessions", "chats");
    expect(r.state).toBe("ready");
    expect(r.value.rowKeys).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

`apps/server/app/ui/readers.ts`:

```ts
import type { Identity } from "@docmind/auth";
import type { DocumentDto, DocumentPassage } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { CHAT_LIST_CAP, CONTENT_CAP, type UiReaders } from "@docmind/experiences";
import type { Pool } from "pg";
import { listChatsPage } from "../chats/routes";
import type { FieldCrypto } from "../crypto/fields";
import { withTenant } from "../db/tenant";
import { decodeContentCursor, readContent } from "../documents/content";
import { getDocument, listDocuments, toDto } from "../documents/repo";
import type { DocumentStatusReads } from "../status/ingestStatus";
import { UPLOADED } from "../status/model";

export interface UiReaderDeps {
  pool: Pool;
  fields: FieldCrypto;
  status: DocumentStatusReads;
}

const PAGE = 100;
const PROCESSING = new Set<DocumentDto["status"]>(["uploaded", "parsing", "embedding"]);
const matches = (status: "all" | "ready" | "processing" | "failed") => (d: DocumentDto): boolean =>
  status === "all" || (status === "processing" ? PROCESSING.has(d.status) : d.status === status);

/**
 * The readers the UI plane serves from, bound to ONE caller. The tenant and the owner come from `identity` (the session)
 * and are applied by `withTenant` and by the owner column of every query; no argument names either. Each reader uses the
 * same functions as the REST route of the same data, so what a user can see here is exactly what the API lets them see.
 */
export function readersFor(deps: UiReaderDeps, identity: Identity): UiReaders {
  const cipher = () => deps.fields.forUser(identity.userId);
  return {
    async documents({ limit, status }) {
      const items: DocumentDto[] = [];
      let after: { t: string; i: string } | null = null;
      let truncated = false;
      for (;;) {
        const cursor = after;
        const page = await withTenant(deps.pool, identity.tenantId, (tx) => listDocuments(tx, identity.userId, { cursor, limit: PAGE }));
        const views = await deps.status.forPage(identity, page.rows.map((r) => ({ id: r.id, generation: r.ingest_generation })));
        for (const row of page.rows) items.push(await toDto(row, cipher(), views.get(row.id) ?? UPLOADED));
        if (!page.next) break;
        if (items.length >= limit) {
          truncated = true;
          break;
        }
        after = { t: page.next.created_cursor, i: page.next.id };
      }
      return { items: items.slice(0, limit).filter(matches(status)), truncated };
    },

    async document({ id }) {
      const row = await withTenant(deps.pool, identity.tenantId, (tx) => getDocument(tx, identity.userId, id));
      if (!row) return null;
      return toDto(row, cipher(), await deps.status.forDocument(identity, row.id, row.ingest_generation));
    },

    async content({ id, upto, passage }) {
      const out: DocumentPassage[] = [];
      let after = -1;
      let more = false;
      for (;;) {
        const page = await readContent(deps.pool, deps.fields, identity, id, after);
        if (page === null) return null;
        out.push(...page.items);
        const next = page.nextCursor === null ? null : decodeContentCursor(page.nextCursor);
        more = next !== null;
        if (next === null) break;
        after = next;
        if ((out.length >= upto && (passage === undefined || out.some((p) => p.passageId === passage))) || out.length >= CONTENT_CAP) break;
      }
      const cited = passage === undefined ? -1 : out.findIndex((p) => p.passageId === passage);
      const keep = Math.max(upto, cited + 1);
      return { items: out.slice(0, keep), more: more || out.length > keep };
    },

    async chats({ q }) {
      const page = await listChatsPage(deps, identity, null, q === "" ? undefined : q);
      return page.items.slice(0, CHAT_LIST_CAP);
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/server/app/ui/routes.ts`:

```ts
import type { Authenticator, Identity } from "@docmind/auth";
import { SurfaceName, UiHydrateRequest, type UiHydrateResponse, type UiViewResponse, ViewName } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { UiReaders, Views } from "@docmind/experiences";
import { readJson } from "../http/body";
import { requireAuth } from "../http/requireAuth";
import { type Handler, json, sendError } from "../http/respond";
import { type UiReaderDeps, readersFor as realReadersFor } from "./readers";

export interface UiRouteDeps {
  authenticator: Authenticator;
  views: Views;
  readers: UiReaderDeps;
  /** Test seam: replaces the identity-bound readers. */
  readersFor?: (identity: Identity) => UiReaders;
}

/** `GET /api/ui/views/:view` (the compiled view) and `POST /api/ui/hydrate` (its data). Both take the caller from the session only. */
export function uiRoutes(deps: UiRouteDeps): Record<string, Handler> {
  const readers = deps.readersFor ?? ((identity: Identity) => realReadersFor(deps.readers, identity));
  return {
    "GET /api/ui/views/:view": requireAuth(deps.authenticator, async (req, res, _identity, ctx) => {
      const view = ViewName.safeParse(ctx.params.view);
      if (!view.success) return sendError(res, 404, "not_found", "view not found");
      const surface = SurfaceName.safeParse(new URL(req.url ?? "/", "http://localhost").searchParams.get("surface") ?? "web");
      if (!surface.success) return sendError(res, 400, "invalid_request", "unknown surface");
      res.setHeader("cache-control", "private, no-store");
      json(res, 200, deps.views.payload(view.data, surface.data) satisfies UiViewResponse as unknown as UiViewResponse);
    }),

    "POST /api/ui/hydrate": requireAuth(deps.authenticator, async (req, res, identity) => {
      const body = await readJson(req, res, UiHydrateRequest);
      if (!body) return;
      const results = await deps.views.hydrate(body.view, body.requests, readers(identity), identity.userId);
      res.setHeader("cache-control", "no-store");
      json(res, 200, { results } satisfies UiHydrateResponse);
    }),
  };
}
```

## Phase E: the browser: a thin web surface

### Task 189: the web surface's host for an experience: `ViewSession` and `<dm-view>`

**Spec:** §7, Decisions D6, D12, D13, D14. Findings U15, U17, U18. This is the whole of the web's loom-ui plumbing; every page after it only mounts a view and performs intents.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/ui/session.ts`, `apps/web/src/ui/elements.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-view.ts`, `apps/web/src/testing/fakeUi.ts`, `apps/web/src/ui/session.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-view.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/api/client.ts` (`uiView`, `uiHydrate`), `apps/web/src/api/client.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `UiViewResponse`, `UiHydrateResponse`, `ViewName` (contracts); `HydrationStore`, `SplitHydrationController`, `HandleLoader`, `HandleLoadRequest` (`@loom-ui/hydration/client`); `renderBlueprintDom` (`@loom-ui/renderer-lit/dom`), `createChatLitRegistry` (`/chat`), `referenceContainers` (`/containers`); `compileFingerprint`, `isStaleAction` (`@loom-ui/core/browser`); `InMemoryProjectionRuntime` (`@loom/projection-runtime`); `ServicesController`, `ToastStore`, `report`.
- Produces (catalogue): `ViewAction`, `ActionAnswer`, `ActionHandler`, `ViewSession`, `<dm-view>`; `Api.uiView(name, signal?)` (`GET /api/ui/views/:name?surface=web`) and `Api.uiHydrate(view, requests, signal?)` (`POST /api/ui/hydrate`, returns `results`); the test helper `fakeUi(readers)` (`testing/fakeUi.ts`: an in-process `Pick<Api, "uiView" | "uiHydrate">` that serves the **real** compiled views and the **real** `hydrate` of `@docmind/experiences` over the readers a test supplies).
- Behavior:
  - `ViewSession.open(api, name, { onStale })` fetches the payload and builds a runtime, a `HydrationStore` and a `SplitHydrationController` whose `HandleLoader.loadMany` posts to `uiHydrate`. `start(params?)` applies the params to every handle, then loads the inventory. `mount(host, onAction)` draws the blueprint with `renderBlueprintDom` (`executionId: store.executionId`, `createChatLitRegistry()`, `referenceContainers()`, `title: "none"`, the compile fingerprint of the payload).
  - **The stale-view check.** Every widget action reaches `dispatch`: if `isStaleAction(action, head)` the action is refused (`{ ok: false, reason: "stale" }`), `onStale` fires and the view reloads (`reload()` refetches, `split.load(newInventory)` recomposes, `handle.update(newBlueprint)` patches in place). The reads that a transcript's own movement would always trip (`LOAD_OLDER`, `LOAD_REASONING`, `EDIT_TURN`, `CHAT_REF_OPEN`) are compared against the composition and the compile fingerprint only, not the binding sequence (loom-ui README: a transcript moves by design).
  - A `version-mismatch` finding in any hydrate result reloads the view once.
  - `publish(handle, value, kind, { streaming })` puts a value the page folds itself into the store (`publishValue`); `false` for a handle the inventory does not list or a value the kind refuses.
  - `<dm-view name params onAct>` draws a fixed loading line, then the view; on failure a fixed sentence and a "Try again" button; it fires `view-ready` (detail `{session}`) and `view-problem`. A changed `name` replaces the session; a changed `params` calls `setParams`. It disposes the session when removed.

`apps/web/src/testing/fakeUi.ts` (not a test; the tests import it):

```ts
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import type { UiViewResponse, ViewName } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { UiReaders } from "@docmind/experiences";
import type { Api } from "../api/client";

// `@docmind/experiences` is a Node (CommonJS) package: tests load it the way Node does, never through the browser bundler.
const experiences = createRequire(import.meta.url)("@docmind/experiences") as typeof import("@docmind/experiences");

export const emptyReaders = (): UiReaders => ({
  documents: async () => ({ items: [], truncated: false }),
  document: async () => null,
  content: async () => null,
  chats: async () => [],
});

/** The server's UI plane in-process: the real compiled views and the real `hydrate`, over the readers a test gives. */
export function fakeUi(readers: UiReaders = emptyReaders()) {
  const views = experiences.createViews({ placeholderKey: "k".repeat(32), revisionKey: "r".repeat(32) });
  const calls = { views: [] as ViewName[], hydrate: [] as { view: ViewName; handles: string[] }[] };
  const api: Pick<Api, "uiView" | "uiHydrate"> = {
    async uiView(name) {
      calls.views.push(name);
      return structuredClone(views.payload(name, "web")) as unknown as UiViewResponse;
    },
    async uiHydrate(view, requests) {
      calls.hydrate.push({ view, handles: requests.map((r) => r.handle) });
      return views.hydrate(view, requests, readers, "user-1");
    },
  };
  return { api, views, calls };
}
```

`apps/web/src/ui/session.test.ts`:

```ts
import type { DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { fakeUi } from "../testing/fakeUi";
import { ViewSession } from "./session";

const id = (n: number) => `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`;
const doc = (n: number, over: Partial<DocumentDto> = {}): DocumentDto => ({
  id: id(n), title: `Title ${n}`, filename: `f${n}.pdf`, mime: "application/pdf", sizeBytes: 100, status: "ready", error: null, stats: null, summary: null,
  createdAt: "2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z", ...over,
});
const readers = (items: DocumentDto[]) => ({
  documents: vi.fn(async () => ({ items, truncated: false })),
  document: vi.fn(async (a: { id: string }) => items.find((d) => d.id === a.id) ?? null),
  content: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [], more: false })),
  chats: vi.fn(async () => []),
});
const tick = () => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));

async function open(name: "library" | "detail" | "chat", items = [doc(1), doc(2, { status: "failed", error: "Could not read this file." })]) {
  const r = readers(items);
  const ui = fakeUi(r);
  const onStale = vi.fn();
  const session = await ViewSession.open(ui.api, name, { onStale });
  const host = document.createElement("div");
  document.body.append(host);
  const seen: unknown[] = [];
  return { r, ui, session, host, seen, onStale, mount: () => session.mount(host, (a) => { seen.push(a); return { ok: true }; }) };
}

describe("ViewSession", () => {
  it("hydrates a view from the server's plane and draws it with loom-ui's elements", async () => {
    const t = await open("library");
    t.session.start();
    t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.host.querySelector("lu-table")?.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Title 1"));
    expect(t.host.querySelectorAll("lu-kpi").length).toBe(2);
    expect(t.host.querySelector("lu-status-card")?.shadowRoot?.textContent).toMatch(/failed/i);
    expect(t.ui.calls.hydrate.flatMap((c) => c.handles).sort()).toEqual(["doc-ready", "doc-total", "documents", "ingest"]);
    expect(t.r.documents).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2); // one shared step for the counts and the health, one for the table
  });

  it("a parameter set before start reaches the handles, and a later one reloads them", async () => {
    const t = await open("detail", [doc(1), doc(2, { status: "failed", error: "Could not read this file." })]);
    t.session.start({ id: id(1) });
    t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.session.statusOf("doc-status")).toBe("ready"));
    expect(t.host.querySelector("lu-status-card")?.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Ready");
    t.session.setParams({ id: id(2) });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.host.querySelector("lu-status-card")?.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Failed"));
    expect(t.host.querySelectorAll("[role=tab]").length).toBe(3);
  });

  it("hands a widget action to the handler, with the type, the widget and the payload, when the view is fresh", async () => {
    const t = await open("library");
    t.session.start();
    t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.session.statusOf("documents")).toBe("ready"));
    const select = t.host.querySelector("lu-select-input")?.shadowRoot?.querySelector("select") as HTMLSelectElement;
    select.value = "failed";
    select.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.seen).toHaveLength(1));
    expect(t.seen[0]).toMatchObject({ type: "SELECT", widgetId: "filter", payload: { value: "failed" } });
  });

  it("refuses a stale action, tells the page, and reloads the view", async () => {
    const t = await open("library");
    t.session.start();
    t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.session.statusOf("documents")).toBe("ready"));
    const stale = { type: "TABLE_ROW_ACTION", widgetId: "table", payload: { action: "delete", rowKey: id(1) }, viewedVersion: { experienceId: "docmind.library", versionHash: "sha256:old", compile: "x" } };
    expect(t.session.isFresh(stale)).toBe(false);
    await expect(t.session.dispatch(stale as never, () => ({ ok: true }))).resolves.toEqual({ ok: false, reason: "stale" });
    expect(t.onStale).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.ui.calls.views).toEqual(["library", "library"]));
    expect(t.seen).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("refuses an action that carries no stamp at all", async () => {
    const t = await open("library");
    t.session.start();
    t.mount();
    expect(t.session.isFresh({ type: "SELECT", widgetId: "filter" })).toBe(false);
  });

  it("reloads once when the server says the version moved", async () => {
    const t = await open("library");
    const real = t.ui.api.uiHydrate;
    let first = true;
    const api = { ...t.ui.api, uiHydrate: vi.fn(async (view, requests, signal) => (first ? ((first = false), requests.map(() => ({ state: "error", finding: { code: "version-mismatch", severity: "error", message: "m" } }))) : real(view, requests, signal))) };
    const s = await ViewSession.open(api as never, "library", {});
    s.start();
    s.mount(t.host, () => undefined);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(s.statusOf("documents")).toBe("ready"));
    expect(t.ui.calls.views.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
  });

  it("publishes a value the page folds itself, for a listed handle and a kind that accepts it", async () => {
    const t = await open("chat");
    t.session.start();
    t.mount();
    const value = { entries: [{ id: "t1.assistant", turn: 1, role: "assistant", status: "complete", parts: [{ type: "text", text: "Hello" }] }] };
    expect(t.session.publish("transcript", value, "Chat")).toBe(true);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.host.querySelector("lu-chat")?.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Hello"));
    expect(t.session.valueOf("transcript")).toEqual(value);
    expect(t.session.publish("nope", value, "Chat")).toBe(false);
    expect(t.session.publish("transcript", { entries: "x" }, "Chat")).toBe(false);
    expect(t.ui.calls.hydrate).toEqual([]); // a published handle is never requested
  });

  it("disposes: nothing more is drawn or accepted", async () => {
    const t = await open("chat");
    t.session.start();
    t.mount();
    t.session.dispose();
    expect(t.session.publish("transcript", { entries: [] }, "Chat")).toBe(false);
    await expect(t.session.reload()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(t.ui.calls.views).toEqual(["chat"]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { createServices } from "../services";
import { fakeUi } from "../testing/fakeUi";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import "./dm-view";

const settle = (el: Element) => (el as unknown as { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete;
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as T;

describe("dm-view", () => {
  it("shows a loading line, then the view, and says it is ready", async () => {
    const ui = fakeUi();
    const services = createServices({ api: ui.api as never });
    const ready = vi.fn();
    const mounted = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-view", { name: "library-empty" });
    mounted.addEventListener("view-ready", ready); // the view is fetched asynchronously, so this is in time
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(mounted, "#host lu-note")).not.toBeNull());
    expect(q(mounted, "#host lu-note").shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("No documents yet");
    expect(q(mounted, "[data-loading]")).toBeNull();
    expect(ready).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("shows a fixed sentence and a Try again button when the view cannot be fetched, and recovers", async () => {
    const ui = fakeUi();
    let fail = true;
    const api = { ...ui.api, uiView: vi.fn(async (name: never) => { if (fail) throw new ApiError(0, "network"); return ui.api.uiView(name); }) };
    const services = createServices({ api: api as never });
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-view", { name: "library-empty" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "[role=alert]")).not.toBeNull());
    expect(q(el, "[role=alert]").textContent).toContain("could not be loaded");
    fail = false;
    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-retry]").click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(q(el, "#host lu-note")).not.toBeNull());
  });

  it("swaps the experience when the name changes, and disposes the old one", async () => {
    const ui = fakeUi();
    const services = createServices({ api: ui.api as never });
    const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement & { name: string; session?: { name: string } }>(services, "dm-view", { name: "library-empty" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(el.session?.name).toBe("library-empty"));
    el.name = "sessions";
    await settle(el);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(el.session?.name).toBe("sessions"));
    expect(q(el, "#host lu-note")).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`ui/session.ts`:

```ts
import type { UiViewResponse, ViewName } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { type StampedAction, compileFingerprint, isStaleAction } from "@loom-ui/core/browser";
import { type HandleLoadRequest, type HandleLoadResult, type HandleLoader, HydrationStore, SplitHydrationController } from "@loom-ui/hydration/client";
import { createChatLitRegistry } from "@loom-ui/renderer-lit/chat";
import { referenceContainers } from "@loom-ui/renderer-lit/containers";
import { type DomRenderHandle, renderBlueprintDom } from "@loom-ui/renderer-lit/dom";
import { InMemoryProjectionRuntime } from "@loom/projection-runtime";
import type { Api } from "../api/client";
import "./elements";

export interface ViewAction {
  type: string;
  widgetId: string;
  payload: Record<string, unknown>;
}
export type ActionAnswer = { ok: boolean; reason?: string } | undefined;
export type ActionHandler = (a: ViewAction) => Promise<ActionAnswer> | ActionAnswer;

/** Reads a chat transcript makes by moving: compared against the composition and compile fingerprint, not the binding sequence. */
const MOVING_READS = new Set(["LOAD_OLDER", "LOAD_REASONING", "EDIT_TURN", "CHAT_REF_OPEN"]);
const isLoadResult = (r: unknown): r is { finding?: { code?: string } } => typeof r === "object" && r !== null;

export class ViewSession {
  readonly store: HydrationStore;
  readonly split: SplitHydrationController;
  private readonly runtime = new InMemoryProjectionRuntime();
  private handle: DomRenderHandle | undefined;
  private reloading: Promise<void> | undefined;
  private disposed = false;

  private constructor(
    private readonly api: Pick<Api, "uiView" | "uiHydrate">,
    readonly name: ViewName,
    private payload: UiViewResponse,
    private readonly onStale: (() => void) | undefined,
  ) {
    this.store = new HydrationStore(this.runtime, { experienceId: payload.experienceId });
    const loader: HandleLoader = {
      load: async (request, signal) => (await loadMany([request], signal))[0] as HandleLoadResult,
      loadMany: (requests, signal) => loadMany(requests, signal),
    };
    const loadMany = async (requests: readonly HandleLoadRequest[], signal: AbortSignal): Promise<HandleLoadResult[]> => {
      const results = (await api.uiHydrate(name, [...requests], signal)) as HandleLoadResult[];
      if (results.some((r) => isLoadResult(r) && r.finding?.code === "version-mismatch")) void this.reload();
      return results;
    };
    this.split = new SplitHydrationController({ loader, store: this.store });
  }

  static async open(api: Pick<Api, "uiView" | "uiHydrate">, name: ViewName, o: { onStale?(): void } = {}): Promise<ViewSession> {
    return new ViewSession(api, name, await api.uiView(name), o.onStale);
  }

  /** Applies the params to every handle, then loads the inventory (a handle loads with the params it declares, no others). */
  start(params?: Record<string, string | number>): void {
    if (params) this.split.setParams(params);
    this.split.load(this.payload.inventory);
  }

  mount(host: HTMLElement, onAction: ActionHandler): void {
    const fp = this.payload.fingerprint;
    this.handle = renderBlueprintDom(host, this.payload.blueprint as never, {
      projectionRuntime: this.runtime,
      executionId: this.store.executionId,
      registry: createChatLitRegistry(),
      containers: referenceContainers(),
      title: "none",
      compile: compileFingerprint({ locale: "en", ...(fp.audience !== undefined && { audience: fp.audience }), restrictedHandles: fp.restrictedHandles, restrictedProjectionNodes: fp.restrictedProjectionNodes }),
      onAction: (a: StampedAction) => this.dispatch(a, onAction),
    });
  }

  isFresh(action: { type: string; widgetId?: string; viewedVersion?: unknown }): boolean {
    if (this.handle === undefined) return false;
    const head = this.handle.head();
    const current = MOVING_READS.has(action.type)
      ? { experienceId: head.experienceId, versionHash: head.versionHash, ...(head.compile !== undefined && { compile: head.compile }) }
      : head;
    return !isStaleAction(action as never, current);
  }

  async dispatch(a: StampedAction, onAction: ActionHandler): Promise<ActionAnswer> {
    if (!this.isFresh(a)) {
      this.onStale?.();
      void this.reload();
      return { ok: false, reason: "stale" };
    }
    return (await onAction({ type: a.type, widgetId: a.widgetId, payload: (typeof a.payload === "object" && a.payload !== null ? a.payload : {}) as Record<string, unknown> })) ?? { ok: true };
  }

  setParams(params: Record<string, string | number>, handle?: string): void {
    this.split.setParams(params, handle === undefined ? undefined : { handle });
  }
  invalidate(handle?: string): void {
    if (handle === undefined) for (const h of this.payload.inventory.handles) this.split.invalidate(h.handle);
    else this.split.invalidate(handle);
  }

  publish(handle: string, value: unknown, kind: string, o: { streaming?: boolean } = {}): boolean {
    if (this.disposed) return false;
    return this.split.publishValue(handle, value, { state: o.streaming ? "pending" : "ready", kind }).ok;
  }
  valueOf(handle: string): unknown {
    return (this.store.state().values as Record<string, unknown>)[handle];
  }
  statusOf(handle: string): string | undefined {
    return (this.store.state().status as Record<string, { state: string } | undefined>)[handle]?.state;
  }

  /** Refetches the view and recomposes in place (a deploy changed it, or an action was stale). One reload at a time. */
  reload(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.disposed) return Promise.resolve();
    this.reloading ??= (async () => {
      try {
        this.payload = await this.api.uiView(this.name);
        if (this.disposed) return;
        this.split.load(this.payload.inventory);
        this.handle?.update(this.payload.blueprint as never);
      } catch {
        // the next action or the page's "Try again" asks again
      } finally {
        this.reloading = undefined;
      }
    })();
    return this.reloading;
  }

  dispose(): void {
    this.disposed = true;
    this.handle?.dispose();
    this.handle = undefined;
    this.split.dispose();
  }
}
```

`components/dm-view.ts`:

```ts
import type { ViewName } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { report } from "../report";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import { type ActionHandler, ViewSession } from "../ui/session";

/** Mounts one loom-ui experience: a view session, drawn by renderer-lit into this element's shadow root. Draws no data itself. */
export class DmView extends LitElement {
  static properties = { name: { type: String }, params: { attribute: false }, onAct: { attribute: false }, phase: { state: true } };
  static styles = [baseStyles, css`:host { display: block; } [data-loading] { padding: 16px; color: var(--dm-muted); } [role="alert"] { padding: 16px; display: grid; gap: 8px; justify-items: start; }`];
  declare name: ViewName | undefined;
  declare params: Record<string, string | number> | undefined;
  declare onAct: ActionHandler | undefined;
  declare phase: "loading" | "ready" | "failed";
  session: ViewSession | undefined;
  private opened: string | undefined;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this);
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.phase = "loading";
  }

  protected updated(changed: Map<string, unknown>): void {
    if (this.ctx.services && this.name !== undefined && this.opened !== this.name) void this.open();
    else if (changed.has("params") && this.session && this.params) this.session.setParams(this.params);
  }

  private async open(): Promise<void> {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    const name = this.name;
    if (!svc || name === undefined) return;
    this.opened = name;
    this.session?.dispose();
    this.session = undefined;
    this.phase = "loading";
    try {
      const session = await ViewSession.open(svc.api, name, { onStale: () => svc.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "This view changed. It has been refreshed; try again." }) });
      if (this.name !== name || !this.isConnected) return session.dispose();
      this.session = session;
      session.start(this.params);
      await this.updateComplete;
      session.mount(this.renderRoot.querySelector("#host") as HTMLElement, (a) => this.onAct?.(a));
      this.phase = "ready";
      this.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("view-ready", { detail: { session }, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
    } catch (err) {
      report("view", err);
      this.opened = undefined;
      this.phase = "failed";
      this.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("view-problem", { bubbles: true, composed: true }));
    }
  }

  disconnectedCallback(): void {
    this.session?.dispose();
    this.session = undefined;
    this.opened = undefined;
    super.disconnectedCallback();
  }

  render() {
    return html`
      ${this.phase === "loading" ? html`<p data-loading role="status">Loading...</p>` : null}
      ${this.phase === "failed" ? html`<div role="alert"><p>This section could not be loaded.</p><button class="btn secondary" data-retry @click=${() => { this.opened = undefined; this.requestUpdate(); }}>Try again</button></div>` : null}
      <div id="host" part="host"></div>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-view", DmView);
```

  `components/index.ts`: `import "./dm-view";`. (`update()` of `handle` mutates the same element; `session.start(params)` reads `this.params` at open time.)

- [ ] **Step 4: Run them.** `pnpm --filter @docmind/web exec vitest run`. Expected: PASS (session 9, dm-view 3, client +1).
  **Probe.** (a) `HydrationStore.state()` keys are `values` and `status` (verified); if `executionId` is not public in the installed version, `grep -n executionId ~/workspace/loom-ui/packages/hydration/src/projection.ts`. (b) `SplitHydrationController.setParams(params, { handle })` takes an options object in the installed version (as the plan's probe used); if it takes a string, adapt `setParams` and say so. (c) If an element is not drawn, the first suspect is `executionId`; the second is a missing element import in `ui/elements.ts`. (d) If `lu-select-input` is not found, `createChatLitRegistry` defines it only when called: `ViewSession.mount` calls it. (e) The unstamped-action test relies on `actionFreshness` returning `unstamped`, which `isStaleAction` treats as stale; that is loom-ui's contract.

## Phase F: documents (spec §9 items 42 to 44)

### Task 190: `UploadsStore` and `dm-upload-zone`

**Spec:** §5.1 (upload: 50 MB per file, quotas, dedupe returns the existing document), §5.2 (progress over `GET /api/documents/events`), §9 item 42. Decisions D13, D14; Findings U10. The zone is app-specific because loom-ui's `MediaInput` is one file, has no drop target and one status line (open question 11).

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/state/uploads.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-upload-zone.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/services.ts` (add `uploads`), `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/state/uploads.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-upload-zone.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Api.uploadDocument`, `OpenSse`, `DocumentDto`, `DocumentProgressEvent`, `ApiError`, `ServicesController`.
- Produces (catalogue): `UploadsStore`, `UploadItem`, `UploadReason`, `UploadResult`. Constants `MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 50 * 1024 * 1024`, `UPLOAD_CONCURRENCY = 2`, `UPLOAD_EXTENSIONS = ["txt","md","org","doc","docx","pdf","png","jpg","jpeg","webp"]`.
  - `upload(files)` never rejects. A file over the size cap or with an extension not in the list becomes a failed item at once (`too_large`, `unsupported`) without a request; the rest go through a pool of `UPLOAD_CONCURRENCY`. A success removes its item; a failure keeps it with a `reason`: 403 `quota_exceeded` is `quota`, 413 `too_large`, 415 `unsupported`, status 0 `network`, anything else `server`. It returns one `UploadResult` per file, in the order given.
  - `startEvents(onProgress)` opens `GET /api/documents/events`; each valid `progress` frame calls `onProgress(event)`; after a reconnect it calls `onProgress("resync")` (frames may have been missed). `stopEvents()` closes it.
  - `<dm-upload-zone variant="compact"|"empty">`: draws the drop zone (what it takes and how big), a named browse button, a hidden multiple-file input, and the queue of the store (name, "Waiting" / "Uploading N%" with a named `<progress>` / the failure reason, and a Dismiss button on a failed one). It uploads through `services.uploads` itself and fires `dm-uploaded {results}` (bubbles, composed) when a batch settles. While a drag is over it, it says "Release to upload N files". A dropped folder is ignored.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

```ts
import type { DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import type { SseOptions } from "../api/sse";
import { MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES, UploadsStore } from "./uploads";

const doc = (n: number): DocumentDto => ({
  id: `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`, title: `T${n}`, filename: `f${n}.pdf`, mime: "application/pdf", sizeBytes: 10, status: "uploaded",
  error: null, stats: null, summary: null, createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z",
});
const file = (name: string, size = 3) => {
  const f = new File(["abc"], name);
  Object.defineProperty(f, "size", { value: size });
  return f;
};
function setup(upload = vi.fn(async (f: File) => ({ document: doc(f.name.length), duplicate: false }))) {
  const sse: { opts?: SseOptions; closed: number } = { closed: 0 };
  const open = vi.fn((opts: SseOptions) => { sse.opts = opts; return { close: () => { sse.closed++; } }; });
  const store = new UploadsStore({ uploadDocument: upload as never }, open as never);
  const frame = (data: unknown, event = "progress") => sse.opts?.onFrame({ event, data: JSON.stringify(data), id: undefined });
  return { store, upload, open, sse, frame };
}

describe("UploadsStore.upload", () => {
  it("refuses too large and unsupported files without a request, and uploads the rest in order", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    const out = await t.store.upload([file("big.pdf", MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES + 1), file("run.exe"), file("ok.TXT")]);
    expect(out.map((r) => r.reason ?? "ok")).toEqual(["too_large", "unsupported", "ok"]);
    expect(t.upload).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(t.store.get().uploads.map((u) => [u.name, u.state, u.reason])).toEqual([["big.pdf", "failed", "too_large"], ["run.exe", "failed", "unsupported"]]);
  });
  it("reports progress on the item and removes it on success, keeping the duplicate flag", async () => {
    const t = setup(vi.fn(async (_f: File, o?: { onProgress?(p: number): void }) => { o?.onProgress?.(40); return { document: doc(1), duplicate: true }; }));
    const seen: number[] = [];
    t.store.subscribe((s) => { const u = s.uploads[0]; if (u?.state === "uploading") seen.push(u.percent); });
    const [r] = await t.store.upload([file("a.pdf")]);
    expect(r).toMatchObject({ duplicate: true, document: { id: doc(1).id } });
    expect(seen).toContain(40);
    expect(t.store.get().uploads).toEqual([]);
  });
  it.each([
    [new ApiError(403, "quota_exceeded"), "quota"], [new ApiError(413, "too_large"), "too_large"], [new ApiError(415, "unsupported_type"), "unsupported"],
    [new ApiError(0, "network"), "network"], [new ApiError(500, "internal"), "server"], [new TypeError("x"), "server"],
  ])("a failure %# keeps its item with the reason %s", async (err, reason) => {
    const t = setup(vi.fn(async () => { throw err; }));
    const [r] = await t.store.upload([file("a.pdf")]);
    expect(r?.reason).toBe(reason);
    expect(t.store.get().uploads[0]).toMatchObject({ state: "failed", reason });
    t.store.dismissUpload(t.store.get().uploads[0]?.key as string);
    expect(t.store.get().uploads).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("runs at most two uploads at once", async () => {
    let live = 0, peak = 0;
    const gates: (() => void)[] = [];
    const t = setup(vi.fn(async (f: File) => { live++; peak = Math.max(peak, live); await new Promise<void>((r) => gates.push(r)); live--; return { document: doc(f.name.length), duplicate: false }; }));
    const done = t.store.upload(["a.pdf", "bb.pdf", "ccc.pdf", "dddd.pdf"].map((n) => file(n)));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(gates).toHaveLength(2));
    for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { await vi.waitFor(() => expect(gates.length).toBeGreaterThan(i)); gates[i]?.(); }
    await done;
    expect(peak).toBe(2);
  });
});

describe("UploadsStore events", () => {
  it("passes valid progress frames on, ignores junk, and asks for a resync after a reconnect", () => {
    const t = setup();
    const heard: unknown[] = [];
    t.store.startEvents((e) => heard.push(e));
    t.store.startEvents(() => heard.push("second")); // already open: ignored
    const id = "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10";
    t.sse.opts?.onOpen?.();
    t.frame({ documentId: id, step: "parse", status: "parsing" });
    t.frame({ documentId: "nope", status: "parsing" });
    t.sse.opts?.onFrame({ event: "progress", data: "{not json", id: undefined });
    t.frame({ documentId: id, status: "ready", step: "finalize" }, "other");
    t.sse.opts?.onOpen?.(); // the second open is a reconnect
    expect(heard).toEqual([{ documentId: id, step: "parse", status: "parsing" }, "resync"]);
    t.store.stopEvents();
    expect(t.sse.closed).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createServices } from "../services";
import { UploadsStore } from "../state/uploads";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import "./dm-upload-zone";

const doc = { id: "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11", title: "T", filename: "a.pdf", mime: "application/pdf", sizeBytes: 1, status: "uploaded", error: null, stats: null, summary: null, createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z" };
async function mount(upload = vi.fn(async () => ({ document: doc, duplicate: false }))) {
  const uploads = new UploadsStore({ uploadDocument: upload as never }, (() => ({ close() {} })) as never);
  const services = createServices({ uploads, fetch: (async () => new Response("{}")) as never });
  const el = await mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-upload-zone", { variant: "compact" });
  return { el, upload, uploads };
}
const q = <T extends Element>(el: Element, sel: string) => el.shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as T;
const file = (n: string) => new File(["x"], n);
const drag = (type: string, files: File[], items?: { kind: string; webkitGetAsEntry?: () => { isDirectory: boolean } | null }[]) => {
  const e = new Event(type, { bubbles: true, cancelable: true }) as Event & { dataTransfer?: unknown };
  e.dataTransfer = { files, items: items ?? files.map(() => ({ kind: "file" })), types: ["Files"], dropEffect: "none" };
  return e;
};

describe("dm-upload-zone", () => {
  it("states what it takes and how big, and has a named browse button and a multiple-file input", async () => {
    const { el } = await mount();
    expect(el.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("PDF, DOCX, DOC, TXT, Markdown, Org, PNG, JPG, WEBP - up to 50 MB each");
    expect(q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-browse]").textContent).toContain("browse");
    expect(q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[type=file]").multiple).toBe(true);
  });
  it("uploads the files chosen with the browse button and says when the batch settled", async () => {
    const { el, upload } = await mount();
    const settled = vi.fn();
    el.addEventListener("dm-uploaded", (e) => settled((e as CustomEvent).detail.results.length));
    const input = q<HTMLInputElement>(el, "input[type=file]");
    Object.defineProperty(input, "files", { value: [file("a.pdf")], configurable: true });
    input.dispatchEvent(new Event("change"));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(settled).toHaveBeenCalledWith(1));
    expect(upload).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
  it("says how many files it would take while a drag is over it, uploads a drop, and ignores a folder", async () => {
    const { el, upload } = await mount();
    const zone = q<HTMLElement>(el, "[data-zone]");
    zone.dispatchEvent(drag("dragenter", [file("a.pdf"), file("b.pdf")]));
    await (el as unknown as { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete;
    expect(zone.textContent).toContain("Release to upload 2 files");
    zone.dispatchEvent(drag("dragleave", []));
    const drop = drag("drop", [file("a.pdf")], [{ kind: "file", webkitGetAsEntry: () => ({ isDirectory: true }) }]);
    zone.dispatchEvent(drop);
    expect(drop.defaultPrevented).toBe(true);
    expect(upload).not.toHaveBeenCalled(); // the only item was a folder
    zone.dispatchEvent(drag("drop", [file("c.pdf")]));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(upload).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1));
  });
  it("shows a failed upload with its reason and a Dismiss button, and a running one with a named progress bar", async () => {
    const { el, uploads } = await mount();
    uploads.set({ uploads: [{ key: "u1", name: "big.pdf", size: 1, percent: 0, state: "failed", reason: "too_large" }, { key: "u2", name: "b.pdf", size: 1, percent: 40, state: "uploading" }, { key: "u3", name: "c.pdf", size: 1, percent: 0, state: "queued" }] });
    await (el as unknown as { updateComplete: Promise<unknown> }).updateComplete;
    const rows = [...(el.shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll("[data-upload]") ?? [])].map((r) => r.textContent?.replace(/\s+/g, " ").trim());
    expect(rows[0]).toContain("Too large (50 MB limit).");
    expect(rows[1]).toContain("Uploading 40%");

    q<HTMLButtonElement>(el, "button[data-dismiss]").click();
    expect(uploads.get().uploads.map((u) => u.key)).toEqual(["u2", "u3"]);
  });
});
```

`state/uploads.ts`:

```ts
import { type DocumentDto, DocumentProgressEvent } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { Api } from "../api/client";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import type { OpenSse } from "../api/sse";
import { Store } from "./store";

export const MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES = 50 * 1024 * 1024;
export const UPLOAD_CONCURRENCY = 2;
export const UPLOAD_EXTENSIONS = ["txt", "md", "org", "doc", "docx", "pdf", "png", "jpg", "jpeg", "webp"] as const;

export type UploadReason = "too_large" | "unsupported" | "quota" | "network" | "server";
export interface UploadItem {
  key: string;
  name: string;
  size: number;
  percent: number;
  state: "queued" | "uploading" | "failed";
  reason?: UploadReason;
}
export interface UploadResult {
  file: File;
  document?: DocumentDto;
  duplicate?: boolean;
  reason?: UploadReason;
}

const reasonOf = (err: unknown): UploadReason => {
  if (err instanceof ApiError) {
    if (err.status === 403 && err.code === "quota_exceeded") return "quota";
    if (err.status === 413) return "too_large";
    if (err.status === 415) return "unsupported";
    if (err.status === 0) return "network";
  }
  return "server";
};
const extensionOf = (name: string) => name.slice(name.lastIndexOf(".") + 1).toLowerCase();

/** The browser's upload queue and the document progress stream. Which documents exist is the library view's business, not this store's. */
export class UploadsStore extends Store<{ uploads: UploadItem[] }> {
  private events: { close(): void } | undefined;
  private opens = 0;
  private seq = 0;
  constructor(
    private readonly api: Pick<Api, "uploadDocument">,
    private readonly open: OpenSse,
  ) {
    super({ uploads: [] });
  }

  dismissUpload(key: string): void {
    this.update((s) => ({ uploads: s.uploads.filter((u) => u.key !== key) }));
  }
  private patch(key: string, patch: Partial<UploadItem>): void {
    this.update((s) => ({ uploads: s.uploads.map((u) => (u.key === key ? { ...u, ...patch } : u)) }));
  }

  async upload(files: File[]): Promise<UploadResult[]> {
    const results: UploadResult[] = files.map((file) => ({ file }));
    const queue: { file: File; key: string; at: number }[] = [];
    files.forEach((file, at) => {
      const key = `u${++this.seq}`;
      const reason: UploadReason | undefined = file.size > MAX_UPLOAD_BYTES ? "too_large" : (UPLOAD_EXTENSIONS as readonly string[]).includes(extensionOf(file.name)) ? undefined : "unsupported";
      this.update((s) => ({ uploads: [...s.uploads, { key, name: file.name, size: file.size, percent: 0, state: reason ? "failed" : "queued", ...(reason && { reason }) }] }));
      if (reason) (results[at] as UploadResult).reason = reason;
      else queue.push({ file, key, at });
    });
    const worker = async () => {
      for (let next = queue.shift(); next; next = queue.shift()) {
        const { file, key, at } = next;
        this.patch(key, { state: "uploading" });
        try {
          const out = await this.api.uploadDocument(file, { onProgress: (percent) => this.patch(key, { percent }) });
          this.dismissUpload(key);
          results[at] = { file, document: out.document, duplicate: out.duplicate };
        } catch (err) {
          const reason = reasonOf(err);
          this.patch(key, { state: "failed", reason, percent: 0 });
          results[at] = { file, reason };
        }
      }
    };
    await Promise.all(Array.from({ length: Math.min(UPLOAD_CONCURRENCY, queue.length) }, worker));
    return results;
  }

  startEvents(onProgress: (e: DocumentProgressEvent | "resync") => void): void {
    if (this.events) return;
    this.events = this.open({
      url: "/api/documents/events",
      onOpen: () => {
        if (++this.opens > 1) onProgress("resync");
      },
      onFrame: (frame) => {
        if (frame.event !== "progress") return;
        try {
          const parsed = DocumentProgressEvent.safeParse(JSON.parse(frame.data));
          if (parsed.success) onProgress(parsed.data);
        } catch {
          // a frame that is not JSON is dropped
        }
      },
    });
  }
  stopEvents(): void {
    this.events?.close();
    this.events = undefined;
    this.opens = 0;
  }
}
```

`components/dm-upload-zone.ts`:

```ts
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { UPLOAD_EXTENSIONS, type UploadReason } from "../state/uploads";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import "./dm-icon";

const REASONS: Record<UploadReason, string> = {
  too_large: "Too large (50 MB limit).",
  unsupported: "File type not supported.",
  quota: "Storage limit reached.",
  network: "Connection lost.",
  server: "Something went wrong. Try again.",
};

export class DmUploadZone extends LitElement {
  static properties = { variant: { type: String }, dragging: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; }
      [data-zone] { display: grid; justify-items: center; gap: 6px; padding: 18px 16px; border: 1px dashed var(--dm-divider); border-radius: var(--dm-radius-lg); text-align: center; }
      :host([variant="empty"]) [data-zone] { padding: 48px 16px; }
      [data-zone][data-over] { border-color: var(--dm-accent); background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--dm-accent) 8%, transparent); }
      ul { list-style: none; margin: 10px 0 0; padding: 0; display: grid; gap: 6px; }
      li { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 10px; font-size: 13px; }
      .name { flex: 1; min-width: 0; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
      progress { width: 120px; }
      .err { color: var(--dm-err); }
    `,
  ];
  declare variant: "compact" | "empty";
  declare dragging: number;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.uploads]);
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.variant = "compact";
    this.dragging = 0;
  }
  private async send(files: File[]): Promise<void> {
    const uploads = this.ctx.services?.uploads;
    if (!uploads || files.length === 0) return;
    const results = await uploads.upload(files);
    this.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-uploaded", { detail: { results }, bubbles: true, composed: true }));
  }
  private onDrag = (e: Event) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    const dt = (e as DragEvent).dataTransfer;
    this.dragging = e.type === "dragleave" ? 0 : (dt?.items?.length ?? 0);
  };
  private onDrop = (e: Event) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    this.dragging = 0;
    const dt = (e as DragEvent).dataTransfer;
    const items = [...(dt?.items ?? [])] as DataTransferItem[];
    const folder = items.some((i) => i.kind === "file" && i.webkitGetAsEntry?.()?.isDirectory);
    if (folder || !dt) return; // a dropped folder is ignored
    void this.send([...dt.files]);
  };
  private onPick = (e: Event) => {
    const input = e.target as HTMLInputElement;
    const files = [...(input.files ?? [])];
    try { input.value = ""; } catch { /* a file input may refuse; the files are already read */ }
    void this.send(files);
  };
  render() {
    const uploads = this.ctx.services?.uploads;
    const items = uploads?.get().uploads ?? [];
    return html`
      <div data-zone ?data-over=${this.dragging > 0} @dragenter=${this.onDrag} @dragover=${this.onDrag} @dragleave=${this.onDrag} @drop=${this.onDrop}>
        <dm-icon name="upload-simple"></dm-icon>
        <p>${this.dragging > 0 ? `Release to upload ${this.dragging} ${this.dragging === 1 ? "file" : "files"}` : html`Drop files here or <button class="btn ghost" data-browse @click=${() => this.shadowRoot?.querySelector<HTMLInputElement>("input[type=file]")?.click()}>browse</button>`}</p>
        <p class="muted">PDF, DOCX, DOC, TXT, Markdown, Org, PNG, JPG, WEBP - up to 50 MB each</p>
        <input type="file" multiple hidden accept=${UPLOAD_EXTENSIONS.map((x) => `.${x}`).join(",")} @change=${this.onPick} />
      </div>
      ${items.length === 0 ? null : html`<ul>${items.map((u) => html`
        <li data-upload>
          <span class="name">${u.name}</span>
          ${u.state === "failed" ? html`<span class="err">${REASONS[u.reason ?? "server"]}</span><button class="btn ghost" data-dismiss aria-label=${`Dismiss ${u.name}`} @click=${() => uploads?.dismissUpload(u.key)}>Dismiss</button>`
            : u.state === "uploading" ? html`<span>Uploading ${u.percent}%</span><progress max="100" .value=${u.percent} aria-label=${`Upload progress for ${u.name}`}></progress>`
            : html`<span>Waiting</span>`}
        </li>`)}</ul>`}`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-upload-zone", DmUploadZone);
```

### Task 191: the library: `dm-documents-page` over the `library` and `library-empty` experiences

**Spec:** §9 item 43, §7 (Document table, Empty states). Decisions D11, D13, D14. Findings U15, U18. The page draws no data: a table, counts, a health card and a filter are the `library` experience; the page performs intents.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/surface/perform.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/confirm.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-documents-page.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/views.ts` (`documents` -> `dm-documents-page`), `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/surface/perform.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/confirm.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-documents-page.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `intentOf`, `Intent` from `@docmind/contracts` (Task 186d: the one action vocabulary every surface imports; the web never imports `@docmind/experiences`).
- Produces: `performIntent(intent, ctx)` (`surface/perform.ts`): the web's execution of intents: `ctx = { api, router, toasts, uploads?, session, confirm }`. In this task it handles `document.open` (navigate to the document), `document.ask` (`createChat({scope: {documentIds: [id]}})` then navigate to the chat), `document.retry` (`retryDocument`, then `session.invalidate()` and a toast "Retrying."), `document.delete` (after `confirm("Delete this document?", ...)`: `deleteDocument`, `session.invalidate()`, toast "Document deleted." and a toast on failure with a fixed sentence), and `documents.filter` (`session.setParams({ status }, "documents")`). Other intents are handled by later tasks (the function returns `false` for an intent it does not know). `confirmDialog(heading, body, confirmLabel, danger?): Promise<boolean>` (`components/confirm.ts`) builds a `<dm-dialog>` on `document.body`, resolves on a button or Escape, and removes itself.
- `<dm-documents-page>` (route `documents`): a header (`<h1>Documents</h1>`, the compact `<dm-upload-zone>`), then `<dm-view name="library">`, swapped for `<dm-view name="library-empty">` plus the `empty` zone when the library has no documents (`doc-total` is ready and its value is 0), and swapped back when an upload completes. It starts the progress events when it connects (`uploads.startEvents`) and stops them when it leaves; each event invalidates the `documents`, `doc-total`, `doc-ready` and `ingest` handles, at most once per 400 ms (an injectable `schedule`); a `resync` does the same at once; a completed upload (`dm-uploaded`) invalidates too.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import { performIntent } from "./perform";

const A = "0b4f7d52-6c53-4c6e-9d49-2b0e7a3f9a10";
function ctx(over: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  const api = {
    createChat: vi.fn(async () => ({ id: "c1" })), retryDocument: vi.fn(async () => ({})), deleteDocument: vi.fn(async () => {}), ...over,
  };
  const router = { navigate: vi.fn() };
  const toasts = { push: vi.fn() };
  const session = { invalidate: vi.fn(), setParams: vi.fn() };
  const confirm = vi.fn(async () => true);
  return { c: { api, router, toasts, session, confirm } as never, api, router, toasts, session, confirm };
}

describe("performIntent: documents", () => {
  it("opens a document", async () => {
    const t = ctx();
    expect(await performIntent({ type: "document.open", id: A }, t.c)).toBe(true);
    expect(t.router.navigate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ name: "document", id: A });
  });
  it("starts a chat scoped to the document and opens it", async () => {
    const t = ctx();
    await performIntent({ type: "document.ask", id: A }, t.c);
    expect(t.api.createChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ scope: { documentIds: [A] } });
    expect(t.router.navigate).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ name: "chat", chatId: "c1" });
  });
  it("retries, refreshes the view and says so; a failure is a fixed sentence", async () => {
    const t = ctx();
    await performIntent({ type: "document.retry", id: A }, t.c);
    expect(t.session.invalidate).toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(t.toasts.push).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ kind: "ok", text: "Retrying." }));
    const bad = ctx({ retryDocument: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(409, "not_failed"); }) });
    await performIntent({ type: "document.retry", id: A }, bad.c);
    expect(bad.toasts.push).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ kind: "err", text: "That document is not in a failed state." }));
  });
  it("deletes only after the user confirms, and treats a missing document as deleted", async () => {
    const no = ctx();
    no.confirm.mockResolvedValueOnce(false);
    await performIntent({ type: "document.delete", id: A }, no.c);
    expect(no.api.deleteDocument).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    const yes = ctx();
    await performIntent({ type: "document.delete", id: A }, yes.c);
    expect(yes.confirm).toHaveBeenCalledWith("Delete this document?", expect.any(String), "Delete", true);
    expect(yes.api.deleteDocument).toHaveBeenCalledWith(A);
    expect(yes.session.invalidate).toHaveBeenCalled();
    const gone = ctx({ deleteDocument: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(404, "not_found"); }) });
    await performIntent({ type: "document.delete", id: A }, gone.c);
    expect(gone.toasts.push).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ kind: "ok" }));
  });
  it("filters by status through the table's parameter, and leaves an intent it does not know", async () => {
    const t = ctx();
    await performIntent({ type: "documents.filter", status: "failed" }, t.c);
    expect(t.session.setParams).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ status: "failed" }, "documents");
    expect(await performIntent({ type: "chat.older" }, t.c)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { confirmDialog } from "./confirm";

const buttons = () => [...document.querySelectorAll("dm-dialog button")] as HTMLButtonElement[];
describe("confirmDialog", () => {
  it("resolves true on the confirm button and removes itself", async () => {
    const p = confirmDialog("Delete this document?", "It cannot be undone.", "Delete", true);
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    expect(document.querySelector("dm-dialog")?.getAttribute("heading")).toBe("Delete this document?");
    buttons().find((b) => b.textContent === "Delete")?.click();
    expect(await p).toBe(true);
    expect(document.querySelector("dm-dialog")).toBeNull();
  });
  it("resolves false on Cancel and on dm-close (Escape)", async () => {
    const a = confirmDialog("Sure?", "x", "Yes");
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    buttons().find((b) => b.textContent === "Cancel")?.click();
    expect(await a).toBe(false);
    const b = confirmDialog("Sure?", "x", "Yes");
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
    document.querySelector("dm-dialog")?.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-close"));
    expect(await b).toBe(false);
    expect(document.querySelector("dm-dialog")).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

```ts
import type { DocumentDto } from "@docmind/contracts";

import { fakeUi } from "../testing/fakeUi";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import { UploadsStore } from "../state/uploads";
import "./dm-documents-page";

const id = (n: number) => `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`;
const doc = (n: number, over: Partial<DocumentDto> = {}): DocumentDto => ({
  id: id(n), title: `Title ${n}`, filename: `f${n}.pdf`, mime: "application/pdf", sizeBytes: 100, status: "ready", error: null, stats: null, summary: null,
  createdAt: "2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z", ...over,
});
type Page = HTMLElement & { schedule: (fn: () => void) => void; updateComplete: Promise<unknown> };

async function setup(items: DocumentDto[]) {
  const live = { items };
  const ui = fakeUi({
    documents: async () => ({ items: live.items, truncated: false }), document: async () => null, content: async () => null, chats: async () => [],
  });
  const sse: { onFrame?: (f: { event: string; data: string; id: undefined }) => void } = {};
  const open = vi.fn((o: { onFrame: never }) => { sse.onFrame = o.onFrame; return { close: vi.fn() }; });
  const api = { ...ui.api, deleteDocument: vi.fn(async () => { live.items = live.items.filter((d) => d.id !== id(1)); }), retryDocument: vi.fn(async () => doc(2)), createChat: vi.fn(async () => ({ id: "c1" })), uploadDocument: vi.fn() };
  const uploads = new UploadsStore(api as never, open as never);
  const services = createServices({ api: api as never, uploads, open: open as never });
  const page = await mountWith<Page>(services, "dm-documents-page", { schedule: (fn: () => void) => fn() });
  return { page, services, api, live, sse, ui };
}
const view = (p: Element) => p.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-view") as HTMLElement & { session?: { statusOf(h: string): string | undefined; valueOf(h: string): any } };
const table = (p: Element) => view(p).shadowRoot?.querySelector("lu-table") as HTMLElement;

describe("dm-documents-page", () => {
  it("draws the library through loom-ui: counts, health, the filter and a table with a named button per row action", async () => {
    const t = await setup([doc(1), doc(2, { status: "failed", error: "Could not read this file." })]);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(table(t.page)?.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Title 1"));
    const labels = [...(table(t.page).shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll("td.row-actions button") ?? [])].map((b) => b.getAttribute("aria-label"));
    expect(labels).toEqual(["Open: Title 1", "Ask: Title 1", "Delete: Title 1", "Open: Title 2", "Retry: Title 2", "Delete: Title 2"]);
    expect(t.page.shadowRoot?.querySelector("h1")?.textContent).toBe("Documents");
  });

  it("shows the empty state, with the big drop zone, when the library has nothing", async () => {
    const t = await setup([]);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(view(t.page).getAttribute("name")).toBe("library-empty"));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(view(t.page).shadowRoot?.querySelector("lu-note")?.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("No documents yet"));
    expect(t.page.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-upload-zone[variant=empty]")).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("opens a document and starts a chat about it from the row buttons", async () => {
    const t = await setup([doc(1)]);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(table(t.page)?.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Title 1"));
    const click = (label: string) => (table(t.page).shadowRoot?.querySelector(`td.row-actions button[aria-label='${label}']`) as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    click("Open: Title 1");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "document", id: id(1) }));
    click("Ask: Title 1");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.api.createChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ scope: { documentIds: [id(1)] } }));
  });

  it("deletes after a confirmation and the table follows", async () => {
    const t = await setup([doc(1), doc(2)]);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(table(t.page)?.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Title 1"));
    (table(t.page).shadowRoot?.querySelector("td.row-actions button[aria-label='Delete: Title 1']") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(document.querySelector("dm-dialog")).not.toBeNull());
    ([...document.querySelectorAll("dm-dialog button")].find((b) => b.textContent === "Delete") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(table(t.page).shadowRoot?.textContent).not.toContain("Title 1"));
    expect(t.api.deleteDocument).toHaveBeenCalledWith(id(1));
  });

  it("refreshes when the progress stream says a document moved, and again after a reconnect", async () => {
    const t = await setup([doc(1, { status: "parsing" })]);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(table(t.page)?.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Parsing"));
    t.live.items = [doc(1)];
    t.sse.onFrame?.({ event: "progress", data: JSON.stringify({ documentId: id(1), step: "finalize", status: "ready" }), id: undefined });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(table(t.page).shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Ready"));
  });

  it("filters the table by status through the experience's own control", async () => {
    const t = await setup([doc(1), doc(2, { status: "failed", error: "x" })]);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(table(t.page)?.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Title 2"));
    const select = view(t.page).shadowRoot?.querySelector("lu-select-input")?.shadowRoot?.querySelector("select") as HTMLSelectElement;
    select.value = "failed";
    select.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(table(t.page).shadowRoot?.textContent).not.toContain("Title 1"));
    expect(table(t.page).shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Title 2");
  });
});
```

`surface/perform.ts`:

```ts
import type { Intent } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { Api } from "../api/client";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import type { Router } from "../router";
import type { ToastStore } from "../state/toasts";
import type { ViewSession } from "../ui/session";

export interface PerformContext {
  api: Api;
  router: Router;
  toasts: ToastStore;
  session: Pick<ViewSession, "invalidate" | "setParams">;
  confirm(heading: string, body: string, confirmLabel: string, danger?: boolean): Promise<boolean>;
}

const err = (toasts: ToastStore, text: string) => toasts.push({ kind: "err", text });

/** The web's execution of the surface-neutral intents. Returns false for an intent another page handles. Texts are fixed sentences. */
export async function performIntent(intent: Intent, c: PerformContext): Promise<boolean> {
  switch (intent.type) {
    case "document.open":
      c.router.navigate({ name: "document", id: intent.id });
      return true;
    case "document.ask":
      try {
        const chat = await c.api.createChat({ scope: { documentIds: [intent.id] } });
        c.router.navigate({ name: "chat", chatId: chat.id });
      } catch {
        err(c.toasts, "Could not start a chat about that document.");
      }
      return true;
    case "document.retry":
      try {
        await c.api.retryDocument(intent.id);
        c.session.invalidate();
        c.toasts.push({ kind: "ok", text: "Retrying." });
      } catch (e) {
        err(c.toasts, e instanceof ApiError && e.code === "not_failed" ? "That document is not in a failed state." : "Could not retry that document.");
      }
      return true;
    case "document.delete": {
      if (!(await c.confirm("Delete this document?", "It is removed for good, and chats can no longer cite it.", "Delete", true))) return true;
      try {
        await c.api.deleteDocument(intent.id);
      } catch (e) {
        if (!(e instanceof ApiError && e.status === 404)) {
          err(c.toasts, "Could not delete that document.");
          return true;
        }
      }
      c.session.invalidate();
      c.toasts.push({ kind: "ok", text: "Document deleted." });
      return true;
    }
    case "documents.filter":
      c.session.setParams({ status: intent.status }, "documents");
      return true;
    default:
      return false;
  }
}
```

`components/confirm.ts`:

```ts
import "./dm-dialog";

/** A modal yes/no on `document.body`. Resolves false for Cancel and Escape; removes itself either way. */
export function confirmDialog(heading: string, body: string, confirmLabel: string, danger = false): Promise<boolean> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const dialog = document.createElement("dm-dialog") as HTMLElement & { open: boolean };
    dialog.setAttribute("heading", heading);
    dialog.open = true;
    const text = document.createElement("p");
    text.textContent = body;
    const cancel = document.createElement("button");
    cancel.slot = "actions";
    cancel.className = "btn secondary";
    cancel.textContent = "Cancel";
    const ok = document.createElement("button");
    ok.slot = "actions";
    ok.className = `btn ${danger ? "danger" : "primary"}`;
    ok.textContent = confirmLabel;
    const done = (yes: boolean) => {
      dialog.remove();
      resolve(yes);
    };
    cancel.addEventListener("click", () => done(false));
    ok.addEventListener("click", () => done(true));
    dialog.addEventListener("dm-close", () => done(false));
    dialog.append(text, cancel, ok);
    document.body.append(dialog);
  });
}
```

  (`dm-dialog` buttons use the page's global classes only if the dialog's styles style slotted buttons; if not, the executor adds `::slotted(.btn)` rules to the dialog from Task 184a; the tests read the text only.)

`components/dm-documents-page.ts`:

```ts
import { type DocumentProgressEvent, type Intent, intentOf } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { performIntent } from "../surface/perform";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import type { ActionHandler, ViewSession } from "../ui/session";
import { confirmDialog } from "./confirm";
import "./dm-upload-zone";
import "./dm-view";

const HANDLES = ["documents", "doc-total", "doc-ready", "ingest"] as const;

export class DmDocumentsPage extends LitElement {
  static properties = { empty: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; padding: 24px; max-width: 1100px; margin: 0 auto; }
      header { display: grid; gap: 12px; margin-bottom: 16px; }
      h1 { font-size: 24px; }
    `,
  ];
  declare empty: boolean;
  /** Test seam: how a refresh is delayed. */
  schedule: (fn: () => void, ms: number) => void = (fn, ms) => void setTimeout(fn, ms);
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.uploads]);
  private session: ViewSession | undefined;
  private unwatch: (() => void) | undefined;
  private pending = false;
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.empty = false;
  }

  connectedCallback(): void {
    super.connectedCallback();
    this.ctx.services?.uploads.startEvents((e) => this.onProgress(e));
  }
  updated(): void {
    // The services arrive through the context after the first connect.
    if (!this.started && this.ctx.services) {
      this.started = true;
      this.ctx.services.uploads.startEvents((e) => this.onProgress(e));
    }
  }
  private started = false;
  disconnectedCallback(): void {
    this.ctx.services?.uploads.stopEvents();
    this.unwatch?.();
    super.disconnectedCallback();
  }

  /** At most one refresh per 400 ms; a reconnect refreshes at once. */
  private onProgress(e: DocumentProgressEvent | "resync"): void {
    if (e === "resync") return this.refresh();
    if (this.pending) return;
    this.pending = true;
    this.schedule(() => {
      this.pending = false;
      this.refresh();
    }, 400);
  }
  private refresh(): void {
    if (this.empty) {
      // a document arrived while the empty state was showing: go back to the library
      this.empty = false;
      return;
    }
    for (const h of HANDLES) this.session?.invalidate(h);
  }

  private onReady = (e: Event) => {
    const session = (e as CustomEvent<{ session: ViewSession }>).detail.session;
    this.session = session;
    this.unwatch?.();
    if ((e.target as HTMLElement).getAttribute("name") !== "library") return;
    const check = () => {
      const total = session.valueOf("doc-total") as { value?: number } | undefined;
      if (session.statusOf("doc-total") === "ready" && total?.value === 0) this.empty = true;
    };
    this.unwatch = session.store.runtimeSubscribe(check);
    check();
  };

  private act: ActionHandler = async (a) => {
    const session = this.session;
    if (!session || !this.ctx.services) return { ok: false };
    const keys = (session.valueOf("documents") as { rowKeys?: string[] } | undefined)?.rowKeys;
    const intent: Intent | undefined = intentOf("library", a, { ...(keys && { rowKeys: keys }) });
    if (!intent) return { ok: false, reason: "refused" };
    const { api, router, toasts } = this.ctx.services;
    return { ok: await performIntent(intent, { api, router, toasts, session, confirm: confirmDialog }) };
  };

  render() {
    return html`
      <header>
        <h1>Documents</h1>
        <dm-upload-zone variant=${this.empty ? "empty" : "compact"} @dm-uploaded=${() => (this.empty ? ((this.empty = false), undefined) : this.refresh())}></dm-upload-zone>
      </header>
      <dm-view name=${this.empty ? "library-empty" : "library"} .onAct=${this.act} @view-ready=${this.onReady}></dm-view>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-documents-page", DmDocumentsPage);
```

  Clean-up while implementing (the draft above double-starts the events): keep only the `updated()` start guarded by `started`, and drop the `connectedCallback` start; `startEvents` is itself idempotent, so a double call is harmless, but one call site is clearer. `dm-view`'s `name` is a property: `name=${...}` binds the attribute and Lit reflects it into the property (declared `type: String`). The `view-ready` event is retargeted at the page level; `e.target` is the `dm-view`.

  `views.ts`: `documents: "dm-documents-page"`. `components/index.ts`: `import "./dm-documents-page";`.

Expected: PASS (perform 5, confirm 2, page 6). **Probe.** (a) `session.store.runtimeSubscribe` is the store's change hook; if absent in the installed version, subscribe with `session.split`'s controller or poll `valueOf` after each `invalidate` (a `queueMicrotask` chain in the test is not allowed: use the hook). (b) The row-action buttons exist only after the pin of Task 177b; if they are missing, the pin is behind. (c) The empty-state test relies on `doc-total` becoming `ready` with `value: 0`: a `Kpi` with value 0 is valid.
