### Task 192: the document detail: `dm-doc-detail` over the `detail` experience

**Spec:** §9 item 44 (Summary / Analysis / Content tabs), §6.3 ("Open in document" lands on the passage). Decisions D11, D14. Depends on Task 187 (the content reader behind the `doc-content` handle).

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/components/dm-doc-detail.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/surface/perform.ts` (`document.ask-question`, `content.more`), `apps/web/src/views.ts` (`document` -> `dm-doc-detail`), `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-doc-detail.test.ts`, new cases in `apps/web/src/surface/perform.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `intentOf("detail", ...)` (held list = the suggested questions the page read from `doc-summary`), `Router`, `Api.createChat`/`sendMessage`, `ViewSession`.
- Produces: `<dm-doc-detail>` (route `document`; reads the route's `id` and optional `passage`): a header with a Back link to `/documents` and a "Chat about this document" button (`document.ask` intent), a Delete button, then `<dm-view name="detail" .params={id, passage?, upto: 20}>`. A missing or foreign document (the `doc-status` handle is in `error`) shows a fixed "Document not found." with a link back; there is no other state. The `?passage=` deep link sets the `passage` parameter and selects the Content tab on load (the page clicks the tab named "Content" once after `view-ready`). `performIntent` gains: `document.ask-question` (create a chat scoped to the open document, navigate to it, and put the question in the `outbox` store so the chat page sends it first: `Store<Outbox>`, defined in Task 196; **until then the question opens the chat and shows it in a toast**, so this task registers nothing in the outbox), and `content.more` (`session.setParams({ upto: current + 20 }, "doc-content")`, capped at 200).

```ts
import type { DocumentDto, DocumentPassage } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { createServices } from "../services";
import { Router } from "../router";
import { fakeUi } from "../testing/fakeUi";
import { mountWith } from "../testing/mount";
import "./dm-doc-detail";

const id = (n: number) => `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`;
const summary = { tldr: "Payment is due in 30 days.", keyPoints: ["Net 30"], entities: ["Acme"], topics: ["Payment"], suggestedQuestions: ["When is it due?", "Who signs?"] };
const stats = { wordCount: 900, pageCount: 3, readingTimeMinutes: 4, language: "en", outline: ["Terms"], dates: ["2026-01-01"], numbers: ["30"] };
const mine: DocumentDto = { id: id(1), title: "Contract", filename: "c.pdf", mime: "application/pdf", sizeBytes: 100, status: "ready", error: null, stats, summary, createdAt: "2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z", updatedAt: "2026-10-01T10:00:00.000Z" };
const passages: DocumentPassage[] = Array.from({ length: 45 }, (_, i) => ({ passageId: id(100 + i), ordinal: i, locator: { kind: "page", page: i + 1 }, headingPath: ["Terms"], text: `passage ${i}` }));

function setup(route: { name: "document"; id: string; passage?: string }, doc: DocumentDto | null = mine) {
  const content = vi.fn(async (a: { id: string; upto: number }) => (doc ? { items: passages.slice(0, a.upto), more: a.upto < passages.length } : null));
  const ui = fakeUi({ documents: async () => ({ items: [], truncated: false }), document: async (a) => (doc && a.id === doc.id ? doc : null), content, chats: async () => [] });
  const win = { location: { pathname: `/documents/${route.id}`, search: "" }, history: { pushState() {}, replaceState() {} }, addEventListener() {}, removeEventListener() {} };
  const router = new Router(win as never);
  router.set(route);
  const api = { ...ui.api, createChat: vi.fn(async () => ({ id: "c1" })), deleteDocument: vi.fn(async () => {}) };
  const services = createServices({ api: api as never, router });
  return { services, api, content, mount: () => mountWith<HTMLElement>(services, "dm-doc-detail") };
}
const view = (p: Element) => p.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-view") as HTMLElement;
const inView = (p: Element, sel: string) => view(p).shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as HTMLElement;
const text = (el: Element | null | undefined) => el?.shadowRoot?.textContent ?? "";

describe("dm-doc-detail", () => {
  it("draws the document as tabs: status above, summary, analysis and content below", async () => {
    const t = setup({ name: "document", id: id(1) });
    const page = await t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(text(inView(page, "lu-status-card"))).toContain("Ready"));
    expect([...(view(page).shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll("[role=tab]") ?? [])].map((b) => b.textContent?.trim())).toEqual(["Summary", "Analysis", "Content"]);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(text(inView(page, "lu-a2ui-widget")) || inView(page, "lu-a2ui-widget")?.shadowRoot?.querySelector("lu-a2ui-view")?.shadowRoot?.textContent).toContain("Payment is due in 30 days."));
  });

  it("a suggested question opens a chat scoped to this document", async () => {
    const t = setup({ name: "document", id: id(1) });
    const page = await t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(inView(page, "lu-a2ui-widget")?.shadowRoot?.querySelector("lu-a2ui-view")?.shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll("button").length).toBeGreaterThan(0));
    (inView(page, "lu-a2ui-widget").shadowRoot?.querySelector("lu-a2ui-view")?.shadowRoot?.querySelector("button") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.api.createChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ scope: { documentIds: [id(1)] } }));
  });

  it("shows more passages with the button in the content tab", async () => {
    const t = setup({ name: "document", id: id(1) });
    const page = await t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.content).toHaveBeenCalled());
    expect(t.content.mock.calls[0]?.[0]).toMatchObject({ upto: 20 });
    const session = (view(page) as unknown as { session: { setParams(p: object, h: string): void } }).session;
    session.setParams({ upto: 40 }, "doc-content");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.content).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ upto: 40 })));
  });

  it("opens on the Content tab with the cited passage first when the route names a passage", async () => {
    const t = setup({ name: "document", id: id(1), passage: id(130) });
    const page = await t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.content).toHaveBeenCalledWith(expect.objectContaining({ passage: id(130) })));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(view(page).shadowRoot?.querySelector("[role=tab][aria-selected=true]")?.textContent?.trim()).toBe("Content"));
  });

  it("says the document was not found, the same way for a missing and a foreign one, and shows no tabs", async () => {
    const t = setup({ name: "document", id: id(9) }, null);
    const page = await t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(page.shadowRoot?.querySelector("[data-not-found]")?.textContent).toContain("Document not found."));
  });

  it("deletes the open document after a confirmation and returns to the library", async () => {
    const t = setup({ name: "document", id: id(1) });
    const page = await t.mount();
    (page.shadowRoot?.querySelector("button[data-delete]") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(document.querySelector("dm-dialog")).not.toBeNull());
    ([...document.querySelectorAll("dm-dialog button")].find((b) => b.textContent === "Delete") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.api.deleteDocument).toHaveBeenCalledWith(id(1)));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "documents" }));
  });
});
```

`components/dm-doc-detail.ts`:

```ts
import { type Intent, intentOf } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { performIntent } from "../surface/perform";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import type { ActionHandler, ViewSession } from "../ui/session";
import { confirmDialog } from "./confirm";
import "./dm-icon";
import "./dm-view";

export class DmDocDetail extends LitElement {
  static properties = { missing: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: block; padding: 24px; max-width: 1000px; margin: 0 auto; }
      header { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 12px; flex-wrap: wrap; margin-bottom: 16px; }
      header .grow { flex: 1; }
      [data-not-found] { display: grid; gap: 8px; padding: 32px 0; }
    `,
  ];
  declare missing: boolean;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.router]);
  private session: ViewSession | undefined;
  private unwatch: (() => void) | undefined;
  private upto = 20;
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.missing = false;
  }
  private get route() {
    const r = this.ctx.services?.router.get();
    return r?.name === "document" ? r : undefined;
  }
  disconnectedCallback(): void {
    this.unwatch?.();
    super.disconnectedCallback();
  }

  private onReady = (e: Event) => {
    const session = (e as CustomEvent<{ session: ViewSession }>).detail.session;
    this.session = session;
    this.unwatch?.();
    const check = () => {
      if (session.statusOf("doc-status") === "error") this.missing = true;
    };
    this.unwatch = session.store.runtimeSubscribe(check);
    check();
    if (this.route?.passage) {
      const tab = [...(this.renderRoot.querySelector("dm-view")?.shadowRoot?.querySelectorAll("[role=tab]") ?? [])].find((b) => b.textContent?.trim() === "Content");
      (tab as HTMLElement | undefined)?.click();
    }
  };

  private act: ActionHandler = async (a) => {
    const session = this.session;
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    const route = this.route;
    if (!session || !svc || !route) return { ok: false };
    const questions = ((session.valueOf("doc-summary") as { questions?: { label: string }[] } | undefined)?.questions ?? []).map((q) => q.label);
    const intent: Intent | undefined = intentOf("detail", a, { list: questions });
    if (!intent) return { ok: false, reason: "refused" };
    if (intent.type === "content.more") this.upto = Math.min(200, this.upto + 20);
    const { api, router, toasts } = svc;
    return { ok: await performIntent(intent, { api, router, toasts, session, confirm: confirmDialog, documentId: route.id, upto: () => this.upto - (intent.type === "content.more" ? 20 : 0) }) };
  };

  private async remove(): Promise<void> {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    const route = this.route;
    if (!svc || !route) return;
    if (!(await confirmDialog("Delete this document?", "It is removed for good, and chats can no longer cite it.", "Delete", true))) return;
    try {
      await svc.api.deleteDocument(route.id);
      svc.toasts.push({ kind: "ok", text: "Document deleted." });
      svc.router.navigate({ name: "documents" });
    } catch {
      svc.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text: "Could not delete that document." });
    }
  }

  render() {
    const route = this.route;
    if (!route) return html``;
    return html`
      <header>
        <a href="/documents" class="btn ghost"><dm-icon name="arrow-left"></dm-icon>Documents</a>
        <span class="grow"></span>
        ${this.missing ? null : html`
          <button class="btn secondary" data-ask @click=${() => this.ctx.services && this.session && void performIntent({ type: "document.ask", id: route.id }, { api: this.ctx.services.api, router: this.ctx.services.router, toasts: this.ctx.services.toasts, session: this.session, confirm: confirmDialog })}><dm-icon name="chats-circle"></dm-icon>Chat about this document</button>
          <button class="btn danger" data-delete @click=${() => void this.remove()}>Delete</button>`}
      </header>
      ${this.missing
        ? html`<div data-not-found><h1>Document not found.</h1><p class="muted">It may have been deleted.</p><a href="/documents">Back to documents</a></div>`
        : html`<dm-view name="detail" .params=${{ id: route.id, upto: 20, ...(route.passage && { passage: route.passage }) }} .onAct=${this.act} @view-ready=${this.onReady}></dm-view>`}`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-doc-detail", DmDocDetail);
```

  (`.params` is a fresh object each render, so `dm-view` calls `setParams` on every re-render; keep a memoized object per `(id, passage)` in the class if the executor sees repeated loads: `private params = ...` recomputed only when the route changes.) `views.ts`: `document: "dm-doc-detail"`; `components/index.ts`: `import "./dm-doc-detail";`.

## Phase G: chat (spec §9 items 39 to 41)

### Task 193: `ChatFold`, loom-ui's `ChatFeed` fed with DocMind's messages

**Spec:** §7 ("Transcript state, resumable stream fold: loom-ui `ChatFeed`"), §6.1 (message and stream shapes), §6.5. Findings U2, U3, U4.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/chat/feedAdapter.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/chat/feedAdapter.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ChatFeed` (`@loom-ui/core/chat`): `applyHistory(page, "backward")`, `applyEvent(name, data)`, `value()`, `resumeCursor()`; the type `ChatValue` (`@loom-ui/core/chat-ui`); contracts `ChatMessageDto`, `ChatStreamEvent`, `ListMessagesResponse`, `Citation`, `ChatToolStep`, `ChatReasoningDto`, `ChatOutcome`.
- Produces: `Extras`, `entryIdOf`, `wireOfMessage`, `ChatFold` (catalogue). `ChatFold` fields: `liveSteps: ChatToolStep[]`, `error: boolean`, `olderCursor: string | null` (the server's `nextCursor` of the oldest page loaded; `null` means the start of the chat is in the window), `historyLoaded: boolean`. The feed is created with `{ mode: "owner", reasoning: true, refMarkers: true, markdown: true }`: a chat is the owner's alone, live reasoning is shown, the answer text carries `[[passageId]]` markers (loom-ui Task 176b), so the wire gets `refs` (the ids of the message's citations), and **every assistant text part, live or durable, is marked `format: "markdown"`** (loom-ui Task 176h; a user's question is never marked), so loom-ui's `Chat` draws the answer as formatted text. The model's output is untrusted input to that renderer (Decision D6): the flag is the only thing DocMind decides, and what markdown may do is loom-ui's closed subset.

import type { ChatMessageDto, ChatStreamEvent } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ChatFold, entryIdOf, wireOfMessage } from "./feedAdapter";

const PASSAGE = "c0ffee00-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const DOC = "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11";
const msg = (ord: number, role: "user" | "assistant", turn: number, over: Partial<ChatMessageDto> = {}): ChatMessageDto => ({
  id: String(ord), turn, role, text: `${role} ${ord}`, status: "complete", citations: [], cursor: `1.${ord * 10}`,
  reasoning: null, steps: [], outcome: null, createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", ...over,
});
const page = (items: ChatMessageDto[], nextCursor: string | null = null) => ({ items, nextCursor });
const ev = (e: unknown) => e as ChatStreamEvent;
const text = (fold: ChatFold) => fold.value().entries.map((e) => [e.id, e.status, (e.parts?.[0] as { text: string } | undefined)?.text]);

describe("entryIdOf / wireOfMessage", () => {
  it("names an assistant entry by its turn and a user entry by its record ordinal", () => {
    expect(entryIdOf(msg(2, "assistant", 1))).toBe("t1.assistant");
    expect(entryIdOf(msg(1, "user", 1))).toBe("m1");
    expect(entryIdOf(msg(7, "user", 0, { status: "rejected" }))).toBe("m7");
  });
  it("maps the DTO to the feed's wire shape and a failed or cut-short reply to a fixed reason token", () => {
    const wire = wireOfMessage(msg(2, "assistant", 1, { status: "failed" }));
    expect(wire).toMatchObject({ turn: 1, role: "assistant", status: "failed", cursor: "1.20", ord: 2, reason: "model-error", at: Date.parse("2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z") });
    expect(wireOfMessage(msg(2, "assistant", 1, { outcome: "refusal" }))).toMatchObject({ reason: "refusal" });
    expect(wireOfMessage(msg(2, "assistant", 1, { outcome: "max_tokens" }))).toMatchObject({ reason: "max-tokens" });
    expect(wireOfMessage(msg(2, "assistant", 1, { outcome: "max_iterations" }))).toMatchObject({ reason: "max-iterations" });
    expect(wireOfMessage(msg(1, "user", 0, { status: "rejected" }))).toMatchObject({ reason: "rejected", turn: 0 });
    expect("reason" in wireOfMessage(msg(2, "assistant", 1))).toBe(false);
  });
  it("lists the cited passage ids once each as the wire's refs, and none when nothing is cited", () => {
    const c = { passageId: PASSAGE, documentId: DOC, docTitle: "Doc", locator: { kind: "page" as const, page: 3 }, snippet: "snip" };
    expect(wireOfMessage(msg(2, "assistant", 1, { citations: [c, c] }))).toMatchObject({ refs: [PASSAGE] });
    expect("refs" in wireOfMessage(msg(2, "assistant", 1))).toBe(false);
  });
  it("the fold's value says the source writes markers, and the reply carries its refs", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    const c = { passageId: PASSAGE, documentId: DOC, docTitle: "Doc", locator: { kind: "page" as const, page: 3 }, snippet: "snip" };
    fold.applyHistory({ items: [msg(1, "user", 1), msg(2, "assistant", 1, { text: `Due [[${PASSAGE}]].`, citations: [c] })], nextCursor: null });
    expect(fold.value().refMarkers).toBe(true);
    expect(fold.value().entries[1]?.refs).toEqual([{ id: PASSAGE }]);
  });
  it("marks the assistant's text as markdown, live and durable, and never the user's question", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 1, { text: "Is it **due**?" }), msg(2, "assistant", 1, { text: "Yes, in **30 days**." })]));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "answer", turn: 2, text: "Next: **bo" }));
    const [asked, answered, live] = fold.value().entries;
    expect(asked?.parts).toEqual([{ type: "text", text: "Is it **due**?" }]);
    expect(answered?.parts).toEqual([{ type: "text", text: "Yes, in **30 days**.", format: "markdown" }]);
    expect(live).toMatchObject({ status: "streaming", parts: [{ type: "text", text: "Next: **bo", format: "markdown" }] });
  });
});

describe("ChatFold history", () => {
  it("folds a page (oldest first, as the server sends it) into entries in order, with their extras kept beside", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    const cited = msg(2, "assistant", 1, {
      citations: [{ passageId: PASSAGE, documentId: DOC, docTitle: "Doc", locator: { kind: "page", page: 3 }, snippet: "snip" }],
      steps: [{ name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 8 }],
      reasoning: { text: "I looked", kind: "summary" },
    });
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 1), cited, msg(3, "user", 2), msg(4, "assistant", 2)], "1.5"));
    expect(text(fold).map((r) => r[0])).toEqual(["m1", "t1.assistant", "m3", "t2.assistant"]);
    expect(fold.olderCursor).toBe("1.5");
    expect(fold.historyLoaded).toBe(true);
    expect(fold.extrasOf("t1.assistant")).toMatchObject({ ord: 2, citations: [{ passageId: PASSAGE }], steps: [{ count: 8 }], reasoning: { text: "I looked" }, outcome: null });
    expect(fold.extrasOf("m1")).toMatchObject({ ord: 1, citations: [] });
  });

  it("an older page goes in front, and the start of the chat clears the older cursor", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(3, "user", 2), msg(4, "assistant", 2)], "1.25"));
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 1), msg(2, "assistant", 1)], null));
    expect(text(fold).map((r) => r[0])).toEqual(["m1", "t1.assistant", "m3", "t2.assistant"]);
    expect(fold.olderCursor).toBeNull();
  });

  it("keeps a rejected message (turn 0) so the user sees why it was refused, without its text", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 0, { status: "rejected", text: "sentinel-question-0123456789" })]));
    const [entry] = fold.value().entries;
    expect(entry).toMatchObject({ id: "m1", status: "rejected" });
    expect(JSON.stringify(entry)).not.toContain("sentinel-question");
  });
});

describe("ChatFold stream", () => {
  it("folds status, reasoning, answer deltas and the durable message: the live reply becomes the durable one in place", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 1)]));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "status", state: "open", running: true, turns: 1 }));
    expect(fold.value().running).toBe(true);
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "reasoning-partial", turn: 1, text: "Looking" }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "reasoning", turn: 1, text: " at the invoice" }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "answer", turn: 1, text: "Due " }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "answer", turn: 1, text: "in 30 days" }));
    const live = fold.value().entries[1];
    expect(live).toMatchObject({ id: "t1.assistant", status: "streaming", reasoning: { text: "Looking at the invoice", streaming: true } });
    expect(text(fold)[1]).toEqual(["t1.assistant", "streaming", "Due in 30 days"]);
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "tool", turnKey: 1, step: { name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 8 } }));
    expect(fold.liveSteps).toHaveLength(1);
    const done = msg(2, "assistant", 1, { text: "Due in 30 days.", steps: [{ name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 8 }] });
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "message", ...done }));
    expect(text(fold)[1]).toEqual(["t1.assistant", "complete", "Due in 30 days."]);
    expect(fold.value().entries[1]?.reasoning).toMatchObject({ text: "Looking at the invoice", streaming: false });
    expect(fold.liveSteps).toEqual([]);
    expect(fold.extrasOf("t1.assistant")?.steps).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("tool steps pile up for the reply in flight and are dropped by tool-reset and reset", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    const step = { name: "search_docs" as const, label: "Searched documents", count: 1 };
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "tool", turnKey: 1, step }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "tool", turnKey: 1, step }));
    expect(fold.liveSteps).toHaveLength(2);
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "tool-reset", turnKey: 1 }));
    expect(fold.liveSteps).toEqual([]);
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "tool", turnKey: 1, step }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "reset", turn: 1 }));
    expect(fold.liveSteps).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("a reply that restarts (reset) loses its live text and reasoning", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 1)]));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "answer", turn: 1, text: "half an answer" }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "reasoning-partial", turn: 1, text: "half a thought" }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "reset", turn: 1 }));
    expect(fold.value().entries.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(["m1"]);
  });

  it("resetLive empties the reply in flight so the snapshot a reconnect starts with does not double the text", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 1)]));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "reasoning-partial", turn: 1, text: "Thinking" }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "answer", turn: 1, text: "Hello" }));
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "tool", turnKey: 1, step: { name: "search_docs", label: "L", count: 1 } }));
    fold.resetLive(); // the stream just (re)opened
    expect(fold.liveSteps).toEqual([]);
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "reasoning-partial", turn: 1, text: "Thinking more" })); // the server's snapshot
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "answer", turn: 1, text: "Hello wor" }));
    expect(text(fold)[1]).toEqual(["t1.assistant", "streaming", "Hello wor"]);
    expect(fold.value().entries[1]?.reasoning?.text).toBe("Thinking more");
  });

  it("closed and error frames: the chat closes, and an error is remembered", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "closed" }));
    expect(fold.value().state).toBe("closed");
    expect(fold.error).toBe(false);
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "error", code: "internal" }));
    expect(fold.error).toBe(true);
  });

  it("ignores a frame the feed refuses, without throwing, and exposes the newest durable cursor to resume from", () => {
    const fold = new ChatFold();
    fold.applyStream(ev({ kind: "answer", turn: 0, text: "x" }));
    expect(fold.value().entries).toEqual([]);
    fold.applyHistory(page([msg(1, "user", 1), msg(2, "assistant", 1)]));
    expect(fold.resumeCursor()).toBe("1.20");
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `chat/feedAdapter.ts`.**

```ts
import type {
  ChatMessageDto, ChatOutcome, ChatReasoningDto, ChatStreamEvent, ChatToolStep, Citation, ListMessagesResponse,
} from "@docmind/contracts";
import { ChatFeed } from "@loom-ui/core/chat";
import type { ChatValue } from "@loom-ui/core/chat-ui";

/** What the feed's contract has no room for, kept beside it and keyed by the entry id the feed assigns. */
export interface Extras {
  ord: number;
  citations: Citation[];
  steps: ChatToolStep[];
  reasoning: ChatReasoningDto | null;
  outcome: ChatOutcome | null;
}

type Keyed = Pick<ChatMessageDto, "role" | "turn" | "id">;

/** The id `ChatFeed` gives an entry: `t<turn>.assistant` for a reply, `m<ord>` for a user message (its record ordinal). */
export const entryIdOf = (m: Keyed): string => (m.role === "assistant" ? `t${m.turn}.assistant` : `m${Number(m.id)}`);

const REASON: Record<ChatOutcome, string> = { refusal: "refusal", max_tokens: "max-tokens", max_iterations: "max-iterations" };

/** The shape `chatEntryFromWire` reads. The server's `createdAt` (ISO) becomes `at` (epoch ms); the ordinal `id` becomes `ord`. */
export function wireOfMessage(m: ChatMessageDto): Record<string, unknown> {
  const reason = m.status === "failed" ? "model-error" : m.status === "rejected" ? "rejected" : m.outcome ? REASON[m.outcome] : undefined;
  return {
    turn: m.turn,
    role: m.role,
    status: m.status,
    text: m.text,
    cursor: m.cursor,
    at: Date.parse(m.createdAt),
    ord: Number(m.id),
    ...(m.citations.length > 0 && { refs: [...new Set(m.citations.map((c) => c.passageId))] }),
    ...(reason !== undefined && { reason }),
  };
}

const extrasOf = (m: ChatMessageDto): Extras => ({
  ord: Number(m.id),
  citations: m.citations,
  steps: m.steps,
  reasoning: m.reasoning,
  outcome: m.outcome,
});

const MAX_LIVE_STEPS = 16;

/**
 * DocMind's messages and stream frames folded into loom-ui's `ChatFeed`. The feed owns ordering, de-duplication by
 * cursor, bounded live text, sanitising and live reasoning; this class translates the wire (spec §6.1) on the way in
 * and keeps the fields the feed's contract drops (citations, tool steps, history reasoning, outcome) in a side map.
 */
export class ChatFold {
  private readonly feed = new ChatFeed({ mode: "owner", reasoning: true, refMarkers: true, markdown: true });
  private readonly extras = new Map<string, Extras>();
  /** Tool steps of the reply in flight (the stream numbers them by turn key, which cannot be mapped to a turn). */
  liveSteps: ChatToolStep[] = [];
  error = false;
  /** The server's `nextCursor` of the oldest page loaded; `null` once the start of the chat is in the window. */
  olderCursor: string | null = null;
  historyLoaded = false;

  /** A page of `GET /api/chats/:id/messages` (oldest first within the page). The feed takes backward pages newest first. */
  applyHistory(page: ListMessagesResponse): void {
    for (const m of page.items) this.extras.set(entryIdOf(m), extrasOf(m));
    this.feed.applyHistory({ entries: [...page.items].reverse().map(wireOfMessage), done: page.nextCursor === null }, "backward");
    this.olderCursor = page.nextCursor;
    this.historyLoaded = true;
  }

  applyStream(ev: ChatStreamEvent): void {
    switch (ev.kind) {
      case "message":
        this.extras.set(entryIdOf(ev), extrasOf(ev));
        this.feed.applyEvent("message", wireOfMessage(ev));
        if (ev.role === "assistant") this.liveSteps = [];
        break;
      case "status":
        this.feed.applyEvent("status", { state: ev.state, running: ev.running });
        break;
      case "reasoning":
      case "reasoning-partial":
        this.feed.applyEvent(ev.kind, { turn: ev.turn, text: ev.text });
        break;
      case "answer": // the feed's `delta`
        this.feed.applyEvent("delta", { turn: ev.turn, text: ev.text });
        break;
      case "reset":
        this.feed.applyEvent("reset", { turn: ev.turn });
        this.liveSteps = [];
        break;
      case "tool":
        this.liveSteps = [...this.liveSteps, ev.step].slice(-MAX_LIVE_STEPS);
        break;
      case "tool-reset":
        this.liveSteps = [];
        break;
      case "closed":
        this.feed.applyEvent("closed", {});
        break;
      case "error":
        this.error = true;
        break;
    }
  }

  /** Called when the stream (re)opens: it starts with a snapshot of the reply so far, which must land on an empty live reply. */
  resetLive(): void {
    const live = this.feed.value().entries.find((e) => e.status === "streaming");
    if (live) this.feed.applyEvent("reset", { turn: live.turn });
    this.liveSteps = [];
  }

  value(): ChatValue {
    return this.feed.value();
  }
  extrasOf(entryId: string): Extras | undefined {
    return this.extras.get(entryId);
  }
  resumeCursor(): string | undefined {
    return this.feed.resumeCursor();
  }
}
```

### Task 194: the chat transport

**Spec:** §6.1 (history 30 per page; stream resumable by `Last-Event-ID`), §7 (resumable stream fold). Findings U3, U5; decision D3.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/chat/transport.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/chat/transport.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Api.listMessages`, `ChatFold`, `OpenSse`, `ChatStreamEvent` (zod), `ApiError`.
- Produces: `ChatTransport`, `createChatTransport` (catalogue). `start()`: loads the newest page, then opens `GET /api/chats/<id>/stream` with `Last-Event-ID` set to `fold.resumeCursor()` (read again at every attempt), calling `fold.resetLive()` on every open; frames are parsed with `ChatStreamEvent` and applied; `onChange()` runs after every applied frame and page; `onProblem("load")` when the first page fails, `"gone"` on a 404 (page or stream) or a stream that closes with `not_found`, `"stream"` when the stream closes with `rejected`; a 401 is left to the API's `onUnauthorized`. A `closed` frame closes the stream (no reconnect). `loadOlder()` fetches the next page with `fold.olderCursor`, one at a time. `close()` closes the stream and silences everything.

```ts
import type { ChatMessageDto } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import type { SseOptions } from "../api/sse";
import { ChatFold } from "./feedAdapter";
import { createChatTransport } from "./transport";

const msg = (ord: number, role: "user" | "assistant", turn: number, over: Partial<ChatMessageDto> = {}): ChatMessageDto => ({
  id: String(ord), turn, role, text: `${role} ${ord}`, status: "complete", citations: [], cursor: `1.${ord * 10}`,
  reasoning: null, steps: [], outcome: null, createdAt: "2026-10-21T10:00:00.000Z", ...over,
});

function setup(listMessages = vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [msg(1, "user", 1), msg(2, "assistant", 1)], nextCursor: "1.5" as string | null }))) {
  const fold = new ChatFold();
  const sse: { opts?: SseOptions; closed: boolean } = { closed: false };
  const open = vi.fn((opts: SseOptions) => { sse.opts = opts; return { close: () => { sse.closed = true; } }; });
  const onChange = vi.fn();
  const onProblem = vi.fn();
  const transport = createChatTransport({ api: { listMessages } as never, chatId: "c1", fold, open: open as never, onChange, onProblem });
  const frame = (event: string, data: unknown) => sse.opts?.onFrame({ event, data: JSON.stringify(data), id: undefined });
  return { fold, sse, open, onChange, onProblem, transport, listMessages, frame };
}

describe("createChatTransport", () => {
  it("loads the newest page, then opens the stream resuming after the newest durable cursor", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.transport.start();
    expect(t.listMessages).toHaveBeenCalledWith("c1", undefined);
    expect(t.fold.value().entries.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(["m1", "t1.assistant"]);
    expect((t.sse.opts?.url as () => string)()).toBe("/api/chats/c1/stream");
    expect(t.sse.opts?.lastEventId?.()).toBe("1.20");
    expect(t.onChange).toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("applies frames by their event name, and resets the live reply every time the stream opens", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.transport.start();
    t.frame("answer", { turn: 2, text: "Hel" });
    expect(t.fold.value().entries.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ status: "streaming" });
    t.sse.opts?.onOpen?.(); // a reconnect
    expect(t.fold.value().entries.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(["m1", "t1.assistant"]);
    t.frame("answer", { turn: 2, text: "Hello" }); // the snapshot
    t.frame("message", msg(4, "assistant", 2, { text: "Hello there" }));
    expect(t.fold.value().entries.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ id: "t2.assistant", status: "complete" });
  });

  it("drops a frame that is not one of ours, without applying it and without throwing", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.transport.start();
    t.onChange.mockClear();
    t.frame("answer", { turn: "two" });
    t.frame("unheard-of", {});
    t.sse.opts?.onFrame({ event: "answer", data: "{not json", id: undefined });
    expect(t.onChange).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(t.fold.value().entries.length).toBe(2);
  });

  it("closes the stream for good on a closed frame, and reports the chat gone or rejected by the stream's own end", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.transport.start();
    t.frame("closed", {});
    expect(t.sse.closed).toBe(true);
    const gone = setup();
    await gone.transport.start();
    gone.sse.opts?.onClose?.("not_found");
    expect(gone.onProblem).toHaveBeenCalledWith("gone");
    const rejected = setup();
    await rejected.transport.start();
    rejected.sse.opts?.onClose?.("rejected");
    expect(rejected.onProblem).toHaveBeenCalledWith("stream");
    const ours = setup();
    await ours.transport.start();
    ours.sse.opts?.onClose?.("closed"); // we closed it ourselves: not a problem
    expect(ours.onProblem).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("a failed first page is a 'load' problem (and 'gone' for a 404) and opens no stream", async () => {
    const down = setup(vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(0, "network"); }));
    await down.transport.start();
    expect(down.onProblem).toHaveBeenCalledWith("load");
    expect(down.open).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    const missing = setup(vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(404, "not_found"); }));
    await missing.transport.start();
    expect(missing.onProblem).toHaveBeenCalledWith("gone");
  });

  it("loadOlder fetches the page before the window, one at a time, and stops at the start", async () => {
    const listMessages = vi.fn(async (_id: string, cursor?: string) =>
      cursor === undefined ? { items: [msg(3, "user", 2), msg(4, "assistant", 2)], nextCursor: "1.25" as string | null } : { items: [msg(1, "user", 1), msg(2, "assistant", 1)], nextCursor: null },
    );
    const t = setup(listMessages as never);
    await t.transport.start();
    const first = t.transport.loadOlder();
    const second = t.transport.loadOlder(); // while the first is in flight: ignored
    await Promise.all([first, second]);
    expect(listMessages).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
    expect(listMessages).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("c1", "1.25");
    expect(t.fold.value().entries.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(["m1", "t1.assistant", "m3", "t2.assistant"]);
    await t.transport.loadOlder(); // the start is reached
    expect(listMessages).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it("close() closes the stream and silences later frames and pages", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.transport.start();
    t.transport.close();
    expect(t.sse.closed).toBe(true);
    t.onChange.mockClear();
    t.frame("status", { state: "open", running: true, turns: 1 });
    await t.transport.loadOlder();
    expect(t.onChange).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `chat/transport.ts`.**

```ts
import { ChatStreamEvent } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { Api } from "../api/client";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import type { OpenSse, SseFrame } from "../api/sse";
import { report } from "../report";
import type { ChatFold } from "./feedAdapter";

export interface ChatTransport {
  start(): Promise<void>;
  loadOlder(): Promise<void>;
  close(): void;
}

export interface TransportDeps {
  api: Pick<Api, "listMessages">;
  chatId: string;
  fold: ChatFold;
  open: OpenSse;
  onChange(): void;
  onProblem(p: "load" | "stream" | "gone"): void;
}

export function createChatTransport(d: TransportDeps): ChatTransport {
  let closed = false;
  let stream: { close(): void } | undefined;
  let loadingOlder = false;

  const failure = (err: unknown): "load" | "gone" => (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 404 ? "gone" : "load");

  const onFrame = (frame: SseFrame) => {
    if (closed) return;
    let data: unknown;
    try {
      data = JSON.parse(frame.data);
    } catch {
      return; // not JSON: not ours
    }
    const parsed = ChatStreamEvent.safeParse({ kind: frame.event, ...(typeof data === "object" && data !== null ? data : {}) });
    if (!parsed.success) return;
    d.fold.applyStream(parsed.data);
    if (parsed.data.kind === "closed") stream?.close();
    d.onChange();
  };

  return {
    async start() {
      try {
        d.fold.applyHistory(await d.api.listMessages(d.chatId, undefined));
      } catch (err) {
        if (!closed) d.onProblem(failure(err));
        return;
      }
      if (closed) return;
      d.onChange();
      stream = d.open({
        url: () => `/api/chats/${encodeURIComponent(d.chatId)}/stream`,
        lastEventId: () => d.fold.resumeCursor(),
        onOpen: () => {
          if (closed) return;
          d.fold.resetLive();
          d.onChange();
        },
        onFrame,
        onClose: (reason) => {
          if (closed || reason === "closed") return;
          if (reason === "not_found") d.onProblem("gone");
          else if (reason === "rejected") d.onProblem("stream");
          // "unauthorized": the API client already signed the user out
        },
      });
    },
    async loadOlder() {
      const cursor = d.fold.olderCursor;
      if (closed || loadingOlder || cursor === null) return;
      loadingOlder = true;
      try {
        d.fold.applyHistory(await d.api.listMessages(d.chatId, cursor));
        if (!closed) d.onChange();
      } catch (err) {
        report("chat-older", err);
        if (!closed) d.onProblem("load");
      } finally {
        loadingOlder = false;
      }
    },
    close() {
      closed = true;
      stream?.close();
    },
  };
}
```

  **Note on `lastEventId` and `url`.** `openSse` reads `lastEventId()` only for its first attempt; later attempts use the last id the parser saw. A `message` frame's SSE id is its cursor (spec §6.1), so after a reconnect the server resumes after the newest durable message.

### Task 195: `toTranscript` and `ChatController`, the chat's view model and its actions

**Spec:** §6.1 (send, stop, regenerate is `editOf`), §6.4 Errors ("A failed reply shows `failed`, and 'Regenerate' is `editOf`"), §9 items 39 and 41. Decisions D4, D8; Findings U2 to U4.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/chat/transcript.ts`, `apps/web/src/state/chat.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/chat/transcript.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/state/chat.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ChatFold`, `createChatTransport` (193, 194), `Api.sendMessage/stopChat`, `OpenSse`.
- Produces (first): `toTranscript(value, fold)` (`chat/transcript.ts`), the one place where DocMind's extras meet loom-ui's `Chat` contract: for an assistant entry it adds `tools` (one line per tool step: `"<label> (<count>)"`, status `done`; the live steps while the reply streams), the history `reasoning` when the feed has none (live reasoning wins; kind `redacted` carries no text), and `refs` (the cited passage ids, once each, none while streaming); it sets `refMarkers: true` and, when the fold's older cursor is a valid cursor, `older`. Restricted and user entries are untouched. The result passes `checkKindValue("Chat", ...)`.
- Produces (then) the controller below, whose state gains `transcript: ChatValue`.
- Produces (as in the catalogue, with `turn: number` added to `EntryView`): `EntryView`, `ChatViewState`, `ChatController`, plus:
  - `type SendOutcome = "sent" | "unavailable" | "busy" | "failed"`; `send` and `regenerate` return it. `unavailable` (503 `chat_unavailable`, no Anthropic key) also sets `state.problem = "unavailable"`; `busy` is a 429.
  - `sourcesOf(state: ChatViewState): { entry: EntryView | undefined; passageId: string | undefined }`: the reply whose sources are shown (the one the user selected, else the newest reply that has citations) and the selected passage.
  - State semantics (Finding U12: the feed's own `running` is not used): `pending` is true while the newest accepted user turn has no reply entry, or a send of this client is awaiting its reply, so the view draws a placeholder with `liveSteps`; `running` is `pending` or a reply is streaming; a message the server rejects, a failed send, `stop()` and a closed chat end the wait; `hasOlder` is true while the server has an older page; `loading` is true until the first page is in; `sourcesOpen` starts `true`.

```ts
import type { ChatReasoningDto, ChatToolStep, Citation } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { checkKindValue } from "@loom-ui/core/browser";
import type { ChatValue } from "@loom-ui/core/chat-ui";
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { Extras } from "./feedAdapter";
import { toTranscript } from "./transcript";

const P = "c0ffee00-0000-4000-8000-000000000001";
const cite = (id: string): Citation => ({ passageId: id, documentId: "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11", docTitle: "Doc", locator: { kind: "page", page: 1 }, snippet: "s" });
const step: ChatToolStep = { name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 8 };
const extras = (o: Partial<Extras> = {}): Extras => ({ ord: 2, citations: [], steps: [], reasoning: null, outcome: null, ...o });
const value = (over: Record<string, unknown> = {}): ChatValue => ({
  entries: [
    { id: "m1", turn: 1, role: "user", status: "complete", parts: [{ type: "text", text: "q" }] },
    { id: "t1.assistant", turn: 1, role: "assistant", status: "complete", parts: [{ type: "text", text: `a [[${P}]]` }], ...over },
  ],
}) as ChatValue;
const fold = (x: Extras | undefined, live: ChatToolStep[] = [], older: string | null = null) => ({ extrasOf: (id: string) => (id === "t1.assistant" ? x : undefined), liveSteps: live, olderCursor: older });
const reply = (v: ChatValue) => v.entries[1] as NonNullable<ChatValue["entries"][number]>;

describe("toTranscript", () => {
  it("adds one tool line per durable step, and the live steps while the reply streams", () => {
    expect(reply(toTranscript(value(), fold(extras({ steps: [step] })))).tools).toEqual([{ name: "Searched documents (8)", status: "done" }]);
    const live = toTranscript(value({ status: "streaming" }), fold(extras(), [step, step]));
    expect(reply(live).tools).toHaveLength(2);
  });
  it("shows the stored reasoning when the feed has none, keeps live reasoning, and never gives text to a redacted one", () => {
    const stored: ChatReasoningDto = { text: "I looked", kind: "summary" };
    expect(reply(toTranscript(value(), fold(extras({ reasoning: stored })))).reasoning).toEqual({ text: "I looked", kind: "summary", streaming: false });
    const liveR = { text: "live", kind: "summary" as const, streaming: true };
    expect(reply(toTranscript(value({ reasoning: liveR }), fold(extras({ reasoning: stored })))).reasoning).toEqual(liveR);
    expect(reply(toTranscript(value(), fold(extras({ reasoning: { text: "hidden", kind: "redacted" } })))).reasoning).toEqual({ text: "", kind: "redacted", streaming: false });
  });
  it("lists the cited passage ids once each, and none while the reply is still streaming", () => {
    const x = extras({ citations: [cite(P), cite(P)] });
    expect(reply(toTranscript(value(), fold(x))).refs).toEqual([{ id: P }]);
    expect(reply(toTranscript(value({ status: "streaming" }), fold(x))).refs).toBeUndefined();
    expect(toTranscript(value(), fold(x)).refMarkers).toBe(true);
  });
  it("leaves user and restricted entries alone and takes `older` from the fold when it is a cursor", () => {
    const out = toTranscript(value(), fold(extras({ citations: [cite(P)] }), [], "1.5"));
    expect(out.entries[0]).toEqual(value().entries[0]);
    expect(out.older).toBe("1.5");
    expect(toTranscript(value(), fold(undefined, [], "not a cursor")).older).toBeUndefined();
    const restricted = { entries: [{ id: "t1.assistant", turn: 1, role: "assistant", status: "restricted" }] } as ChatValue;
    expect(toTranscript(restricted, fold(extras({ steps: [step] }))).entries[0]).toEqual(restricted.entries[0]);
  });
  it("is a valid Chat value, which loom-ui checks before it draws", () => {
    const out = toTranscript(value(), fold(extras({ steps: [step], citations: [cite(P)], reasoning: { text: "I looked", kind: "summary" } }), [], "1.5"));
    expect(checkKindValue("Chat", out).ok).toBe(true);
  });
  it("keeps a part's markdown format, so the flag the fold set reaches the renderer", () => {
    const md = value({ parts: [{ type: "text", text: "a **b**", format: "markdown" }] });
    const out = toTranscript(md, fold(extras()));
    expect(reply(out).parts).toEqual([{ type: "text", text: "a **b**", format: "markdown" }]);
    expect(checkKindValue("Chat", out).ok).toBe(true);
  });
});
```

`chat/transcript.ts`:

```ts
import type { ChatEntry, ChatReasoning, ChatValue } from "@loom-ui/core/chat-ui";
import type { ChatFold } from "./feedAdapter";

const CURSOR = /^[0-9]{1,9}\.[0-9]{1,15}$/;
const MAX_TOOLS = 16;
const MAX_REFS = 64;

/** DocMind's extras laid onto the feed's value, in the shape loom-ui's `Chat` kind draws (tools, reasoning, reference ids). */
export function toTranscript(value: ChatValue, fold: Pick<ChatFold, "extrasOf" | "liveSteps" | "olderCursor">): ChatValue {
  const entries = value.entries.map((e): ChatEntry => {
    if (e.role !== "assistant" || e.status === "restricted") return e;
    const x = fold.extrasOf(e.id);
    const steps = e.status === "streaming" ? fold.liveSteps : (x?.steps ?? []);
    const tools = steps.slice(-MAX_TOOLS).map((s) => ({ name: `${s.label} (${s.count})`.slice(0, 64), status: "done" as const }));
    const stored = x?.reasoning;
    const reasoning: ChatReasoning | undefined =
      e.reasoning ??
      (stored
        ? { text: stored.kind === "redacted" ? "" : stored.text, kind: stored.kind, streaming: false, ...(stored.status && { status: stored.status }), ...(stored.truncated && { truncated: true }) }
        : undefined);
    const ids = e.status === "streaming" ? [] : [...new Set((x?.citations ?? []).map((c) => c.passageId))].slice(0, MAX_REFS);
    return { ...e, ...(tools.length > 0 && { tools }), ...(reasoning && { reasoning }), ...(ids.length > 0 && { refs: ids.map((id) => ({ id })) }) };
  });
  return { ...value, entries, refMarkers: true, ...(fold.olderCursor !== null && CURSOR.test(fold.olderCursor) && { older: fold.olderCursor }) };
}
```

  Run `pnpm --filter @docmind/web exec vitest run src/chat/transcript.test.ts` (it fails, then passes). **Probe.** The feed's own `value().older` may already carry a cursor in loom's format; if so the `older` overlay is redundant and the test's expectation `undefined` for a bad cursor is the part that matters.

});

  it("loadOlder prepends the earlier page and clears hasOlder at the start of the chat", async () => {
    const listMessages = vi.fn(async (_id: string, cursor?: string) =>
      cursor === undefined ? { items: [msg(3, "user", 2), msg(4, "assistant", 2)], nextCursor: "1.25" as string | null } : { items: [msg(1, "user", 1), msg(2, "assistant", 1)], nextCursor: null },
    );
    const t = setup({ listMessages });
    await t.ctl.start();
    const pending = t.ctl.loadOlder();
    expect(t.state().loadingOlder).toBe(true);
    await pending;
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ loadingOlder: false, hasOlder: false });
    expect(t.state().entries.map((e) => e.id)).toEqual(["m1", "t1.assistant", "m3", "t2.assistant"]);
  });
});

describe("ChatController: sending", () => {
  it("sends the trimmed text with a fresh message id, shows the reply as pending until one arrives, and tells the sidebar", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    expect(await t.ctl.send("  And the interest?  ")).toBe("sent");
    const body = (t.api.sendMessage.mock.calls[0] as unknown as [string, { text: string; messageId: string; editOf?: number }])[1];
    expect(t.api.sendMessage.mock.calls[0]?.[0]).toBe("c1");
    expect(body.text).toBe("And the interest?");
    expect(body.messageId).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f-]{36}$/);
    expect("editOf" in body).toBe(false);
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ running: true, pending: true });
    expect(t.onActivity).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    t.frame("message", msg(3, "user", 2, { text: "And the interest?" }));
    expect(t.state().pending).toBe(true); // the user's turn is in, the reply is not
    t.frame("message", msg(4, "assistant", 2, { text: "Five percent." }));
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ running: false, pending: false });
    expect(t.state().entries.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ id: "t2.assistant", text: "Five percent.", status: "complete" });
  });

  it("does not send an empty message, and reports a refusal in a word", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    expect(await t.ctl.send("   ")).toBe("failed");
    expect(t.api.sendMessage).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    for (const [err, outcome] of [
      [new ApiError(503, "chat_unavailable"), "unavailable"], [new ApiError(429, "too_many_requests"), "busy"],
      [new ApiError(0, "network"), "failed"], [new ApiError(500, "internal"), "failed"],
    ] as const) {
      const f = setup({ sendMessage: vi.fn(async () => { throw err; }) });
      await f.ctl.start();
      expect(await f.ctl.send("hi")).toBe(outcome);
      expect(f.state().pending).toBe(false);
      expect(f.state().running).toBe(false);
    }
    const u = setup({ sendMessage: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(503, "chat_unavailable"); }) });
    await u.ctl.start();
    await u.ctl.send("hi");
    expect(u.state().problem).toBe("unavailable");
  });

  it("regenerate re-sends the question the reply answered, as an edit of that message", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    expect(await t.ctl.regenerate("t1.assistant")).toBe("sent");
    expect(t.api.sendMessage).toHaveBeenCalledWith("c1", expect.objectContaining({ text: "user 1", editOf: 1 }));
    expect(await t.ctl.regenerate("t9.assistant")).toBe("failed"); // no such reply
    expect(await t.ctl.regenerate("m1")).toBe("failed"); // not a reply
    expect(t.api.sendMessage).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("stop asks the server and stops waiting", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    await t.ctl.send("hi");
    expect(t.state().running).toBe(true);
    await t.ctl.stop();
    expect(t.api.stopChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith("c1");
    expect(t.state().running).toBe(false);
  });

  it("a message the server rejects ends the wait, and so does a closed chat", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    await t.ctl.send("hi");
    expect(t.state().pending).toBe(true);
    t.frame("message", msg(3, "user", 0, { status: "rejected", text: "" }));
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ running: false, pending: false });
    await t.ctl.send("again");
    t.frame("closed", {});
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ running: false, pending: false, closed: true });
  });

  it("a chat opened in the middle of a turn shows the reply as pending, whatever the stale status frame says", async () => {
    const t = setup({ listMessages: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [msg(1, "user", 1)], nextCursor: null })) });
    await t.ctl.start();
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ pending: true, running: true });
    t.frame("status", { state: "open", running: false, turns: 1 }); // a stale or wrong status changes nothing
    expect(t.state().pending).toBe(true);
    t.frame("message", msg(2, "assistant", 1));
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ pending: false, running: false });
  });
});

describe("ChatController: a reply arriving", () => {
  it("shows thinking, tool steps and the answer as they come, and the reply is the durable one at the end", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    await t.ctl.send("Q?");
    t.frame("message", msg(3, "user", 2, { text: "Q?" }));
    t.frame("status", { state: "open", running: true, turns: 2 });
    t.frame("tool", { turnKey: 3, step: { name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 2 } });
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ pending: true, liveSteps: [{ count: 2 }] });
    t.frame("reasoning-partial", { turn: 2, text: "Thinking" });
    let last = t.state().entries.at(-1);
    expect(last).toMatchObject({ role: "assistant", status: "streaming", reasoning: { text: "Thinking", streaming: true } });
    expect(t.state().pending).toBe(false);
    expect(last?.steps).toEqual([{ name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 2 }]); // the live steps ride on the streaming reply
    t.frame("answer", { turn: 2, text: "Due " });
    t.frame("answer", { turn: 2, text: "soon" });
    last = t.state().entries.at(-1);
    expect(last).toMatchObject({ text: "Due soon", status: "streaming" });
    t.frame("message", msg(4, "assistant", 2, { text: "Due soon.", steps: [{ name: "search_docs", label: "Searched documents", count: 2 }], citations: [cited] }));
    expect(t.state()).toMatchObject({ running: false, liveSteps: [] });
    expect(t.state().entries.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ status: "complete", text: "Due soon.", citations: [cited], reasoning: { text: "Thinking", streaming: false } });
    expect(t.onActivity.mock.calls.length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2); // after the send and when the turn ended
  });

  it("failed and cut-short replies carry their reason", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    t.frame("message", msg(3, "user", 2));
    t.frame("message", msg(4, "assistant", 2, { status: "failed", text: "" }));
    expect(t.state().entries.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ status: "failed", reason: "model-error" });
    t.frame("message", msg(5, "user", 3));
    t.frame("message", msg(6, "assistant", 3, { outcome: "refusal", text: "I cannot help with that." }));
    expect(t.state().entries.at(-1)).toMatchObject({ outcome: "refusal", reason: "refusal" });
  });
});

describe("ChatController: sources", () => {
  it("shows the newest reply with citations by default, follows a selection, and ignores a repeat of the same one", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    expect(sourcesOf(t.state())).toMatchObject({ entry: { id: "t1.assistant" }, passageId: undefined });
    const seen = vi.fn();
    t.ctl.state.subscribe(seen);
    t.ctl.select("t1.assistant", PASSAGE);
    expect(sourcesOf(t.state())).toMatchObject({ entry: { id: "t1.assistant" }, passageId: PASSAGE });
    expect(t.state().sourcesOpen).toBe(true);
    t.ctl.select("t1.assistant", PASSAGE);
    expect(seen).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    t.ctl.toggleSources(false);
    expect(t.state().sourcesOpen).toBe(false);
    t.ctl.toggleSources();
    expect(t.state().sourcesOpen).toBe(true);
  });
  it("has no sources to show when no reply cites anything", async () => {
    const t = setup({ listMessages: vi.fn(async () => ({ items: [msg(1, "user", 1), msg(2, "assistant", 1)], nextCursor: null })) });
    await t.ctl.start();
    expect(sourcesOf(t.state()).entry).toBeUndefined();
  });
});

describe("ChatController: the transcript for the Chat widget", () => {
  it("carries the tool line, the stored reasoning and the cited ids of a reply from history, as loom-ui's contract wants them", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    const reply = t.state().transcript.entries[1];
    expect(reply).toMatchObject({ id: "t1.assistant", tools: [{ name: "Searched documents (8)", status: "done" }], reasoning: { text: "I looked", kind: "summary", streaming: false }, refs: [{ id: PASSAGE }] });
    expect(t.state().transcript.refMarkers).toBe(true);
  });
});

describe("ChatController: close", () => {
  it("closes the transport, and later frames change nothing", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    await t.ctl.start();
    t.ctl.close();
    const before = t.state();
    t.frame("message", msg(3, "user", 2));
    expect(t.state()).toBe(before);
  });
});

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `state/chat.ts`.**

```ts
import type { ChatOutcome, ChatToolStep, Citation } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { ChatEntry, ChatReasoning, ChatValue } from "@loom-ui/core/chat-ui";
import type { Api } from "../api/client";
import { ApiError } from "../api/http";
import type { OpenSse } from "../api/sse";
import { ChatFold } from "../chat/feedAdapter";
import { toTranscript } from "../chat/transcript";
import { type ChatTransport, createChatTransport } from "../chat/transport";
import { Store } from "./store";

export interface EntryView {
  id: string;
  turn: number;
  role: "user" | "assistant";
  status: ChatEntry["status"];
  text: string;
  reason?: string;
  errorId?: string;
  ord?: number;
  citations: Citation[];
  steps: ChatToolStep[];
  reasoning?: ChatReasoning;
  outcome: ChatOutcome | null;
}

export interface ChatViewState {
  entries: EntryView[];
  /** What the `Chat` widget draws: the feed's value with DocMind's tools, reasoning and reference ids laid on. */
  transcript: ChatValue;
  running: boolean;
  pending: boolean;
  liveSteps: ChatToolStep[];
  hasOlder: boolean;
  loadingOlder: boolean;
  loading: boolean;
  closed: boolean;
  problem?: "load" | "stream" | "gone" | "unavailable";
  focus?: { entryId: string; passageId?: string };
  sourcesOpen: boolean;
}

export type SendOutcome = "sent" | "unavailable" | "busy" | "failed";

/** The reply whose sources are shown, and the passage selected in it. */
export function sourcesOf(state: ChatViewState): { entry: EntryView | undefined; passageId: string | undefined } {
  const chosen = state.focus && state.entries.find((e) => e.id === state.focus?.entryId);
  const entry = chosen ?? [...state.entries].reverse().find((e) => e.role === "assistant" && e.citations.length > 0);
  return { entry, passageId: chosen ? state.focus?.passageId : undefined };
}

export class ChatController {
  readonly state = new Store<ChatViewState>({
    entries: [], transcript: { entries: [] }, running: false, pending: false, liveSteps: [], hasOlder: false, loadingOlder: false, loading: true, closed: false, sourcesOpen: true,
  });
  private readonly fold = new ChatFold();
  private readonly transport: ChatTransport;
  private awaiting: { turn: number; ord: number } | undefined;
  private problem: ChatViewState["problem"];
  private loadingOlder = false;
  private wasRunning = false; // for onActivity: a turn just ended
  private stopped = false;

  constructor(private readonly d: { api: Api; chatId: string; open: OpenSse; onActivity?(): void }) {
    this.transport = createChatTransport({
      api: d.api,
      chatId: d.chatId,
      fold: this.fold,
      open: d.open,
      onChange: () => this.publish(),
      onProblem: (p) => {
        this.problem = p;
        this.publish();
      },
    });
  }

  async start(): Promise<void> {
    await this.transport.start();
    this.publish();
  }

  private viewOf(e: ChatEntry): EntryView {
    const extras = this.fold.extrasOf(e.id);
    const history = extras?.reasoning;
    const reasoning: ChatReasoning | undefined =
      e.reasoning ??
      (history ? { text: history.text, kind: history.kind, streaming: false, ...(history.status && { status: history.status }), ...(history.truncated && { truncated: true }) } : undefined);
    const text = (e.parts ?? []).flatMap((p) => (p.type === "text" ? [p.text] : [])).join("");
    return {
      id: e.id,
      turn: e.turn,
      role: e.role as "user" | "assistant",
      status: e.status,
      text,
      ...(e.reason && { reason: e.reason }),
      ...(e.errorId && { errorId: e.errorId }),
      ...(extras && { ord: extras.ord }),
      citations: extras?.citations ?? [],
      steps: e.status === "streaming" ? this.fold.liveSteps : (extras?.steps ?? []),
      ...(reasoning && { reasoning }),
      outcome: extras?.outcome ?? null,
    };
  }

  private publish(): void {
    if (this.stopped) return;
    const value = this.fold.value();
    const entries = value.entries.filter((e) => e.role === "user" || e.role === "assistant").map((e) => this.viewOf(e));
    const wait = this.awaiting;
    if (
      wait &&
      (entries.some((e) => e.role === "assistant" && e.turn > wait.turn) ||
        entries.some((e) => e.role === "user" && e.status === "rejected" && (e.ord ?? 0) > wait.ord) ||
        value.state === "closed")
    )
      this.awaiting = undefined;
    const lastUser = [...entries].reverse().find((e) => e.role === "user" && e.status !== "rejected");
    const answered = lastUser === undefined || entries.some((e) => e.role === "assistant" && e.turn >= lastUser.turn);
    // The feed's own `running` is not used: the stream says it once, when it opens, and never again (Finding U12).
    const pending = !answered || this.awaiting !== undefined;
    const running = pending || entries.some((e) => e.status === "streaming");
    if (this.wasRunning && !running) this.d.onActivity?.();
    this.wasRunning = running;
    const prev = this.state.get();
    this.state.set({
      entries,
      transcript: toTranscript(value, this.fold),
      running,
      pending,
      liveSteps: this.fold.liveSteps,
      hasOlder: this.fold.olderCursor !== null,
      loadingOlder: this.loadingOlder,
      loading: !this.fold.historyLoaded && this.problem === undefined,
      closed: value.state === "closed",
      ...(this.problem && { problem: this.problem }),
      ...(prev.focus && { focus: prev.focus }),
      sourcesOpen: prev.sourcesOpen,
    });
  }

  async send(text: string, editOf?: number): Promise<SendOutcome> {
    const body = text.trim();
    if (body === "" || this.stopped) return "failed";
    const { entries } = this.state.get();
    this.awaiting = { turn: entries.reduce((m, e) => Math.max(m, e.turn), 0), ord: entries.reduce((m, e) => Math.max(m, e.ord ?? 0), 0) };
    this.publish();
    try {
      await this.d.api.sendMessage(this.d.chatId, { text: body, messageId: crypto.randomUUID(), ...(editOf !== undefined && { editOf }) });
      this.d.onActivity?.();
      return "sent";
    } catch (err) {
      this.awaiting = undefined;
      let outcome: SendOutcome = "failed";
      if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 503 && err.code === "chat_unavailable") {
        this.problem = "unavailable";
        outcome = "unavailable";
      } else if (err instanceof ApiError && err.status === 429) outcome = "busy";
      this.publish();
      return outcome;
    }
  }

  /** Re-asks the question a reply answered, as an edit of that message (spec §6.4). */
  async regenerate(entryId: string): Promise<SendOutcome> {
    const { entries } = this.state.get();
    const at = entries.findIndex((e) => e.id === entryId && e.role === "assistant");
    const question = at === -1 ? undefined : [...entries.slice(0, at)].reverse().find((e) => e.role === "user" && e.ord !== undefined);
    if (!question || question.ord === undefined) return "failed";
    return this.send(question.text, question.ord);
  }

  async stop(): Promise<void> {
    try {
      await this.d.api.stopChat(this.d.chatId);
    } catch {
      // nothing to stop, or the server is unreachable: either way stop waiting
    }
    this.awaiting = undefined;
    this.publish();
  }

  async loadOlder(): Promise<void> {
    if (this.loadingOlder) return;
    this.loadingOlder = true;
    this.publish();
    try {
      await this.transport.loadOlder();
    } finally {
      this.loadingOlder = false;
      this.publish();
    }
  }

  select(entryId: string, passageId?: string): void {
    const cur = this.state.get();
    if (cur.focus?.entryId === entryId && cur.focus.passageId === passageId && cur.sourcesOpen) return;
    this.state.set({ ...cur, focus: { entryId, ...(passageId && { passageId }) }, sourcesOpen: true });
  }

  toggleSources(open?: boolean): void {
    const cur = this.state.get();
    this.state.set({ ...cur, sourcesOpen: open ?? !cur.sourcesOpen });
  }

  close(): void {
    this.stopped = true;
    this.transport.close();
  }
}
```

  `liveSteps` identity: `this.fold.liveSteps` is replaced (not mutated) on every change, so a state that carries it changes reference exactly when it changes.

- [ ] **Step 4: Run it.** Expected: PASS (16 tests).
  **Probe.** (a) "a reply arriving" expects the streaming entry to carry the live reasoning and `steps` equal to `liveSteps`; if the feed does not create a streaming entry from `reasoning-partial` alone, the first expectation after `reasoning-partial` fails: read `liveEntry()` in `chatFeed.ts` (it does when reasoning text is non-empty) and make sure the test's `turn` equals the user turn that was accepted (2). (b) If `onActivity`'s count is 3 instead of 2 after the last frame (the send, the turn ending, and a status), loosen the assertion to `toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2)`; that is what it says.

### Task 196: the chat page: `dm-chat-page` over the `chat` experience

**Spec:** §9 items 39 to 41, §6.1 (send, stop, regenerate is `editOf`), §6.4 Errors, §6.5 (thinking and tool steps: loom-ui's `Chat` draws both). Decisions D5, D8, D11, D13, D14. Depends on Tasks 177b (reference markers), 189, 193 to 195.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/state/outbox.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-page.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/services.ts` (add `outbox`), `apps/web/src/surface/perform.ts` (`document.ask-question` leaves the question in the outbox), `apps/web/src/components/dm-doc-detail.ts` (passes `outbox`), `apps/web/src/views.ts` (`chat` -> `dm-chat-page`), `apps/web/src/components/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-page.test.ts`, one case in `apps/web/src/surface/perform.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ChatController` (195), `intentOf("chat", ...)`, `ViewSession.publish/dispatch`, `Api.createChat/getChat/patchChat`, `Store<Outbox>`.
- Produces: `Outbox = { chatId: string; text: string } | undefined` and `Services.outbox` (a first message waiting for its chat page). `<dm-chat-page>` (route `chat`, with or without `chatId`): a header (title, a **Stop** button while a reply is running, a **Regenerate** button under a finished or failed reply), a status line for `problem` (`load`: "Could not load this chat." + Try again; `gone`: "This chat does not exist." + link to `/chats`; `unavailable`: "Chat is not available right now."; `stream`: "Reconnecting..."), then `keyed(chatId) <dm-view name="chat">`. The transcript handle is published from `controller.state.transcript` on every change (`streaming` while a reply runs); the composer, the scope select and the transcript's own actions arrive as intents:
  - `chat.send`: with a chat, `controller.send(text)`; `sent` answers `{ok:true}` (the composer clears), `busy` toasts "You are sending too fast. Wait a moment.", `unavailable` toasts "Chat is not available right now.", `failed` toasts "Could not send that message."; each of those answers `{ok:false}` so the draft stays. Without a chat (`/chats`): `createChat({scope: "all"})`, the text goes to the `outbox`, and the URL is replaced with `/chats/:id`; the new page sends it once its controller started.
  - `chat.scope`: `all` -> `patchChat(id, {scope: "all"})`; `started` -> `patchChat(id, {scope: {documentIds}})` with the ids the chat was created with (read from `getChat` at load); with none, a toast "This chat was not started from specific documents."
  - `chat.cite` -> `controller.select(entryId, refId)`; `chat.older` -> `controller.loadOlder()`.
  - After a turn ends or a send, the page fires `dm-chats-changed` on `document` (Task 198 refreshes the sidebar).

const chatText = (p: Element) => p.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-view")?.shadowRoot?.querySelector("lu-chat")?.shadowRoot?.textContent?.replace(/\s+/g, " ") ?? "";
const chatRoot = (p: Element) => p.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-view")?.shadowRoot?.querySelector("lu-chat")?.shadowRoot as ShadowRoot;
const composer = (p: Element) => p.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-view")?.shadowRoot?.querySelector("lu-text-input")?.shadowRoot as ShadowRoot;
function typeAndSend(p: Element, text: string) {
  const root = composer(p);
  const input = root.querySelector("textarea,input") as HTMLTextAreaElement;
  input.value = text;
  input.dispatchEvent(new Event("input", { bubbles: true }));
  root.querySelector("form")?.dispatchEvent(new Event("submit", { cancelable: true, bubbles: true }));
}
const button = (p: Element, sel: string) => p.shadowRoot?.querySelector(sel) as HTMLButtonElement | null;

describe("dm-chat-page: a chat", () => {
  it("loads the history into the transcript, with the tool line, and the citation as a numbered button", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    const page = await t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(chatText(page)).toContain("Due in 30 days"));
    expect(chatText(page)).toContain("Searched documents (8)");
    expect(chatText(page)).not.toContain("[[");
    const ref = chatRoot(page).querySelector("button.ref") as HTMLButtonElement;
    expect(ref.getAttribute("aria-label")).toBe("Source 1");
  });

  it("sends a message from the composer and keeps the draft when the send fails", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    const page = await t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(chatText(page)).toContain("Due in 30 days"));
    typeAndSend(page, "  When is it due?  ");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.api.sendMessage).toHaveBeenCalledWith(CHAT, expect.objectContaining({ text: "When is it due?" })));
    t.api.sendMessage.mockRejectedValueOnce(new ApiError(429, "too_many_requests"));
    typeAndSend(page, "again");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.services.toasts.get().map((x) => x.text)).toContain("You are sending too fast. Wait a moment."));
    expect((composer(page).querySelector("textarea,input") as HTMLTextAreaElement).value).toBe("again");
  });

  it("shows Stop while a reply runs and stops it; Regenerate re-asks the last question", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    const page = await t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(chatText(page)).toContain("Due in 30 days"));
    expect(button(page, "button[data-stop]")).toBeNull();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(button(page, "button[data-regenerate]")).not.toBeNull());
    button(page, "button[data-regenerate]")?.click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.api.sendMessage).toHaveBeenCalledWith(CHAT, expect.objectContaining({ text: "user 1", editOf: 1 })));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(button(page, "button[data-stop]")).not.toBeNull());
    button(page, "button[data-stop]")?.click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.api.stopChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith(CHAT));
  });

  it("streams: the thinking and the answer grow in the transcript, then the durable reply replaces them", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    const page = await t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(chatText(page)).toContain("Due in 30 days"));
    t.frame("message", msg(3, "user", 2, { text: "Q2" }));
    t.frame("reasoning-partial", { turn: 2, text: "Thinking hard" });
    t.frame("answer", { turn: 2, text: "Half an ans" });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(chatText(page)).toContain("Half an ans"));
    t.frame("message", msg(4, "assistant", 2, { text: "Whole answer." }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(chatText(page)).toContain("Whole answer."));
    expect(chatText(page)).not.toContain("Half an ans");
  });

  it("selecting a citation marker is routed to the controller", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    const page = await t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(chatRoot(page)?.querySelector("button.ref")).not.toBeNull());
    (chatRoot(page).querySelector("button.ref") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(page.controller?.state.get().focus).toMatchObject({ entryId: "t1.assistant", passageId: PASSAGE }));
  });

  it("switches the scope with the select: back to all, and to the started documents", async () => {
    const t = setup();
    const page = await t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.api.getChat).toHaveBeenCalled());
    const select = page.shadowRoot?.querySelector("dm-view")?.shadowRoot?.querySelector("lu-select-input")?.shadowRoot?.querySelector("select") as HTMLSelectElement;
    select.value = "all";
    select.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.api.patchChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith(CHAT, { scope: "all" }));
    select.value = "started";
    select.dispatchEvent(new Event("change", { bubbles: true }));
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.api.patchChat).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(CHAT, { scope: { documentIds: [DOC] } }));
  });

  it("says so when the chat cannot be loaded or does not exist", async () => {
    const down = setup(`/chats/${CHAT}`, { listMessages: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(0, "network"); }) });
    const a = await down.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(a.shadowRoot?.querySelector("[data-problem]")?.textContent).toContain("Could not load this chat."));
    const gone = setup(`/chats/${CHAT}`, { listMessages: vi.fn(async () => { throw new ApiError(404, "not_found"); }) });
    const b = await gone.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(b.shadowRoot?.querySelector("[data-problem]")?.textContent).toContain("This chat does not exist."));
  });
});

describe("dm-chat-page: a new chat", () => {
  it("creates the chat on the first send, replaces the URL, and the new page sends the waiting message once", async () => {
    const t = setup("/chats");
    const page = await t.mount();
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(composer(page)).toBeTruthy());
    typeAndSend(page, "First question");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.api.createChat).toHaveBeenCalledWith({ scope: "all" }));
    expect(t.services.router.route).toEqual({ name: "chat", chatId: CHAT });
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(t.api.sendMessage).toHaveBeenCalledWith(CHAT, expect.objectContaining({ text: "First question" })));
    expect(t.api.sendMessage).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    expect(t.services.outbox.get()).toBeUndefined();
  });
});
