`components/dm-chat-page.ts`:

```ts
import { type ChatScope, intentOf } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { keyed } from "lit/directives/keyed.js";
import { ChatController, type SendOutcome } from "../state/chat";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import type { ActionHandler, ViewSession } from "../ui/session";
import "./dm-icon";
import "./dm-view";

const SEND_TEXT: Record<Exclude<SendOutcome, "sent">, string> = {
  busy: "You are sending too fast. Wait a moment.",
  unavailable: "Chat is not available right now.",
  failed: "Could not send that message.",
};

export class DmChatPage extends LitElement {
  static properties = { tick: { state: true } };
  static styles = [
    baseStyles,
    css`
      :host { display: grid; grid-template-rows: auto auto 1fr; height: 100%; min-height: 0; padding: 16px 24px; max-width: 900px; margin: 0 auto; }
      header { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding-bottom: 8px; }
      header .grow { flex: 1; }
      h1 { font-size: 18px; }
      [data-problem] { display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px; padding: 8px 0; color: var(--dm-muted); }
      dm-view { min-height: 0; }
    `,
  ];
  declare tick: number;
  controller: ChatController | undefined;
  private session: ViewSession | undefined;
  private bound: string | undefined | null = null;
  private off: (() => void) | undefined;
  private startedIds: string[] | undefined;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.router]);
  constructor() {
    super();
    this.tick = 0;
  }
  private get chatId(): string | undefined {
    const r = this.ctx.services?.router.get();
    return r?.name === "chat" ? r.chatId : undefined;
  }

  protected updated(): void {
    const id = this.chatId;
    if (this.ctx.services && this.bound !== id) this.bind(id);
  }
  disconnectedCallback(): void {
    this.release();
    super.disconnectedCallback();
  }
  private release(): void {
    this.off?.();
    this.controller?.close();
    this.controller = undefined;
    this.session = undefined;
  }

  private bind(id: string | undefined): void {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!svc) return;
    this.release();
    this.bound = id;
    this.startedIds = undefined;
    if (id === undefined) return void this.requestUpdate();
    const controller = new ChatController({ api: svc.api, chatId: id, open: svc.open, onActivity: () => document.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent("dm-chats-changed")) });
    this.controller = controller;
    this.off = controller.state.subscribe(() => this.sync());
    void controller.start().then(() => {
      if (this.controller !== controller) return;
      const waiting = svc.outbox.get();
      if (waiting?.chatId === id) {
        svc.outbox.set(undefined);
        void controller.send(waiting.text);
      }
    });
    void svc.api.getChat(id).then((c) => {
      if (this.controller === controller && typeof c.scope === "object") this.startedIds = [...c.scope.documentIds];
    }, () => {});
    this.requestUpdate();
  }

  /** The page folds the transcript itself, so it publishes it into the experience's store. */
  private sync(): void {
    const st = this.controller?.state.get();
    if (st) this.session?.publish("transcript", st.transcript, "Chat", { streaming: st.running });
    this.requestUpdate();
  }
  private onReady = (e: Event) => {
    this.session = (e as CustomEvent<{ session: ViewSession }>).detail.session;
    this.sync();
  };

  private toast(text: string): void {
    this.ctx.services?.toasts.push({ kind: "err", text });
  }

  private async send(text: string) {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!svc) return { ok: false };
    if (this.controller === undefined) {
      try {
        const chat = await svc.api.createChat({ scope: "all" });
        svc.outbox.set({ chatId: chat.id, text });
        svc.router.navigate({ name: "chat", chatId: chat.id }, { replace: true });
        return { ok: true };
      } catch {
        this.toast("Could not start a chat.");
        return { ok: false };
      }
    }
    const out = await this.controller.send(text);
    if (out === "sent") return { ok: true };
    this.toast(SEND_TEXT[out]);
    return { ok: false };
  }

  private async scope(value: "all" | "started") {
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    const id = this.chatId;
    if (!svc || id === undefined) return { ok: false };
    let scope: ChatScope = "all";
    if (value === "started") {
      if (!this.startedIds) {
        this.toast("This chat was not started from specific documents.");
        return { ok: false };
      }
      scope = { documentIds: this.startedIds };
    }
    try {
      await svc.api.patchChat(id, { scope });
      return { ok: true };
    } catch {
      this.toast("Could not change where this chat searches.");
      return { ok: false };
    }
  }

  private act: ActionHandler = async (a) => {
    const intent = intentOf("chat", a);
    if (!intent) return { ok: false, reason: "refused" };
    switch (intent.type) {
      case "chat.send":
        return this.send(intent.text);
      case "chat.scope":
        return this.scope(intent.scope);
      case "chat.cite":
        this.controller?.select(intent.entryId, intent.refId);
        return { ok: true };
      case "chat.older":
        void this.controller?.loadOlder();
        return { ok: true };
      default:
        return { ok: false, reason: "refused" };
    }
  };

  private problemText(p: NonNullable<ReturnType<ChatController["state"]["get"]>["problem"]>): string {
    return p === "load" ? "Could not load this chat." : p === "gone" ? "This chat does not exist." : p === "unavailable" ? "Chat is not available right now." : "Reconnecting...";
  }

  render() {
    const st = this.controller?.state.get();
    const last = st?.entries.at(-1);
    const canRegenerate = st !== undefined && !st.running && last?.role === "assistant" && last.status !== "streaming";
    return html`
      <header>
        <h1>${this.chatId ? "Chat" : "New chat"}</h1>
        <span class="grow"></span>
        ${st?.running ? html`<button class="btn secondary" data-stop @click=${() => void this.controller?.stop()}><dm-icon name="stop"></dm-icon>Stop</button>` : null}
        ${canRegenerate ? html`<button class="btn secondary" data-regenerate @click=${async () => { const out = last && (await this.controller?.regenerate(last.id)); if (out && out !== "sent") this.toast(SEND_TEXT[out]); }}><dm-icon name="arrow-clockwise"></dm-icon>Regenerate</button>` : null}
      </header>
      <div>${st?.problem ? html`<div data-problem role=${st.problem === "stream" ? "status" : "alert"}>${this.problemText(st.problem)}${st.problem === "load" ? html`<button class="btn ghost" @click=${() => void this.controller?.start()}>Try again</button>` : st.problem === "gone" ? html`<a href="/chats">Start a new chat</a>` : null}</div>` : null}</div>
      ${keyed(this.chatId ?? "new", html`<dm-view name="chat" .onAct=${this.act} @view-ready=${this.onReady}></dm-view>`)}`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-chat-page", DmChatPage);
```

  `views.ts`: `chat: "dm-chat-page"`; `components/index.ts`: `import "./dm-chat-page";`.

### Task 197: the sources panel

**Spec:** §9 item 40, §6.3 (citations, "Open in document"), Decision D8, open question 7. The panel is the `sources` experience (an `A2UIView`) with a value the page publishes from the citations it already holds; the shaping of that value is shared code in `@docmind/contracts`, so another surface draws the same panel from the same function.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/contracts/src/sources.ts`, `packages/contracts/src/sources.test.ts`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-page.ts`, `apps/web/src/surface/perform.ts` (`source.open`), `packages/contracts/src/index.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/dm-chat-page.test.ts` (new cases), `apps/web/src/surface/perform.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces (contracts): `SourcesData = { note: string; sources: { title: string; where: string; snippet: string; passageId: string; documentId: string }[] }`; `sourcesValue(citations: readonly Citation[], selected?: string): SourcesData`: the selected passage first with `where` prefixed `"Selected - "`; `note` is `"Sources appear when the answer is complete."` for none, else `"<n> source(s) for this answer."`; `title` is the document title (fixed `"Untitled document"` when empty); `where` is `locatorText(locator)` (`Page 3`, `Section 2`, `Characters 120-480`, `Paragraphs 4-6`, `Page 2, region`, else `Somewhere in the document`); `locatorText` is the one from Task 186c, shared with the server's reshapers.
- Produces (web): the page shows a docked column (at least 1100 px wide; an overlay below that) holding `<dm-view name="sources">` and a header **Sources** toggle button (`aria-expanded`); the panel value is `sourcesValue(entry?.citations ?? [], passageId)` for `sourcesOf(state)` and is republished on every state change; `chat.cite` selects the passage and opens the panel; the `source.open` intent (index into the published `sources`) navigates to `/documents/<id>?passage=<passage>`. `performIntent` handles `source.open` when the context carries `held`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { Citation } from "./chats";
import { sourcesValue } from "./sources";

const c = (n: number, over: Partial<Citation> = {}): Citation => ({
  passageId: `00000000-0000-4000-8000-${String(n).padStart(12, "0")}`, documentId: "7f6d1b8e-3b0a-4a56-8f2f-0a8e0f7c4d11", docTitle: `Doc ${n}`,
  locator: { kind: "page", page: n }, snippet: `snippet ${n}`, ...over,
});

describe("sourcesValue", () => {
  it("says sources appear later when there are none", () => expect(sourcesValue([])).toEqual({ note: "Sources appear when the answer is complete.", sources: [] }));
  it("lists each citation with its document, place and snippet, in order", () => {
    const v = sourcesValue([c(1), c(2, { docTitle: "" })]);
    expect(v.note).toBe("2 sources for this answer.");
    expect(v.sources.map((s) => [s.title, s.where, s.snippet])).toEqual([["Doc 1", "Page 1", "snippet 1"], ["Untitled document", "Page 2", "snippet 2"]]);
    expect(sourcesValue([c(1)]).note).toBe("1 source for this answer.");
  });
  it("puts the selected passage first and marks it", () => {
    const v = sourcesValue([c(1), c(2), c(3)], c(3).passageId);
    expect(v.sources.map((s) => s.passageId)).toEqual([c(3).passageId, c(1).passageId, c(2).passageId]);
    expect(v.sources[0]?.where).toBe("Selected - Page 3");
  });
  it("names every kind of place, and a place it does not know", () => {
    const where = (locator: Citation["locator"]) => sourcesValue([c(1, { locator })]).sources[0]?.where;
    expect(where({ kind: "section", index: 1 })).toBe("Section 2");
    expect(where({ kind: "chars", start: 120, end: 480 })).toBe("Characters 120-480");
    expect(where({ kind: "paragraphs", from: 4, to: 6 })).toBe("Paragraphs 4-6");
    expect(where({ kind: "region", page: 2 })).toBe("Page 2, region");
    expect(where({ kind: "page" } as never)).toBe("Somewhere in the document");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.**

`packages/contracts/src/sources.ts`:

```ts
import type { Citation } from "./chats";
import { locatorText } from "./locator";

export interface SourcesData {
  note: string;
  sources: { title: string; where: string; snippet: string; passageId: string; documentId: string }[];
}

/**
 * The data of the `sources` experience, from the citations of one answer. Surface-neutral and pure: the browser publishes it
 * into the view's store because citations are built on read and only the client holds them.
 */
export function sourcesValue(citations: readonly Citation[], selected?: string): SourcesData {
  if (citations.length === 0) return { note: "Sources appear when the answer is complete.", sources: [] };
  const pick = selected === undefined ? undefined : citations.find((c) => c.passageId === selected);
  const ordered = pick ? [pick, ...citations.filter((c) => c !== pick)] : [...citations];
  return {
    note: `${citations.length} ${citations.length === 1 ? "source" : "sources"} for this answer.`,
    sources: ordered.map((c) => ({
      title: c.docTitle === "" ? "Untitled document" : c.docTitle,
      where: `${c === pick ? "Selected - " : ""}${locatorText(c.locator)}`,
      snippet: c.snippet,
      passageId: c.passageId,
      documentId: c.documentId,
    })),
  };
}
```

  (`locatorText` already lives in `contracts/src/locator.ts`, from Task 186c.) `contracts/src/index.ts`: `export * from "./sources";`.

```ts
function setup(path = "/chats") {
  const live = { chats: [chat(1, "Invoices"), chat(2, null)] };

  const services = createServices({ api: api as never, router: new Router(win as never) });

}

    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(rows(p)).toHaveLength(2));
```

```ts
import "./dm-dialog";

/** A modal text prompt. Resolves the trimmed new value, or undefined for Cancel, Escape, an empty value or no change. */
export function promptDialog(heading: string, label: string, initial: string, confirmLabel: string): Promise<string | undefined> {
  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    const dialog = document.createElement("dm-dialog") as HTMLElement & { open: boolean };
    dialog.setAttribute("heading", heading);
    dialog.open = true;
    const input = document.createElement("input");
    input.className = "input";
    input.value = initial;
    input.maxLength = 200;
    input.setAttribute("aria-label", label);
    input.autofocus = true;
    const cancel = document.createElement("button");
    cancel.slot = "actions";
    cancel.className = "btn secondary";
    cancel.textContent = "Cancel";
    const ok = document.createElement("button");
    ok.slot = "actions";
    ok.className = "btn primary";
    ok.textContent = confirmLabel;
    const done = (value: string | undefined) => {
      dialog.remove();
      resolve(value);
    };
    const save = () => {
      const v = input.value.trim();
      done(v === "" || v === initial ? undefined : v);
    };
    cancel.addEventListener("click", () => done(undefined));
    ok.addEventListener("click", save);
    input.addEventListener("keydown", (e) => {
      if (e.key === "Enter") save();
    });
    dialog.addEventListener("dm-close", () => done(undefined));
    dialog.append(input, cancel, ok);
    document.body.append(dialog);
  });
}
```

`components/dm-sessions.ts`:

```ts
import { type Intent, intentOf } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { LitElement, css, html } from "lit";
import { performIntent } from "../surface/perform";
import { ServicesController } from "../state/services-controller";
import { baseStyles } from "../styles/base";
import type { ActionHandler, ViewSession } from "../ui/session";
import { confirmDialog } from "./confirm";
import { promptDialog } from "./prompt";
import "./dm-view";

/** The chat list of the sidebar: the `sessions` experience, and the intents it raises. */
export class DmSessions extends LitElement {
  static styles = [baseStyles, css`:host { display: block; }`];
  private session: ViewSession | undefined;
  private readonly ctx = new ServicesController(this, (s) => [s.router]);
  private refresh = () => this.session?.invalidate("chats");
  connectedCallback(): void {
    super.connectedCallback();
    document.addEventListener("dm-chats-changed", this.refresh);
  }
  disconnectedCallback(): void {
    document.removeEventListener("dm-chats-changed", this.refresh);
    super.disconnectedCallback();
  }
  private act: ActionHandler = async (a) => {
    const session = this.session;
    const svc = this.ctx.services;
    if (!session || !svc) return { ok: false };
    const table = session.valueOf("chats") as { rowKeys?: string[]; rows?: (string | number | null)[][] } | undefined;
    const intent: Intent | undefined = intentOf("sessions", a, { ...(table?.rowKeys && { rowKeys: table.rowKeys }) });
    if (!intent) return { ok: false, reason: "refused" };
    const route = svc.router.get();
    const titleOf = (id: string) => {
      const i = table?.rowKeys?.indexOf(id) ?? -1;
      const title = i < 0 ? "" : String(table?.rows?.[i]?.[0] ?? "");
      return title === "New chat" ? "" : title;
    };
    return { ok: await performIntent(intent, { api: svc.api, router: svc.router, toasts: svc.toasts, session, confirm: confirmDialog, prompt: promptDialog, titleOf, ...(route.name === "chat" && route.chatId && { openChatId: route.chatId }) }) };
  };
  render() {
    return html`<dm-view name="sessions" .onAct=${this.act} @view-ready=${(e: Event) => { this.session = (e as CustomEvent<{ session: ViewSession }>).detail.session; }}></dm-view>`;
  }
}
customElements.define("dm-sessions", DmSessions);
```

  `dm-app-shell.ts`: replace `<div class="list"><slot name="sidebar"></slot></div>` with `<div class="list"><dm-sessions></dm-sessions></div>` and `import "./dm-sessions";`. `components/index.ts`: `import "./dm-sessions";`.

## Phase H: acceptance and documentation

### Task 199a: the fake server and the DOM helpers the acceptance test needs

**Spec:** §8 (Component tests with happy-dom; the Playwright golden path is M7 item 45), §7 Security and accessibility. This task builds the test support; Task 199b uses it. The data views are served by the **real** `@docmind/experiences` (compiled views and `hydrate`) over the fake's own state, so the acceptance test exercises loom-ui, not a stand-in.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/testing/fakeServer.ts`, `apps/web/src/testing/dom.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/testing/fakeServer.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/testing/dom.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Produces:
  - `class FakeServer`: an in-memory implementation of the API the UI uses (auth, documents, content, chats, messages, stop, receipt) and of both event streams. Members: `fetch: typeof fetch`; `xhr(): XMLHttpRequest` (a fake for uploads); `calls: string[]` (`"METHOD /path"` per request); `loggedIn: boolean`, `docs`, `chats`, `messages` (state, readable by a test); `push(path: string, event: string, data: unknown, id?: string): void` (a frame to every open stream whose path matches); `receipt: string`. Behavior is scripted to be deterministic: sign-up answers 202 and logging in with the same credentials works; an upload creates a `uploaded` document and then pushes `parsing`, `embedding` and `ready` progress frames and fills the document's summary, statistics and one passage; sending a message pushes the user's `message`, then `reasoning-partial`, a `tool` step, `answer` deltas and the assistant's `message` (with one citation to the uploaded document's passage); `DELETE /api/me` with the right password answers 202 with `receipt` and logs out; `GET /api/erasure/:receipt` answers `pending` once and then `done`. The UI plane: `GET /api/ui/views/:view` answers the real compiled view for the `web` surface and `POST /api/ui/hydrate` answers the real `hydrate`, with readers over the fake's documents, passages and chats (so uploads, deletions, renames and new chats show in the views). Every request that needs a session answers `401 unauthorized` without one.
  - `deepFind(root: ParentNode, selector: string): Element | null`, `deepAll(root, selector): Element[]`, `deepText(root): string` (text through open shadow roots), `unnamedControls(root: ParentNode): string[]`: descriptors (`tag`, plus `data-*` attribute names; never text) of every button, link with an href, input (not hidden), select, textarea and `[role=tab]` that has no accessible name (`aria-label`, `aria-labelledby`, an associated `<label>`, its own text, `title`, or an `<img alt>` inside), looking through shadow roots.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests.**

`dom.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { deepAll, deepFind, deepText, unnamedControls } from "./dom";

function host(html: string): HTMLElement {
  const el = document.createElement("div");
  const root = el.attachShadow({ mode: "open" });
  root.innerHTML = html;
  document.body.append(el);
  return el;
}

describe("deep DOM helpers", () => {
  it("find, list and read text through nested open shadow roots", () => {
    const outer = host("<p>outer</p><section id='slot'></section>");
    const inner = document.createElement("span");
    inner.attachShadow({ mode: "open" }).innerHTML = "<b class='x'>deep</b>";
    outer.shadowRoot?.getElementById("slot")?.append(inner);
    expect(deepFind(outer, "b.x")?.textContent).toBe("deep");
    expect(deepAll(outer, "p, b").map((e) => e.tagName)).toEqual(["P", "B"]);
    expect(deepText(outer)).toContain("outer");
    expect(deepText(outer)).toContain("deep");
    expect(deepFind(outer, "i")).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("unnamedControls", () => {
  it("accepts a control named by aria-label, aria-labelledby, a label, its text, a title or an image alt", () => {
    const el = host(`
      <button aria-label="Close"></button>
      <span id="n">Name</span><button aria-labelledby="n"></button>
      <label for="e">Email</label><input id="e">
      <label>Wrapped <input></label>
      <button>Send</button>
      <a href="/x" title="Home"></a>
      <a href="/y"><img alt="Logo"></a>
      <select aria-label="Type"></select><textarea aria-label="Message"></textarea>
      <div role="tab" aria-label="Summary"></div>`);
    expect(unnamedControls(el)).toEqual([]);
  });
  it("reports a control with no name, by tag and data attributes only, never by text", () => {
    const el = host(`
      <button data-copy></button>
      <input type="text" placeholder="only a placeholder">
      <a href="/z"></a>
      <input type="hidden">
      <a>no href, not a link</a>
      <button aria-label=""></button>
      <div role="tab"></div>`);
    expect(unnamedControls(el).sort()).toEqual(["a", "button", "button[data-copy]", "input", "tab"].sort());
  });
  it("looks inside shadow roots", () => {
    const outer = host("<section></section>");
    const inner = document.createElement("span");
    inner.attachShadow({ mode: "open" }).innerHTML = "<button data-x></button>";
    outer.shadowRoot?.querySelector("section")?.append(inner);
    expect(unnamedControls(outer)).toEqual(["button[data-x]"]);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { ChatMessageDto, ListDocumentsResponse, ListMessagesResponse, MeResponse } from "@docmind/contracts";
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { FakeServer } from "./fakeServer";

const post = (s: FakeServer, path: string, body?: unknown, method = "POST") =>
  s.fetch(path, { method, body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body) });
const frames = (s: FakeServer, path: string) => {
  const seen: { event: string; data: Record<string, unknown> }[] = [];
  const decoder = new TextDecoder();
  void (async () => {
    const res = await s.fetch(path, {});
    const reader = (res.body as ReadableStream<Uint8Array>).getReader();
    let buffer = "";
    for (;;) {
      const { value, done } = await reader.read();
      if (done) return;
      buffer += decoder.decode(value);
      for (const block of buffer.split("\n\n").slice(0, -1)) {
        const event = /^event: (.*)$/m.exec(block)?.[1];
        const data = /^data: (.*)$/m.exec(block)?.[1];
        if (event && data) seen.push({ event, data: JSON.parse(data) });
      }
      buffer = buffer.slice(buffer.lastIndexOf("\n\n") + 2);
    }
  })();
  return seen;
};
async function signedIn() {
  const s = new FakeServer();
  expect((await post(s, "/api/auth/sign-up", { email: "ada@example.com", password: "correct horse battery" })).status).toBe(202);
  const res = await post(s, "/api/auth/login", { email: "ada@example.com", password: "correct horse battery" });
  expect(MeResponse.safeParse(await res.json()).success).toBe(true);
  return s;
}

describe("FakeServer: auth", () => {
  it("refuses every call that needs a session, and a wrong password, and records the calls", async () => {
    const s = new FakeServer();
    expect((await s.fetch("/api/me", {})).status).toBe(401);
    expect(((await (await s.fetch("/api/documents", {})).json()) as { error: { code: string } }).error.code).toBe("unauthorized");
    await post(s, "/api/auth/sign-up", { email: "ada@example.com", password: "correct horse battery" });
    expect((await post(s, "/api/auth/login", { email: "ada@example.com", password: "wrong" })).status).toBe(401);
    expect(s.calls).toContain("GET /api/me");
  });
  it("erases an account behind the right password and answers the receipt route pending, then done", async () => {
    const s = await signedIn();
    expect((await post(s, "/api/me", { password: "nope" }, "DELETE")).status).toBe(401);
    const res = await post(s, "/api/me", { password: "correct horse battery" }, "DELETE");
    expect(res.status).toBe(202);
    const { receipt } = (await res.json()) as { receipt: string };
    expect(s.loggedIn).toBe(false);
    expect(await (await s.fetch(`/api/erasure/${receipt}`, {})).json()).toEqual({ state: "pending" });
    expect(await (await s.fetch(`/api/erasure/${receipt}`, {})).json()).toEqual({ state: "done" });
    expect((await s.fetch("/api/erasure/unknown", {})).status).toBe(404);
  });
});

describe("FakeServer: documents", () => {
  it("takes an upload, tells the progress stream parsing, embedding and ready, and then serves summary and content", async () => {
    const s = await signedIn();
    const seen = frames(s, "/api/documents/events");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(s.calls).toContain("GET /api/documents/events"));
    const xhr = s.xhr() as unknown as { open(m: string, u: string): void; send(f: FormData): void; onload?: () => void; status: number; responseText: string; upload: { onprogress?: (e: unknown) => void } };
    xhr.open("POST", "/api/documents");
    const form = new FormData();
    form.append("file", new File(["hello"], "contract.pdf"));
    const done = new Promise<void>((r) => { xhr.onload = () => r(); });
    xhr.send(form);
    await done;
    expect(xhr.status).toBe(200);
    const created = JSON.parse(xhr.responseText) as { document: { id: string; status: string }; duplicate: boolean };
    expect(created.document.status).toBe("uploaded");
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(seen.map((f) => (f.data as { status?: string }).status)).toEqual(["parsing", "embedding", "ready"]));
    const list = ListDocumentsResponse.parse(await (await s.fetch("/api/documents", {})).json());
    expect(list.items[0]).toMatchObject({ id: created.document.id, status: "ready", filename: "contract.pdf" });
    expect(list.items[0]?.summary?.tldr).toBeTruthy();
    const content = (await (await s.fetch(`/api/documents/${created.document.id}/content`, {})).json()) as { items: { text: string }[] };
    expect(content.items.length).toBe(1);
    expect((await s.fetch(`/api/documents/${created.document.id}`, { method: "DELETE" })).status).toBe(204);
    expect(ListDocumentsResponse.parse(await (await s.fetch("/api/documents", {})).json()).items).toEqual([]);
  });
});

describe("FakeServer: the UI plane", () => {
  it("serves the real compiled views and hydrates them from its own state; an anonymous caller gets 401", async () => {
    const anon = new FakeServer();
    expect((await anon.fetch("/api/ui/views/library", {})).status).toBe(401);
    const s = await signedIn();
    const view = (await (await s.fetch("/api/ui/views/library", {})).json()) as { experienceId: string; versionHash: string; inventory: { handles: { handle: string }[] } };
    expect(view.inventory.handles.map((h) => h.handle).sort()).toEqual(["doc-ready", "doc-total", "documents", "ingest"]);
    expect((await s.fetch("/api/ui/views/nope", {})).status).toBe(404);
    const load = async () => {
      const res = await post(s, "/api/ui/hydrate", { view: "library", requests: [{ experienceId: view.experienceId, versionHash: view.versionHash, handle: "doc-total", params: {} }] });
      return ((await res.json()) as { results: { state: string; value: { value: number } }[] }).results[0];
    };
    expect((await load())?.value.value).toBe(0);
    const xhr = s.xhr() as unknown as { open(m: string, u: string): void; send(f: FormData): void; onload?: () => void };
    xhr.open("POST", "/api/documents");
    const form = new FormData();
    form.append("file", new File(["hello"], "contract.pdf"));
    const up = new Promise<void>((r) => { xhr.onload = () => r(); });
    xhr.send(form);
    await up;
    expect((await load())?.value.value).toBe(1);
  });
});

describe("FakeServer: chat", () => {
  it("runs a turn on send: the user's message, reasoning, a tool step, answer deltas, then the cited reply", async () => {
    const s = await signedIn();
    const xhr = s.xhr() as unknown as { open(m: string, u: string): void; send(f: FormData): void; onload?: () => void };
    xhr.open("POST", "/api/documents");
    const form = new FormData();
    form.append("file", new File(["hello"], "contract.pdf"));
    const up = new Promise<void>((r) => { xhr.onload = () => r(); });
    xhr.send(form);
    await up;
    const chat = (await (await post(s, "/api/chats", { scope: "all" })).json()) as { id: string };
    const seen = frames(s, `/api/chats/${chat.id}/stream`);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(seen.map((f) => f.event)).toContain("status"));
    expect((await post(s, `/api/chats/${chat.id}/messages`, { text: "When is payment due?" })).status).toBe(202);
    await vi.waitFor(() => expect(seen.some((f) => f.event === "message" && f.data.role === "assistant")).toBe(true));
    expect(seen.map((f) => f.event)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(["message", "reasoning-partial", "tool", "answer"]));
    const history = ListMessagesResponse.parse(await (await s.fetch(`/api/chats/${chat.id}/messages`, {})).json());
    expect(history.items.map((m) => m.role)).toEqual(["user", "assistant"]);
    const reply = ChatMessageDto.parse(history.items[1]);
    expect(reply.citations.length).toBe(1);
    expect(reply.text).toContain(`[[${reply.citations[0]?.passageId}]]`);
    expect(reply.steps.length).toBe(1);
    expect(reply.reasoning?.text).toBeTruthy();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement `testing/dom.ts`.**

```ts
const CONTROLS = "button, a[href], input:not([type=hidden]), select, textarea, [role=tab]";

export function deepAll(root: ParentNode, selector: string): Element[] {
  const out: Element[] = [...root.querySelectorAll(selector)];
  for (const host of root.querySelectorAll("*")) if (host.shadowRoot) out.push(...deepAll(host.shadowRoot, selector));
  if ((root as Element).shadowRoot) out.push(...deepAll((root as Element).shadowRoot as ShadowRoot, selector));
  return out;
}
export const deepFind = (root: ParentNode, selector: string): Element | null => deepAll(root, selector)[0] ?? null;

export function deepText(root: ParentNode): string {
  let text = (root as Element).textContent ?? "";
  const hosts = [...root.querySelectorAll("*")].filter((e) => e.shadowRoot);
  if ((root as Element).shadowRoot) hosts.push(root as Element);
  for (const h of hosts) text += ` ${deepText(h.shadowRoot as ShadowRoot)}`;
  return text;
}

function nameOf(el: Element): string {
  const attr = (n: string) => el.getAttribute(n)?.trim() ?? "";
  if (attr("aria-label")) return attr("aria-label");
  const labelled = attr("aria-labelledby");
  const scope = el.getRootNode() as Document | ShadowRoot;
  if (labelled) {
    const text = labelled.split(/\s+/).map((id) => scope.getElementById?.(id)?.textContent?.trim() ?? "").join(" ").trim();
    if (text) return text;
  }
  const id = el.getAttribute("id");
  const byFor = id ? scope.querySelector?.(`label[for="${id}"]`)?.textContent?.trim() : "";
  if (byFor) return byFor;
  const wrapping = el.closest("label")?.textContent?.trim();
  if (wrapping && el.tagName !== "BUTTON") return wrapping;
  if (attr("title")) return attr("title");
  const alt = el.querySelector("img[alt]")?.getAttribute("alt")?.trim();
  if (alt) return alt;
  if (el.tagName === "BUTTON" || el.tagName === "A" || el.getAttribute("role") === "tab") return el.textContent?.trim() ?? "";
  return "";
}

/** Descriptors of the controls that have no accessible name: the tag and the names of its data attributes, never any text. */
export function unnamedControls(root: ParentNode): string[] {
  return deepAll(root, CONTROLS)
    .filter((el) => nameOf(el) === "")
    .map((el) => {
      const tag = el.getAttribute("role") === "tab" ? "tab" : el.tagName.toLowerCase();
      const data = [...el.attributes].filter((a) => a.name.startsWith("data-")).map((a) => `[${a.name}]`).join("");
      return `${tag}${data}`;
    });
}
```

  (A wrapped control like `<label>Wrapped <input></label>` is named by its label text; a button inside a label is named by its own text. In the `dom.test.ts` case "reports a control with no name", `<input type="text" placeholder="...">` is unnamed because a placeholder is not a name, and an `<a>` without an href is not a control.)

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement `testing/fakeServer.ts`.**

```ts
import { createRequire } from "node:module";
import { type ChatMessageDto, type ChatSessionDto, type DocumentDto, type DocumentPassage, UiHydrateRequest, ViewName } from "@docmind/contracts";
import type { UiReaders } from "@docmind/experiences";

// `@docmind/experiences` is a Node (CommonJS) package: tests load it the way Node does.
const experiences = createRequire(import.meta.url)("@docmind/experiences") as typeof import("@docmind/experiences");
const views = experiences.createViews({ placeholderKey: "k".repeat(32), revisionKey: "r".repeat(32) });

const json = (status: number, body: unknown) => new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { "content-type": "application/json" } });
const fail = (status: number, code: string) => json(status, { error: { code, message: "m" } });
const stamp = () => new Date().toISOString();
const frame = (event: string, data: unknown, id?: string) => `${id ? `id: ${id}\n` : ""}event: ${event}\ndata: ${JSON.stringify(data)}\n\n`;
const tick = async (n = 3) => { for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) await Promise.resolve(); };

interface Account { email: string; password: string; userId: string; tenantId: string }

/** The fake's answer: markdown with a citation marker, raw HTML and an unsafe link in it (see `reply`). */
export const answerMarkdown = (marker: string): string =>
  [
    `Payment is due in **30 days**.${marker}`,
    "",
    "- Net terms apply to every invoice.",
    "- <b>Late</b> fees accrue monthly.",
    "",
    "Read the [terms](https://example.com/terms) or [click](javascript:alert(1)).",
  ].join("\n");

export class FakeServer {
  loggedIn = false;
  docs: DocumentDto[] = [];
  chats: ChatSessionDto[] = [];
  messages = new Map<string, ChatMessageDto[]>();
  calls: string[] = [];
  readonly receipt = "R".repeat(43);
  private account: Account | undefined;
  private passages = new Map<string, DocumentPassage[]>();
  private streams: { path: string; send(chunk: string): void }[] = [];
  private checks = 0;
  private ord = 0;

  private identity() {
    const a = this.account as Account;
    return { userId: a.userId, tenantId: a.tenantId, email: a.email };
  }

  /** A frame to every open stream on `path`. */
  push(path: string, event: string, data: unknown, id?: string): void {
    for (const s of this.streams) if (s.path === path) s.send(frame(event, data, id));
  }
  private open(path: string, first?: string): Response {
    const enc = new TextEncoder();
    let entry: { path: string; send(chunk: string): void } | undefined;
    const body = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
      start: (c) => {
        entry = { path, send: (chunk) => { try { c.enqueue(enc.encode(chunk)); } catch { /* closed */ } } };
        this.streams.push(entry);
        entry.send("retry: 3000\n\n");
        if (first) entry.send(first);
      },
      cancel: () => { this.streams = this.streams.filter((s) => s !== entry); },
    });
    return new Response(body, { status: 200, headers: { "content-type": "text/event-stream" } });
  }

  readonly fetch = (async (input: RequestInfo | URL, init: RequestInit = {}): Promise<Response> => {
    const url = new URL(String(input), "http://fake.test");
    const method = (init.method ?? "GET").toUpperCase();
    const path = url.pathname;
    this.calls.push(`${method} ${path}`);
    const body = typeof init.body === "string" ? (JSON.parse(init.body) as Record<string, unknown>) : {};
    let m: RegExpExecArray | null;

    if (method === "POST" && path === "/api/auth/sign-up") {
      this.account = { email: String(body.email), password: String(body.password), userId: crypto.randomUUID(), tenantId: crypto.randomUUID() };
      return json(202, { status: "accepted" });
    }
    if (method === "POST" && path === "/api/auth/login") {
      if (!this.account || body.email !== this.account.email || body.password !== this.account.password) return fail(401, "invalid_credentials");
      this.loggedIn = true;
      return json(200, this.identity());
    }
    if (method === "POST" && path === "/api/auth/logout") {
      this.loggedIn = false;
      return new Response(null, { status: 204 });
    }
    if ((m = /^\/api\/erasure\/([^/]+)$/.exec(path)) && method === "GET")
      return m[1] === this.receipt ? json(200, { state: ++this.checks <= 1 ? "pending" : "done" }) : fail(404, "not_found");
    if (!this.loggedIn || !this.account) return fail(401, "unauthorized");

    if (path === "/api/me" && method === "GET") return json(200, this.identity());
    if (path === "/api/me" && method === "DELETE") {
      if (body.password !== this.account.password) return fail(401, "invalid_credentials");
      this.loggedIn = false;
      this.checks = 0;
      return json(202, { erasure: "accepted", receipt: this.receipt });
    }

    if ((m = /^\/api\/ui\/views\/([^/]+)$/.exec(path)) && method === "GET") {
      const name = ViewName.safeParse(m[1]);
      return name.success ? json(200, views.payload(name.data, "web")) : fail(404, "not_found");
    }
    if (path === "/api/ui/hydrate" && method === "POST") {
      const req = UiHydrateRequest.safeParse(body);
      if (!req.success) return fail(400, "invalid_request");
      return json(200, { results: await views.hydrate(req.data.view, req.data.requests as never, this.readers(), this.account.userId) });
    }
    if (path === "/api/documents/events" && method === "GET") return this.open(path);
    if (path === "/api/documents" && method === "GET") return json(200, { items: [...this.docs], nextCursor: null });
    if ((m = /^\/api\/documents\/([^/]+)$/.exec(path))) {
      const doc = this.docs.find((d) => d.id === m?.[1]);
      if (!doc) return fail(404, "not_found");
      if (method === "GET") return json(200, doc);
      if (method === "DELETE") {
        this.docs = this.docs.filter((d) => d !== doc);
        return new Response(null, { status: 204 });
      }
    }
    if ((m = /^\/api\/documents\/([^/]+)\/content$/.exec(path)) && method === "GET")
      return this.docs.some((d) => d.id === m?.[1]) ? json(200, { items: this.passages.get(m[1] as string) ?? [], nextCursor: null }) : fail(404, "not_found");

    if (path === "/api/chats" && method === "GET") return json(200, { items: [...this.chats].reverse(), nextCursor: null });
    if (path === "/api/chats" && method === "POST") {
      const chat: ChatSessionDto = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), title: null, scope: (body.scope as ChatSessionDto["scope"]) ?? "all", createdAt: stamp(), lastMessageAt: null, messageCount: 0 };
      this.chats.push(chat);
      this.messages.set(chat.id, []);
      return json(200, chat);
    }
    if ((m = /^\/api\/chats\/([^/]+)(\/messages|\/stream|\/stop)?$/.exec(path))) {
      const chat = this.chats.find((c) => c.id === m?.[1]);
      if (!chat) return fail(404, "not_found");
      const sub = m[2];
      if (!sub && method === "GET") return json(200, chat);
      if (!sub && method === "PATCH") { Object.assign(chat, body); return json(200, chat); }
      if (!sub && method === "DELETE") { this.chats = this.chats.filter((c) => c !== chat); return new Response(null, { status: 204 }); }
      if (sub === "/messages" && method === "GET") return json(200, { items: [...(this.messages.get(chat.id) ?? [])], nextCursor: null });
      if (sub === "/stop" && method === "POST") return json(202, { stopped: false });
      if (sub === "/stream" && method === "GET") return this.open(path, frame("status", { state: "open", running: false, turns: (this.messages.get(chat.id) ?? []).filter((x) => x.role === "user").length }));
      if (sub === "/messages" && method === "POST") {
        const text = String(body.text);
        const user = this.record(chat, "user", text);
        this.push(`/api/chats/${chat.id}/stream`, "message", { ...user }, user.cursor);
        void this.runTurn(chat, user.turn);
        return json(202, { messageId: String(body.messageId ?? "m") });
      }
    }
    return fail(404, "not_found");
  }) as typeof fetch;

  /** The readers the server's UI plane would be given, over this fake's state. */
  private readers(): UiReaders {
    const ready = new Set(["uploaded", "parsing", "embedding"]);
    return {
      documents: async ({ limit, status }) => ({
        items: this.docs.filter((d) => status === "all" || (status === "processing" ? ready.has(d.status) : d.status === status)).slice(0, limit),
        truncated: false,
      }),
      document: async ({ id }) => this.docs.find((d) => d.id === id) ?? null,
      content: async ({ id, upto, passage }) => {
        if (!this.docs.some((d) => d.id === id)) return null;
        const all = this.passages.get(id) ?? [];
        const keep = Math.max(upto, passage === undefined ? 0 : all.findIndex((p) => p.passageId === passage) + 1);
        return { items: all.slice(0, keep), more: all.length > keep };
      },
      chats: async ({ q }) => [...this.chats].reverse().filter((c) => (c.title ?? "New chat").toLowerCase().includes(q.toLowerCase())),
    };
  }

  private record(chat: ChatSessionDto, role: "user" | "assistant", text: string, over: Partial<ChatMessageDto> = {}): ChatMessageDto {
    const list = this.messages.get(chat.id) as ChatMessageDto[];
    const turn = role === "user" ? list.filter((x) => x.role === "user").length + 1 : list.filter((x) => x.role === "user").length;
    const ord = ++this.ord;
    const message: ChatMessageDto = { id: String(ord), turn, role, text, status: "complete", citations: [], cursor: `1.${ord * 10}`, reasoning: null, steps: [], outcome: null, createdAt: stamp(), ...over };
    list.push(message);
    chat.messageCount = list.length;
    chat.lastMessageAt = message.createdAt;
    return message;
  }

  /** The scripted answer to a question: thinking, one search step, a streamed answer, then the cited reply. */
  private async runTurn(chat: ChatSessionDto, turn: number): Promise<void> {
    const stream = `/api/chats/${chat.id}/stream`;
    const doc = this.docs[0];
    const passage = doc ? this.passages.get(doc.id)?.[0] : undefined;
    await tick();
    this.push(stream, "reasoning-partial", { turn, text: "Looking through the documents" });
    await tick();
    const step = { name: "search_docs" as const, label: "Searched documents", count: passage ? 1 : 0 };
    this.push(stream, "tool", { turnKey: turn, step });
    await tick();
    const marker = passage ? ` [[${passage.passageId}]]` : "";
    // The answer is markdown on purpose, and hostile on purpose: emphasis split across two deltas, a list, raw HTML, a safe link and a
    // `javascript:` link. loom-ui must draw the first three as elements and leave the last two inert (Task 199b).
    const body = answerMarkdown(marker);
    this.push(stream, "answer", { turn, text: body.slice(0, 21) });
    this.push(stream, "answer", { turn, text: body.slice(21) });
    await tick();
    const citations = doc && passage ? [{ passageId: passage.passageId, documentId: doc.id, docTitle: doc.title, locator: passage.locator, snippet: passage.text }] : [];
    const reply = this.record(chat, "assistant", body, { citations, steps: [step], reasoning: { text: "Looking through the documents", kind: "summary" } });
    this.push(stream, "message", { ...reply }, reply.cursor);
  }

  /** A fake XMLHttpRequest for `uploadDocument`: stores the file as a document and walks it to ready on the progress stream. */
  xhr(): XMLHttpRequest {
    const server = this;
    const x = {
      status: 0, responseText: "", withCredentials: false, responseType: "", upload: {} as { onprogress?: (e: unknown) => void },
      onload: undefined as (() => void) | undefined, onerror: undefined as (() => void) | undefined, ontimeout: undefined as (() => void) | undefined, onabort: undefined as (() => void) | undefined,
      open() {}, setRequestHeader() {}, getResponseHeader: () => null, abort() { x.onabort?.(); },
      send(form: FormData) {
        void (async () => {
          await tick();
          server.calls.push("POST /api/documents");
          if (!server.loggedIn) { x.status = 401; x.responseText = JSON.stringify({ error: { code: "unauthorized", message: "m" } }); return x.onload?.(); }
          const file = form.get("file") as File;
          x.upload.onprogress?.({ lengthComputable: true, loaded: 50, total: 100 });
          const doc = server.addDocument(file.name, file.size);
          x.status = 200;
          x.responseText = JSON.stringify({ document: doc, duplicate: false });
          x.onload?.();
          void server.process(doc.id);
        })();
      },
    };
    return x as unknown as XMLHttpRequest;
  }
  private addDocument(filename: string, size: number): DocumentDto {
    const doc: DocumentDto = { id: crypto.randomUUID(), title: filename.replace(/\.[^.]+$/, ""), filename, mime: "application/pdf", sizeBytes: size, status: "uploaded", error: null, stats: null, summary: null, createdAt: stamp(), updatedAt: stamp() };
    this.docs.unshift(doc);
    return doc;
  }
  private async process(id: string): Promise<void> {
    const at = () => this.docs.find((d) => d.id === id);
    for (const [status, step] of [["parsing", "parse"], ["embedding", "embed"]] as const) {
      await tick();
      const d = at();
      if (!d) return;
      d.status = status;
      this.push("/api/documents/events", "progress", { documentId: id, step, status });
    }
    await tick();
    const d = at();
    if (!d) return;
    this.passages.set(id, [{ passageId: crypto.randomUUID(), ordinal: 0, locator: { kind: "page", page: 1 }, headingPath: ["Payment terms"], text: "Payment is due within 30 days of the invoice date." }]);
    Object.assign(d, {
      status: "ready",
      stats: { wordCount: 420, pageCount: 2, readingTimeMinutes: 2, language: "en", outline: ["Payment terms"], dates: [], numbers: ["30 days"] },
      summary: { tldr: "Payment is due in 30 days.", keyPoints: ["Net 30"], entities: [], topics: ["Payments"], suggestedQuestions: ["When is payment due?"] },
    });
    this.push("/api/documents/events", "progress", { documentId: id, step: "finalize", status: "ready" });
  }
}
```

  (`server = this` inside `xhr()` is the one place a self-reference alias is needed, because the fake XHR object has its own `this`. Use an arrow-function-free object literal as written; Biome's `noUselessThisAlias` allows it when the alias is used from a nested function.)

- [ ] **Step 5: Run them.** `pnpm --filter @docmind/web exec vitest run src/testing`. Expected: PASS (dom 4, server 6). 
  **Probe.** If `getElementById` is missing on a shadow root in the installed happy-dom, `nameOf`'s `scope.getElementById?.` returns `undefined` and the `aria-labelledby` test fails; fall back to `scope.querySelector(\`#${CSS.escape(id)}\`)` there.

- [ ] **Step 6: Gate and commit.**

```bash
pnpm lint:fix && pnpm build && pnpm typecheck && pnpm typecheck:tests && pnpm lint && pnpm test
git add apps/web
git commit -m "test(web): a fake server and DOM helpers for the acceptance test

Co-Authored-By: <executing model>"
```

### Task 199b: acceptance: the whole app, from sign-up to a cited answer, a reload and an erasure

**Spec:** §8 (the E2E row: "Sign up → upload → ready → ask → cited answer → reload → history intact"; here against the in-process fake server in happy-dom, with Playwright and the real server in M7 item 45), §7 Security and accessibility, the Global Constraints (nothing sensitive in the console or in browser storage), Decision D14 (the data views are loom-ui's, drawn from the real compiled experiences).

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/acceptance.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `FakeServer` and the DOM helpers (199a), `createServices`, `Router`, `dm-app` and every page.
- Produces: one scenario test, nothing else. No production code: if a step fails, the defect is in the task that built the element, and the fix belongs there (a follow-up commit on that task's branch, or a new small PR), not in this test.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test** `acceptance.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { Router } from "./router";
import { createServices } from "./services";
import { deepAll, deepFind, deepText, unnamedControls } from "./testing/dom";
import { FakeServer } from "./testing/fakeServer";
import "./components";

// Obviously fake values; the test fails if any of them is ever logged, stored or sent in a URL.
const SENTINEL = {
  email: "ada.sentinel@example.com",
  password: "sentinel-password-0123456789",
  file: "sentinel-contract.pdf",
  title: "sentinel-contract",
  question: "sentinel-question: when is payment due?",
};
const wait = (check: () => unknown) => vi.waitFor(check, { timeout: 8000 });

function boot(server: FakeServer, path: string) {
  const win = {
    location: { pathname: path, search: "" },
    history: {
      pushState: (_s: unknown, _t: string, u: string) => { const url = new URL(u, "http://fake.test"); win.location.pathname = url.pathname; win.location.search = url.search; },
      replaceState: (_s: unknown, _t: string, u: string) => { const url = new URL(u, "http://fake.test"); win.location.pathname = url.pathname; win.location.search = url.search; },
    },
    addEventListener: () => {},
    removeEventListener: () => {},
  };
  const services = createServices({ fetch: server.fetch, xhr: () => server.xhr(), router: new Router(win as never) });
  const app = document.createElement("dm-app") as HTMLElement & { services: typeof services };
  app.services = services;
  document.body.append(app);
  return { app, services };
}
const find = (root: ParentNode, selector: string) => deepFind(root, selector) as HTMLElement;
const text = (root: ParentNode) => deepText(root).replace(/\s+/g, " ");
const labelled = (root: ParentNode, selector: string, label: string) => deepAll(root, selector).find((e) => e.getAttribute("aria-label") === label) as HTMLButtonElement | undefined;
const follow = (a: Element) => a.dispatchEvent(new MouseEvent("click", { bubbles: true, composed: true, cancelable: true }));
const type = (input: Element, value: string) => { (input as HTMLInputElement).value = value; input.dispatchEvent(new Event("input", { bubbles: true })); };
const submit = (form: Element) => form.dispatchEvent(new Event("submit", { cancelable: true, bubbles: true }));
const route = (p: ReturnType<typeof boot>) => p.services.router.get();

describe("M6 acceptance", () => {
  it("sign up, upload a document, ask about it, read the cited answer, reload, rename the chat, then delete the account", async () => {
    const logged: string[] = [];
    for (const level of ["log", "info", "warn", "error", "debug"] as const)
      vi.spyOn(console, level).mockImplementation((...args: unknown[]) => { logged.push(args.map(String).join(" ")); });
    const server = new FakeServer();

    // 1. A visitor with no session is sent to sign in.
    let page = boot(server, "/documents");
    await wait(() => expect(route(page)).toEqual({ name: "sign-in" }));
    await wait(() => expect(find(page.app, "form")).not.toBeNull());
    expect(unnamedControls(page.app)).toEqual([]);

    // 2. Sign up. Sign-up answers 202, the app signs in, and the first chat screen opens inside the shell, with the chat list beside it.
    page.services.router.navigate({ name: "sign-up" });
    await wait(() => expect(text(page.app)).toContain("Create your account"));
    type(find(page.app, "input[type=email]"), SENTINEL.email);
    type(find(page.app, "input[name=password]"), SENTINEL.password);
    submit(find(page.app, "form"));
    await wait(() => expect(route(page)).toEqual({ name: "chat" }));
    await wait(() => expect(find(page.app, "dm-sessions")).not.toBeNull());
    await wait(() => expect(find(page.app, "lu-chat")).not.toBeNull());
    expect(unnamedControls(page.app)).toEqual([]);

    // 3. The library is empty (a loom-ui Note); upload a file; it becomes Ready on the live stream, in a loom-ui Table.
    follow(find(page.app, "a[data-documents]"));
    await wait(() => expect(route(page)).toEqual({ name: "documents" }));
    await wait(() => expect(text(page.app)).toContain("No documents yet"));
    const zone = find(page.app, "dm-upload-zone");
    const input = zone.shadowRoot?.querySelector("input[type=file]") as HTMLInputElement;
    Object.defineProperty(input, "files", { value: [new File(["%PDF-1.7 fake"], SENTINEL.file)], configurable: true });
    input.dispatchEvent(new Event("change"));
    await wait(() => expect(find(page.app, "lu-table")).not.toBeNull());
    await wait(() => expect(text(find(page.app, "lu-table"))).toContain(SENTINEL.title));
    await wait(() => expect(text(find(page.app, "lu-table"))).toContain("Ready"));
    expect(unnamedControls(page.app)).toEqual([]);

    // 4. Open the document from its row: its summary (loom-ui A2UI), analysis and tabs; ask a suggested question.
    labelled(page.app, "td.row-actions button", `Open: ${SENTINEL.title}`)?.click();
    await wait(() => expect(route(page).name).toBe("document"));
    await wait(() => expect(text(page.app)).toContain("Payment is due in 30 days."));
    expect(deepAll(page.app, "[role=tab]").map((t) => t.textContent?.trim())).toEqual(["Summary", "Analysis", "Content"]);
    expect(unnamedControls(page.app)).toEqual([]);
    const ask = deepAll(page.app, "button").find((b) => b.textContent?.trim() === "When is payment due?") as HTMLButtonElement;
    ask.click();

    // 5. The chat opens scoped to the document, the question is sent for us, and the answer streams and ends cited.
    await wait(() => expect(route(page).name).toBe("chat"));
    const chatId = (route(page) as { chatId: string }).chatId;
    await wait(() => expect(find(page.app, "lu-chat button.ref")).not.toBeNull());
    expect(find(page.app, "lu-chat button.ref").textContent).toBe("1");
    expect(text(find(page.app, "lu-chat"))).toContain("When is payment due?");
    expect(text(find(page.app, "lu-chat"))).toContain("Searched documents");
    expect(text(find(page.app, "lu-chat"))).not.toContain("[[");
    // The sources panel is the `sources` experience: the card names the document, and the marker selects it.
    await wait(() => expect(text(find(page.app, "aside"))).toContain(SENTINEL.title));
    find(page.app, "lu-chat button.ref").click();
    await wait(() => expect(text(find(page.app, "aside"))).toContain("Selected - Page 1"));
    expect(unnamedControls(page.app)).toEqual([]);

    // 5b. Ask our own question in the composer (a loom-ui TextInput).
    const box = find(page.app, "lu-text-input textarea, lu-text-input input");
    type(box, SENTINEL.question);
    submit(box.closest("form") as HTMLElement);
    await wait(() => expect(text(find(page.app, "lu-chat"))).toContain(SENTINEL.question));
    await wait(() => expect(deepAll(page.app, "lu-chat button.ref").length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2));

    // 5c. The answer is markdown, drawn as elements by loom-ui: emphasis, a list and a safe link; raw HTML and an unsafe link stay inert.
    const answer = find(page.app, "lu-chat .md");
    expect(answer.querySelector("strong")?.textContent).toBe("30 days");
    expect(answer.querySelectorAll("ul > li")).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(answer.textContent).toContain("<b>Late</b> fees accrue monthly.");
    expect(answer.querySelector("b")).toBeNull();
    expect([...answer.querySelectorAll("a")].map((a) => a.getAttribute("href"))).toEqual(["https://example.com/terms"]);
    expect(answer.querySelector("a")?.getAttribute("rel")).toBe("noopener noreferrer nofollow");
    expect(answer.innerHTML).not.toContain("javascript:");
    expect(answer.textContent).not.toContain("**");

    // 6. "Open in document" goes to the passage in the Content tab.
    const open = deepAll(find(page.app, "aside"), "button").find((b) => b.textContent?.trim() === "Open in document") as HTMLButtonElement;
    open.click();
    await wait(() => expect(route(page).name).toBe("document"));
    expect((route(page) as { passage?: string }).passage).toBeTruthy();
    await wait(() => expect(find(page.app, "[role=tab][aria-selected=true]")?.textContent?.trim()).toBe("Content"));
    await wait(() => expect(text(page.app)).toContain("Cited passage"));
    expect(text(page.app)).toContain("Payment is due within 30 days of the invoice date.");

    // 7. Reload: a new page over the same server. The session is still there and the history comes back whole, citations included.
    page.app.remove();
    page = boot(server, `/chats/${chatId}`);
    await wait(() => expect(find(page.app, "lu-chat button.ref")).not.toBeNull());
    expect(server.calls.filter((c) => c === "GET /api/me").length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
    expect(text(find(page.app, "lu-chat"))).toContain("Payment is due in 30 days.");
    expect(text(find(page.app, "lu-chat"))).toContain("Searched documents");
    expect(unnamedControls(page.app)).toEqual([]);

    // 7b. Rename the chat from the sidebar's own row action.
    await wait(() => expect(labelled(page.app, "td.row-actions button", "Rename: New chat")).toBeDefined());
    labelled(page.app, "td.row-actions button", "Rename: New chat")?.click();
    await wait(() => expect(document.querySelector("dm-dialog input")).not.toBeNull());
    (document.querySelector("dm-dialog input") as HTMLInputElement).value = "Payment terms";
    ([...document.querySelectorAll("dm-dialog button")].find((b) => b.textContent === "Save") as HTMLButtonElement).click();
    await wait(() => expect(text(find(page.app, "dm-sessions"))).toContain("Payment terms"));

    // 8. Settings: delete the account with the password, read the receipt once, and check the state.
    follow(find(page.app, "a[href='/settings']"));
    await wait(() => expect(text(page.app)).toContain("Delete account"));
    expect(unnamedControls(page.app)).toEqual([]);
    find(page.app, "button[data-delete]").click();
    await wait(() => expect(deepFind(page.app, "input[type=password]")).not.toBeNull());
    type(find(page.app, "input[type=password]"), SENTINEL.password);
    find(page.app, "button[data-confirm]").click();
    await wait(() => expect(route(page)).toEqual({ name: "account-deleted" }));
    await wait(() => expect(find(page.app, "[data-receipt]").textContent).toBe(server.receipt));
    find(page.app, "button[data-check]").click();
    await wait(() => expect(text(page.app)).toContain("In progress"));
    find(page.app, "button[data-check]").click();
    await wait(() => expect(text(page.app)).toContain("Done."));
    page.app.remove();

    // 9. The data views were loom-ui's: the fake served the REAL compiled experiences, and every stamped action was fresh.
    expect(server.calls.filter((c) => c === "GET /api/ui/views/library").length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    expect(server.calls).toContain("POST /api/ui/hydrate");

    // 10. What the browser kept and said: nothing sensitive, anywhere.
    const secrets = [SENTINEL.email, SENTINEL.password, SENTINEL.file, SENTINEL.title, SENTINEL.question, server.receipt, "Payment is due"];
    for (const line of logged) for (const secret of secrets) expect(line.includes(secret), "console line carried a secret").toBe(false);
    expect(Object.keys(localStorage).every((k) => k === "dm-theme")).toBe(true);
    expect(JSON.stringify(Object.entries(localStorage))).not.toMatch(new RegExp(secrets.map((s) => s.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, "\\$&")).join("|")));
    expect(sessionStorage.length).toBe(0);
    for (const call of server.calls) for (const secret of [SENTINEL.email, SENTINEL.title, SENTINEL.question, SENTINEL.password]) expect(call.includes(secret)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Run it.** `pnpm --filter @docmind/web exec vitest run src/acceptance.test.ts`.
  Expected: PASS in one run. This is a test of work that already exists (Tasks 177 to 199a), so it should pass the first time; it is written last because it needs all of it.
  **If a step times out** (the failure names the `wait` that expired), diagnose with names only, never content: add a temporary `console.info(deepAll(page.app, "*").map((e) => e.localName).join(","))` before the wait (remove it afterwards), and read which screen is showing. The usual causes: (a) an element is rendered after the services arrive through the context, so `find` is called one microtask early (wrap in `wait`); (b) happy-dom's `click()` on an `<a>` does not bubble `composed`, so use `follow(a)`; (c) the fake XHR's `onload` is assigned after `send` (the order in `uploadDocument` is: handlers first, then `send`); (d) a `lu-*` element is drawn in a hidden tab (the panel is `hidden`), so click the tab first; (e) the sidebar's table needs the new chat in the fake's `chats` list, which the fake adds on `POST /api/chats`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Check the test is not vacuous.** (The markdown block, step 5c, is not vacuous by construction: the fake's answer carries the raw `<b>` and the `javascript:` link, so an app that parsed HTML or kept the link fails it. To see it fail, temporarily mark the fold's feed `markdown: false`: `.md` is gone and 5c goes red; revert.) Temporarily add `console.info(d.title)` to the library reshaper's caller in `dm-documents-page.ts` (or any `console` call with a title): the final console check must go red with "console line carried a secret", and the `guards.test.ts` of Task 177 must not catch it (it does not scan `console`; `report` is the only logger by convention). Revert.

## Execution notes

Probes are small checks the implementer makes where the plan could not run the code (this plan was written without installing the web dependencies; the loom-ui facts were probed against the built loom-ui). Each has a written fallback, and none needs a Loom change.

| Task and step | Probe | Fallback |
|---|---|---|
| 173a step 5 | The metadata keys of a real raw log are a subset of `kernel`, `fence`, `computeTarget` | A key outside the list that holds an id, number or boolean is added to `METADATA` with that shape; a key that can hold text stops the task and goes to the owner |
| 173b step 4 | The running-turn test parks the shred at least once, so `__loom_erasure__` has a row for the user | Make the park deterministic (wait for the eraser's first `ErasureParkedError`), never weaken the assertion |
| 176a, 176b | The differential test of `kindValues`, the messages catalogue test and the bundle budgets accept the new code | Add the fixtures and raise a budget by the measured amount, saying so in the PR |
| 176c to 176k | Biome accepts the code as written (`pnpm lint:fix` may reflow it); the `kindValues` fuzz and differential tests accept the `format` key; the bundle budgets accept the parser in `chat-ui`; `i18nFitness` and `genericity` pass on the new text; the terminal registry test and the hydration test (written, not run when this plan was written: they need loom-ui built) pass | Adapt the test's expectation only where it is a formatting or fixture matter; never loosen a safety assertion (links, images, html, bounds) |
| 177 step 5 | Vitest resolves the loom-ui entries to their ESM build | Add `resolve.conditions: ["import", "browser"]` |
| 177 (CI) | `LOOM_TOKEN` can read `chhetrisushil/loom-ui`; loom-ui builds against Loom `c81d521a` | Ask the owner for a token with both repositories |
| 178a step 4 | Every colour pair passes 4.5:1 in both themes, `--lu-*` included | Nudge the failing token in that theme only and record it in `ui-mocks.md`; never lower the threshold |
| 178b step 3 | `@phosphor-icons/core/assets/regular/<name>.svg?raw` resolves | Use the path the package's `exports` map exposes |
| 184a step 3 | happy-dom's `assignedElements` answers for the dialog's slots | Use the host's light-DOM children |
| 186a to 186e | `serveHandles`' option names (`maxBatch`, `principalId`), the `version-mismatch` code, the `MCPToolNode.kind` field and the catalogue entry shape | Read `split/server.ts` and `core/src/node.ts` and use the real names; the tests pin behavior |
| 189 step 4 | `SplitHydrationController.setParams(params, { handle })`; `HydrationStore.runtimeSubscribe`; the nesting of `lu-a2ui-widget` | Read the installed d.ts and adapt the call, not the design |
| 191 to 198 | happy-dom lays nothing out; `lu-*` shadow roots are open; a submit on a `lu-text-input` form fires its action | Dispatch the event the widget listens to (read `lu-inputs.ts`) |
| 195 step 4 | `ChatFeed` shows a streaming entry from `reasoning-partial` alone and keeps live reasoning on the durable entry | Read `liveEntry()` and `afterDurable()` in `chatFeed.ts`; change a test's expectation only where the feed's behavior is by design |
| 199b step 2 | Every wait finds its element in one run | The notes of that step |

Other things the implementer must know:

- **No Loom change** is part of this plan (Finding L1, open questions 8 and 16). loom-ui changes only through Tasks 176a to 176k. If a web task seems to need another loom-ui change, stop: it is an owner decision, and the answer is almost certainly "solve it in `apps/web`" (surface-specific) or "in `@docmind/experiences`" (surface-neutral).
- **Surface neutrality is checked, not hoped for:** `guards.test.ts` (Task 177) keeps `apps/web` free of composition imports, `terminal.test.ts` (Task 186e) keeps the experiences drawable without a DOM, and `intents.test.ts` (Task 186d) keeps the action vocabulary in one place.
- **Order of the pages.** 185 (shell) and 189 (host) are routed to nothing by themselves; the pages register their routes in `views.ts` as they land: `sign-in` and `sign-up` (183), `settings` and `account-deleted` (184b), `documents` (191), `document` (192), `chat` (196). The shell is drawn around every signed-in route from 185; its chat list appears with 198.
- **Services grow task by task** (`auth` 183, `receipt` 184b, `uploads` 190, `outbox` 196). Each task edits `services.ts`; tests build a `Services` from `createServices`' defaults.
- **Lockfile and migrations.** Tasks 177, 178b, 186a, 188 change `pnpm-lock.yaml`; list it in the PR body. Task 174a is the only migration; it is `0025`.
- **Formatting.** Code blocks are written for readability and some lines exceed Biome's width; each task's lint step is `pnpm lint:fix && pnpm lint`.
- **Draft PRs and the gate.** Every PR is a draft, rebase-merged, with `DOCMIND_TEST_PG_URL` exported in the same command as the gate. Tasks 173 to 175, 182, 187 and 188 are server tasks and need it; the web tasks do not, but run the whole gate anyway.
- **loom-ui PRs** (176a to 176k) are separate, generic, and merged before 177b. If the owner wants them reviewed by the loom-ui maintainers first, everything else up to 186b can proceed on the old pin.
- **No Playwright here.** M7 item 45 runs the real server and a real browser. Task 199b is the component-level substitute and is expected to be replaced by it, not extended.

## Self-review

**Spec coverage (spec §9 items 35 to 44, §7, §10, and the M5 carries).**
- Item 35 (the spike): Task 176 records the probes, the composition decision and the two generic requests (Findings U1, U13 to U20).
- Item 36 (scaffold, aliases, router, tokens, API client, SSE helper): 177 (package, links, CI), 178a and 178b (tokens, theme, icons, contrast), 179 (client), 180 (SSE), 181 (router, services, app), 182 (serving it).
- Item 37 (sign-in, sign-up, account settings, Delete my account): 183, 184a, 184b (the receipt page included).
- Item 38 (app shell and session sidebar): 185 (shell), 198 (the chat list as an experience).
- Item 39 (chat view, history paging, streaming, thinking): 193 to 196 (loom-ui's `Chat` draws the transcript, tool lines and reasoning). Item 40 (citation markers, sources panel): 176b (markers) and 197. Item 41 (composer, scope, stop, regenerate): 196 (loom-ui `TextInput` and `SelectInput`; stop and regenerate are page chrome). Item 42 (upload zone): 190. Item 43 (library): 186a to 186c, 188, 191. Item 44 (document detail tabs): 186b, 186c, 187, 192.
- §7 Security and accessibility: origin guard (same-origin requests, the dev proxy keeps it), text-only rendering (D6, `guards.test.ts`), stale-view checks on every action (189), `aria-live` and keyboard semantics from loom-ui, AA contrast (178a), the CSP (182), unnamed-control scans (199b).
- §10 risks: the `lu-chat` hook (176, 176b), Loom CJS-only in the browser (U6), the lockstep CI checkout (177).
- M5 carries: (a) F26 in 173b; (b) metadata shape in 173a; (c) keyset paging in 174a to 174c; (d) the Loom residual in Finding L1 and Task 175.
- Owner decision 4 (markdown in answers): the design section and Tasks 176c to 176k (loom-ui), 193 (`ChatFold` marks assistant parts), 195 (the format survives `toTranscript`), 199a and 199b (a markdown answer with a citation, raw HTML and an unsafe link, proven inert), 200 (docs, security note).
- The owner's rules: loom-ui native (D10, D11, D13), composition location (D10), generic loom-ui first (176a to 176k, 177b), `dm-*` only for what loom-ui does not draw (D13), tenancy intact (188), surface-agnostic (D14, 186d, 186e, guards).

**Placeholder scan.** Every code step shows its code. The `<local cluster URL>` in the gate commands stands for a value the owner's shell already holds and this plan must not print. `<executing model>` in commit trailers is the model that runs the task. The loom-ui tasks 176a and 176b give contracts, tests and the code of the new files, and describe the edits to existing files (`lu-table.ts`, `lu-chat.ts`, `chatValue.ts`) by the lines they replace, because those files belong to another repository the executor reads first (its `AGENTS.md`). Tasks 176c to 176k give the complete code of every new file and every edit to an existing file as exact old and new text; that code was run in scratch copies of loom-ui's sources when this plan was written (the parser stages one by one, the contract and feed tests, the web renderer against a happy-dom `lu-chat` with 176b's markers applied, the conformance kit with its six faults against the reference renderer); the terminal registry test and the hydration test were written in the style of their neighbours but not run. The probes in the execution notes each have a written fallback.

**Type consistency.** Names are the catalogue's. The ones most likely to drift: `ViewSession.start/mount/dispatch/isFresh/publish`; `UiReaders.documents/document/content/chats`; `Views.payload(name, surface)` and `Views.hydrate(name, requests, readers, principalId)`; `intentOf(view, action, held)` and `Intent`'s `type` strings; `ChatFold.applyStream` (not `applyEvent`) and `resetLive`; `ChatController.send/regenerate` return `SendOutcome`; `ChatViewState.transcript`; `UploadsStore.startEvents(onProgress)` with `"resync"`; `Services.outbox` is a `Store<Outbox>`; `ServicesController` is how every element reaches `Services`; `ThemeStore.choose` is the setter.

**Known risks.**
1. The first full run of the web suite is the first time these elements meet happy-dom; the probes above are where it most likely needs a small adjustment. They change tests or one line of an element, not the design.
2. `ChatFeed` bounds a window to 500 entries (250 turns). "Load earlier" stops adding past that. A chat that long needs a notice; it is not built.
3. The stream's `status` frame is sent once (Finding U12). A reply that never arrives (a server crash mid-turn) shows "pending" until the user presses Stop or reloads.
4. The sources panel cannot show "N cited so far" while a reply streams (open question 7).
5. Chat is gated by `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` on the server (503 `chat_unavailable`); the UI says so in a notice.
6. Task 182 serves static files from a directory the operator names. Its tests cover traversal, dotfiles and symlinks; the CSP is the second line of defence.
7. `LOOM_UI_REF` must be a loom-ui commit that builds against Loom `c81d521a`; the CI probe in Task 177 is where a mismatch would show. loom-ui's `main` after Tasks 176a and 176b is what Task 177b pins.
9. The hand-written markdown parser is the largest piece of code M6 asks of loom-ui (about 700 lines with its helpers). Its tests pin the subset, the link rule and the bounds, but it is not CommonMark-complete by design. If loom-ui's maintainers prefer a dependency, open question 4's alternative can sit behind `parseChatMarkdown`'s signature without touching any renderer or DocMind.
8. The UI plane imports the root of `@loom-ui/hydration` on the server, which loads QuickJS and the MCP plugin (about 190 ms at start). It is server-only and never reaches the browser bundle (the build fails on `node:crypto`); if the owner prefers not to load them, the alternative is a small DocMind `serve` that skips providers, giving up loom-ui's batching and contract checks.
